FILE: .github/workflows/dev-pipe.yml

name: Dev Pipeline

on:
  push:
    branches: ["feature/dev-pipe", "feature/pipelines"]
    paths:
      - "dev_pipe/**"
      - "dev_agent/**" # gate checks used by Steps 4 and 5
      - "prompts/dev/**"
      - "config/dev-pipe.yaml"
      - "azure_openai_proxy/**"
      - ".claude/skills/**" # agent skills copied into the checkout
      - "sample_app/**" # local-mode target app
      - "requirements*.txt"
      - ".github/workflows/dev-pipe.yml"
      - ".github/workflows/dev-pipe-approval.yml"
      - ".github/actions/setup-dev-pipe/**"
      - ".github/actions/save-stage/**"
      - "!**.md"
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      work_item_id:
        description: "ADO user story ID"
        required: true
        type: string
      dry_run:
        description: "Dry run: no writes to ADO or GitHub"
        type: choice
        options: ["true", "false"]
        default: "true"
      feedback:
        description: "Optional feedback for the agent (after a rejected plan or change)"
        required: false
        type: string
      target_repo:
        description: "External mode only: owner/repo to use instead of auto-detection (optional)"
        required: false
        type: string
      target_branch:
        description: "External mode only: branch to start from and raise the PR into (optional)"
        required: false
        type: string

permissions:
  contents: read

# One run at a time PER STORY (two runs of the same story would fight over its branch, PR and ADO tasks);
# different stories run side by side. Push runs without a story ID fall back to the branch.
concurrency:
  group: dev-pipe-${{ inputs.work_item_id || vars.TEST_WORK_ITEM_ID || github.ref }}
  cancel-in-progress: false

env:
  WORK_ITEM_ID: ${{ inputs.work_item_id || vars.TEST_WORK_ITEM_ID }}
  DRY_RUN: ${{ inputs.dry_run || vars.DRY_RUN || 'true' }}
  DEV_FEEDBACK: ${{ inputs.feedback || vars.DEV_FEEDBACK }}
  ADO_ORG_URL: ${{ vars.ADO_ORG_URL }}
  ADO_PROJECT: ${{ vars.ADO_PROJECT }}
  TARGET_MODE: ${{ vars.TARGET_MODE }}
  TARGET_REPO: ${{ inputs.target_repo || vars.TARGET_REPO }}
  TARGET_BRANCH: ${{ inputs.target_branch || vars.TARGET_BRANCH }}
  DISCOVERY_ORG: ${{ vars.DISCOVERY_ORG }}
  AAVA_CONTEXT_BASE_URL: ${{ vars.AAVA_CONTEXT_BASE_URL }}
  PYTHONUNBUFFERED: "1"
  SONAR_SCANNER_DEBUG: ${{ vars.SONAR_SCANNER_DEBUG }} # "true" = scanner debug log in Steps 5 and 7
  PIPE_DEPS: ${{ vars.TARGET_MODE == 'external' && 'pipe' || 'full' }}
  AZURE_OPENAI_ENDPOINT: ${{ vars.AZURE_OPENAI_ENDPOINT }}
  AZURE_OPENAI_DEPLOYMENT: ${{ vars.AZURE_OPENAI_DEPLOYMENT }}
  SONAR_HOST_URL: ${{ vars.SONAR_HOST_URL || secrets.SONAR_HOST_URL }}
  SONAR_PROJECT_KEY: ${{ vars.SONAR_PROJECT_KEY || secrets.SONAR_PROJECT_KEY }}
  SONAR_ORGANIZATION: ${{ vars.SONAR_ORGANIZATION || secrets.SONAR_ORGANIZATION }}

jobs:
  # ---------------------------------------------------- Steps 1-2: User Story Analysis + Development Planning
  plan:
    name: "1-2 User Story Analysis + Development Planning"
    if: github.event_name == 'workflow_dispatch' || vars.TEST_WORK_ITEM_ID != ''
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 30
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with: { persist-credentials: false } # AI job: no GitHub token left in .git/config for the AI to read
      - uses: ./.github/actions/setup-dev-pipe
        with:
          deps: ${{ env.PIPE_DEPS }}
          claude-cli: "true"
      - run: exec python -m dev_pipe.steps.s1_2_story_analysis_planning # exec: no parent shell left holding the secrets
        env:
          TARGET_GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GH_PAT }} # external mode: read/clone target repos, push + PR there
          ADO_PAT: ${{ secrets.ADO_PAT }}
          ANTHROPIC_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY }}
          AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY }}
      - uses: ./.github/actions/save-stage
        if: always() # keep the story data, repo/branch decision log and partial plan when this step fails
        with:
          name: dev-stage-1

  # ----------------------------------------------------------- Step 3: Autonomous Development
  # Only one human approval in this pipeline: gate-ado (Step 8). Steps 1-7 run without stopping.
  implement:
    name: "3 Autonomous Development"
    needs: plan
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 60
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with: { persist-credentials: false } # AI job: no GitHub token left in .git/config for the AI to read
      - uses: ./.github/actions/setup-dev-pipe
        with:
          previous-stage: dev-stage-1
          deps: ${{ env.PIPE_DEPS }}
          claude-cli: "true"
          node: "true" # the target repo may be a JS/TS project
          target-deps-cache: "true"
      - id: implement
        run: exec python -m dev_pipe.steps.s4_autonomous_development # exec: no parent shell left holding the secrets
        env:
          TARGET_GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GH_PAT }}
          ADO_PAT: ${{ secrets.ADO_PAT }}
          ANTHROPIC_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY }}
          AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY }}
      - uses: ./.github/actions/save-stage
        if: always()
        with:
          name: dev-stage-2

  # ------------------------------------------------------------ Step 4: Build + Test + Coverage
  test-gates:
    name: "4 Build + Test + Coverage"
    needs: implement
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 60
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with: { persist-credentials: false } # AI job: no GitHub token left in .git/config for the AI to read
      - uses: ./.github/actions/setup-dev-pipe
        with:
          previous-stage: dev-stage-2
          deps: ${{ env.PIPE_DEPS }}
          claude-cli: "true"
          node: "true" # the target repo may be a JS/TS project
          target-deps-cache: "true"
      - run: exec python -m dev_pipe.steps.s6_build_test_coverage # exec: no parent shell left holding the secrets
        env:
          TARGET_GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GH_PAT }} # external mode: read/clone target repos, push + PR there
          ANTHROPIC_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY }}
          AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY }}
      - uses: ./.github/actions/save-stage
        if: always()
        with:
          name: dev-stage-3

  # ------------------------------------------------------------ Step 5: Sonar Pre-check (before the PR is raised)
  # Scans the change on SonarQube Cloud (every stack). Blocking issues on changed lines go back to the agent;
  # build + tests re-run after each fix. Fails = no PR.
  sonar-precheck:
    name: "5 Sonar Pre-check"
    needs: test-gates
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 60
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with: { persist-credentials: false } # AI job: no GitHub token left in .git/config for the AI to read
      - uses: actions/download-artifact@v4 # first: the check below needs out/target.json (per-repo Sonar keys)
        with:
          name: dev-stage-3
          path: out
      # Same rule as the step itself. Sonar off + optional -> skip the heavy setup (Claude CLI, Node, the target's
      # packages, the scanner); Step 5 then only passes Step 4's output through. Any doubt -> full setup.
      - id: sonar
        name: Is the Sonar pre-check needed?
        run: python3 -m dev_pipe.steps.sonar_needed
        env:
          SONAR_TOKEN_PRESENT: ${{ secrets.SONAR_TOKEN != '' }} # only whether it is set, never the token
      - uses: ./.github/actions/setup-dev-pipe
        with:
          deps: ${{ steps.sonar.outputs.needed == 'true' && env.PIPE_DEPS || 'pipe' }}
          claude-cli: ${{ steps.sonar.outputs.needed }}
          node: ${{ steps.sonar.outputs.needed }}
          target-deps-cache: ${{ steps.sonar.outputs.needed }}
          sonar-scanner: ${{ steps.sonar.outputs.needed }}
      - run: exec python -m dev_pipe.steps.s6b_sonar_precheck # exec: no parent shell left holding the secrets
        env:
          TARGET_GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GH_PAT }} # external mode: read/clone target repos
          ANTHROPIC_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY }}
          AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY }}
          SONAR_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SONAR_TOKEN }}
      - uses: ./.github/actions/save-stage
        if: always()
        with:
          name: dev-stage-3b

  # ------------------------------------------------------------ Step 6: Pull Request Creation (PR is opened, never merged)
  create-pr:
    name: "6 Pull Request Creation"
    needs: sonar-precheck
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 15
    permissions:
      contents: write
      pull-requests: write
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with: { fetch-depth: "${{ vars.TARGET_MODE == 'external' && 1 || 0 }}" } # full history only in local mode (external: work happens in target-checkout/)
      - uses: ./.github/actions/setup-dev-pipe # no AI and no build in this step: no Claude CLI, no target cache
        with:
          previous-stage: dev-stage-3b
          deps: pipe # no tests here: requests + PyYAML are enough
      - run: python -m dev_pipe.steps.s7_pull_request_creation
        env:
          TARGET_GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GH_PAT }} # external mode: read/clone target repos, push + PR there
          ADO_PAT: ${{ secrets.ADO_PAT }}
          # Use a PAT only if the org blocks GITHUB_TOKEN from creating PRs.
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GH_PAT || secrets.GITHUB_TOKEN }}
      - uses: ./.github/actions/save-stage
        if: always() # keep the step log and what was pushed when this step fails
        with:
          name: dev-stage-4
          retention-days: 14 # can be the last stage of a run (kept for review)

  # ------------------------------------------------------------ Step 7: SonarQube Quality Validation
  # Skipped as a whole job in a dry run (no PR exists to check), so no runner is set up for nothing.
  # Same rule as dev_pipe/config.is_dry_run: only an explicit "false" is a real run.
  quality-gate:
    name: "7 SonarQube Quality Validation"
    needs: create-pr
    if: (inputs.dry_run || vars.DRY_RUN || 'true') == 'false'
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 60
    permissions:
      contents: write # pushes fixes to the PR branch on the NO loop
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with: { fetch-depth: "${{ vars.TARGET_MODE == 'external' && 1 || 0 }}", persist-credentials: false } # AI job: no token in .git/config; full history only in local mode
      - uses: ./.github/actions/setup-dev-pipe
        with:
          previous-stage: dev-stage-4
          deps: ${{ env.PIPE_DEPS }}
          claude-cli: "true"
          node: "true" # JS/TS target builds and Sonar's JS/TS analysis
          target-deps-cache: "true"
          sonar-scanner: "true" # PR analysis (Step 7); skipped at runtime if Sonar is off
      - run: exec python -m dev_pipe.steps.s8_sonarqube_quality_validation # exec: no parent shell left holding the secrets
        env:
          LOCAL_GIT_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }} # local mode: pushes the AI's fixes (passed per git command, hidden from the AI)
          TARGET_GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GH_PAT }} # external mode: read/clone target repos, push + PR there
          ANTHROPIC_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.ANTHROPIC_API_KEY }}
          AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY: ${{ secrets.AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY }}
          SONAR_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.SONAR_TOKEN }}
      - uses: ./.github/actions/save-stage
        if: always()
        with:
          name: dev-stage-5
          retention-days: 14 # can be the last stage of a run (kept for review)

  # ------------------------------------------------------------ Step 8: approve ADO Update & Development Complete (THE ONLY HUMAN GATE)
  # Review the PR on GitHub before approving here; approving closes the DEV/PR tasks in ADO.
  # Runs after Step 7, or after Step 6 when Step 7 was skipped (dry run): then the reviewer sees dev-stage-4.
  #
  # Two approval modes (repository variable APPROVAL_MODE):
  #   wait (default) : gate-ado + ado-complete below. A runner waits on the issue (up to APPROVAL_TIMEOUT_MINUTES).
  #   comment        : request-ado-approval opens the issue and the run ends. The approver's comment starts
  #                    dev-pipe-approval-decision.yml, which runs Step 8. No runner waits. That workflow must be
  #                    on the DEFAULT branch (GitHub only runs issue_comment workflows from there).
  gate-ado:
    name: "8 Approval: ADO Update & Development Complete"
    needs: [create-pr, quality-gate]
    if: >-
      ${{ !cancelled() && vars.APPROVAL_MODE != 'comment' && needs.create-pr.result == 'success' &&
      (needs.quality-gate.result == 'success' || needs.quality-gate.result == 'skipped') }}
    permissions:
      contents: read
      issues: write
    uses: ./.github/workflows/dev-pipe-approval.yml
    with:
      gate: approve-ado-update
      artifact: ${{ needs.quality-gate.result == 'success' && 'dev-stage-5' || 'dev-stage-4' }}
      timeout_minutes: ${{ fromJSON(vars.APPROVAL_TIMEOUT_MINUTES || '240') }}

  # ------------------------------------------------------------ Step 8 (comment mode): open the approval issue, then stop
  request-ado-approval:
    name: "8 Approval request: ADO Update & Development Complete"
    needs: [create-pr, quality-gate]
    if: >-
      ${{ !cancelled() && vars.APPROVAL_MODE == 'comment' && needs.create-pr.result == 'success' &&
      (needs.quality-gate.result == 'success' || needs.quality-gate.result == 'skipped') }}
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 5
    permissions:
      contents: read
      issues: write # create the approval issue
    env:
      GATE: approve-ado-update
      APPROVERS: ${{ vars.APPROVERS || github.actor }}
      APPROVAL_TIMEOUT_MINUTES: ${{ vars.APPROVAL_TIMEOUT_MINUTES || '240' }}
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with:
          sparse-checkout: | # standard library only, no installs
            dev_pipe
            .github/actions
          persist-credentials: false
      - uses: actions/download-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: ${{ needs.quality-gate.result == 'success' && 'dev-stage-5' || 'dev-stage-4' }}
          path: out
      - name: Record the approval request (approvers, time limit, dry run)
        run: python3 -m dev_pipe.steps.gate_comment prepare "$GATE"
      # Uploaded BEFORE the issue opens, so the decision workflow always finds it.
      - uses: ./.github/actions/save-stage
        with:
          name: dev-stage-approval
          retention-days: 14 # can be the last stage of a run (kept for review)
      - name: Open the approval issue (no waiting)
        run: python3 -m dev_pipe.steps.gate_comment open
        env:
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}

  # ------------------------------------------------------------ Step 8: ADO Update & Development Complete (wait mode)
  ado-complete:
    name: "8 ADO Update & Development Complete"
    needs: [gate-ado, quality-gate]
    if: ${{ !cancelled() && needs.gate-ado.result == 'success' }}
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 10
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: ./.github/actions/setup-dev-pipe # no AI and no build in this step
        with:
          previous-stage: ${{ needs.quality-gate.result == 'success' && 'dev-stage-5' || 'dev-stage-4' }}
          deps: pipe # no tests here: requests + PyYAML are enough
      - run: python -m dev_pipe.steps.s9_ado_update_complete
        env:
          ADO_PAT: ${{ secrets.ADO_PAT }}
      - uses: ./.github/actions/save-stage
        if: always()
        with:
          name: pipeline-report
          path: out/pipeline-report.md
          retention-days: 30

  # ------------------------------------------------------------ On failure: tell the team on the ADO story
  # Step 3 moves the story to Active, so a failed run would otherwise leave an Active story with no PR and no
  # explanation. Posts "failed at Step X" + the run link (dev_pipe/steps/notify_failure.py). Nothing in a dry run.
  notify-failure:
    name: "Report failure on the ADO story"
    needs: [plan, implement, test-gates, sonar-precheck, create-pr, quality-gate, gate-ado, request-ado-approval, ado-complete]
    if: ${{ failure() && needs.implement.result != 'skipped' }}
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 5
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with: { persist-credentials: false }
      - uses: ./.github/actions/setup-dev-pipe # no AI and no build in this step
        with:
          deps: pipe
      - run: python -m dev_pipe.steps.notify_failure
        env:
          ADO_PAT: ${{ secrets.ADO_PAT }}
          NEEDS_JSON: ${{ toJSON(needs) }}

FILE: .github/workflows/dev-pipe-approval.yml

name: Dev Pipeline approval gate

on:
  workflow_call:
    inputs:
      gate:
        description: "Gate name, e.g. approve-plan (see dev_pipe/steps/gate.py)"
        required: true
        type: string
      artifact:
        description: "Artifact from the previous stage to show the reviewer"
        required: true
        type: string
      timeout_minutes:
        required: false
        type: number
        default: 240

permissions:
  contents: read
  issues: write

jobs:
  approve:
    name: Waiting for approval (comment on the issue)
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: ${{ inputs.timeout_minutes }}
    permissions:
      contents: read
      issues: write                       # create the approval issue, read comments, close it
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with:
          sparse-checkout: dev_pipe       # the gate only needs dev_pipe/ (standard library, no installs)
      - uses: actions/download-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: ${{ inputs.artifact }}
          path: out
      - name: Open approval issue and wait for a decision
        run: python3 -m dev_pipe.steps.gate "$GATE"
        env:
          GATE: ${{ inputs.gate }}
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
          APPROVERS: ${{ vars.APPROVERS || github.actor }}
          APPROVAL_TIMEOUT_MINUTES: ${{ inputs.timeout_minutes }}

FILE: .github/workflows/dev-pipe-approval-decision.yml

name: Dev Pipeline approval decision

# Comment-triggered approval (repository variable APPROVAL_MODE = comment), see dev_pipe/steps/gate_comment.py.
# The Dev Pipeline run opens the approval issue and ends; a comment on that issue starts this workflow.
# No runner waits while the reviewer decides.
#
# GitHub only runs issue_comment workflows from the DEFAULT branch: this file must be there for approvals to work.
# Every comment on any issue starts it, but the `decide` job is skipped in seconds unless the issue is an open
# Dev Pipeline approval issue, and only an allowed approver's `approve` runs Step 8 (the only job with ADO_PAT).

on:
  issue_comment:
    types: [created]

permissions: {}

concurrency:
  group: dev-pipe-approval-${{ github.event.issue.number }} # one decision at a time per issue
  cancel-in-progress: false

jobs:
  decide:
    name: "8 Approval decision"
    if: >-
      ${{ !github.event.issue.pull_request && github.event.issue.state == 'open' &&
      startsWith(github.event.issue.title, '[Dev Pipeline]') &&
      contains(github.event.issue.body, '<!-- dev-pipe-approval run_id=') }}
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 5
    permissions:
      contents: read
      issues: write # comment on and close the approval issue
      actions: read # look up the pipeline run and download its artifact
    outputs:
      decision: ${{ steps.decide.outputs.decision }}
      dry_run: ${{ steps.decide.outputs.dry_run }}
      run_id: ${{ steps.locate.outputs.run_id }}
      head_sha: ${{ steps.locate.outputs.head_sha }}
      run_url: ${{ steps.locate.outputs.run_url }}
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with:
          sparse-checkout: dev_pipe # standard library only, no installs
          persist-credentials: false
      - id: locate
        name: Find the pipeline run behind this issue
        run: python3 -m dev_pipe.steps.gate_comment locate
        env:
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
      - if: steps.locate.outputs.found == 'true'
        uses: actions/download-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: dev-stage-approval
          path: out
          run-id: ${{ steps.locate.outputs.run_id }}
          github-token: ${{ github.token }}
      - id: decide
        if: steps.locate.outputs.found == 'true'
        name: Read the comment (approve / reject / ignore)
        run: python3 -m dev_pipe.steps.gate_comment decide
        env:
          GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}

  # ------------------------------------------------------------ Step 8: ADO Update & Development Complete
  # Runs the pipeline run's own code (its commit) on its own out/ artifact.
  ado-complete:
    name: "8 ADO Update & Development Complete"
    needs: decide
    if: ${{ needs.decide.outputs.decision == 'approve' }}
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 10
    permissions:
      contents: read
      actions: read # download the pipeline run's artifact
    env:
      PYTHONUNBUFFERED: "1"
      ADO_ORG_URL: ${{ vars.ADO_ORG_URL }}
      ADO_PROJECT: ${{ vars.ADO_PROJECT }}
      TARGET_MODE: ${{ vars.TARGET_MODE }}
      DRY_RUN: ${{ needs.decide.outputs.dry_run }} # the pipeline run's dry-run setting, from its artifact
      DEV_PIPE_RUN_URL: ${{ needs.decide.outputs.run_url }} # links in ADO point to the pipeline run
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
        with:
          ref: ${{ needs.decide.outputs.head_sha }}
          persist-credentials: false
      - uses: ./.github/actions/setup-dev-pipe # no AI and no build in this step
        with:
          deps: pipe # no tests here: requests + PyYAML are enough
      - uses: actions/download-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: dev-stage-approval
          path: out
          run-id: ${{ needs.decide.outputs.run_id }}
          github-token: ${{ github.token }}
      - run: python -m dev_pipe.steps.s9_ado_update_complete
        env:
          ADO_PAT: ${{ secrets.ADO_PAT }}
      - uses: ./.github/actions/save-stage
        if: always()
        with:
          name: pipeline-report
          path: out/pipeline-report.md
          retention-days: 30

....................................................................................................

FILE: .github/workflows/aava-index.yml

name: AAVA Index

# Keeps the AAVA Code Context Service index of each candidate repo's base branch fresh, so the
# Dev Pipeline (target.mode: external) does not wait for first-time indexing.
on:
  schedule:
    - cron: "0 2 * * 1-5"      # 02:00 UTC on weekdays
  workflow_dispatch:

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  index:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    timeout-minutes: 30
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4
      - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
        with: { python-version: "3.12", cache: pip }
      - run: python -m pip install PyYAML requests
      - run: python -m dev_pipe.index_base_branches
        env:
          TARGET_GITHUB_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.GH_PAT }}
          DISCOVERY_ORG: ${{ vars.DISCOVERY_ORG }}
          AAVA_CONTEXT_BASE_URL: ${{ vars.AAVA_CONTEXT_BASE_URL }}

FILE: .github/actions/setup-dev-pipe/action.yml

name: Setup Dev pipeline
description: Python, dependencies, Claude CLI check (AI jobs only), optional Node.js and Sonar scanner, the previous stage's out/ folder and the target repo's dependency cache.
inputs:
  previous-stage:
    description: "Artifact name to restore into out/ (empty = first stage)"
    default: ""
  deps:
    description: "pipe = requirements-pipe.txt (external mode); full = requirements-dev.txt (local mode: sample_app's tests need it)"
    default: "pipe"
  claude-cli:
    description: "Make sure the Claude Code CLI is available (only jobs that run the agent). Normally the copy bundled in the claude-agent-sdk package is used; a pinned npm install is only the fallback"
    default: "false"
  node:
    description: "Set up Node.js 20 (jobs that may build a JS/TS target repo, and the Sonar JS/TS analysis)"
    default: "false"
  sonar-scanner:
    description: "Install the sonar-scanner CLI (cached between runs)"
    default: "false"
  sonar-scanner-version:
    description: "sonar-scanner CLI version (changing it creates a new cache automatically)"
    default: "6.2.1.4610"
  target-deps-cache:
    description: "Cache the target repo's package downloads (npm/yarn/pnpm, Maven, Gradle, Go, NuGet) for jobs that build it"
    default: "false"
runs:
  using: composite
  steps:
    - uses: actions/setup-python@v5
      with:
        python-version: "3.12"
        cache: pip
        cache-dependency-path: |
          requirements.txt
          requirements-dev.txt
          requirements-pipe.txt

    - name: Install Python dependencies
      shell: bash
      env:
        DEPS: ${{ inputs.deps }}
      run: |
        if [ "$DEPS" = "full" ]; then
          python -m pip install -r requirements-dev.txt   # includes requirements.txt + pytest-cov
        else
          python -m pip install -r requirements-pipe.txt
        fi

    - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
      if: inputs.node == 'true'
      with:
        node-version: "20"

    # The claude-agent-sdk wheel ships the Claude Code CLI as a native binary (no Node needed) and the SDK uses
    # it first. Only if it is missing: install the npm package pinned to the SDK's own CLI version, so every
    # job in every run uses the same version (an unpinned install took "latest" on each job).
    - name: Check the Claude Code CLI bundled with the SDK
      id: claude-cli
      if: inputs.claude-cli == 'true'
      shell: bash
      run: |
        python - <<'PY' >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
        import os, pathlib, subprocess
        import claude_agent_sdk
        pkg = pathlib.Path(claude_agent_sdk.__file__).parent
        cli = pkg / "_bundled" / "claude"
        version = ""
        try:
            from claude_agent_sdk._cli_version import __cli_version__ as version
        except ImportError:
            pass
        ok = cli.exists() and os.access(cli, os.X_OK) and subprocess.run(
            [str(cli), "--version"], capture_output=True, timeout=60).returncode == 0
        print(f"bundled={'true' if ok else 'false'}")
        print(f"version={version}")
        PY
        cat "$GITHUB_OUTPUT" | tail -2 | sed 's/^/[claude-cli] /'

    - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
      if: inputs.claude-cli == 'true' && steps.claude-cli.outputs.bundled != 'true' && inputs.node != 'true'
      with:
        node-version: "20"

    - name: Install Claude Code CLI (fallback, only when the bundled CLI is missing)
      if: inputs.claude-cli == 'true' && steps.claude-cli.outputs.bundled != 'true'
      shell: bash
      env:
        CLI_VERSION: ${{ steps.claude-cli.outputs.version }}
      run: |
        echo "::warning::Bundled Claude Code CLI not found; installing @anthropic-ai/claude-code${CLI_VERSION:+@$CLI_VERSION} from npm"
        npm install -g --no-audit --no-fund "@anthropic-ai/claude-code${CLI_VERSION:+@$CLI_VERSION}"

    # The AI's shell commands run in Claude Code's sandbox with no network (dev_pipe/agent_isolation.py). On Linux
    # that needs bubblewrap + socat, and Ubuntu 24.04 runners block the unprivileged user namespaces bubblewrap
    # uses unless this AppArmor switch is off. The pipeline checks again before each AI run and stops (agent.sandbox:
    # required) if the sandbox still cannot start, so nothing here fails the job by itself.
    - name: Prepare the AI sandbox (no network for the AI's shell)
      if: inputs.claude-cli == 'true'
      shell: bash
      run: |
        if ! command -v bwrap >/dev/null || ! command -v socat >/dev/null; then
          sudo apt-get update -qq && sudo DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive apt-get install -y -qq bubblewrap socat \
            || echo "::warning::could not install bubblewrap/socat (no sudo?)"
        fi
        if [ "$(sysctl -n kernel.apparmor_restrict_unprivileged_userns 2>/dev/null)" = "1" ]; then
          sudo sysctl -q -w kernel.apparmor_restrict_unprivileged_userns=0 \
            || echo "::warning::could not allow user namespaces for bubblewrap"
        fi
        if bwrap --ro-bind / / --dev /dev --proc /proc --unshare-net --unshare-pid true 2>/tmp/bwrap.err; then
          echo "[sandbox] bubblewrap works: the AI's shell will run without network"
        else
          echo "::warning::bubblewrap cannot start here: $(head -c 300 /tmp/bwrap.err)"
        fi

    - name: Cache Sonar Scanner CLI
      id: sonar-cache
      if: inputs.sonar-scanner == 'true'
      uses: actions/cache@v4
      with:
        path: ~/.sonar-scanner-cli/sonar-scanner-${{ inputs.sonar-scanner-version }}-linux-x64
        key: sonar-scanner-cli-${{ runner.os }}-${{ inputs.sonar-scanner-version }}

    - name: Download Sonar Scanner CLI (first run or new version only)
      if: inputs.sonar-scanner == 'true' && steps.sonar-cache.outputs.cache-hit != 'true'
      shell: bash
      env:
        VERSION: ${{ inputs.sonar-scanner-version }}
      run: |
        mkdir -p ~/.sonar-scanner-cli
        curl -sSLo /tmp/sonar-scanner.zip "https://binaries.sonarsource.com/Distribution/sonar-scanner-cli/sonar-scanner-cli-${VERSION}-linux-x64.zip"
        unzip -q /tmp/sonar-scanner.zip -d ~/.sonar-scanner-cli

    - name: Add Sonar Scanner CLI to PATH
      if: inputs.sonar-scanner == 'true'
      shell: bash
      env:
        VERSION: ${{ inputs.sonar-scanner-version }}
      run: echo "$HOME/.sonar-scanner-cli/sonar-scanner-${VERSION}-linux-x64/bin" >> "$GITHUB_PATH"

    - name: Restore previous stage output
      if: inputs.previous-stage != ''
      uses: actions/download-artifact@v4
      with:
        name: ${{ inputs.previous-stage }}
        path: out

    - name: Target dependency cache key
      if: inputs.target-deps-cache == 'true'
      id: target-key
      shell: bash
      run: |
        python - <<'PY' >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
        import json, os, pathlib, re
        p = pathlib.Path("out/target.json")
        t = json.loads(p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if p.exists() else {}
        print("repo=" + re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9._-]", "_", t.get("full_name") or "local"))
        print("sha=" + re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9]", "", t.get("base_sha") or os.environ.get("GITHUB_SHA", "none")))
        PY

    - name: Restore target dependency cache
      if: inputs.target-deps-cache == 'true'
      uses: actions/cache@v4
      with:
        path: |
          ~/.npm
          ~/.cache/yarn
          ~/.yarn/berry/cache
          ~/.local/share/pnpm/store
          ~/.m2/repository
          ~/.gradle/caches
          ~/.gradle/wrapper
          ~/go/pkg/mod
          ~/.nuget/packages
        key: target-deps-${{ runner.os }}-${{ steps.target-key.outputs.repo }}-${{ steps.target-key.outputs.sha }}
        restore-keys: |
          target-deps-${{ runner.os }}-${{ steps.target-key.outputs.repo }}-

FILE: .github/actions/save-stage/action.yml

name: Save stage output
description: Uploads a job's out/ folder (or one file) as an artifact. Keeps the upload settings in one place for every Dev Pipeline job.
inputs:
  name:
    description: "Artifact name, e.g. dev-stage-2"
    required: true
  path:
    description: "What to upload"
    default: "out/"
  retention-days:
    description: "Days GitHub keeps it. Middle stages are short-lived: each later stage carries everything earlier ones had"
    default: "5"
runs:
  using: composite
  steps:
    # always(): the caller's own `if:` decides whether the upload runs (e.g. `if: always()` to keep a failed job's
    # output for debugging); inside the action it must not be skipped again.
    - uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
      if: always()
      with:
        name: ${{ inputs.name }}
        path: ${{ inputs.path }}
        retention-days: ${{ inputs.retention-days }}
        include-hidden-files: true

"""Dev pipeline (GitHub Actions) for the 9-step Dev Agent flow.

Each module in dev_pipe/steps/ is one pipeline job. Jobs hand data to each
other through out/ (uploaded as run artifacts), never through the repo.

FILE: dev_pipe/README.md

# Dev Pipeline (`.github/workflows/dev-pipe.yml`)

Runs the Dev Agent flow in GitHub Actions. Job names are numbered 1-2 to 8 in run order.

| Job | Step (job name) | Human approval before it (GitHub Issue) | Module | Output (artifact) |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `plan` | 1-2 User Story Analysis + Development Planning | - | `s1_2_story_analysis_planning` | `dev-stage-1`: story.json, plan.md, plan.json |
| `implement` | 3 Autonomous Development (story → In Progress, code change, build) | - (plan approval not connected) | `s4_autonomous_development` | `dev-stage-2`: changes.patch, changes.md |
| `test-gates` | 4 Build + Test + Coverage (unit tests, coverage, test quality, fix loop) | - | `s6_build_test_coverage` | `dev-stage-3`: changes.patch, gates.json |
| `sonar-precheck` | 5 Sonar Pre-check before the PR (every stack; agent fix loop, build + tests re-run after each fix) | - | `s6b_sonar_precheck` | `dev-stage-3b`: changes.patch, gates.json, sonar_precheck.json |
| `create-pr` | 6 Pull Request Creation (commit, push, PR, link PR on the story, then DEV/PR tasks created, or reused from an earlier run of the story) | - | `s7_pull_request_creation` | `dev-stage-4`: pr.json, ado.json |
| `quality-gate` | 7 SonarQube Quality Validation (NO → agent fix loop) | - | `s8_sonarqube_quality_validation` | `dev-stage-5`: quality.json |
| `gate-ado` + `ado-complete` (or `request-ado-approval`, see *Approval modes*) | 8 Approval + ADO Update & Development Complete (tasks updated + closed, PR linked, summary) | `approve-ado-update` (the only gate) | `s9_ado_update_complete` | - |
| `notify-failure` | Only when a step from 3 on fails: comments "failed at Step X" + run link on the ADO story (nothing in dry run) | - | `notify_failure` | - |

Module file names keep the original 9-step diagram numbers (`s4_`, `s6_`, ...). More gates exist in
`dev_pipe/steps/gate.py` (plan, implementation, verification, tests, PR, quality) but are not wired into the workflow.

Each job downloads the previous stage's `out/` folder, adds to it and uploads it again.

**No repeated work on unchanged code:** Step 4 hands its coverage files to Step 5 (and Step 5 to Step 7, through
Step 6) in `out/coverage-files/` with a fingerprint of the code (starting commit + `changes.patch`). When the next
step has exactly that code, its first Sonar scan reuses them instead of running the same tests again; after any AI
fix, tests run as before. Step 7 also requires the PR head to still be Step 6's commit. Not used for Java/Kotlin
code (Sonar needs the compiled classes). Step 3 reuses the AI loop's last build check when the code did not change
since, and Python project dependencies are installed once per job (again only if a dependency file changes).

**Job setup** (`.github/actions/setup-dev-pipe`): AI jobs use the Claude Code CLI bundled in the `claude-agent-sdk`
package (an npm install pinned to the SDK's CLI version runs only if it is missing). Node.js is set up only for jobs
that may build a JS/TS target or run Sonar's JS/TS analysis (Steps 3, 4, 5, 7). The Sonar scanner is cached
between runs (`sonar-scanner-version` input). Step 5 first checks whether the Sonar pre-check will run
(`dev_pipe/steps/sonar_needed.py`, same rule as the step); if Sonar is off and optional, it skips the heavy setup.
Runs are queued per story (`concurrency`), so different stories run side by side. Uploads go through `.github/actions/save-stage` (one place for the settings). Middle stages
(`dev-stage-1` to `dev-stage-3b`) are kept 5 days, the stages that can end a run (`dev-stage-4`, `dev-stage-5`,
`dev-stage-approval`) 14 days, `pipeline-report` 30 days. Each stage carries everything the earlier ones had. Nothing is pushed until Step 6 (Pull Request Creation). The agent never commits, pushes or writes to ADO; the pipeline does, after approval.

## Logs and the pipeline report

Every job's log ends with one plain-English block (`dev_pipe/steplog.py`), written for people who do not read code:
a title per step, short sections (THE USER STORY, FINDING THE CODE (AAVA), THE PLAN, WRITING THE CODE, RUNNING THE
CHECKS, WHAT SONARQUBE FOUND, QUALITY GATE, ...), lines marked ✔ done / ✘ problem / → action taken, and
`Result : PASSED in 1m 20s` plus `Next : ...`. Everything technical the job printed (AI activity, build output,
scanner output) sits above it in a collapsed "Step N technical details" group on GitHub.

The pipeline report (one row per step with key facts, total time, AI sessions and fix rounds, approvals and Azure
DevOps updates) is rebuilt after every step in `out/pipeline-report.md`, shown on the run summary page when the run
ends or stops, and uploaded as the `pipeline-report` artifact by the last job.

No personal names: whoever started the run, the story's assignee and the approver appear only as a role or yes/no
(GitHub's own run page and the approval issue still show GitHub users). No secrets; long text is shortened, with the
full text in the `out/` artifacts.

## 1. One-time setup

**Secrets** (Settings > Secrets and variables > Actions > Secrets)

| Name | Used by |
|---|---|
| `ADO_PAT` | All ADO steps (Work Items: Read & Write) |
| `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` | Agent, when `agent.backend: anthropic` |
| `AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY` | Agent, when `agent.backend: azure_openai` |
| `SONAR_TOKEN` | Steps 5 (Sonar Pre-check) and 7 (SonarQube Quality Validation), only when `sonar.enabled: true` in `config/dev-pipe.yaml` |
| `GH_PAT` | Optional. Only if the org blocks `GITHUB_TOKEN` from creating PRs |

**Variables** (same page > Variables)

| Name | Example | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| `ADO_ORG_URL` | `https://dev.azure.com/<org>` | ADO organization |
| `ADO_PROJECT` | `<project>` | ADO project |
| `TEST_WORK_ITEM_ID` | `12345` | Story used for push-triggered runs. **Unset = push runs do nothing** |
| `DRY_RUN` | `true` | `false` enables writes to ADO and GitHub |
| `DEV_FEEDBACK` | | Optional feedback for the agent on a re-run |
| `SONAR_HOST_URL`, `SONAR_PROJECT_KEY`, `SONAR_ORGANIZATION` | | Sonar (organization only for SonarCloud) |
| `AZURE_OPENAI_ENDPOINT`, `AZURE_OPENAI_DEPLOYMENT` | | Only for `agent.backend: azure_openai` |

**Approvals (GitHub Issues, works on any plan)**: at each gate the pipeline opens an issue showing what to review (plan, diff, test results...) and waits. An allowed approver comments `approve` to continue or `reject: <reason>` to stop. Setup:
- Issues must be enabled on the repo (Settings > General > Features > Issues).
- Variable `APPROVERS`: comma-separated GitHub logins allowed to approve (default: whoever started the run). Others' comments are ignored.
- Variable `APPROVAL_TIMEOUT_MINUTES` (optional, default 240): the run stops if nobody answers in time (comment mode: later answers are refused).
- **Approval modes** (variable `APPROVAL_MODE`):
  - `wait` (default, or unset): a runner waits on the issue until a decision or the timeout. Uses Actions minutes while waiting.
  - `comment`: the run opens the issue and ends (job `request-ado-approval`). The approver's comment starts the
    **Dev Pipeline approval decision** workflow (`dev-pipe-approval-decision.yml`), which runs Step 8. No runner waits.
    Requirement: that workflow file must be on the repo's **default branch** (GitHub runs `issue_comment` workflows
    only from there); until then keep `wait`. Allowed approvers, time limit and dry run are read from the run's own
    artifact (`dev-stage-approval`), not from the issue. Step 8 runs the pipeline run's own commit.
- GitHub Environments are not used; any `approve-*` environments can be deleted.

**Org setting:** Settings > Actions > General > Workflow permissions > allow GitHub Actions to create pull requests (or use `GH_PAT`).

**Settings file:** `config/dev-pipe.yaml` holds app folder, branch pattern, coverage threshold, ADO state names and retry limits.

## 2. How to run

- **Trial (now):** set `TEST_WORK_ITEM_ID`, then push to `feature/dev-pipe`. The run starts automatically, but only when the push changes a file the pipeline uses (`dev_pipe/`, `dev_agent/`, `prompts/dev/`, `config/dev-pipe.yaml`, `sample_app/`, the dev-pipe workflows, ...; see `on.push.paths` in the workflow). Otherwise use Run workflow. PRs target `feature/dev-pipe` while `git.base_branch` is empty.
- **Run workflow button (later):** once the file is on the default branch: Actions > Dev Pipeline > Run workflow, enter the story ID, dry-run choice and optional feedback.
- **Approve or reject:** open the run > *Review deployments*. Read the run summary first (plan, diff, gate results).
- **Developer Verification (manual, between Steps 3 and 4):** download `dev-stage-2` (kept 5 days; later stages contain the same files), then locally on the same commit: `git apply out/changes.patch`, run and check the app.
- **Changes needed:** reject, set `DEV_FEEDBACK` (or the `feedback` input) and re-run.

## 3. Sonar

Sonar is **on** (`sonar.enabled: true` in `config/dev-pipe.yaml`) and is checked twice:

| When | What | Blocks |
|---|---|---|
| **Step 5 job, before the PR is raised** (pre-check) | Temporary branch analysis of the agent's change on SonarQube Cloud, for **every stack** | Blocking issues on the lines the agent added or changed. The agent fixes them (up to `sonar.precheck.max_fix_attempts`, default 3); build, tests and coverage re-run after each fix; no PR until there are none |
| Step 7, after the PR is raised | PR analysis + project quality gate | Quality gate ERROR (agent fix loop on the PR branch) |

**How the pre-check works** (`dev_pipe/sonar_precheck.py`):
1. Finds the exact lines the agent changed (`git diff -U0` against the starting commit).
2. Scans with `sonar-scanner` for JS/TS (Angular), Python, Java/Kotlin (compiled classes from the build check), Go,
   and with **SonarScanner for .NET** (begin → `dotnet build` → end) for each changed .NET project. Each scan is
   uploaded as a temporary branch `devpipe-precheck/<run>`, so the main branch and PR analyses are never touched.
3. Waits until SonarQube Cloud has finished processing the analysis, then reads the branch's open issues.
4. Keeps only issues on changed lines (`block_on: changed_lines`) with severity BLOCKER/CRITICAL/MAJOR (or MQR
   impact BLOCKER/HIGH/MEDIUM). Existing issues elsewhere, and issues of other repos sharing the project key, never block.
5. Sends them to the agent to fix (up to `sonar.precheck.max_fix_attempts`), re-runs build + tests + coverage +
   scan after each fix, then deletes the temporary branches.

If the pre-check cannot run (Sonar settings missing, scanner or SonarQube Cloud error), Step 5 fails straight away
without raising a PR, because `sonar.precheck.required: true`. Set it to `false` to skip with a warning instead.

**Settings:** `SONAR_TOKEN` (secret); `SONAR_HOST_URL` (`https://sonarcloud.io`), `SONAR_PROJECT_KEY`,
`SONAR_ORGANIZATION` as variables **or** secrets (both work). External mode with several repos: map each repo to
its own key in `sonar.project_keys` (`{"owner/repo": "key"}`); unmapped repos use `SONAR_PROJECT_KEY`.
Other options under `sonar.precheck`: `max_fix_attempts`, `block_on`, `severities`, `impact_severities`, `wait_seconds`, `keep_branch`.

**Coverage sent to Sonar (Steps 5 and 7):** every report written by this run's tests, found anywhere in the repo:
LCOV (`coverage/lcov.info`, `coverage/<app>/lcov.info`), Python `coverage.xml` (Python projects with a dependency file
run pytest with pytest-cov automatically) and JaCoCo XML. With coverage sent, the "Sonar way" gate in Step 7 also
checks coverage on new code (80% by default), so thin tests now fail Step 7.

**Baseline scan (Step 7):** if the PR's target branch has never been analysed on SonarQube Cloud, Step 7 analyses
it once before the PR scan; otherwise the PR shows "0 New lines of code". `quality_gate.baseline_scan: false` turns
this off. A failed baseline scan is logged and never fails the step.

**SonarQube Cloud requirements:** branch analysis (any public project, or a paid plan for private ones; Step 7's
PR analysis needs the same) and *Automatic Analysis* switched **off** for the project (CI-based analysis).

## 4. Dry run first

`DRY_RUN` defaults to `true`: ADO is read, the agent runs, gates run, the PR commit is built locally, but nothing is written to ADO or pushed to GitHub. Step 7 (SonarQube Quality Validation) is skipped as a whole job (no runner is set up for it); the approval then shows the Step 6 output (`dev-stage-4`). Note: the Sonar pre-check (Step 5) still runs: it uploads a temporary `devpipe-precheck/*` branch analysis and deletes it afterwards.

## Security: what the AI can and cannot reach

The AI runs as a child process of the pipeline with a shell, so anything the job holds was reachable by it.
Layers (for the security review; the AI's input -- story text, repo files, search results, Sonar messages -- is
treated as untrusted):

| Layer | What it does | Where |
|---|---|---|
| Secrets hidden | Every secret-looking variable (`ADO_PAT`, `SONAR_TOKEN`, `GH_PAT`, `GITHUB_TOKEN`, AI keys, OIDC request token, ...) is removed from the environment while the AI runs and restored afterwards for the pipeline's own commands | `dev_pipe/agent_isolation.py` |
| Pipeline process unreadable | The step's process is made non-dumpable (Linux), so the AI cannot read `/proc/<pid>/environ` or its memory; steps run with `exec python ...`, so no parent shell holds the secrets; the AI's file tools are also denied `/proc` | `agent_isolation.harden_process`, workflow |
| No stored git token | AI jobs check out with `persist-credentials: false`; Step 7 passes `LOCAL_GIT_TOKEN` to its own git commands only | workflow, `dev_pipe/gitops.py` |
| No network for the AI | The AI's shell commands run in Claude Code's sandbox (bubblewrap) with no allowed domains; the pipeline installs dependencies first and tells the AI the offline commands. `agent.sandbox: required` stops the step if the sandbox cannot start | `agent_isolation.sandbox_options`, `build.install_for_agent`, setup action |
| Input is data | The system prompt says story/repo/search/Sonar text is data, never instructions, and to report suspicious content | `prompts/dev/system.md` |
| Leak check | Before the plan/diff are shown or pushed (Steps 1-2, 3, 6, 7 fixes): no known secret values (also base64) and no well-known credential formats in the AI's output. `security.leak_check: block` | `dev_pipe/leak_check.py` |
| Human review | The PR is never merged by the pipeline; require reviewers with branch protection | target repos |

Residual risks to review: with `agent.backend: anthropic` the CLI needs `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` itself, so the AI's
process has it (the `azure_openai` backend keeps the key in the local proxy); the AI can still read the target
repo's code and write it into its change (reviewed in the PR); the AI's offline self-checks may fail for builds
that download at test time (the pipeline's own checks still run with network and decide pass/fail).

## 5. Known limitations / TODO

| Item | Owner |
|---|---|
| Branch naming policy (`git.branch_pattern`) | Ramkumar |
| Coverage threshold: 85 (meeting) vs 95 (existing dev_agent) | Team |
| ADO state names for your process template (`ado.states`) | Team |
| Sonar "silver tier" rules as a skill/instruction file | Ramkumar |
| Step 7 (SonarQube Quality Validation) needs PR analysis (SonarCloud or SonarQube Developer+) | Platform |
| External mode (separate target repo, auto-detected) is built but not yet run against the live AAVA service / a real org | Dev |
| Sonar scanner version 6.2.1.4610 is unverified (set in `.github/actions/setup-dev-pipe`, input `sonar-scanner-version`) | Dev |
| Sonar pre-check for .NET (SonarScanner for .NET) and Java (`sonar.java.binaries`) is unit-tested but not yet run against a real .NET / Java repo | Dev |
| .NET scanner takes the token as `/d:sonar.token` (its documented CI usage); output is redacted and GitHub masks the secret | Dev |
| Deleting the temporary `devpipe-precheck/*` branches needs *Administer* permission on the Sonar project for `SONAR_TOKEN`; without it they stay (logged, not fatal) until SonarQube Cloud purges inactive branches | Platform |

## 6. External mode: find the repo/branch from the story

Set `target.mode: external` in `config/dev-pipe.yaml`. The pipeline then works on an existing repo in your
GitHub org instead of `sample_app/`, and raises the PR there. The story does not need to name the repo or branch.

**How the repo and branch are chosen (Steps 1-2, `dev_pipe/resolver.py`):**

1. **Manual override:** if the `target_repo` (and optionally `target_branch`) run input is set, that is used.
2. **Base branches:** list the org's repos (GitHub REST), take each repo's base branch (`git.base_branch` if it
   exists there, else the default branch), make sure it is indexed in AAVA (`/api/index/progress`, trigger
   `/api/index` if missing), and search it with the story (`/api/search`, 3 queries from title / acceptance
   criteria / description). Best similarity ≥ `discovery.base_min_similarity` wins.
3. **Branch fallback** (nothing good on base branches): for the top `repos_for_branch_scan` repos, list the
   branches (GitHub, one GraphQL call per 100 branches), newest last commit first, and keep at most
   `discovery.max_branches_scanned` (default 50; `0` = all, the old behaviour). No name or "ahead of base"
   filter. Each kept branch is indexed in AAVA if needed and searched; the best one above `base_min_similarity`
   wins. First runs on repos with many branches are slow (first-time indexing is 49 s to ~1 h per branch);
   `aava.index_wait_seconds` caps the wait. An older branch beyond the cap can still be chosen with `target_branch`.
   GitHub look-ups, AAVA index checks and searches run `discovery.parallel_requests` at a time (default 6).
4. **Nothing qualifies:** use the most likely repo's base branch with confidence **low** and list the shortlisted
   branches with their AAVA scores in the run summary, so the reviewer can reject and re-run with
   `target_repo` / `target_branch`. Stop if there is no signal at all.

The chosen branch is where the work starts **and** the PR target, so a PR never carries someone else's unmerged
work. The run summary shows the repo, branch, confidence, AAVA matches, other candidates and the full resolution
log, and the reviewer confirms it at the plan approval gate (before Step 3). Result: `out/target.json`.

**Checkout:** every job checks the target repo out in `target-checkout/` at the exact commit chosen in Steps 1-2
(`dev_pipe/workspace.py`), applies `changes.patch`, and in Step 6 pushes the branch and opens the PR on that repo.
The token is removed from the environment before the agent runs and is never written to disk or a remote URL.

**Setup:**

| Where | Name | Purpose |
|---|---|---|
| Secret | `GH_PAT` | Token with read access to all candidate repos and contents + pull-requests write on the repos that get PRs (fine-grained PAT or GitHub App token). Also sent to AAVA for private repos (`aava.send_github_token`) |
| Variable | `DISCOVERY_ORG` | GitHub org to search (or `discovery.github_org`; default: owner of this repo) |
| Variable | `AAVA_CONTEXT_BASE_URL` | Optional; overrides `aava.base_url` |
| Config | `discovery.*`, `aava.*` | Thresholds, limits, include/exclude lists |
| Config | `target.overrides` | Optional, per repo: `app_dir`, `app_package`, `test_dir`, `language`, `build_commands` (only when detection gets it wrong) |
| Workflow | `AAVA Index` (`aava-index.yml`) | Nightly: (re)indexes every candidate repo's base branch so runs don't wait for first-time indexing. Must be on the default branch to run on schedule |

**Build + test commands are detected per repo** (`dev_pipe/stack.py`, `target.language: auto`). Every project under
`app_dir` is found from its marker files, so monorepos (`frontend/`, `backend/`, ...) work, and only the projects
that contain a changed file are built:

| Marker | Commands |
|---|---|
| `package.json` | install (`npm ci` / `yarn` / `pnpm`, picked from the lockfile), the `typecheck` script or `tsc --noEmit` when TypeScript is used, `build`, `test` |
| `pyproject.toml`, `setup.py`, `requirements.txt`, `Pipfile` (or plain `.py` files) | install the repo's own dependencies into a separate virtualenv (`requirements*.txt`, `pip install -e .[test/dev extras]`, Poetry, `Pipfile.lock`), then `compileall`, `pytest` with that virtualenv. No dependency file = the pipeline's Python, with a warning |
| `pom.xml` / `build.gradle(.kts)` | `mvn -B -q verify` / `gradle build` (wrapper used when present) |
| `*.sln`, `*.csproj` | `dotnet build`, `dotnet test` when a test project exists |
| `go.mod` | `go build`, `go vet`, `go test` |

Precedence: `target.overrides["owner/repo"]` > the target repo's own `.devpipe.yml` (same keys) > global
`target.build_commands` > detection. If nothing is detected the step fails with a clear message (it never falls back
to Python). A project **without tests passes with a warning** shown in the run summary; its test command is skipped.
The agent is given the same commands, and the pipeline re-runs them itself. Build commands run without the
pipeline's secrets in their environment (`NPM_TOKEN` / `NODE_AUTH_TOKEN` are kept for private registries).
Python coverage and test-quality gates apply to a single Python project; other stacks are gated on their
build/test commands and the Sonar pre-check (every stack). For JS/TS projects whose `test` script writes an LCOV report (`coverage/lcov.info`,
e.g. `vitest run --coverage`), Step 4 reports the line coverage (report only, not gated) and Step 7 sends
it to Sonar (`sonar.javascript.lcov.reportPaths`); otherwise coverage shows as "not measured".
Step 7 also sets `sonar.scm.revision` to the target repo's PR head commit, so Sonar can see the PR's
changed lines (without it the scanner took this pipeline repo's `GITHUB_SHA` and reported 0 new lines).

**Limits to know:**
- AAVA indexes only `.md` and `.json`. Repos with little documentation score low, so the GitHub branch signals
  (keywords, recency) and the AAVA search on the shortlisted branches carry more weight. `base_min_similarity` (0.75) must be tuned on real stories.
- The AAVA response format is parsed tolerantly (`dev_pipe/aava.py`) because the guide does not pin the exact
  JSON nesting; verify against the live service on the first run.
- One `SONAR_PROJECT_KEY` is used for every repo unless `sonar.project_keys` maps the repo to its own key.

## 7. Local test

```bash
pip install -r requirements-dev.txt
python -m pytest tests/test_dev_pipe.py tests/test_dev_pipe_external.py -q
```

FILE: dev_pipe/aava.py

"""Client for the AAVA Code Context Service (see USAGE_GUIDE.md).

Endpoints: POST /api/index, GET /api/index/progress, POST /api/search.
Behaviour taken from the guide:
- Look at data.status (not the outer status) after POST /api/index: TRIGGERED or SKIPPED_RUNNING.
- Progress 404 = never indexed (or deleted). FAILED may still leave partial chunks searchable.
- Zero matches is not an error. Only .md and .json files are indexed.
- `query` must always be a string.

The exact JSON nesting of progress/search responses is not fully specified in the guide, so the
parsers below accept the fields either at the top level or under `data`, and the match list under
`matches`, `results`, `items` or `chunks`.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import time

import requests

DEFAULT_BASE_URL = "https://aava-code-indexer.redbush-6668be79.eastus2.azurecontainerapps.io"
DONE_STATES = {"COMPLETED", "DONE"}
FAILED_STATES = {"FAILED", "ERROR"}


class AavaError(RuntimeError):
    pass


def _data(payload):
    if isinstance(payload, dict) and isinstance(payload.get("data"), (dict, list)):
        return payload["data"]
    return payload


def normalize_matches(payload) -> list[dict]:
    """Search response -> [{filePath, name, startLine, endLine, contentPreview, similarity}]."""
    data = _data(payload)
    items = data
    if isinstance(data, dict):
        items = next((data[k] for k in ("matches", "results", "items", "chunks") if isinstance(data.get(k), list)), [])
    out = []
    for m in items or []:
        if not isinstance(m, dict):
            continue
        try:
            sim = float(m.get("similarity", m.get("score", 0)) or 0)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            sim = 0.0
        out.append({
            "filePath": m.get("filePath") or m.get("path") or "",
            "name": m.get("name") or "",
            "startLine": m.get("startLine"),
            "endLine": m.get("endLine"),
            "contentPreview": (m.get("contentPreview") or m.get("content") or "")[:1200],
            "similarity": sim,
        })
    return sorted(out, key=lambda x: x["similarity"], reverse=True)


class Aava:
    def __init__(self, base_url: str = "", github_token: str = "", session: requests.Session | None = None,
                 sleep=time.sleep, clock=time.monotonic):
        self.base = (base_url or DEFAULT_BASE_URL).rstrip("/")
        self.github_token = github_token
        self.session = session or requests.Session()
        self._sleep, self._clock = sleep, clock

    def _body(self, owner: str, repo: str, branch: str, **extra) -> dict:
        body = {"owner": owner, "repo": repo, "branch": branch, **extra}
        if self.github_token:
            body["githubToken"] = self.github_token
        return body

    def trigger_index(self, owner: str, repo: str, branch: str) -> str:
        resp = self.session.post(f"{self.base}/api/index", timeout=60,
                                 json=self._body(owner, repo, branch, source="github"))
        if resp.status_code >= 400:
            raise AavaError(f"AAVA index {owner}/{repo}@{branch} -> HTTP {resp.status_code}: {resp.text[:300]}")
        data = _data(resp.json() if resp.content else {})
        return str((data or {}).get("status", "")) if isinstance(data, dict) else ""

    def progress(self, owner: str, repo: str, branch: str) -> dict | None:
        """Progress dict ({state, percentComplete, chunksProduced, errorMessage...}) or None if never indexed."""
        resp = self.session.get(f"{self.base}/api/index/progress", timeout=30,
                                params={"owner": owner, "repo": repo, "branch": branch})
        if resp.status_code == 404:
            return None
        if resp.status_code >= 400:
            raise AavaError(f"AAVA progress {owner}/{repo}@{branch} -> HTTP {resp.status_code}: {resp.text[:300]}")
        data = _data(resp.json() if resp.content else {})
        return data if isinstance(data, dict) else {}

    @staticmethod
    def state_of(progress: dict | None) -> str:
        if progress is None:
            return "NOT_INDEXED"
        return str(progress.get("state") or progress.get("status") or "").upper()

    def wait_until_done(self, targets: list[tuple[str, str, str]], timeout: float, poll: float) -> dict:
        """Polls several (owner, repo, branch) until each is done/failed or the shared deadline passes.
        Returns {(owner, repo, branch): final state}."""
        deadline = self._clock() + max(0.0, timeout)
        states = {t: "PENDING" for t in targets}
        while True:
            for t in [t for t, s in states.items() if s not in DONE_STATES | FAILED_STATES]:
                states[t] = self.state_of(self.progress(*t)) or "RUNNING"
            pending = [t for t, s in states.items() if s not in DONE_STATES | FAILED_STATES]
            if not pending or self._clock() >= deadline:
                for t in pending:
                    states[t] = f"TIMEOUT({states[t]})"
                return states
            self._sleep(poll)

    def search(self, owner: str, repo: str, branch: str, query: str, intent: str = "feature_flow",
               limit: int = 5) -> list[dict]:
        resp = self.session.post(f"{self.base}/api/search", timeout=60,
                                 json=self._body(owner, repo, branch, query=query or "", intent=intent,
                                                 limit=int(limit)))
        if resp.status_code >= 400:
            raise AavaError(f"AAVA search {owner}/{repo}@{branch} -> HTTP {resp.status_code}: {resp.text[:300]}")
        return normalize_matches(resp.json() if resp.content else {})

FILE: dev_pipe/ado.py

"""Azure DevOps Work Items REST client (v7.1) for the pipeline's deterministic ADO steps.

The agent never writes to ADO itself: state changes, task creation, links and
summaries are done here, so every write is explicit, logged and skipped in dry run.
Auth: Basic with empty user + ADO_PAT (needs Work Items: Read & Write).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import html
import json
import re
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta, timezone

import requests

from dev_pipe.config import is_dry_run, secret, settings


def strip_html(text: str) -> str:
    text = re.sub(r"<br\s*/?>|</p>|</li>|</div>", "\n", text or "", flags=re.I)
    text = re.sub(r"<[^>]+>", "", text)
    return re.sub(r"\n{3,}", "\n\n", html.unescape(text)).strip()


def simplify(raw: dict) -> dict:
    """ADO work item JSON -> the fields the pipeline uses."""
    f = raw.get("fields", {})
    assigned = f.get("System.AssignedTo") or {}
    return {
        "id": raw.get("id"),
        "url": raw.get("url", ""),
        "title": f.get("System.Title", ""),
        "type": f.get("System.WorkItemType", ""),
        "state": f.get("System.State", ""),
        "area_path": f.get("System.AreaPath", ""),
        "iteration_path": f.get("System.IterationPath", ""),
        "assigned_to": assigned.get("uniqueName", "") if isinstance(assigned, dict) else str(assigned),
        "description": strip_html(f.get("System.Description", "")),
        "acceptance_criteria": strip_html(f.get("Microsoft.VSTS.Common.AcceptanceCriteria", "")),
    }


# ---------- JSON Patch builders (pure, unit-tested)

def patch_fields(fields: dict) -> list[dict]:
    return [{"op": "add", "path": f"/fields/{k}", "value": v} for k, v in fields.items() if v not in (None, "")]


_DATE_RE = re.compile(r"^\{today(?:\s*\+\s*(\d+))?\}$")


def resolve_field_value(value, today: date | None = None):
    """Config value -> ADO value. '{today}' / '{today+N}' become an ISO date (UTC midnight), N days ahead;
    anything else is passed through unchanged."""
    if isinstance(value, str):
        m = _DATE_RE.match(value.strip())
        if m:
            day = (today or datetime.now(timezone.utc).date()) + timedelta(days=int(m.group(1) or 0))
            return f"{day.isoformat()}T00:00:00Z"
    return value


def patch_new_task(title: str, parent_url: str, assigned_to: str, area: str, iteration: str,
                   remaining_hours: float = 0, extra_fields: dict | None = None) -> list[dict]:
    fields = {
        "System.Title": title,
        "System.AssignedTo": assigned_to,
        "System.AreaPath": area,
        "System.IterationPath": iteration,
    }
    if remaining_hours:
        fields["Microsoft.VSTS.Scheduling.RemainingWork"] = remaining_hours
    # Fields the ADO process template makes required on Tasks (config: ado.task_fields).
    for name, value in (extra_fields or {}).items():
        fields.setdefault(name, resolve_field_value(value))
    ops = patch_fields(fields)
    ops.append({"op": "add", "path": "/relations/-",
                "value": {"rel": "System.LinkTypes.Hierarchy-Reverse", "url": parent_url}})
    return ops


def rule_errors(body: str) -> str:
    """ADO 400 body -> one line per failing field, e.g. 'Microsoft.VSTS.Scheduling.OriginalEstimate: Required'.
    ADO truncates its own ErrorMessage ('2 additional errors occurred'), so list them all from the JSON."""
    try:
        errors = json.loads(body).get("customProperties", {}).get("RuleValidationErrors") or []
    except (ValueError, AttributeError):
        return ""
    def hint(e: dict) -> str:
        flags = (e.get("fieldStatusFlags") or "").lower()
        if "invalidnotempty" in flags:
            return " -> must be EMPTY: remove it from the config"
        if "required" in flags or "invalidempty" in flags:
            return " -> required: add it to the config"
        return ""
    lines = [f"- {e.get('fieldReferenceName')}: {e.get('errorMessage', '')}{hint(e)}"
             for e in errors if isinstance(e, dict)]
    return ("\nFields ADO rejected (config/dev-pipe.yaml: ado.task_fields if it failed creating a task, "
            "ado.close_fields if it failed closing one):\n"
            + "\n".join(lines)) if lines else ""


def pick_existing_task(tasks: list[dict], title: str, closed_states: set[str]) -> dict | None:
    """The open task with this title (same story, earlier run), or None. Closed/removed tasks are never reused:
    a new run after the work was closed is new work and gets new tasks."""
    want = title.strip().lower()
    closed = {s.lower() for s in closed_states}
    for t in tasks:
        if str(t.get("title", "")).strip().lower() == want and str(t.get("state", "")).lower() not in closed:
            return t
    return None


def patch_hyperlink(url: str, comment: str) -> list[dict]:
    return [{"op": "add", "path": "/relations/-",
             "value": {"rel": "Hyperlink", "url": url, "attributes": {"comment": comment}}}]


class AdoClient:
    def __init__(self):
        org = secret("ADO_ORG_URL").rstrip("/")
        project = secret("ADO_PROJECT")
        self.api = f"{org}/{project}/_apis/wit"
        self.version = settings().get("ado", {}).get("api_version", "7.1")
        self.dry_run = is_dry_run()
        self.session = requests.Session()
        self.session.auth = ("", secret("ADO_PAT"))

    def _call(self, method: str, url: str, **kwargs) -> dict:
        # No redirects: an invalid PAT makes ADO redirect to its sign-in page.
        resp = self.session.request(method, url, timeout=30, allow_redirects=False, **kwargs)
        where = f"ADO {method} {url.split('?')[0]}"
        if resp.status_code >= 400:
            # Never echo headers/auth; body is ADO's error message.
            raise SystemExit(f"{where} -> HTTP {resp.status_code}: {resp.text[:400]}{rule_errors(resp.text)}")
        content_type = resp.headers.get("Content-Type", "")
        if resp.status_code in (203, 301, 302) or (resp.content and "json" not in content_type):
            # ADO answers a bad/expired PAT or no-access org with a sign-in page, not a 401.
            raise SystemExit(
                f"{where} -> HTTP {resp.status_code} ({content_type or 'no content type'}): ADO returned a "
                "sign-in page instead of data. Check: (1) ADO_PAT secret is valid, not expired, and has "
                "Work Items Read & Write; (2) the PAT was created in the same organization as ADO_ORG_URL; "
                "(3) ADO_ORG_URL looks like https://dev.azure.com/<org> and ADO_PROJECT is the exact project name."
            )
        return resp.json() if resp.content else {}

    def _write(self, label: str, method: str, url: str, **kwargs) -> dict:
        if self.dry_run:
            print(f"[dry-run] ADO write skipped: {label}", flush=True)
            return {"dry_run": True, "id": 0, "url": ""}
        print(f"[ado] {label}", flush=True)
        return self._call(method, url, **kwargs)

    # ---------- reads
    def get_work_item(self, wid: int) -> dict:
        raw = self._call("GET", f"{self.api}/workitems/{wid}", params={"api-version": self.version})
        return simplify(raw)

    # ---------- writes (skipped in dry run)
    def _patch(self, label: str, wid: int, ops: list[dict]) -> dict:
        return self._write(label, "PATCH", f"{self.api}/workitems/{wid}",
                           params={"api-version": self.version}, json=ops,
                           headers={"Content-Type": "application/json-patch+json"})

    def set_state(self, wid: int, state: str, extra_fields: dict | None = None) -> dict:
        """extra_fields: fields ADO requires for that state, sent in the same update (e.g. ado.close_fields)."""
        fields = {"System.State": state}
        for name, value in (extra_fields or {}).items():
            fields.setdefault(name, resolve_field_value(value))
        return self._patch(f"set #{wid} state -> {state}", wid, patch_fields(fields))

    def has_link(self, wid: int, url: str) -> bool:
        """True if the work item already links to url (so a re-run doesn't fail on a duplicate link)."""
        if self.dry_run:
            return False
        raw = self._call("GET", f"{self.api}/workitems/{wid}",
                         params={"api-version": self.version, "$expand": "relations"})
        return any(r.get("url") == url for r in raw.get("relations") or [])

    def create_task(self, story: dict, title: str) -> dict:
        cfg = settings().get("ado", {})
        ops = patch_new_task(title, story["url"], story["assigned_to"], story["area_path"],
                             story["iteration_path"], cfg.get("remaining_work_hours", 0),
                             cfg.get("task_fields") or {})
        return self._write(f"create Task '{title}' under #{story['id']}", "POST",
                           f"{self.api}/workitems/$Task", params={"api-version": self.version},
                           json=ops, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json-patch+json"})

    def child_tasks(self, wid: int) -> list[dict]:
        """Child Tasks of a work item: [{"id", "title", "state"}]. A read, so it also runs in dry run."""
        raw = self._call("GET", f"{self.api}/workitems/{wid}",
                         params={"api-version": self.version, "$expand": "relations"})
        ids = [r["url"].rstrip("/").rsplit("/", 1)[-1] for r in raw.get("relations") or []
               if r.get("rel") == "System.LinkTypes.Hierarchy-Forward" and r.get("url")]
        ids = [i for i in ids if i.isdigit()][:200]   # ADO batch limit
        if not ids:
            return []
        items = self._call("GET", f"{self.api}/workitems", params={
            "ids": ",".join(ids), "fields": "System.Title,System.WorkItemType,System.State",
            "api-version": self.version}).get("value") or []
        return [{"id": it.get("id"), "title": (it.get("fields") or {}).get("System.Title", ""),
                 "state": (it.get("fields") or {}).get("System.State", "")}
                for it in items if (it.get("fields") or {}).get("System.WorkItemType") == "Task"]

    def find_or_create_task(self, story: dict, title: str, existing: list[dict]) -> tuple[dict, bool]:
        """Reuses an open child Task with the same title (a re-run of the same story), else creates one.
        Returns (task, created)."""
        done = settings().get("ado", {}).get("states", {}).get("task_done", "Closed")
        found = pick_existing_task(existing, title, closed_states={done, "Closed", "Done", "Removed"})
        if found:
            print(f"[ado] reusing Task #{found['id']} '{title}' under #{story['id']}", flush=True)
            return found, False
        return self.create_task(story, title), True

    def add_hyperlink(self, wid: int, url: str, comment: str) -> dict:
        if self.has_link(wid, url):
            print(f"[ado] #{wid} already links to {url}; skipped", flush=True)
            return {}
        return self._patch(f"link {url} on #{wid}", wid, patch_hyperlink(url, comment))

    def add_comment(self, wid: int, markdown_text: str) -> dict:
        body = "<br>".join(html.escape(line) for line in markdown_text.splitlines())
        return self._write(f"comment on #{wid}", "POST", f"{self.api}/workItems/{wid}/comments",
                           params={"api-version": f"{self.version}-preview.4"}, json={"text": body})
