FILE: dev_pipe/agent_isolation.py

"""Keeps the pipeline's secrets and the network away from the AI agent.

The AI runs as a child process of the pipeline step and has a shell (Bash). A child process gets a copy of its
parent's environment, so every secret the workflow gave the step (ADO_PAT, SONAR_TOKEN, GH_PAT, AI keys, ...)
was readable with one `printenv`. Three layers close that:

1. hidden_secrets(): every secret-looking variable is REMOVED from the environment while the AI runs and put
   back afterwards, for the pipeline's own commands (ADO, Sonar, git push). The AI process starts without them.
2. harden_process(): the pipeline's own process is marked "not dumpable" (Linux), so another process of the
   same user (the AI, its shell) can no longer read /proc/<pipeline pid>/environ or its memory, where the
   original values still sit. The workflow runs the step with `exec python ...`, so no parent shell process
   holding the secrets remains either.
3. sandbox_options(): the AI's shell commands run in Claude Code's sandbox (bubblewrap on Linux) with NO
   network ("install first, then no network": the pipeline installs dependencies before the AI starts).
   The AI's own API traffic goes through the CLI, not through its shell; WebFetch/WebSearch are blocked.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import platform
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
from contextlib import contextmanager

# Same rule as the build commands' secret filter (dev_pipe/build.py), plus GitHub's own job tokens.
SECRET_NAME = re.compile(r"(TOKEN|_KEY|_PAT|SECRET|PASSWORD)$|^(ADO_PAT|GH_PAT|ANTHROPIC_|AZURE_OPENAI_API)", re.I)
ALWAYS_HIDE = {"GITHUB_TOKEN", "TARGET_GITHUB_TOKEN", "LOCAL_GIT_TOKEN", "ACTIONS_RUNTIME_TOKEN",
               "ACTIONS_ID_TOKEN_REQUEST_TOKEN", "ACTIONS_ID_TOKEN_REQUEST_URL"}

_HELD: dict[str, str] = {}   # values removed while the AI runs (read by the pipeline's own commands, never the AI)


def is_secret_name(name: str) -> bool:
    return name in ALWAYS_HIDE or bool(SECRET_NAME.search(name))


def held(name: str) -> str:
    """A secret's value while it is hidden from the AI (for the pipeline's own build checks)."""
    return _HELD.get(name, "")


@contextmanager
def hidden_secrets():
    """Removes every secret-looking variable from os.environ for the duration (the AI run), then restores it."""
    removed = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if is_secret_name(k)}
    for k in removed:
        os.environ.pop(k, None)
    _HELD.update(removed)
    if removed:
        print(f"[isolation] {len(removed)} secret(s) removed from the environment while the AI runs "
              f"({', '.join(sorted(removed))})", flush=True)
    try:
        yield removed
    finally:
        for k in removed:
            _HELD.pop(k, None)
        os.environ.update(removed)


_HARDENED = False


def harden_process() -> bool:
    """Marks this process non-dumpable (Linux prctl PR_SET_DUMPABLE=0): /proc/<pid>/environ and memory become
    unreadable to other processes of the same user. Returns True if applied. Safe to call more than once."""
    global _HARDENED
    if _HARDENED:
        return True
    if platform.system() != "Linux":
        return False
    try:
        import ctypes
        libc = ctypes.CDLL(None, use_errno=True)
        pr_set_dumpable = 4
        if libc.prctl(pr_set_dumpable, 0, 0, 0, 0) != 0:
            return False
    except (OSError, AttributeError):
        return False
    _HARDENED = True
    return True


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- sandbox (no network for the AI's shell)
SANDBOX_MODES = ("required", "best_effort", "off")


def sandbox_mode(cfg: dict) -> str:
    """agent.sandbox in config/dev-pipe.yaml: required (default) | best_effort | off."""
    mode = str(cfg.get("sandbox", "required")).strip().lower()
    return mode if mode in SANDBOX_MODES else "required"


def sandbox_available() -> tuple[bool, str]:
    """Linux + bubblewrap + socat installed, and bubblewrap can create a network-less sandbox here."""
    if platform.system() != "Linux":
        return False, f"sandboxing needs Linux (this is {platform.system()})"
    missing = [t for t in ("bwrap", "socat") if not shutil.which(t)]
    if missing:
        return False, f"missing {', '.join(missing)} (apt-get install bubblewrap socat)"
    try:
        probe = subprocess.run(["bwrap", "--ro-bind", "/", "/", "--dev", "/dev", "--proc", "/proc",
                                "--unshare-net", "--unshare-pid", "true"], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30)
    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as err:
        return False, f"bubblewrap could not start ({err})"
    if probe.returncode != 0:
        return False, ("bubblewrap is blocked on this machine (" + (probe.stderr.strip()[:160] or f"exit {probe.returncode}")
                       + "). On Ubuntu 24.04: sudo sysctl -w kernel.apparmor_restrict_unprivileged_userns=0")
    return True, "available"


def sandbox_options(cfg: dict, edit: bool) -> dict | None:
    """SandboxSettings for the agent, or None (no sandbox). Read-only agents have no shell, so no sandbox.
    required + unavailable = the step stops with a clear message (never silently runs without it)."""
    mode = sandbox_mode(cfg)
    if not edit or mode == "off":
        return None
    ok, why = sandbox_available()
    if not ok:
        if mode == "required":
            raise SystemExit(f"The AI's shell sandbox (no network) is required but not available: {why}. "
                             "Fix the runner, or set agent.sandbox: best_effort / off in config/dev-pipe.yaml "
                             "(only with a security sign-off).")
        print(f"[isolation] WARNING: AI shell sandbox not available ({why}); running WITHOUT network isolation "
              "(agent.sandbox: best_effort)", flush=True)
        return None
    return {
        "enabled": True,
        "autoAllowBashIfSandboxed": True,
        "allowUnsandboxedCommands": False,          # the AI cannot opt a command out of the sandbox
        "network": {"allowedDomains": []},           # no network at all for shell commands
    }


SANDBOX_NOTE = (
    "\n\nEnvironment: your shell commands run in a sandbox WITHOUT network access. The project's dependencies "
    "are already installed. Do not run install commands (npm install/ci, pip install, mvn without -o, "
    "dotnet restore, go get ...); run only build and test commands, offline (`mvn -o`, `gradle --offline`). "
    "If something truly needs a new package, say so in your final message instead of installing it.")

....................................................................................................

FILE: dev_pipe/agent_runner.py

"""Runs one Claude Agent SDK conversation for a pipeline step.

Two tool profiles:
- read_only: Read/Grep/Glob -- used for Step 2 (Development Planning), can't change anything.
- edit: adds Write/Edit/Bash -- used for implementation and fixes. git commit/push and
  network tools are blocked; the pipeline does those itself after approval.

Isolation (dev_pipe/agent_isolation.py): secrets are removed from the environment while the agent runs, the
pipeline process is made unreadable to it, and its shell commands run in a sandbox with no network (the
pipeline installs the project's dependencies first).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio
import json
import os
import re
import time
from contextlib import contextmanager
from typing import Callable

from claude_agent_sdk import (
    AssistantMessage,
    ClaudeAgentOptions,
    ClaudeSDKClient,
    ResultMessage,
    TextBlock,
    ToolResultBlock,
    ToolUseBlock,
    UserMessage,
)

from dev_pipe import agent_isolation
from dev_pipe.config import settings, work_root
from dev_pipe.report_data import is_test_path  # noqa: F401  (one shared rule; used below, re-exported)

# A completion check: given the agent's final message, returns None when the work is complete,
# otherwise the follow-up message to send (first line = short reason for the log).
Check = Callable[[str], "str | None"]
MAX_FOLLOWUPS = 2

READ_ONLY_TOOLS = ["Read", "Grep", "Glob"]
EDIT_TOOLS = READ_ONLY_TOOLS + ["Write", "Edit", "Bash"]
BLOCKED_TOOLS = [
    "WebFetch", "WebSearch",
    # Other processes' environment/memory (defence in depth; the pipeline process is also made non-dumpable).
    "Read(//proc/**)",
    "Bash(git push:*)", "Bash(git commit:*)", "Bash(git checkout:*)", "Bash(git reset:*)",
    "Bash(curl:*)", "Bash(wget:*)",
]


class MaxTurnsReached(Exception):
    """The agent used up agent.max_turns for one message before finishing."""


MAX_TURNS_FOLLOWUP = (
    "the agent ran out of turns before finishing\n\nYou ran out of turns before finishing. Do not start over and do "
    "not re-read files you already read. Check what is still missing against the plan, make only the remaining "
    "changes now (use Write to rewrite a whole file if an Edit keeps failing), then give your final summary."
)


def _truncate(text: str, limit: int = 300) -> str:
    text = str(text).strip()
    return text if len(text) <= limit else text[:limit] + "... [truncated]"


@contextmanager
def _backend_env():
    """Yields (model, env) for the configured LLM backend."""
    cfg = settings()["agent"]
    backend = cfg.get("backend", "anthropic")
    model = cfg.get("model") or None
    if backend == "anthropic":
        if not os.environ.get("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"):
            raise SystemExit("Missing 'ANTHROPIC_API_KEY' secret (agent.backend = anthropic).")
        # The CLI itself needs this key, so it is passed to it explicitly (all other secrets are hidden while the
        # agent runs). The azure_openai backend avoids even this: the key stays in the local proxy.
        yield model, {"ANTHROPIC_API_KEY": os.environ["ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"]}
    elif backend == "azure_openai":
        from azure_openai_proxy import DEFAULT_API_VERSION, start_proxy, stop_proxy
        for name in ("AZURE_OPENAI_ENDPOINT", "AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY", "AZURE_OPENAI_DEPLOYMENT"):
            if not os.environ.get(name):
                raise SystemExit(f"Missing '{name}' (agent.backend = azure_openai).")
        proxy = start_proxy(
            endpoint=os.environ["AZURE_OPENAI_ENDPOINT"], api_key=os.environ["AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY"],
            deployment=os.environ["AZURE_OPENAI_DEPLOYMENT"],
            api_version=os.environ.get("AZURE_OPENAI_API_VERSION", DEFAULT_API_VERSION),
        )
        try:
            port = proxy[2]
            yield (model or os.environ["AZURE_OPENAI_DEPLOYMENT"]), {
                "ANTHROPIC_BASE_URL": f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}",
                "ANTHROPIC_AUTH_TOKEN": "azure-openai-proxy",
                "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY": "azure-openai-proxy",
            }
        finally:
            stop_proxy(proxy[0], proxy[1])
    else:
        raise SystemExit(f"Unknown agent.backend '{backend}' in config/dev-pipe.yaml")


def skills_for(step: str | None) -> list[str] | None:
    """Skills an agent call may load: agent.step_skills[step] (limited to the installed agent.skills), else all
    installed skills. None = no filter (SDK default)."""
    cfg = settings()["agent"]
    installed = list(cfg.get("skills") or [])
    per_step = cfg.get("step_skills") or {}
    if step and step in per_step:
        return [s for s in (per_step[step] or []) if s in installed]
    return installed or None


async def _run(prompts: list[str], system_prompt: str, edit: bool, model, env,
               check: Check | None = None, max_followups: int = 0, on_incomplete: str = "fail",
               step: str | None = None, sandbox: dict | None = None) -> str:
    cfg = settings()["agent"]
    if sandbox:
        system_prompt = system_prompt + agent_isolation.SANDBOX_NOTE
    options = ClaudeAgentOptions(
        cwd=str(work_root()),  # this repo (local mode) or the target repo checkout (external mode)
        system_prompt=system_prompt,
        allowed_tools=EDIT_TOOLS if edit else READ_ONLY_TOOLS,
        disallowed_tools=BLOCKED_TOOLS,
        permission_mode="bypassPermissions",  # headless; human gates are the GitHub Environments
        setting_sources=["project"],
        skills=skills_for(step),
        model=model,
        max_turns=int(cfg.get("max_turns", 40)),
        env=env or {},
        sandbox=sandbox,
    )
    async with ClaudeSDKClient(options=options) as client:
        return await _converse(client, prompts, check, max_followups, on_incomplete)


# Numbers about the last run_agent() call, for the step logs (dev_pipe/steplog.py).
LAST_RUN: dict = {"turns": 0, "tool_calls": 0, "followups": 0, "seconds": 0.0, "reads": 0, "edits": 0}


async def _ask(client, prompt: str) -> str:
    """Sends one message and streams the agent's work to the log. Returns its final message."""
    final_text = ""
    await client.query(prompt)
    async for message in client.receive_response():
        if isinstance(message, AssistantMessage):
            LAST_RUN["turns"] += 1
            LAST_RUN["tool_calls"] += sum(isinstance(b, ToolUseBlock) for b in message.content)
            LAST_RUN["reads"] += sum(isinstance(b, ToolUseBlock) and b.name == "Read" for b in message.content)
            LAST_RUN["edits"] += sum(isinstance(b, ToolUseBlock) and b.name in ("Edit", "Write", "MultiEdit")
                                     for b in message.content)
            for block in message.content:
                if isinstance(block, TextBlock):
                    print(f"[agent] {_truncate(block.text)}", flush=True)
                elif isinstance(block, ToolUseBlock):
                    print(f"[tool-call] {block.name} {_truncate(block.input, 200)}", flush=True)
        elif isinstance(message, UserMessage) and isinstance(message.content, list):
            for block in message.content:
                if isinstance(block, ToolResultBlock):
                    print(f"[tool-result] {_truncate(block.content, 200)}", flush=True)
        elif isinstance(message, ResultMessage):
            if message.is_error and message.subtype == "error_max_turns":
                print(f"[agent] reached the turn limit (agent.max_turns = {settings()['agent'].get('max_turns', 40)})",
                      flush=True)
                raise MaxTurnsReached()
            if message.is_error:
                raise SystemExit(f"Agent run failed: {_truncate(message.result or message.subtype, 500)}")
            final_text = message.result or ""
    return final_text


async def _converse(client, prompts: list[str], check: Check | None, max_followups: int,
                    on_incomplete: str) -> str:
    """Runs the prompts, then -- if `check` says the work is incomplete -- asks the agent to finish, in the
    same conversation (it keeps everything it already read), up to `max_followups` times.

    Some models end their turn after announcing what they will do ("I'll read these files...") without
    calling a tool; the SDK treats that as the final answer. The follow-up catches it."""
    final_text, out_of_turns = "", False
    for prompt in prompts:
        try:
            final_text = await _ask(client, prompt)
        except MaxTurnsReached:
            out_of_turns = True
            break  # the remaining prompts would start before this one finished
    if check is None:
        if out_of_turns:
            raise SystemExit("Agent run failed: reached agent.max_turns before finishing (config/dev-pipe.yaml).")
        return final_text
    for attempt in range(1, max_followups + 2):
        # Out of turns: ask it to finish first. Otherwise run the step's check in a thread: it may run a full
        # build (minutes) and must not block the SDK's event loop.
        followup = MAX_TURNS_FOLLOWUP if out_of_turns else await asyncio.to_thread(check, final_text)
        if not followup:
            return final_text
        reason = followup.splitlines()[0]
        if attempt > max_followups:
            message = (f"The agent stopped {attempt} time(s) without finishing ({reason}). Re-run the step; "
                       "if this keeps happening, consider a stronger model (AZURE_OPENAI_DEPLOYMENT).")
            if on_incomplete == "warn":
                print(f"[agent] WARNING: {message}", flush=True)
                return final_text
            raise SystemExit(message)
        print(f"[agent] incomplete output ({reason}); asking it to continue ({attempt}/{max_followups})",
              flush=True)
        LAST_RUN["followups"] += 1
        try:
            final_text, out_of_turns = await _ask(client, followup), False
        except MaxTurnsReached:
            out_of_turns = True
    return final_text


def run_agent(prompts: str | list[str], system_prompt: str, edit: bool, check: Check | None = None,
              max_followups: int = MAX_FOLLOWUPS, on_incomplete: str = "fail", step: str | None = None) -> str:
    """Runs the prompts in one conversation and returns the agent's final message.

    step: name used to pick the skills this call may load (agent.step_skills in config/dev-pipe.yaml).

    check(final_text) -> None when the work is complete, else the follow-up message to send (its first line
    is the reason, used in the log). After `max_followups` follow-ups the step fails (on_incomplete="fail")
    or carries on with a warning (on_incomplete="warn")."""
    if isinstance(prompts, str):
        prompts = [prompts]
    LAST_RUN.update(turns=0, tool_calls=0, followups=0, seconds=0.0, reads=0, edits=0)
    started = time.time()
    try:
        with _backend_env() as (model, env):
            agent_isolation.harden_process()
            sandbox = agent_isolation.sandbox_options(settings()["agent"], edit)
            if sandbox:
                # Install first (outside the sandbox, with registry tokens), then the agent runs without network.
                from dev_pipe.build import install_for_agent
                install_for_agent()
            with agent_isolation.hidden_secrets():
                return asyncio.run(_run(prompts, system_prompt, edit, model, env, check, max_followups,
                                        on_incomplete, step, sandbox))
    finally:
        LAST_RUN["seconds"] = round(time.time() - started, 1)
        from dev_pipe.steplog import AI_TOTALS
        AI_TOTALS["sessions"] += 1
        AI_TOTALS["seconds"] += LAST_RUN["seconds"]


def agent_brief() -> str:
    """'in 2m 05s (14 steps, 11 files read, 2 edits)' for the plain-English step logs."""
    from dev_pipe.steplog import fmt_duration
    parts = [f"{LAST_RUN['turns']} steps", f"{LAST_RUN['reads']} files read"]
    if LAST_RUN["edits"]:
        parts.append(f"{LAST_RUN['edits']} edits")
    if LAST_RUN["followups"]:
        parts.append(f"{LAST_RUN['followups']} follow-up(s)")
    return f"in {fmt_duration(LAST_RUN['seconds'])} ({', '.join(parts)})"


def agent_stats() -> str:
    """'14 turns, 23 tool calls, 2m 05s' for the step logs."""
    from dev_pipe.steplog import fmt_duration
    extra = f", {LAST_RUN['followups']} follow-up(s)" if LAST_RUN["followups"] else ""
    return (f"{LAST_RUN['turns']} turns, {LAST_RUN['tool_calls']} tool calls, "
            f"{fmt_duration(LAST_RUN['seconds'])}{extra}")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- completion checks used by the steps
_JSON_BLOCK = re.compile(r"```json\s*(\{.*?\})\s*```", re.S)


def json_blocks(text: str) -> list[str]:
    """The ```json {...} ``` blocks in the agent's answer (plan metadata), in order."""
    return _JSON_BLOCK.findall(text or "")


def plan_followup(text: str) -> str | None:
    """Steps 1-2: the plan must end with a valid ```json block that lists the files."""
    blocks = json_blocks(text)
    if not blocks:
        reason = "no ```json metadata block in the plan"
    else:
        try:
            meta = json.loads(blocks[-1])
        except ValueError as exc:
            meta, reason = None, f"the ```json block is not valid JSON ({exc.msg})"
        else:
            reason = "" if isinstance(meta.get("files"), list) and meta["files"] else \
                "the ```json block has no `files` list"
        if not reason:
            return None
    return (f"{reason}\n\nYou stopped before finishing. Do not describe what you will do: use your tools now to "
            "read what you still need, then reply with the COMPLETE development plan (all the required headings) "
            "ending with exactly one ```json block in the required format, including the `files` list.")


def changes_followup(changed: Callable[[], list[str]], what: str = "implementation") -> Check:
    """Step 3: the agent must have changed at least one file."""
    def check(_text: str) -> str | None:
        if changed():
            return None
        return (f"no files were changed\n\nNo files have been changed yet. Do not describe what you will do: "
                f"carry out the {what} now with Edit/Write, run the build check, then give your final summary.")
    return check


_ASKS_PERMISSION = re.compile(
    r"\b(let me know if|would you like( me)? to|should I\b|shall I\b|do you want me to|want me to|"
    r"please confirm|if you(?:'d| would) like me to|awaiting (your )?(confirmation|approval)|"
    r"before I proceed|let me know how you(?:'d| would) like)", re.I)


def question_followup(text: str) -> str | None:
    """The agent ended by asking for permission or a decision. Nobody can answer in an unattended run."""
    if not _ASKS_PERMISSION.search(text or ""):
        return None
    return ("the agent asked a question instead of finishing\n\nNobody can answer questions in this automated run. "
            "Decide yourself, following the approved plan, and finish the work now with your tools. "
            "If the work is already complete, reply with your final summary only, without questions.")


def first_of(*checks: Check) -> Check:
    """Combines checks: the first one that finds a problem decides the follow-up."""
    def check(text: str) -> str | None:
        for c in checks:
            followup = c(text)
            if followup:
                return followup
        return None
    return check


def tests_followup(changed: Callable[[], list[str]]) -> Check:
    """Step 4: the agent must have added or changed test files."""
    def check(_text: str) -> str | None:
        if any(is_test_path(p) for p in changed()):
            return None
        return ("no test files were added or changed\n\nNo test files have been written yet. Do not describe "
                "what you will do: write the unit tests now with Edit/Write, run them, then give your final summary. "
                "If the project has no test setup and you cannot add one within the plan, say so explicitly.")
    return check


def extract_json_block(text: str) -> dict:
    """Last ```json ... ``` block in the agent's answer (plan metadata)."""
    blocks = json_blocks(text)
    if not blocks:
        raise SystemExit("The agent's plan did not include the required ```json``` metadata block.")
    return json.loads(blocks[-1])


def strip_json_block(text: str) -> str:
    return _JSON_BLOCK.sub("", text).strip()

FILE: dev_pipe/approval.py

"""Human approval through a GitHub Issue (works on any GitHub plan, no Environments needed).

The pipeline opens an issue describing what needs approval, then polls its comments until an
allowed approver comments `approve` or `reject: <reason>`, or the timeout is reached.

Standard library only, so approval jobs start in seconds without installing dependencies.
Comment text is untrusted input: it is only matched against two keywords, never executed.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.parse
import urllib.request

APPROVE_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*/?approved?\b", re.I)
REJECT_RE = re.compile(r"^\s*/?reject(?:ed)?\b[\s:,\-]*(.*)", re.I | re.S)
BOT_LOGINS = {"github-actions[bot]"}


def parse_approvers(raw: str) -> set[str]:
    """'alice, @Bob  carol' -> {'alice', 'bob', 'carol'} (GitHub logins are case-insensitive)."""
    return {a.strip().lstrip("@").lower() for a in re.split(r"[,\s]+", raw or "") if a.strip()}


def decide(comments: list[dict], approvers: set[str]) -> tuple[str, str, str] | None:
    """First decision by an allowed approver, oldest comment first.
    Returns (decision, reason, login) with decision 'approve' or 'reject', or None if undecided.
    Comments from anyone not in `approvers` (and the bot itself) are ignored."""
    for c in comments:
        login = (c.get("user") or {}).get("login", "")
        if not login or login in BOT_LOGINS or login.lower() not in approvers:
            continue
        body = (c.get("body") or "").strip()
        if APPROVE_RE.match(body):
            return "approve", "", login
        match = REJECT_RE.match(body)
        if match:
            return "reject", match.group(1).strip() or "(no reason given)", login
    return None


class GitHubIssues:
    """Minimal GitHub REST client for one repository's issues."""

    def __init__(self, token: str, repo: str, api: str = "https://api.github.com"):
        if not token or not repo:
            raise SystemExit("GITHUB_TOKEN and GITHUB_REPOSITORY are required for approval gates.")
        self.base = f"{api.rstrip('/')}/repos/{repo}"
        self.headers = {
            "Authorization": f"Bearer {token}",
            "Accept": "application/vnd.github+json",
            "X-GitHub-Api-Version": "2022-11-28",
            "User-Agent": "dev-pipe-approval",
        }

    def _request(self, method: str, path: str, data: dict | None = None, params: dict | None = None):
        url = self.base + path + (f"?{urllib.parse.urlencode(params)}" if params else "")
        body = json.dumps(data).encode() if data is not None else None
        req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=body, method=method,
                                     headers={**self.headers, "Content-Type": "application/json"})
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30) as resp:
                raw = resp.read()
                return json.loads(raw) if raw else {}
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as err:
            detail = err.read().decode(errors="replace")[:300]
            hint = (" Are Issues enabled on the repo (Settings > General > Features > Issues), and does "
                    "the job have 'issues: write' permission?") if err.code in (403, 404, 410) else ""
            raise SystemExit(f"GitHub {method} {path} -> HTTP {err.code}: {detail}{hint}") from None

    def create_issue(self, title: str, body: str) -> dict:
        return self._request("POST", "/issues", {"title": title, "body": body})

    def list_comments(self, number: int) -> list[dict]:
        comments, page = [], 1
        while True:
            batch = self._request("GET", f"/issues/{number}/comments", params={"per_page": 100, "page": page})
            comments.extend(batch)
            if len(batch) < 100:
                return comments
            page += 1

    def comment(self, number: int, body: str) -> None:
        self._request("POST", f"/issues/{number}/comments", {"body": body})

    def close(self, number: int, completed: bool) -> None:
        self._request("PATCH", f"/issues/{number}",
                      {"state": "closed", "state_reason": "completed" if completed else "not_planned"})

    def get_run(self, run_id: int) -> dict:
        """A GitHub Actions workflow run of this repository (comment-triggered approval)."""
        return self._request("GET", f"/actions/runs/{int(run_id)}")


def wait_for_decision(gh: GitHubIssues, number: int, approvers: set[str], timeout_s: float,
                      poll_s: float = 30, sleep=time.sleep, now=time.monotonic):
    """Poll the issue's comments until a decision or the timeout. Returns decide()'s result or None."""
    deadline = now() + timeout_s
    while True:
        decision = decide(gh.list_comments(number), approvers)
        if decision or now() >= deadline:
            return decision
        sleep(poll_s)

FILE: dev_pipe/build.py

"""Deterministic build + test check (the agent also runs these, but the pipeline doesn't trust it).

Which commands run, in order of precedence:
  1. target.build_commands (config/dev-pipe.yaml, a per-repo target.overrides entry, or the target
     repo's own .devpipe.yml) -- run as given in app_dir.
  2. Auto-detection (dev_pipe/stack.py): every project under app_dir is found from its marker files
     (package.json, pyproject.toml, pom.xml, ...). If the changed files are known, only the projects
     that contain a change are built. target.language (if not "auto") limits detection to that stack.
  3. Nothing detected -> the check FAILS with a clear message (it never guesses Python).

A project without tests passes with a warning; its test command is skipped.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import re
import subprocess
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

from dev_pipe.stack import (Command, detect_projects, python_env, python_project, select_projects,
                            stacks_for_language)

COMMAND_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 30 * 60
LOG_TAIL_LINES = 30

# Pipeline secrets are removed from the environment of build/test commands: those commands come from the
# target repo (package.json scripts, .devpipe.yml) and must not be able to read them.
_SECRET_NAME = re.compile(r"(TOKEN|_KEY|_PAT|SECRET|PASSWORD)$|^(ADO_PAT|GH_PAT|ANTHROPIC_|AZURE_OPENAI_API)", re.I)
_ALLOWED_SECRETS = {"NPM_TOKEN", "NODE_AUTH_TOKEN"}  # needed for private package registries


@dataclass
class BuildUnit:
    label: str            # e.g. "frontend (node)"
    cwd: Path
    commands: list[Command]
    agent_commands: list[Command] = field(default_factory=list)   # offline variant for the sandboxed AI
    install_commands: list[Command] = field(default_factory=list)  # run by the pipeline before the AI


def sandboxed() -> bool:
    """True when the AI's shell runs without network (agent.sandbox not 'off'): it is then told the offline
    build/test commands, and the pipeline installs dependencies before it starts."""
    from dev_pipe.config import settings
    return str((settings().get("agent") or {}).get("sandbox", "required")).strip().lower() != "off"


@dataclass
class BuildPlan:
    units: list[BuildUnit] = field(default_factory=list)
    warnings: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    error: str = ""       # set when nothing can be built


@dataclass
class BuildResult:
    ok: bool
    log: str
    warnings: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    projects: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def report(self) -> str:
        """Log plus warnings, for run summaries."""
        head = f"Projects: {', '.join(self.projects) or 'none'}\n"
        warn = "".join(f"WARNING: {w}\n" for w in self.warnings)
        return (head + warn + ("\n" if warn else "") + self.log).strip()


def build_commands(t: dict) -> list[str]:
    return [c for c in (t.get("build_commands") or []) if str(c).strip()]


def is_python_target(t: dict, app_dir: Path) -> bool:
    """True when the Python coverage + test-quality gates apply: no custom build commands, and either
    language is set to python or detection finds exactly one Python project at app_dir itself."""
    if build_commands(t):
        return False
    lang = str(t.get("language") or "auto").strip().lower()
    if lang in ("python", "py"):
        return True
    if lang not in ("", "auto"):
        return False
    projects = detect_projects(app_dir, t.get("test_dir") or "tests")
    return len(projects) == 1 and projects[0].stack == "python" and projects[0].path == app_dir.resolve()


def plan_build(t: dict, app_dir: Path, changed: list[Path] | None = None) -> BuildPlan:
    app_dir = Path(app_dir).resolve()
    cmds = build_commands(t)
    if cmds:
        configured = [Command(c) for c in cmds]
        return BuildPlan([BuildUnit(f"{t.get('app_dir', '.')} (configured)", app_dir, configured, configured)])

    test_dir = t.get("test_dir") or "tests"
    language = str(t.get("language") or "auto")
    wanted = stacks_for_language(language)
    projects = detect_projects(app_dir, test_dir)
    if wanted is not None:
        projects = [p for p in projects if p.stack in wanted]
        if not projects and "python" in wanted:
            projects = [python_project(app_dir, test_dir)]   # explicit python: build app_dir as before
    if not projects:
        where = f"`{t.get('app_dir', '.')}`"
        what = "a supported project" if wanted is None else f"a `{language}` project"
        return BuildPlan(error=(
            f"No build system detected: could not find {what} in {where} (looked for package.json, "
            "pyproject.toml/setup.py/requirements.txt, pom.xml, build.gradle, *.sln/*.csproj, go.mod). "
            "Set target.build_commands for this repo (config/dev-pipe.yaml target.overrides, or a "
            ".devpipe.yml file in the target repo)."))

    selected, note = select_projects(projects, changed)
    plan = BuildPlan(warnings=[note] if note else [])
    for p in selected:
        label = f"{p.rel(app_dir)} ({p.stack})"
        plan.units.append(BuildUnit(label, p.path, p.commands, p.agent_commands(), p.install_commands()))
        plan.warnings += [f"{label}: {w}" for w in p.warnings]
    return plan


def build_check_text(t: dict, app_dir: Path | None = None) -> str:
    """The build instruction given to the agent (the same commands check_build runs)."""
    cmds = build_commands(t)
    if cmds:
        return ("in `" + t["app_dir"] + "` run " + ", then ".join(f"`{c}`" for c in cmds)
                + ". The pipeline re-runs exactly these and fails the step if any fails; only report a check "
                "as passed if you ran it and it passed.")
    if app_dir is None:
        from dev_pipe.config import app_dir as _app_dir
        app_dir = _app_dir()
    plan = plan_build(t, app_dir)
    if plan.error:
        return f"{plan.error} Tell the developer in your final message."
    parts = [f"in `{u.cwd.relative_to(Path(app_dir).resolve()).as_posix() or '.'}` run "
             + ", then ".join(f"`{c.cmd}`" for c in (u.agent_commands if sandboxed() else u.commands))
             for u in plan.units if (u.agent_commands if sandboxed() else u.commands)]
    text = ("; ".join(parts) + " (paths relative to `" + str(t.get("app_dir", ".")) + "`; you only need the "
            "projects you changed). The pipeline re-runs these and fails the step if any fails; only report a "
            "check as passed if you ran it and it passed.")
    if plan.warnings:
        text += " Notes: " + "; ".join(plan.warnings) + "."
    return text


def check_build(t: dict, app_dir: Path, changed: list[Path] | None = None) -> BuildResult:
    """Build + test the projects affected by `changed` (all projects when None)."""
    plan = plan_build(t, app_dir, changed)
    if plan.error:
        return BuildResult(False, plan.error)
    log, ok = [], True
    for unit in plan.units:
        unit_ok, unit_log = _run_commands(unit.cwd, unit.commands)
        log.append(f"### {unit.label}\n{unit_log}")
        ok = ok and unit_ok
    for w in plan.warnings:
        print(f"[build] WARNING: {w}", flush=True)
    return BuildResult(ok, "\n\n".join(log), plan.warnings, [u.label for u in plan.units])


def run_build(app_dir: Path, test_dir: str, commands: list[str] | None = None) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    """Explicit commands, or the Python check (compileall + pytest) on app_dir. Returns (ok, short log).
    Kept for callers that already know the commands; steps use check_build()."""
    if commands:
        return _run_commands(Path(app_dir), [Command(c) for c in commands if str(c).strip()])
    project = python_project(Path(app_dir), test_dir)
    ok, log = _run_commands(Path(app_dir), project.commands)
    return ok, "\n".join([f"WARNING: {w}" for w in project.warnings] + [log])


# Dependency files whose content decides what prepare_python installs (stack.python_install_commands).
_DEP_FILES = ("pyproject.toml", "setup.py", "setup.cfg", "Pipfile", "Pipfile.lock", "poetry.lock")
# app_dir -> (fingerprint of its dependency files, interpreter) after a successful install in THIS job.
_PREPARED: dict[str, tuple[str, str]] = {}


def _deps_fingerprint(app_dir: Path) -> str:
    import hashlib
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    from dev_pipe.stack import PY_REQUIREMENT_FILES
    names = sorted({p.name for p in app_dir.glob("requirements*.txt")} | set(PY_REQUIREMENT_FILES) | set(_DEP_FILES))
    for name in names:
        f = app_dir / name
        if f.is_file():
            h.update(name.encode() + b"\0" + f.read_bytes() + b"\0")
    return h.hexdigest()


def prepare_python(app_dir: Path) -> tuple[bool, str, str]:
    """Installs a Python project's own dependencies into its virtualenv (see stack.python_env).
    Returns (ok, interpreter to run tests with, log). Used before the coverage gate.
    Installs once per job: later check rounds skip it unless a dependency file changed (e.g. the AI added a
    package to requirements.txt), instead of re-running every pip install."""
    app_dir = Path(app_dir)
    key, fp = str(app_dir.resolve()), _deps_fingerprint(app_dir)
    cached = _PREPARED.get(key)
    if cached and cached[0] == fp:
        print("[build] project dependencies already installed in this job (dependency files unchanged)", flush=True)
        return True, cached[1], ""
    py, setup = python_env(app_dir)
    if not setup:
        return True, py, ""
    ok, log = _run_commands(app_dir, setup)
    if ok:
        _PREPARED[key] = (fp, py)
    else:
        _PREPARED.pop(key, None)
    return ok, py, log


def install_for_agent() -> None:
    """"Install first, then no network": downloads every detected project's dependencies (and, for Java/.NET/Go,
    what the build and tests need) BEFORE the AI starts, outside its sandbox. Never fails the step: the
    pipeline's own build check (which has network) reports any real problem. Once per job."""
    global _INSTALLED
    if _INSTALLED:
        return
    _INSTALLED = True
    from dev_pipe.config import app_dir as _app_dir, target_settings
    t = target_settings()
    if build_commands(t):
        print("[install] custom target.build_commands: they run as configured; the AI's sandbox has no network, "
              "so they must work offline once the pipeline's first build check has run", flush=True)
        return
    plan = plan_build(t, _app_dir())
    for unit in plan.units:
        if not unit.install_commands:
            continue
        ok, log = _run_commands(unit.cwd, unit.install_commands)
        print(f"[install] {unit.label}: dependencies {'installed' if ok else 'install FAILED'} before the AI starts"
              + ("" if ok else f"\n{log[-1500:]}"), flush=True)


_INSTALLED = False


def _build_env() -> dict:
    env = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if k in _ALLOWED_SECRETS or not _SECRET_NAME.search(k)}
    # While the AI runs, its secrets are hidden (dev_pipe/agent_isolation.py); the pipeline's own build checks
    # still get the private-registry tokens they need.
    from dev_pipe.agent_isolation import held
    for name in _ALLOWED_SECRETS:
        if name not in env and held(name):
            env[name] = held(name)
    env.setdefault("CI", "true")   # non-interactive test runners (jest/vitest watch mode off)
    return env


def _run_commands(cwd: Path, commands: list[Command]) -> tuple[bool, str]:
    log = []
    env = _build_env()
    for c in commands:
        # Commands come from the reviewed config, the target repo's build files or stack detection,
        # never from the agent's output or the story text.
        try:
            proc = subprocess.run(c.cmd, cwd=cwd, shell=True, capture_output=True, text=True,  # noqa: S602
                                  env=env, timeout=COMMAND_TIMEOUT_SECONDS)
            code, output = proc.returncode, (proc.stdout or "") + (proc.stderr or "")
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as exc:
            code = -1
            parts = [p.decode(errors="replace") if isinstance(p, bytes) else (p or "")
                     for p in (exc.stdout, exc.stderr)]
            output = "".join(parts) + f"\nTimed out after {COMMAND_TIMEOUT_SECONDS}s"
        tail = output.strip().splitlines()[-LOG_TAIL_LINES:]
        log.append(f"$ {c.cmd}  (exit {code})\n" + "\n".join(tail))
        if code not in c.ok_codes:
            return False, "\n".join(log)
    return True, "\n".join(log)

FILE: dev_pipe/config.py

"""Settings, secrets and run context shared by every step."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import re
from pathlib import Path

import yaml

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
OUT = ROOT / "out"
PROMPTS = ROOT / "prompts" / "dev"
SETTINGS_FILE = ROOT / "config" / "dev-pipe.yaml"
# External mode: the target repo is checked out here (git-ignored, never part of this repo).
# NOT a hidden (dot) folder: SonarQube treats everything under a hidden folder as hidden files, which its
# language sensors (Python, Java, JS/TS, ...) skip, so a PR showed "0 New lines of code" and no measures.
TARGET_CHECKOUT = ROOT / "target-checkout"

_settings: dict | None = None


def settings() -> dict:
    """Non-secret settings from config/dev-pipe.yaml (loaded once)."""
    global _settings
    if _settings is None:
        _settings = yaml.safe_load(SETTINGS_FILE.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) or {}
    return _settings


def secret(name: str, required: bool = True) -> str:
    """Read a secret from the environment (set by the workflow from GitHub Secrets)."""
    value = os.environ.get(name, "").strip()
    if required and not value:
        raise SystemExit(
            f"Missing '{name}'. Add it in Settings > Secrets and variables > Actions, "
            f"and pass it to this step in the workflow 'env:' block."
        )
    return value


def is_dry_run() -> bool:
    """Dry run unless DRY_RUN is explicitly 'false'. Dry run = no writes to ADO or GitHub."""
    return os.environ.get("DRY_RUN", "true").strip().lower() != "false"


def work_item_id() -> int:
    value = os.environ.get("WORK_ITEM_ID", "").strip()
    if not value.isdigit():
        raise SystemExit(
            "WORK_ITEM_ID is missing or not a number. Enter it when running the workflow, "
            "or set the TEST_WORK_ITEM_ID repository variable for push-triggered runs."
        )
    return int(value)


def feedback() -> str:
    """Optional reviewer feedback for a re-run (workflow input or DEV_FEEDBACK variable)."""
    return os.environ.get("DEV_FEEDBACK", "").strip()


def sonar_status() -> tuple[bool, str]:
    """(enabled, reason). Sonar runs only when switched on in config AND fully configured,
    so a half-configured Sonar is skipped with a clear reason instead of failing the run."""
    if not settings().get("sonar", {}).get("enabled", False):
        return False, "disabled in config (sonar.enabled: false)"
    missing = [n for n in ("SONAR_TOKEN", "SONAR_HOST_URL") if not os.environ.get(n, "").strip()]
    if not sonar_project_key():
        missing.append("SONAR_PROJECT_KEY")
    if missing:
        return False, f"missing {', '.join(missing)}"
    return True, "enabled"


def sonar_project_key() -> str:
    """Sonar project key for the repo being changed: `sonar.project_keys["owner/repo"]` in
    config/dev-pipe.yaml (external mode, one key per target repo) > the SONAR_PROJECT_KEY variable/secret."""
    keys = settings().get("sonar", {}).get("project_keys") or {}
    full = resolved_target().get("full_name", "") if is_external() else ""
    return str(keys.get(full) or os.environ.get("SONAR_PROJECT_KEY", "")).strip()


def sonar_connection() -> dict:
    """host, token, project_key, organization (call after sonar_status() returned enabled)."""
    return {"host": os.environ["SONAR_HOST_URL"].strip().rstrip("/"),
            "token": os.environ["SONAR_TOKEN"].strip(),
            "project_key": sonar_project_key(),
            "organization": os.environ.get("SONAR_ORGANIZATION", "").strip()}


def sonar_precheck_required() -> bool:
    """sonar.precheck.required: true = a skipped Sonar pre-check (switched off or not configured) FAILS
    Step 5 (Sonar Pre-check), so no pull request is ever raised without it."""
    return bool((settings().get("sonar", {}).get("precheck") or {}).get("required", False))


def is_external() -> bool:
    """external = find the repo/branch from the story (GitHub + AAVA) and raise the PR there.
    local = change target.app_dir inside this repo (original behaviour).
    The TARGET_MODE env var (GitHub repo variable) overrides target.mode in config/dev-pipe.yaml."""
    mode = os.environ.get("TARGET_MODE", "").strip() or settings()["target"].get("mode", "local")
    return str(mode).strip().lower() == "external"


def resolved_target() -> dict:
    """out/target.json written by Steps 1-2 in external mode ({} in local mode or before it exists)."""
    path = OUT / "target.json"
    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if path.exists() else {}


# Keys a target repo may set in its own .devpipe.yml (same keys as target.overrides).
REPO_FILE = ".devpipe.yml"
REPO_FILE_KEYS = ("app_dir", "app_package", "test_dir", "language", "build_commands")


def repo_file_settings() -> dict:
    """External mode: settings from the target repo's own .devpipe.yml ({} if absent or invalid)."""
    path = TARGET_CHECKOUT / REPO_FILE
    if not is_external() or not path.is_file():
        return {}
    try:
        data = yaml.safe_load(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) or {}
    except (OSError, yaml.YAMLError) as exc:
        print(f"[config] ignoring {REPO_FILE}: {exc}", flush=True)
        return {}
    if not isinstance(data, dict):
        return {}
    return {k: v for k, v in data.items() if k in REPO_FILE_KEYS and v not in (None, "")}


def target_settings() -> dict:
    """The `target` section, adjusted for external mode. Precedence (highest last):
    config/dev-pipe.yaml `target` -> the target repo's .devpipe.yml -> `target.overrides["owner/repo"]`.
    app_dir defaults to the repo root in external mode. language "auto" (default) = detect the stack
    from the repo's files (dev_pipe/stack.py)."""
    t = dict(settings()["target"])
    t.pop("overrides", None)
    if is_external():
        t["app_dir"] = "."
        t.update(repo_file_settings())
        full = resolved_target().get("full_name", "")
        overrides = settings()["target"].get("overrides") or {}
        t.update({k: v for k, v in (overrides.get(full) or {}).items() if v not in (None, "")})
    if not t.get("language"):
        t["language"] = "auto"
    t.setdefault("build_commands", [])
    t.setdefault("test_dir", "tests")
    return t


def work_root() -> Path:
    """Git working tree the agent edits and the pipeline commits from."""
    return TARGET_CHECKOUT if is_external() else ROOT


def app_dir() -> Path:
    return (work_root() / target_settings()["app_dir"]).resolve()


def load_prompt(name: str, **values) -> str:
    text = (PROMPTS / f"{name}.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    for key, value in values.items():
        text = text.replace("{{" + key + "}}", str(value))
    return text


def run_url() -> str:
    # Comment-triggered approval: Step 8 runs in the decision workflow, but links to the pipeline run.
    if os.environ.get("DEV_PIPE_RUN_URL", "").strip():
        return os.environ["DEV_PIPE_RUN_URL"].strip()
    server = os.environ.get("GITHUB_SERVER_URL", "")
    repo = os.environ.get("GITHUB_REPOSITORY", "")
    run_id = os.environ.get("GITHUB_RUN_ID", "")
    return f"{server}/{repo}/actions/runs/{run_id}" if run_id else "local run"


def slugify(text: str, max_len: int = 40) -> str:
    slug = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9]+", "-", text.lower()).strip("-")
    return slug[:max_len].rstrip("-") or "change"


def branch_name(wid: int, title: str, suggested: str = "") -> str:
    """Branch from the plan if it follows the policy pattern's prefix, otherwise build one."""
    pattern = settings()["git"]["branch_pattern"]
    prefix = pattern.split("{", 1)[0]
    suggested = (suggested or "").strip()
    if suggested and suggested.startswith(prefix) and re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9._/-]+", suggested):
        return suggested
    return pattern.format(id=wid, slug=slugify(title))

FILE: dev_pipe/coverage_handover.py

"""Hands the test run's coverage files from one job to the next, so the next job does not run the same tests
again on the same code just to recreate them.

    Step 4 (Build + Test + Coverage) --save--> Step 5 (Sonar Pre-check): first scan uses Step 4's files
    Step 5 --save--> Step 6 (passes out/ through) --> Step 7 (Quality gate): first PR scan uses Step 5's files

The files travel inside out/ (the stage artifact) with a manifest holding a FINGERPRINT of the code they were
made from: the starting commit + the exact change set (changes.patch). A job only reuses them when its code has
the same fingerprint; otherwise (or after any AI fix in that job) it runs the tests as before.

Not reused when the repo has Java/Kotlin code: Sonar needs the compiled classes, so that build runs anyway.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import shutil
import time
from pathlib import Path

from dev_pipe.config import OUT

FILES_DIR = "coverage-files"            # out/coverage-files/<path relative to the repo root>
MANIFEST = "coverage-files.json"


def code_fingerprint(base: str, patch: bytes) -> str:
    """Identifies the code: starting commit + change set."""
    return hashlib.sha256(base.encode() + b"\0" + (patch or b"")).hexdigest()


def has_java(scan_dir: Path) -> bool:
    from dev_pipe.sonar_precheck import java_args
    return bool(java_args(Path(scan_dir)))


def save(scan_dir: Path, root: Path, fingerprint: str, newer_than: float | None, out: Path | None = None) -> int:
    """Copies the coverage reports written after newer_than (under scan_dir) into out/coverage-files/.
    Returns the number of files saved (0 = nothing to hand over; the manifest is then removed)."""
    from dev_pipe.sonar_precheck import coverage_reports
    out = out or OUT
    dest = out / FILES_DIR
    shutil.rmtree(dest, ignore_errors=True)
    (out / MANIFEST).unlink(missing_ok=True)
    lcov, py, jacoco = coverage_reports(Path(scan_dir), newer_than)
    root = Path(root).resolve()
    rels: list[str] = []
    out_resolved = out.resolve()
    for f in [*lcov, *py, *jacoco]:
        if out_resolved in Path(f).resolve().parents:
            continue   # our own hand-over copies, never a test run's report
        try:
            rel = Path(f).resolve().relative_to(root).as_posix()
        except ValueError:
            continue
        target = dest / rel
        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        shutil.copy2(f, target)
        rels.append(rel)
    if rels:
        out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (out / MANIFEST).write_text(json.dumps({"fingerprint": fingerprint, "files": rels, "saved_at": time.time()},
                                               indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"[coverage] handed over {len(rels)} coverage file(s) to the next step", flush=True)
    return len(rels)


def restore(root: Path, fingerprint: str, out: Path | None = None) -> list[str]:
    """Puts the saved coverage files back under root if they were made from the same code. Their timestamps are
    set to now, so the Sonar step treats them as this run's reports. Returns the restored paths ([] = not
    reusable: run the tests as before)."""
    out = out or OUT
    manifest_path = out / MANIFEST
    if not manifest_path.exists():
        print("[coverage] no coverage files from the previous step: tests run as usual", flush=True)
        return []
    manifest = json.loads(manifest_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if manifest.get("fingerprint") != fingerprint:
        print("[coverage] the previous step's coverage files are for different code: tests run as usual", flush=True)
        return []
    src_root = out / FILES_DIR
    rels = [r for r in manifest.get("files") or [] if (src_root / r).is_file()]
    if not rels or len(rels) != len(manifest.get("files") or []):
        print("[coverage] the previous step's coverage files are incomplete: tests run as usual", flush=True)
        return []
    now = time.time()
    root = Path(root)
    for rel in rels:
        target = root / rel
        target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        shutil.copy2(src_root / rel, target)
        os.utime(target, (now, now))
    print(f"[coverage] reused {len(rels)} coverage file(s) from the previous step (same code): "
          + ", ".join(rels[:5]), flush=True)
    return rels

FILE: dev_pipe/github_api.py

"""Small GitHub REST client used to discover target repos/branches and to open pull requests.

Read calls used for discovery (external mode):
  GET /orgs/{org}/repos (falls back to /users/{org}/repos), GET /repos/{o}/{r},
  GET /repos/{o}/{r}/branches, GET /repos/{o}/{r}/branches/{b},
  GET /repos/{o}/{r}/compare/{base}...{head}, GET /repos/{o}/{r}/pulls?state=open
  POST /graphql (read-only query: branch names + last commit dates, 100 per call)
Write call (Step 6, after approval): POST /repos/{o}/{r}/pulls

The token is only sent in the Authorization header and never logged.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from urllib.parse import quote

import requests


class GitHubError(RuntimeError):
    pass


class GitHub:
    def __init__(self, token: str, api: str | None = None, session: requests.Session | None = None):
        self.api = (api or os.environ.get("GITHUB_API_URL") or "https://api.github.com").rstrip("/")
        self.session = session or requests.Session()
        self.session.headers.update({"Accept": "application/vnd.github+json",
                                     "X-GitHub-Api-Version": "2022-11-28"})
        if token:
            self.session.headers["Authorization"] = f"Bearer {token}"

    # ---------- low level
    def _request(self, method: str, path: str, *, allow_404: bool = False, **kwargs):
        resp = self.session.request(method, f"{self.api}{path}", timeout=30, **kwargs)
        if allow_404 and resp.status_code == 404:
            return None
        if resp.status_code >= 400:
            raise GitHubError(f"GitHub {method} {path.split('?')[0]} -> HTTP {resp.status_code}: {resp.text[:300]}")
        return resp.json() if resp.content else {}

    def _paged(self, path: str, params: dict | None = None, limit: int = 100, allow_404: bool = False) -> list | None:
        items: list = []
        page = 1
        while len(items) < limit:
            batch = self._request("GET", path, allow_404=allow_404,
                                  params={**(params or {}), "per_page": 100, "page": page})
            if batch is None:
                return None
            items.extend(batch)
            if len(batch) < 100:
                break
            page += 1
        return items[:limit]

    # ---------- discovery
    def list_owner_repos(self, owner: str, limit: int = 100) -> list[dict]:
        """Repos of an org, or of a user account if `owner` is not an org."""
        repos = self._paged(f"/orgs/{quote(owner)}/repos", {"type": "all", "sort": "pushed"}, limit, allow_404=True)
        if repos is None:
            repos = self._paged(f"/users/{quote(owner)}/repos", {"type": "owner", "sort": "pushed"}, limit) or []
        return repos

    def get_repo(self, full_name: str) -> dict | None:
        return self._request("GET", f"/repos/{full_name}", allow_404=True)

    def list_branches(self, full_name: str, limit: int = 100) -> list[str]:
        return [b["name"] for b in (self._paged(f"/repos/{full_name}/branches", limit=limit) or [])]

    def branches_with_dates(self, full_name: str, limit: int = 100_000) -> list[dict]:
        """[{'name', 'sha', 'date'}] for every branch, via GraphQL: one call per 100 branches instead of one
        REST call per branch. Raises GitHubError on any problem (the caller then falls back to REST)."""
        owner, name = full_name.split("/", 1)
        url = (self.api[: -len("/api/v3")] + "/api/graphql") if self.api.endswith("/api/v3") else self.api + "/graphql"
        query = ("query($owner:String!,$name:String!,$after:String){repository(owner:$owner,name:$name){"
                 "refs(refPrefix:\"refs/heads/\",first:100,after:$after){pageInfo{hasNextPage endCursor}"
                 "nodes{name target{... on Commit{oid committedDate}}}}}}")
        out: list[dict] = []
        after = None
        while len(out) < limit:
            resp = self.session.post(url, json={"query": query, "variables": {"owner": owner, "name": name,
                                                                              "after": after}}, timeout=30)
            if resp.status_code >= 400:
                raise GitHubError(f"GitHub GraphQL branches of {full_name} -> HTTP {resp.status_code}: {resp.text[:200]}")
            body = resp.json() if resp.content else {}
            if body.get("errors") or not (body.get("data") or {}).get("repository"):
                raise GitHubError(f"GitHub GraphQL branches of {full_name}: {str(body.get('errors'))[:200]}")
            refs = body["data"]["repository"]["refs"]
            for n in refs.get("nodes") or []:
                target = n.get("target") or {}
                out.append({"name": n.get("name", ""), "sha": target.get("oid", ""),
                            "date": target.get("committedDate", "")})
            if not (refs.get("pageInfo") or {}).get("hasNextPage"):
                break
            after = refs["pageInfo"]["endCursor"]
        return out[:limit]

    def get_branch(self, full_name: str, branch: str) -> dict | None:
        """{'name', 'sha', 'date'} or None if the branch does not exist."""
        data = self._request("GET", f"/repos/{full_name}/branches/{quote(branch, safe='')}", allow_404=True)
        if not data:
            return None
        commit = data.get("commit") or {}
        inner = commit.get("commit") or {}
        date = (inner.get("committer") or {}).get("date") or (inner.get("author") or {}).get("date") or ""
        return {"name": data.get("name", branch), "sha": commit.get("sha", ""), "date": date}

    def compare(self, full_name: str, base: str, head: str) -> dict:
        """What `head` has that `base` does not: ahead_by, changed file paths, commit messages."""
        data = self._request("GET", f"/repos/{full_name}/compare/{quote(base, safe='')}...{quote(head, safe='')}",
                             allow_404=True) or {}
        return {
            "ahead_by": int(data.get("ahead_by", 0) or 0),
            "files": [f.get("filename", "") for f in data.get("files", []) or []],
            "messages": [((c.get("commit") or {}).get("message") or "") for c in data.get("commits", []) or []],
        }

    def open_pulls(self, full_name: str, limit: int = 100) -> list[dict]:
        pulls = self._paged(f"/repos/{full_name}/pulls", {"state": "open"}, limit) or []
        return [{"number": p.get("number"), "title": p.get("title") or "", "body": p.get("body") or "",
                 "head": (p.get("head") or {}).get("ref", ""), "base": (p.get("base") or {}).get("ref", "")}
                for p in pulls]

    # ---------- write
    def find_open_pull(self, full_name: str, head: str, base: str) -> dict | None:
        """The open PR from `head` into `base` (a re-run reuses it instead of failing on 'already exists')."""
        owner = full_name.split("/", 1)[0]
        pulls = self._request("GET", f"/repos/{full_name}/pulls",
                              params={"state": "open", "head": f"{owner}:{head}", "base": base}) or []
        if not pulls:
            return None
        p = pulls[0]
        return {"number": p["number"], "url": p["html_url"], "head": head, "base": base, "repo": full_name}

    def update_pull_request(self, full_name: str, number: int, title: str, body: str) -> None:
        self._request("PATCH", f"/repos/{full_name}/pulls/{number}", json={"title": title, "body": body})

    def create_pull_request(self, full_name: str, head: str, base: str, title: str, body: str) -> dict:
        existing = self.find_open_pull(full_name, head, base)
        if existing:
            self.update_pull_request(full_name, existing["number"], title, body)
            print(f"[github] PR #{existing['number']} already open for {head} -> {base}; updated it", flush=True)
            return {**existing, "reused": True}
        data = self._request("POST", f"/repos/{full_name}/pulls",
                             json={"title": title, "head": head, "base": base, "body": body})
        return {"number": data["number"], "url": data["html_url"], "head": head, "base": base, "repo": full_name}

FILE: dev_pipe/gitops.py

"""Git + GitHub helpers. The agent never commits, pushes or opens PRs; these do, after approval.

Local mode: runs in this repo (ROOT). External mode: runs in the target repo checkout (target-checkout/),
authenticating fetch/push with the target-repo token (see dev_pipe/workspace.py).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import subprocess

import requests

from dev_pipe.config import ROOT, TARGET_CHECKOUT, is_external, secret

BOT_EMAIL = "dev-agent" + "@" + "users.noreply.github.com"

# Never part of a change set: pipeline hand-off files and tool output.
EXCLUDES = [":(exclude)out", ":(exclude,glob)**/.scannerwork/**", ":(exclude,glob)**/.sonarqube/**",
            ":(exclude,glob)**/coverage.xml", ":(exclude,glob)**/.coverage",
            # LCOV output of JS/TS test runs (Vitest/Jest `lcov` reporter):
            ":(exclude,glob)**/coverage/lcov.info", ":(exclude,glob)**/coverage/lcov-report/**",
            # Left behind by the build check (installs, compileall, pytest, pip install -e), never part of a change:
            ":(exclude,glob)**/node_modules/**", ":(exclude,glob)**/__pycache__/**",
            ":(exclude,glob)**/*.egg-info/**", ":(exclude,glob)**/.pytest_cache/**"]

# Commands that talk to the remote and need the target-repo token in external mode.
_REMOTE_COMMANDS = {"push", "fetch", "pull", "ls-remote"}


def _cwd():
    return TARGET_CHECKOUT if is_external() else ROOT


def _env(args: tuple[str, ...]) -> dict | None:
    if not (args and args[0] in _REMOTE_COMMANDS):
        return None
    from dev_pipe.workspace import git_auth_env, take_local_token
    if is_external():
        return {**os.environ, **git_auth_env()}
    local = take_local_token()   # local mode, jobs checked out without stored credentials (Step 7)
    return {**os.environ, **git_auth_env(local)} if local else None


def git(*args: str, check: bool = True) -> str:
    proc = subprocess.run(["git", *args], cwd=_cwd(), capture_output=True, text=True, env=_env(args))
    if check and proc.returncode != 0:
        raise SystemExit(f"git {' '.join(args[:2])} failed: {proc.stderr.strip()[:500]}")
    return proc.stdout.strip()


def git_bytes(*args: str) -> bytes:
    """Binary-safe variant: patches must keep CRLF line endings byte-for-byte."""
    proc = subprocess.run(["git", *args], cwd=_cwd(), capture_output=True)
    if proc.returncode != 0:
        raise SystemExit(f"git {' '.join(args[:2])} failed: {proc.stderr.decode(errors='replace').strip()[:500]}")
    return proc.stdout


def configure_identity() -> None:
    git("config", "user.name", os.environ.get("GIT_AUTHOR_NAME", "dev-agent[bot]"))
    git("config", "user.email", os.environ.get("GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL") or BOT_EMAIL)


def head_sha() -> str:
    return git("rev-parse", "HEAD")


def snapshot_patch(base: str) -> bytes:
    """Everything changed since `base` (tracked + new files, even if the agent committed)
    as one binary-safe patch."""
    git("add", "-A", "--", ".", *EXCLUDES)
    return git_bytes("diff", "--cached", "--binary", base, "--", ".", *EXCLUDES)


def apply_patch(patch_path) -> None:
    if os.path.getsize(patch_path) == 0:
        return
    git("apply", "--index", "--whitespace=nowarn", str(patch_path))


def changed_files_stats(base: str) -> list[dict]:
    """Changed files since `base` with added/removed line counts (call after snapshot_patch)."""
    rows = []
    for line in git("diff", "--cached", "--numstat", base, "--", ".", *EXCLUDES).splitlines():
        added, removed, path = line.split("\t", 2)
        rows.append({"path": path, "added": added, "removed": removed})
    new_files = set(git("diff", "--cached", "--name-only", "--diff-filter=A", base, "--", ".", *EXCLUDES).splitlines())
    for r in rows:
        r["new"] = r["path"] in new_files
    return rows


def changed_paths(base: str) -> list[str]:
    """Paths changed since `base` (tracked edits + new untracked files), without staging anything."""
    tracked = git("diff", "--name-only", base, "--", ".", *EXCLUDES).splitlines()
    untracked = git("ls-files", "--others", "--exclude-standard", "--", ".", *EXCLUDES).splitlines()
    return sorted({p for p in tracked + untracked if p.strip()})


def change_diff(base: str) -> str:
    """Zero-context diff of everything changed since `base` (tracked edits + new files), used to find
    the exact lines the agent added or changed. Stages like snapshot_patch does; nothing is committed."""
    git("add", "-A", "--", ".", *EXCLUDES)
    return git("-c", "core.quotepath=false", "diff", "--cached", "-U0", "--no-color", "--no-ext-diff",
               base, "--", ".", *EXCLUDES)


_SKIP_FIND = {".git", "node_modules", "__pycache__", ".venv", "venv", ".scannerwork", ".sonarqube"}


def fix_architecture_file(base: str, rel_path: str) -> list[str]:
    """The architecture context file must live at the repo root (target.architecture_file), where Steps 1-2
    look for it. The agent sometimes writes it inside a sub-folder (e.g. backend/.github/...). Copies the
    agent CREATED in this run (not in `base`) are moved to the root path, or removed if the root file already
    exists. Files that were already in the repository are never touched. Returns log lines."""
    import shutil
    from pathlib import Path
    root = Path(_cwd())
    target = root / rel_path
    name = Path(rel_path).name
    tail = Path(rel_path).parts
    found = []
    for d, dirs, files in os.walk(root):
        dirs[:] = [x for x in dirs if x not in _SKIP_FIND]
        if name in files:
            p = Path(d) / name
            if p.resolve() != target.resolve() and p.parts[-len(tail):] == tail:
                found.append(p)
    notes = []
    for p in sorted(found):
        rel = p.relative_to(root).as_posix()
        existed = subprocess.run(["git", "cat-file", "-e", f"{base}:{rel}"], cwd=root,
                                 capture_output=True).returncode == 0
        if existed:
            continue   # part of the repository already: not ours to move
        if not target.exists():
            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            shutil.move(str(p), str(target))
            notes.append(f"moved {rel} -> {rel_path}")
        else:
            p.unlink()
            notes.append(f"removed duplicate {rel} ({rel_path} already exists)")
        try:   # drop folders left empty by the move (e.g. backend/.github/)
            p.parent.rmdir()
        except OSError:
            pass
    for n in notes:
        print(f"[workspace] architecture file: {n}", flush=True)
    return notes


def risky_changes(stats: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
    """Edits that remove or rewrite existing code -- the meeting's 'ask before changing existing blocks'."""
    out = []
    for r in stats:
        removed = int(r["removed"]) if r["removed"].isdigit() else 0
        if not r["new"] and removed > 0:
            out.append(r)
    return out


def push_branch(branch: str, base_branch: str = "") -> bool:
    """Pushes `branch`. If it already exists on the remote (a re-run of the same story), it is replaced
    only when every commit on it that the new commit lacks was made by the pipeline itself, so a
    developer's commits are never overwritten. Returns True if an existing branch was replaced.

    External mode checks the target repo out with only its latest commit (shallow). If the base branch moved
    since the earlier run, git could then not see that the old branch's history is part of the base, and listed
    every older base commit as "someone else's work", wrongly blocking the re-run. So before that check a
    shallow checkout fetches the base branch's full history (only on a re-run, when the branch already exists)."""
    remote = git("ls-remote", "origin", f"refs/heads/{branch}")
    if not remote:
        git("push", "--set-upstream", "origin", branch)
        return False
    remote_sha = remote.split()[0]
    git("fetch", "-q", "origin", f"+refs/heads/{branch}:refs/remotes/origin/{branch}")
    if git("rev-parse", "--is-shallow-repository", check=False) == "true":
        refspec = [f"+refs/heads/{base_branch}:refs/remotes/origin/{base_branch}"] if base_branch else []
        print(f"[git] '{branch}' exists from an earlier run; fetching full history of "
              f"'{base_branch or 'the remote'}' to check who made its commits", flush=True)
        git("fetch", "-q", "--unshallow", "origin", *refspec)
    me = git("config", "user.email")
    authors = set(git("log", "--format=%ae", f"origin/{branch}", "--not", "HEAD~1").splitlines())
    others = sorted(a for a in authors if a and a != me)
    if others:
        raise SystemExit(
            f"Branch '{branch}' already exists on the remote with commits by {', '.join(others)}. Not overwriting "
            "someone else's work: merge or close that PR and delete the branch, then re-run this step.")
    print(f"[git] '{branch}' exists from an earlier pipeline run (only pipeline commits); replacing it", flush=True)
    git("push", "--set-upstream", f"--force-with-lease={branch}:{remote_sha}", "origin", branch)
    return True


def create_pull_request(head: str, base: str, title: str, body: str) -> dict:
    if is_external():
        from dev_pipe.config import resolved_target
        from dev_pipe.github_api import GitHub, GitHubError
        from dev_pipe.workspace import take_token
        token = take_token()
        if not token:
            raise SystemExit("Missing TARGET_GITHUB_TOKEN (GH_PAT secret) to open a PR on the target repo.")
        try:
            return GitHub(token).create_pull_request(resolved_target()["full_name"], head, base, title, body)
        except GitHubError as err:
            raise SystemExit(f"GitHub PR creation failed: {err}")
    api = os.environ.get("GITHUB_API_URL", "https://api.github.com")
    repo = os.environ.get("GITHUB_REPOSITORY", "")
    if not repo:
        raise SystemExit("GITHUB_REPOSITORY is not set (this step is meant to run in GitHub Actions).")
    from dev_pipe.github_api import GitHub, GitHubError
    try:
        existing = GitHub(secret("GITHUB_TOKEN"), api).find_open_pull(repo, head, base)
        if existing:
            GitHub(secret("GITHUB_TOKEN"), api).update_pull_request(repo, existing["number"], title, body)
            print(f"[github] PR #{existing['number']} already open for {head} -> {base}; updated it", flush=True)
            return {**existing, "reused": True}
    except GitHubError as err:
        raise SystemExit(f"GitHub PR lookup failed: {err}")
    resp = requests.post(
        f"{api}/repos/{repo}/pulls",
        headers={"Authorization": f"Bearer {secret('GITHUB_TOKEN')}", "Accept": "application/vnd.github+json"},
        json={"title": title, "head": head, "base": base, "body": body},
        timeout=30,
    )
    if resp.status_code >= 400:
        raise SystemExit(f"GitHub PR creation failed: HTTP {resp.status_code}: {resp.text[:400]}")
    data = resp.json()
    return {"number": data["number"], "url": data["html_url"], "head": head, "base": base}

FILE: dev_pipe/index_base_branches.py

"""Keeps the AAVA index of every candidate repo's base branch fresh (run on a schedule).

First-time indexing can take up to ~1 hour, so doing it ahead of time keeps Steps 1-2 fast.
Only triggers indexing; it does not wait. Re-triggering an unchanged repo is a cheap no-op on the
AAVA side (per USAGE_GUIDE.md), and a new commit gets picked up.

Usage (GitHub Actions: .github/workflows/aava-index.yml):
    python -m dev_pipe.index_base_branches
Env: TARGET_GITHUB_TOKEN (or GH_PAT), optional DISCOVERY_ORG, AAVA_CONTEXT_BASE_URL.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os

from dev_pipe.aava import Aava, AavaError
from dev_pipe.config import settings
from dev_pipe.github_api import GitHub
from dev_pipe.io_utils import summary
from dev_pipe.resolver import Resolver
from dev_pipe.workspace import take_token


def main() -> None:
    cfg = settings()
    d = dict(cfg.get("discovery") or {})
    d["github_org"] = (d.get("github_org") or os.environ.get("DISCOVERY_ORG")
                       or os.environ.get("GITHUB_REPOSITORY_OWNER", ""))
    d["base_branch"] = cfg.get("git", {}).get("base_branch", "")
    a = cfg.get("aava") or {}
    token = take_token()
    if not token:
        raise SystemExit("Missing TARGET_GITHUB_TOKEN (GH_PAT secret).")
    gh = GitHub(token)
    aava = Aava(os.environ.get("AAVA_CONTEXT_BASE_URL") or a.get("base_url", ""),
                github_token=token if a.get("send_github_token", True) else "")
    resolver = Resolver(gh, aava, d)

    rows = []
    for repo in resolver.candidate_repos():
        c = resolver._base_candidate(repo, set())
        if not c:
            continue
        try:
            status = aava.trigger_index(c["owner"], c["repo"], c["branch"])
        except AavaError as err:
            status = f"error: {err}"
        rows.append(f"| {c['full_name']} | `{c['branch']}` | {status or 'ok'} |")
    summary("## AAVA index refresh\n\n| Repo | Branch | Result |\n|---|---|---|\n" + ("\n".join(rows) or "| (none) | | |"))


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: dev_pipe/io_utils.py

"""Hand-off files between jobs (out/) and the run summary reviewers see before approving."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os

from dev_pipe.config import OUT


def write_json(name: str, data) -> None:
    OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (OUT / name).write_text(json.dumps(data, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False), encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"[out] wrote out/{name}", flush=True)


def read_json(name: str, default=None):
    path = OUT / name
    if not path.exists():
        if default is not None:
            return default
        raise SystemExit(f"Missing input 'out/{name}'. Did the previous job finish and upload its artifact?")
    return json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def write_text(name: str, text: str) -> None:
    OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (OUT / name).write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
    print(f"[out] wrote out/{name}", flush=True)


def write_bytes(name: str, data: bytes) -> None:
    OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (OUT / name).write_bytes(data)
    print(f"[out] wrote out/{name}", flush=True)


def read_text(name: str, default: str | None = None) -> str:
    path = OUT / name
    if not path.exists():
        if default is not None:
            return default
        raise SystemExit(f"Missing input 'out/{name}'. Did the previous job finish and upload its artifact?")
    return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")


def append_log(line: str) -> None:
    """Running development summary, posted to ADO in Step 8 (ADO Update & Development Complete)."""
    OUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(OUT / "summary.md", "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        f.write(line.rstrip() + "\n")


def set_output(name: str, value: str) -> None:
    """Expose a value to later jobs (GitHub step output). No-op when run locally."""
    path = os.environ.get("GITHUB_OUTPUT")
    if path:
        with open(path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(f"{name}={value}\n")


def summary(markdown: str) -> None:
    """Append to the GitHub run summary (visible on the run page) and print it."""
    from dev_pipe.steplog import write_summary
    write_summary(markdown)


def cell(value, limit: int = 120) -> str:
    """Make a value safe for a markdown table cell."""
    text = str(value if value is not None else "").replace("|", "\\|").replace("\n", " ").strip()
    return text if len(text) <= limit else text[: limit - 3] + "..."

FILE: dev_pipe/lcov.py

"""LCOV coverage reports (JavaScript / TypeScript projects, e.g. Vitest or Jest with the `lcov` reporter).

Used for non-Python targets:
  - Step 4 reads the line coverage from the reports its own test run just wrote (report only, no gate).
  - Step 7 passes the report paths to the Sonar scanner (sonar.javascript.lcov.reportPaths).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from pathlib import Path

LCOV_NAME = "lcov.info"
_SKIP_DIRS = {"node_modules", ".git", ".scannerwork", "dist", "build"}
# File timestamps use a coarser kernel clock than time.time(), so a report written right after
# `newer_than` can carry an mtime a few milliseconds before it.
MTIME_SLACK_SECONDS = 2.0


def find_lcov_reports(root: Path, newer_than: float | None = None) -> list[Path]:
    """Every coverage/lcov.info or coverage/<project>/lcov.info under root (node_modules etc. skipped).
    newer_than: only reports written after this timestamp, so a stale report committed to the
    repo is never mistaken for this run's result."""
    root = Path(root)
    found: list[Path] = []
    for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(root):
        dirnames[:] = [d for d in dirnames if d not in _SKIP_DIRS]
        # coverage/lcov.info (Vitest/Jest) or coverage/<project>/lcov.info (Angular CLI / Karma)
        if LCOV_NAME in filenames and "coverage" in Path(dirpath).relative_to(root).parts[-2:]:
            path = Path(dirpath) / LCOV_NAME
            if newer_than is None or path.stat().st_mtime >= newer_than - MTIME_SLACK_SECONDS:
                found.append(path)
    return sorted(found)


def line_coverage(reports: list[Path]) -> float | None:
    """Total line coverage % across the reports (sum of LH / sum of LF), or None if no lines."""
    found = hit = 0
    for report in reports:
        for line in Path(report).read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").splitlines():
            if line.startswith("LF:"):
                found += _int(line[3:])
            elif line.startswith("LH:"):
                hit += _int(line[3:])
    if found == 0:
        return None
    return round(100.0 * hit / found, 1)


def report_paths_arg(reports: list[Path], base: Path) -> str:
    """Comma-separated paths relative to `base` (the scanner's working directory)."""
    return ",".join(Path(os.path.relpath(r, base)).as_posix() for r in reports)


def _int(text: str) -> int:
    try:
        return int(text.strip())
    except ValueError:
        return 0
