FILE: dev_pipe/leak_check.py

"""Last check before anything the AI wrote leaves the runner: no credentials in the change set or the plan.

Story text and repository content are untrusted: a hidden instruction could try to make the AI copy a secret
into the code, a comment or its notes. The other layers (secrets hidden from the AI, no network) make that very
hard; this one makes sure nothing that looks like a credential is pushed or posted.

Checks only ADDED lines of the patch, plus the plan / AI notes that go into the PR and ADO:
  - the exact values (and their base64 forms) of the secrets this step holds, and
  - well-known credential formats (GitHub, Azure, AWS, Slack, Anthropic/OpenAI keys, private keys, JWTs, ...).
Values are never printed: findings name the file, the line number and the kind of secret only.
security.leak_check in config/dev-pipe.yaml: block (default) | warn | off.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import os
import re
from dataclasses import dataclass

PATTERNS: dict[str, re.Pattern] = {
    "GitHub token": re.compile(r"\b(gh[pousr]_[A-Za-z0-9]{36,}|github_pat_[A-Za-z0-9_]{60,})\b"),
    "private key": re.compile(r"-----BEGIN (?:RSA |EC |DSA |OPENSSH |PGP |ENCRYPTED )?PRIVATE KEY-----"),
    "AWS access key": re.compile(r"\b(AKIA|ASIA)[0-9A-Z]{16}\b"),
    "Azure storage key": re.compile(r"AccountKey=[A-Za-z0-9+/]{80,}={0,2}"),
    "Slack token": re.compile(r"\bxox[abprs]-[A-Za-z0-9-]{10,}\b"),
    "Anthropic key": re.compile(r"\bsk-ant-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}\b"),
    "OpenAI key": re.compile(r"\bsk-(?:proj-)?[A-Za-z0-9_-]{32,}\b"),
    "SonarQube token": re.compile(r"\b(squ|sqp|sqa)_[a-f0-9]{40}\b"),
    "JSON web token": re.compile(r"\beyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.eyJ[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]{10,}"),
    "basic/bearer credential": re.compile(r"(?i)authorization:\s*(basic|bearer)\s+[A-Za-z0-9+/=._-]{20,}"),
}
# Environment variables whose values must never appear in the AI's output.
SECRET_VARS = ("ADO_PAT", "GH_PAT", "TARGET_GITHUB_TOKEN", "GITHUB_TOKEN", "LOCAL_GIT_TOKEN", "SONAR_TOKEN",
               "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY", "NPM_TOKEN", "NODE_AUTH_TOKEN")


@dataclass
class Finding:
    where: str      # file path (or "plan.md") and line number
    kind: str       # what was found, never the value

    def __str__(self) -> str:
        return f"{self.where}: {self.kind}"


def _known_values(extra: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> dict[str, str]:
    values = {n: os.environ.get(n, "") for n in SECRET_VARS}
    values.update(extra or {})
    out: dict[str, str] = {}
    for name, value in values.items():
        value = (value or "").strip()
        if len(value) < 12:
            continue   # too short to search for safely
        out[value] = f"the value of {name}"
        out[base64.b64encode(value.encode()).decode().rstrip("=")] = f"the value of {name} (base64)"
        out[base64.b64encode(f"x-access-token:{value}".encode()).decode().rstrip("=")] = f"the value of {name} (git auth header)"
    return out


def _scan_line(line: str, where: str, known: dict[str, str]) -> list[Finding]:
    found = [Finding(where, kind) for value, kind in known.items() if value and value in line]
    found += [Finding(where, kind) for kind, pattern in PATTERNS.items() if pattern.search(line)]
    return found


def scan_text(text: str, where: str, known: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> list[Finding]:
    known = known if known is not None else _known_values()
    found: list[Finding] = []
    for n, line in enumerate((text or "").splitlines(), 1):
        found += _scan_line(line, f"{where}, line {n}", known)
    return found


def scan_patch(patch: str | bytes, known: dict[str, str] | None = None) -> list[Finding]:
    """Only lines the change ADDS (old content that was already in the repo is not the AI's doing)."""
    text = patch.decode("utf-8", errors="replace") if isinstance(patch, bytes) else (patch or "")
    known = known if known is not None else _known_values()
    found: list[Finding] = []
    path, new_line = "?", 0
    for line in text.splitlines():
        if line.startswith("+++ "):
            path = line[4:].strip()
            path = path[2:] if path.startswith("b/") else path
            continue
        if line.startswith("--- ") or line.startswith("diff --git") or line.startswith("index "):
            continue
        m = re.match(r"^@@ -\d+(?:,\d+)? \+(\d+)", line)
        if m:
            new_line = int(m.group(1))
            continue
        if line.startswith("+"):
            found += _scan_line(line[1:], f"{path}, line {new_line}", known)
            new_line += 1
        elif not line.startswith("-"):
            new_line += 1
    return found


def mode() -> str:
    from dev_pipe.config import settings
    value = str(((settings().get("security") or {}).get("leak_check", "block"))).strip().lower()
    return value if value in ("block", "warn", "off") else "block"


def enforce(findings: list[Finding], what: str) -> None:
    """block: stop the step (nothing is pushed/posted). warn: print and continue. off: nothing."""
    m = mode()
    if m == "off" or not findings:
        return
    lines = "\n".join(f"  - {f}" for f in findings[:20])
    message = (f"Possible credential(s) in {what} (values not shown):\n{lines}\n"
               "Nothing was pushed. Check these lines; if they are harmless (e.g. a fake key in a test), "
               "change them, or set security.leak_check: warn in config/dev-pipe.yaml.")
    if m == "warn":
        print(f"[leak-check] WARNING: {message}", flush=True)
        return
    raise SystemExit(message)

FILE: dev_pipe/report_data.py

"""Small facts for the plain-English step logs: which tests are new, and per-file coverage.

Read-only helpers; every function returns empty results instead of failing, so logging never breaks a step.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import re
import xml.etree.ElementTree as ET
from pathlib import Path

_SKIP = {"node_modules", ".git", "__pycache__", ".venv", "venv", "dist", "build", ".scannerwork", ".sonarqube"}
# The ONE rule for "is this a test file?" (Step 4's completion check in agent_runner uses it too).
_TEST_PATH = re.compile(r"(^|/)([Tt]ests?|__tests__|spec|src/test)/|(^|/)test_[^/]+\.py$|_test\.(py|go)$|"
                        r"\.(test|spec)\.[cm]?[jt]sx?$|[a-z0-9]Tests?\.(cs|fs|vb|java|kt|scala)$|\.Tests?/")
_PY_TEST = re.compile(r"^\+\s*(?:async\s+)?def\s+(test_\w+)\s*\(")
_JS_TEST = re.compile(r"""^\+\s*(?:it|test)(?:\.\w+)?\(\s*(['"`])(.+?)\1""")
_JAVA_CS_TEST = re.compile(r"^\+\s*(?:public\s+)?(?:async\s+)?(?:void|Task)\s+(\w+)\s*\(")


def is_test_path(path: str) -> bool:
    return bool(_TEST_PATH.search(path.replace("\\", "/")))


def new_test_names(diff_text: str) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    """`git diff -U0` text -> {test file: [names of test functions/cases added]}."""
    found: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    current = ""
    prev_annotation = False
    for line in diff_text.splitlines():
        if line.startswith("+++ "):
            target = line[4:].strip()
            current = target[2:] if target.startswith("b/") else ("" if target == "/dev/null" else target)
            continue
        if not current or not is_test_path(current) or not line.startswith("+"):
            prev_annotation = False
            continue
        m = _PY_TEST.match(line) or _JS_TEST.match(line)
        name = (m.group(2) if m and m.re is _JS_TEST else m.group(1)) if m else ""
        if not name and prev_annotation:
            j = _JAVA_CS_TEST.match(line)
            name = j.group(1) if j else ""
        prev_annotation = bool(re.match(r"^\+\s*(@Test|\[(Fact|Theory|Test|TestMethod)\])", line))
        if name:
            found.setdefault(current, []).append(name)
    return found


def _walk(root: Path):
    for d, dirs, files in os.walk(root):
        dirs[:] = [x for x in dirs if x not in _SKIP]
        yield Path(d), files


def file_coverage(root: Path, rel_paths: list[str], newer_than: float | None = None) -> dict[str, float]:
    """{repo-relative source path: % of lines covered} for the given files, read from the coverage reports the
    test run wrote (Cobertura coverage.xml from pytest-cov, LCOV lcov.info from Vitest/Jest)."""
    root = Path(root).resolve()
    wanted = [p.replace("\\", "/") for p in rel_paths]
    result: dict[str, float] = {}

    def match(path: str) -> str | None:
        path = path.replace("\\", "/")
        hits = [w for w in wanted if w == path or w.endswith("/" + path) or path.endswith("/" + w)]
        return max(hits, key=len) if hits else None

    for d, files in _walk(root):
        for f in files:
            report = d / f
            if f not in ("coverage.xml", "lcov.info"):
                continue
            try:
                if newer_than is not None and report.stat().st_mtime < newer_than - 2:
                    continue
                if f == "coverage.xml":
                    tree = ET.parse(report)
                    sources = [Path(s.text.strip()) for s in tree.iter("source") if s.text and s.text.strip()]
                    for cls in tree.iter("class"):
                        name = cls.get("filename", "")
                        candidates = [name]
                        for src in sources or [report.parent]:
                            try:
                                candidates.append((src / name).resolve().relative_to(root).as_posix())
                            except ValueError:
                                pass
                        key = next((k for k in (match(c) for c in candidates) if k), None)
                        if key:
                            result[key] = round(100 * float(cls.get("line-rate", 0)), 1)
                else:
                    sf, lf, lh = "", 0, 0
                    for line in report.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").splitlines():
                        if line.startswith("SF:"):
                            sf, lf, lh = line[3:].strip(), 0, 0
                        elif line.startswith("LF:"):
                            lf = int(line[3:] or 0)
                        elif line.startswith("LH:"):
                            lh = int(line[3:] or 0)
                        elif line.startswith("end_of_record") and sf:
                            path = sf
                            try:
                                path = Path(sf).resolve().relative_to(root).as_posix() if os.path.isabs(sf) else sf
                            except ValueError:
                                pass
                            key = match(path)
                            if key and lf:
                                result[key] = round(100 * lh / lf, 1)
            except (OSError, ValueError, ET.ParseError):
                continue
    return result


_CMD = re.compile(r"^\$ (.+?)  \(exit (-?\d+)\)", re.M)


def friendly_command(cmd: str) -> str:
    c = cmd.lower()
    if any(k in c for k in ("pip install", "npm ci", "npm install", "yarn install", "pnpm install", "poetry install",
                            "venv.create", "dotnet restore", "go mod download")):
        return "Installed dependencies"
    if any(k in c for k in ("pytest", "npm test", "npm run test", "yarn test", "pnpm test", "dotnet test",
                            "go test", "vitest", "jest")):
        return "Ran the tests"
    if any(k in c for k in ("compileall", "tsc", "npm run build", "yarn build", "pnpm build", "dotnet build",
                            "go build", "go vet", "mvn", "gradle", "typecheck")):
        return "Compiled / built the code"
    return "Ran " + cmd.split()[0].rsplit("/", 1)[-1]


def command_steps(build_log: str) -> list[tuple[str, bool]]:
    """Build log -> [(plain label, ok)], one per distinct label, in order."""
    seen: dict[str, bool] = {}
    for cmd, code in _CMD.findall(build_log or ""):
        label = friendly_command(cmd)
        ok = int(code) in (0, 5) if "pytest" in cmd else int(code) == 0
        seen[label] = seen.get(label, True) and ok
    return list(seen.items())

....................................................................................................

FILE: dev_pipe/resolver.py

"""Find which repo and branch a user story belongs to (external mode, Steps 1-2).

Flow:
  0. Manual override: TARGET_REPO ("owner/repo") and optional TARGET_BRANCH skip detection.
  1. Base branches: for each candidate repo (GitHub org listing or discovery.include_repos), make sure its
     base branch is indexed in AAVA, search it with the story, and score it by the best similarity.
     A clear winner above discovery.base_min_similarity is used.
  2. Branch fallback (nothing good on the base branches): for the most likely repos, list the branches
     (GitHub), newest last commit first, at most discovery.max_branches_scanned of them per repo (0 = all),
     make sure each one is indexed in AAVA and search every one of them.
     The branch with the best similarity above discovery.base_min_similarity wins.
GitHub look-ups, AAVA index checks and AAVA searches run discovery.parallel_requests at a time (default 6).
  3. Nothing qualifies: use the most likely repo's base branch with confidence "low" and list the
     branches with their scores for the reviewer to pick at the plan gate, or stop if there is no
     signal at all.
The chosen branch is both where work starts and the PR target (so a PR never drags in someone
else's unmerged work). A human confirms the choice at the plan approval gate (before Step 3).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import fnmatch
import re
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from dev_pipe.aava import DONE_STATES, FAILED_STATES, Aava, AavaError
from dev_pipe.github_api import GitHub, GitHubError

STOPWORDS = {
    "the", "and", "that", "this", "with", "from", "into", "when", "then", "should", "must", "will", "have",
    "user", "users", "able", "want", "need", "needs", "given", "story", "acceptance", "criteria", "as", "so",
    "for", "are", "can", "not", "all", "any", "each", "also", "only", "there", "their", "which", "what",
    "where", "while", "after", "before", "using", "used", "new", "add", "added", "update", "updated",
    "make", "display", "show", "shown", "system", "page", "field", "fields", "value", "values", "data",
}


class ResolveError(SystemExit):
    pass


# ---------- pure helpers (unit-tested)
def _stem(token: str) -> str:
    if token.endswith(("ss", "us", "is")):      # status, process, analysis: not plurals
        return token
    for suffix in ("ing", "ies", "es", "ed", "s"):
        if len(token) > len(suffix) + 3 and token.endswith(suffix):
            return token[: -len(suffix)] + ("y" if suffix == "ies" else "")
    return token


def tokens(text: str) -> set[str]:
    return {_stem(t) for t in re.findall(r"[a-z0-9]+", (text or "").lower())}


def story_keywords(story: dict) -> set[str]:
    text = f"{story.get('title', '')} {story.get('acceptance_criteria', '')}"
    return {t for t in tokens(text) if len(t) >= 4 and t not in STOPWORDS and not t.isdigit()}


def story_queries(story: dict, max_queries: int = 3) -> list[str]:
    candidates = [
        story.get("title", ""),
        f"{story.get('title', '')}. {story.get('acceptance_criteria', '')}"[:400],
        (story.get("description", "") or "")[:400],
    ]
    seen, out = set(), []
    for q in (c.strip() for c in candidates):
        if q and q not in seen:
            seen.add(q)
            out.append(q)
    return out[:max(1, max_queries)]


def keyword_hits(text: str, keywords: set[str]) -> int:
    return len(tokens(text) & keywords)


def excluded(name: str, patterns: list[str]) -> bool:
    return any(fnmatch.fnmatch(name, p) for p in patterns or [])


def _words(text: str) -> str:
    return (text or "").replace("/", " ").replace("-", " ").replace("_", " ").replace(".", " ")


def branch_signals(keywords: set[str], branch: str, compare: dict, pulls: list[dict]) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
    """GitHub-only keyword evidence that `branch` holds the story's code. Returns (score, evidence).
    A score of 0 is normal (generic branch names, short commit messages) and does not exclude the branch."""
    score, ev = 0, []
    hits = keyword_hits(_words(branch), keywords)
    if hits:
        score += 2 * hits
        ev.append(f"{hits} story keyword(s) in branch name")
    for p in (p for p in pulls if p.get("head") == branch):
        text = f"{p.get('title', '')} {p.get('body', '')}"
        h = keyword_hits(text, keywords)
        if h:
            score += 2 * h
            ev.append(f"open PR #{p.get('number')} matches {h} keyword(s)")
    messages = " ".join(compare.get("messages", []))
    h = keyword_hits(messages, keywords)
    if h:
        score += h
        ev.append(f"{h} keyword(s) in commit messages")
    h = keyword_hits(_words(" ".join(compare.get("files", []))), keywords)
    if h:
        score += h
        ev.append(f"{h} keyword(s) in changed file paths")
    return score, ev


def decide(candidates: list[dict], min_similarity: float, margin: float) -> tuple[dict | None, str]:
    """Best candidate by similarity if it clears the threshold; confidence 'high' only with a clear margin."""
    ranked = sorted(candidates, key=lambda c: c.get("similarity", 0), reverse=True)
    if not ranked or ranked[0].get("similarity", 0) < min_similarity:
        return None, "none"
    second = ranked[1].get("similarity", 0) if len(ranked) > 1 else 0.0
    return ranked[0], ("high" if ranked[0]["similarity"] - second >= margin else "low")


# ---------- resolver
class Resolver:
    def __init__(self, gh: GitHub, aava: Aava, cfg: dict, log=print, now: datetime | None = None):
        self.gh, self.aava, self.cfg = gh, aava, cfg
        self.a = cfg.get("aava", {}) or {}
        self.log = log
        self.now = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        self.trace: list[str] = []
        self.workers = max(1, int(cfg.get("parallel_requests", 6) or 1))

    def _parallel(self, fn, items: list) -> list:
        """fn(item) for every item, `parallel_requests` at a time; results in the same order as items."""
        items = list(items)
        if self.workers == 1 or len(items) <= 1:
            return [fn(i) for i in items]
        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=min(self.workers, len(items))) as pool:
            return list(pool.map(fn, items))

    def _note(self, text: str) -> None:
        self.trace.append(text)
        self.log(f"[resolve] {text}")

    # ----- candidates
    def candidate_repos(self) -> list[dict]:
        owner = self.cfg.get("github_org", "")
        include = self.cfg.get("include_repos") or []
        if include:
            repos = []
            for name in include:
                full = name if "/" in name else f"{owner}/{name}"
                info = self.gh.get_repo(full)
                if info:
                    repos.append(info)
                else:
                    self._note(f"{full}: not found or no access, skipped")
            return repos
        if not owner:
            raise ResolveError("discovery.github_org is empty: set it in config/dev-pipe.yaml or the DISCOVERY_ORG variable.")
        exclude = self.cfg.get("exclude_repos") or []
        repos = []
        for r in self.gh.list_owner_repos(owner, limit=300):
            if self.cfg.get("skip_archived", True) and (r.get("archived") or r.get("disabled")):
                continue
            if self.cfg.get("skip_forks", True) and r.get("fork"):
                continue
            if excluded(r.get("name", ""), exclude) or excluded(r.get("full_name", ""), exclude):
                continue
            repos.append(r)
        return repos[: int(self.cfg.get("max_repos", 30))]

    def _candidate(self, repo: dict, branch: str, kind: str, sha: str = "") -> dict:
        owner, name = repo["full_name"].split("/", 1)
        return {"full_name": repo["full_name"], "owner": owner, "repo": name,
                "default_branch": repo.get("default_branch", "main"), "branch": branch, "sha": sha,
                "kind": kind, "similarity": 0.0, "signal": 0, "last_commit": "", "matches": [], "evidence": [],
                "repo_keywords": 0, "index_state": ""}

    def _base_candidate(self, repo: dict, keywords: set[str]) -> dict | None:
        preferred = self.cfg.get("base_branch") or ""
        info = self.gh.get_branch(repo["full_name"], preferred) if preferred else None
        branch = preferred if info else repo.get("default_branch") or "main"
        info = info or self.gh.get_branch(repo["full_name"], branch)
        if not info:
            self._note(f"{repo['full_name']}: base branch '{branch}' not found, skipped")
            return None
        c = self._candidate(repo, branch, "base", info["sha"])
        c["repo_keywords"] = keyword_hits(
            f"{repo.get('name', '')} {repo.get('description') or ''} {' '.join(repo.get('topics') or [])}"
            .replace("-", " ").replace("_", " "), keywords)
        return c

    # ----- AAVA
    def ensure_indexed(self, cands: list[dict]) -> None:
        def check(c: dict) -> str:
            key = (c["owner"], c["repo"], c["branch"])
            try:
                state = self.aava.state_of(self.aava.progress(*key))
                if state == "NOT_INDEXED" and self.a.get("index_missing", True):
                    status = self.aava.trigger_index(*key)
                    self._note(f"{c['full_name']}@{c['branch']}: not indexed, indexing triggered ({status or 'ok'})")
                    state = "RUNNING"
            except AavaError as err:
                state = f"ERROR({err})"
            return state

        pending = []
        for c, state in zip(cands, self._parallel(check, cands)):
            c["index_state"] = state
            if state not in DONE_STATES | FAILED_STATES and not state.startswith("ERROR") and state != "NOT_INDEXED":
                pending.append((c["owner"], c["repo"], c["branch"]))
        if pending:
            final = self.aava.wait_until_done(pending, float(self.a.get("index_wait_seconds", 900)),
                                              float(self.a.get("poll_seconds", 15)))
            for c in cands:
                c["index_state"] = final.get((c["owner"], c["repo"], c["branch"]), c["index_state"])

    def search(self, c: dict, queries: list[str]) -> None:
        if c["index_state"] == "NOT_INDEXED" or c["index_state"].startswith("ERROR"):
            return
        found: dict = {}
        for q in queries:
            try:
                for m in self.aava.search(c["owner"], c["repo"], c["branch"], q,
                                          self.a.get("intent", "feature_flow"), int(self.a.get("limit", 5))):
                    key = (m["filePath"], m["startLine"])
                    if key not in found or m["similarity"] > found[key]["similarity"]:
                        found[key] = m
            except AavaError as err:
                self._note(f"{c['full_name']}@{c['branch']}: search failed ({err})")
        c["matches"] = sorted(found.values(), key=lambda m: m["similarity"], reverse=True)[:10]
        c["similarity"] = c["matches"][0]["similarity"] if c["matches"] else 0.0

    # ----- branch fallback
    def _branch_infos(self, full: str) -> list[dict]:
        """[{name, sha, date}] for every branch, newest last commit first. One GitHub GraphQL call per 100
        branches when available; otherwise one REST call per branch (run in parallel)."""
        bulk = getattr(self.gh, "branches_with_dates", None)
        if bulk:
            try:
                infos = bulk(full)
                return sorted(infos, key=lambda i: i.get("date") or "", reverse=True)
            except GitHubError as err:
                self._note(f"{full}: branch list with dates failed ({err}); listing branches one by one")
        names = self.gh.list_branches(full, limit=100_000)
        infos = [i for i in self._parallel(lambda n: self.gh.get_branch(full, n), names) if i]
        return sorted(infos, key=lambda i: i.get("date") or "", reverse=True)

    def scan_branches(self, base: dict, keywords: set[str]) -> list[dict]:
        """The branches of base's repo except the base branch itself, newest last commit first, at most
        discovery.max_branches_scanned (0 = all). They are indexed and searched in AAVA. The keyword score
        (branch name + open PR) is only shown as evidence and used to break ties."""
        full = base["full_name"]
        pulls = self.gh.open_pulls(full)
        repo = {"full_name": full, "default_branch": base["default_branch"]}
        infos = [i for i in self._branch_infos(full) if i["name"] != base["branch"]]
        cap = int(self.cfg.get("max_branches_scanned", 0) or 0)
        if cap and len(infos) > cap:
            self._note(f"{full}: {len(infos)} branches; only the {cap} with the most recent commits are scanned "
                       f"(discovery.max_branches_scanned). Use target_branch to pick an older one.")
            infos = infos[:cap]
        out = []
        for info in infos:
            name = info["name"]
            score, ev = branch_signals(keywords, name, {}, pulls)
            c = self._candidate(repo, name, "branch", info["sha"])
            c.update({"signal": score, "last_commit": info["date"], "base_of": base["branch"],
                      "evidence": ev or [f"last commit {info['date'][:10] or '?'}"]})
            out.append(c)
        self._note(f"{full}: {len(out)} branch(es) besides '{base['branch']}' will be indexed + searched")
        return out

    # ----- main entry
    def resolve(self, story: dict, override_repo: str = "", override_branch: str = "") -> dict:
        queries = story_queries(story, int(self.a.get("max_queries", 3)))
        self.last_queries = queries
        keywords = story_keywords(story)
        min_sim = float(self.cfg.get("base_min_similarity", 0.75))
        margin = float(self.cfg.get("min_margin", 0.05))

        if override_repo:
            return self._manual(override_repo, override_branch, queries)

        repos = self.candidate_repos()
        if not repos:
            raise ResolveError("No candidate repositories found (check discovery settings and token access).")
        self._note(f"{len(repos)} candidate repo(s); queries: {queries}")

        # Phase 1: base branches
        bases = [c for c in self._parallel(lambda r: self._base_candidate(r, keywords), repos) if c]
        self.ensure_indexed(bases)
        self._parallel(lambda c: self.search(c, queries), bases)
        for c in bases:
            self._note(f"{c['full_name']}@{c['branch']}: top similarity {c['similarity']:.2f} "
                       f"(index {c['index_state']}, repo keywords {c['repo_keywords']})")
        ranked = sorted(bases, key=lambda c: (c["similarity"], c["repo_keywords"]), reverse=True)
        best, confidence = decide(ranked, min_sim, margin)
        if best:
            return self._result(best, "base", confidence,
                                f"found on base branch '{best['branch']}' (similarity {best['similarity']:.2f})", ranked)

        # Phase 2: other branches of the most likely repos
        self._note(f"nothing above {min_sim} on base branches; scanning other branches")
        branch_cands = []
        for base in ranked[: int(self.cfg.get("repos_for_branch_scan", 3))]:
            try:
                branch_cands += self.scan_branches(base, keywords)
            except GitHubError as err:
                self._note(f"{base['full_name']}: branch scan failed ({err})")
        # All branches are searched; this order only decides ties and the order of the log.
        shortlist = sorted(branch_cands, key=lambda c: (c["signal"], c["last_commit"]), reverse=True)
        if shortlist:
            self.ensure_indexed(shortlist)
            self._parallel(lambda c: self.search(c, queries), shortlist)
            for c in shortlist:
                self._note(f"{c['full_name']}@{c['branch']}: top similarity {c['similarity']:.2f}")
        shortlist.sort(key=lambda c: (c["similarity"], c["signal"], c["last_commit"]), reverse=True)
        best, confidence = decide(shortlist, min_sim, margin)
        if best:
            why = f"similarity {best['similarity']:.2f}; " + "; ".join(best["evidence"])
            others = sorted(shortlist, key=lambda c: c["similarity"], reverse=True)
            return self._result(best, "branch", confidence,
                                f"not found on base branches; found on branch '{best['branch']}' ({why})",
                                others + ranked)

        # Phase 3: fall back to the most likely repo (could be brand-new work), or stop.
        top = ranked[0]
        if top["similarity"] > 0 or top["repo_keywords"] > 0 or len(ranked) == 1 or shortlist:
            others = sorted(shortlist, key=lambda c: c["similarity"], reverse=True) + ranked
            return self._result(top, "base", "low",
                                "no strong match anywhere; using the most likely repo's base branch "
                                "(the story may be new work). If the code is on one of the candidate branches "
                                "listed below, reject and re-run with target_repo / target_branch.", others)
        raise ResolveError(
            "Could not decide the target repository for this story. Re-run with the `target_repo` input "
            "(owner/repo) and optionally `target_branch`.\n" + "\n".join(self.trace[-30:]))

    def _manual(self, full: str, branch: str, queries: list[str]) -> dict:
        repo = self.gh.get_repo(full)
        if not repo:
            raise ResolveError(f"TARGET_REPO '{full}' not found or the token has no access to it.")
        branch = branch or self.cfg.get("base_branch") or repo.get("default_branch") or "main"
        info = self.gh.get_branch(full, branch)
        if not info:
            raise ResolveError(f"Branch '{branch}' not found in {full}.")
        c = self._candidate(repo, branch, "manual", info["sha"])
        self.ensure_indexed([c])
        self.search(c, queries)
        return self._result(c, "manual", "manual", f"set manually: {full}@{branch}", [c])

    def _result(self, c: dict, found_on: str, confidence: str, reason: str, others: list[dict]) -> dict:
        alts, seen = [], {(c["full_name"], c["branch"])}
        for o in others:
            if (o["full_name"], o["branch"]) in seen:
                continue
            seen.add((o["full_name"], o["branch"]))
            alts.append({"full_name": o["full_name"], "branch": o["branch"],
                         "similarity": round(o["similarity"], 3), "signal": o["signal"],
                         "last_commit": o.get("last_commit", ""), "evidence": "; ".join(o.get("evidence", []))})
        alts = alts[:15]
        self._note(f"chosen {c['full_name']}@{c['branch']} ({confidence}): {reason}")
        return {
            "full_name": c["full_name"], "owner": c["owner"], "repo": c["repo"],
            "default_branch": c["default_branch"],
            "base_branch": c["branch"],          # work starts here and the PR targets it
            "base_sha": c["sha"],
            "found_on": found_on, "confidence": confidence, "reason": reason,
            "similarity": round(c["similarity"], 3), "evidence": c["evidence"],
            "matches": c["matches"], "alternatives": alts, "trace": self.trace[-50:],
            "index_state": c.get("index_state", ""), "queries": len(getattr(self, "last_queries", []) or []),
        }


def code_context(target: dict, limit: int = 8) -> str:
    """AAVA matches for the chosen repo/branch, as a prompt section."""
    rows = []
    for m in (target.get("matches") or [])[:limit]:
        loc = f"{m['filePath']}:{m.get('startLine') or '?'}-{m.get('endLine') or '?'}"
        preview = (m.get("contentPreview") or "").strip()[:600]
        rows.append(f"### {loc} ({m.get('name') or ''}, similarity {m['similarity']:.2f})\n```\n{preview}\n```")
    return "\n\n".join(rows) or "(no matches from the code context service; explore the repo with Grep/Glob)"

FILE: dev_pipe/sonar.py

"""Step 7: SonarQube Quality Validation on the pull request.

Runs a PR analysis (sonar.pullrequest.*) and reads the gate result from the Web API.
Needs PR analysis support: SonarCloud, or SonarQube Developer edition or higher.
The step 4 pre-check (dev_agent/checks/sonar_precheck.py) is reused unchanged.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import shutil
import subprocess

import requests


def run_pr_scan(app_dir: str, project_key: str, host_url: str, token: str, organization: str,
                pr: dict, wait_seconds: int, extra_args: list[str] | None = None) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
    scanner = shutil.which("sonar-scanner") or "sonar-scanner"
    args = [
        scanner,
        f"-Dsonar.projectKey={project_key}",
        f"-Dsonar.host.url={host_url}",
        "-Dsonar.sourceEncoding=UTF-8",
        f"-Dsonar.pullrequest.key={pr['number']}",
        f"-Dsonar.pullrequest.branch={pr['head']}",
        f"-Dsonar.pullrequest.base={pr['base']}",
        "-Dsonar.qualitygate.wait=true",
        f"-Dsonar.qualitygate.timeout={int(wait_seconds)}",
    ]
    if organization:
        args.append(f"-Dsonar.organization={organization}")
    args += list(extra_args or [])
    # Token via env, not argv, so it never shows in process listings or logs.
    return subprocess.run(args, cwd=app_dir, capture_output=True, text=True,
                          encoding="utf-8", errors="replace",
                          env={**scanner_env(), "SONAR_TOKEN": token})


# CI variables of the PIPELINE run (this repo), not of the PR being scanned.
_CI_ENV_PREFIXES = ("GITHUB_", "ACTIONS_")
_CI_ENV_NAMES = {"CI", "GITHUB_ACTIONS"}


def scanner_env() -> dict:
    """The environment for sonar-scanner, without GitHub Actions' own CI variables.

    The scanner detects GitHub Actions from these and takes the run's context (repository,
    commit, ref, event) as the analysis context. In the Dev Pipeline that context belongs to the
    pipeline repo, not to the target repo's PR, and with it SonarCloud stored nothing for the PR
    ("0 New lines of code", no measures), while the same scan from a laptop stored 249 new lines.
    All PR / revision properties are passed explicitly instead (sonar.pullrequest.*, sonar.scm.revision)."""
    return {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items()
            if k not in _CI_ENV_NAMES and not k.startswith(_CI_ENV_PREFIXES)}


def scanner_problems(scan: subprocess.CompletedProcess, limit: int = 60) -> list[str]:
    """WARN/ERROR lines from the scanner output (e.g. which file has an encoding problem).
    The full output is captured, so without this the reason behind a Sonar 'analysis warning'
    never reaches the job log."""
    out = (scan.stdout or "") + "\n" + (scan.stderr or "")
    lines = [ln.rstrip() for ln in out.splitlines() if "WARN" in ln or "ERROR" in ln]
    return lines[:limit]


_SCM_MARKERS = ("SCM", "changed", "Changed", "Branch name", "pull request", "Pull request", "reference branch",
                "Could not find ref", "shallow", "Shallow", "Base dir", "Working dir", "Project key",
                "files indexed", "languages detected", "lcov", "LCOV", "coverage", "ANALYSIS SUCCESSFUL",
                "Merge base", "merge base", "Git ", "git ", "repository", "Repository", "status")


# Debug noise that would push the useful lines past the limit.
_NOISE = ("is excluded by the scm ignore settings", "Excluding file", "/node_modules/", "/coverage/lcov-report/",
          "Configuration: {", "FeatureFlagResponse")


def scanner_scm_lines(scan: subprocess.CompletedProcess, changed_files: list[str] | None = None,
                      limit: int = 200) -> list[str]:
    """Scanner lines that explain how the PR's changed lines ("New lines of code") were found:
    branch/PR settings, SCM detection and diff, indexed files and coverage import, plus every
    line that mentions one of the PR's changed files. Only WARN/ERROR lines were printed before,
    which hid why a PR could show 0 new lines."""
    out = (scan.stdout or "") + "\n" + (scan.stderr or "")
    all_lines = out.splitlines()
    names = [p for p in (changed_files or []) if p.strip()]
    lines = [ln.rstrip()[:400] for ln in all_lines
             if not any(n in ln for n in _NOISE)
             and (any(m in ln for m in _SCM_MARKERS) or any(p in ln for p in names))]
    return [f"scanner output: {len(all_lines)} line(s), {len(lines)} relevant"] + lines[:limit]


def debug_enabled() -> bool:
    return os.environ.get("SONAR_SCANNER_DEBUG", "false").strip().lower() in ("1", "true", "yes")


_DIAG_MARKERS = ("changed lines", "Changed lines", "changed files", "diff", "Diff", "jgit", "JGit", "Exception",
                 "Caused by", "Failed", "failed", "Unable", "unable", "not supported", "merge base", "Merge base",
                 "reference branch", "dirty", "blame", "Blame", "scm", "SCM")


def scanner_diag_lines(scan: subprocess.CompletedProcess, limit: int = 120) -> list[str]:
    """Debug mode: every scanner line about SCM, diffs, changed lines or errors (unfiltered by the PR's files)."""
    out = (scan.stdout or "") + "\n" + (scan.stderr or "")
    lines = [ln.rstrip()[:400] for ln in out.splitlines()
             if any(m in ln for m in _DIAG_MARKERS) and not any(n in ln for n in _NOISE)]
    return lines[:limit]


def scanner_debug_args() -> list[str]:
    """SONAR_SCANNER_DEBUG=true (repo variable) turns on the scanner's debug log, which states
    how it decided which files and lines a PR changed. Only the SCM-related lines are printed."""
    return ["-Dsonar.verbose=true"] if debug_enabled() else []


def fetch_pr_measures(host_url: str, token: str, project_key: str, pr_number: int) -> dict | str:
    """What SonarCloud stored for the PR (diagnostics only, never fails the step)."""
    try:
        resp = requests.get(f"{host_url.rstrip('/')}/api/measures/component",
                            params={"component": project_key, "pullRequest": pr_number,
                                    "metricKeys": "new_lines,new_coverage,new_lines_to_cover,ncloc"},
                            auth=(token, ""), timeout=30)
        resp.raise_for_status()
        measures = resp.json().get("component", {}).get("measures", [])
        def value(m: dict):  # PR "new_*" metrics come as periods: [{"value": ...}]
            periods = m.get("periods") or ([m["period"]] if m.get("period") else [])
            return m.get("value", periods[0].get("value") if periods else None)
        return {m.get("metric"): value(m) for m in measures} or "(no measures)"
    except Exception as err:  # noqa: BLE001 - diagnostics must not break the step
        return f"(could not read: {type(err).__name__})"


def scanner_tail(scan: subprocess.CompletedProcess, lines: int = 40) -> str:
    out = ((scan.stdout or "") + "\n" + (scan.stderr or "")).strip()
    return "\n".join(out.splitlines()[-lines:])


def fetch_gate(host_url: str, token: str, project_key: str, pr_number: int) -> dict:
    resp = requests.get(f"{host_url.rstrip('/')}/api/qualitygates/project_status",
                        params={"projectKey": project_key, "pullRequest": pr_number},
                        auth=(token, ""), timeout=30)
    resp.raise_for_status()
    return resp.json().get("projectStatus", {})


def fetch_pr_issues(host_url: str, token: str, project_key: str, pr_number: int) -> list[dict]:
    resp = requests.get(f"{host_url.rstrip('/')}/api/issues/search",
                        params={"componentKeys": project_key, "pullRequest": pr_number,
                                "resolved": "false", "ps": 500},
                        auth=(token, ""), timeout=30)
    resp.raise_for_status()
    return resp.json().get("issues", [])


def evaluate_gate(status: dict) -> tuple[bool, list[str]]:
    """Pure: projectStatus JSON -> (passed, failed condition descriptions)."""
    passed = status.get("status") == "OK"
    failed = [
        f"{c.get('metricKey')}: actual {c.get('actualValue')} vs threshold "
        f"{c.get('comparator', '')} {c.get('errorThreshold')}"
        for c in status.get("conditions", []) if c.get("status") == "ERROR"
    ]
    return passed, failed


def issues_text(issues: list[dict], limit: int = 50) -> str:
    lines = [
        f"- [{i.get('severity', '')}] {i.get('component', '').split(':', 1)[-1]}:{i.get('line', '')} "
        f"{i.get('message', '')} ({i.get('rule', '')})"
        for i in issues[:limit]
    ]
    return "\n".join(lines) or "(no open issues returned)"

FILE: dev_pipe/sonar_precheck.py

"""Step 4 Sonar pre-check: a SonarQube Cloud scan of the agent's change BEFORE the pull request is raised.

Works for every stack the pipeline builds (dev_pipe/stack.py), not only Python:

  1. Scan units: one `sonar-scanner` (CLI) run over app_dir for JS/TS, Python, Java, Go, ... plus one
     SonarScanner for .NET run (begin -> dotnet build -> end) per .NET project that contains a change
     (the CLI scanner cannot analyse C#/VB.NET).
  2. Each unit is uploaded as a TEMPORARY branch analysis (sonar.branch.name=devpipe-precheck/...), so the
     main branch and PR analyses on SonarQube Cloud are never touched by unmerged code.
  3. The pipeline waits for SonarQube Cloud to finish processing (Compute Engine task), instead of
     polling the issue list and risking stale results.
  4. Only issues the agent caused count: issues are kept when they sit on lines the agent added or changed
     (`sonar.precheck.block_on: changed_lines`, default) or anywhere in a file it touched (`changed_files`).
     Existing issues elsewhere in the repo, and issues from other repos sharing the same project key,
     never block the PR.
  5. Blocking = severity BLOCKER/CRITICAL/MAJOR (standard mode) or impact severity BLOCKER/HIGH/MEDIUM
     (MQR mode); both lists are configurable.
  6. The temporary branches are deleted afterwards (`sonar.precheck.keep_branch: true` keeps them).

Failures go back to the agent through the Step 4 fix loop. Problems that the agent cannot fix (scanner
missing, Sonar unreachable, analysis failed) are reported as `error` so the loop stops straight away.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import re
import shutil
import subprocess
import tempfile
import time
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

import requests

from dev_pipe.lcov import find_lcov_reports
from dev_pipe.sonar import scanner_env, scanner_problems, scanner_tail
from dev_pipe.stack import SKIP_DIRS, detect_projects

DEFAULTS = {
    "block_on": "changed_lines",                       # changed_lines | changed_files
    "severities": ["BLOCKER", "CRITICAL", "MAJOR"],    # standard experience (issue.severity)
    "impact_severities": ["BLOCKER", "HIGH", "MEDIUM"],  # MQR mode (issue.impacts[].severity)
    "wait_seconds": 300,                               # max wait for SonarQube Cloud to process one analysis
    "poll_seconds": 5,
    "keep_branch": False,                              # true = keep the temporary branch analyses (debugging)
    "branch_prefix": "devpipe-precheck",
    "max_issues_listed": 40,                           # issues shown to the agent / in the run summary
}
SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 30 * 60
LANGUAGE_NAMES = {"py": "Python", "js": "JavaScript", "ts": "TypeScript", "css": "CSS", "web": "HTML",
                  "docker": "Docker", "json": "JSON", "xml": "XML", "yaml": "YAML", "plsql": "SQL", "tsql": "SQL",
                  "powershell": "PowerShell", "java": "Java", "kotlin": "Kotlin", "cs": "C#", "vbnet": "VB.NET",
                  "go": "Go", "terraform": "Terraform", "cloudformation": "CloudFormation", "kubernetes": "Kubernetes",
                  "shell": "Shell", "php": "PHP", "ruby": "Ruby", "scala": "Scala", "c": "C", "cpp": "C++"}
HTTP_TIMEOUT = 30
MAX_ISSUE_PAGES = 20                                   # 20 x 500 = SonarQube Cloud's 10,000 result cap
DOTNET_SCANNER_DIR = Path(tempfile.gettempdir()) / "devpipe-dotnet-sonarscanner"
JAVA_BINARY_DIRS = ("target/classes", "build/classes/java/main", "build/classes/kotlin/main", "out/production")


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ data

@dataclass
class ScanUnit:
    label: str           # "app (sonar-scanner)" / "src/Api (.NET)"
    kind: str            # "cli" | "dotnet"
    cwd: Path            # where the scanner runs (its project base dir)
    branch: str          # temporary branch name on SonarQube Cloud
    build_cmd: str = ""  # .NET only: the build run between begin and end


@dataclass
class PrecheckResult:
    status: str                                        # passed | failed | skipped | error
    blocking: list[dict] = field(default_factory=list)
    ignored: int = 0                                   # issues outside the change, or below the severity bar
    units: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    message: str = ""
    details: dict = field(default_factory=dict)   # for the step log: branches, timings, coverage, totals

    @property
    def passed(self) -> bool:
        return self.status in ("passed", "skipped")

    def label(self) -> str:
        """One-line result for gates.json / the PR body / the ADO summary."""
        if self.status == "failed":
            return f"failed ({len(self.blocking)} blocking in changed code)"
        if self.status == "error":
            return f"error ({self.message[:120]})"
        if self.status == "skipped":
            return f"skipped ({self.message})"
        return f"passed (0 blocking in changed code; {self.ignored} other issue(s) ignored)"

    def report(self, limit: int = DEFAULTS["max_issues_listed"]) -> str:
        """What the agent (fix loop) and the reviewer (run summary) see."""
        if self.status != "failed":
            return f"Sonar pre-check: {self.label()}"
        lines = [f"Sonar pre-check FAILED: {len(self.blocking)} blocking issue(s) in the code you changed "
                 f"({', '.join(self.units)}). Fix each one at its location:"]
        for i in self.blocking[:limit]:
            loc = f"{i['path']}:{i['line']}" if i.get("line") else i["path"]
            lines.append(f"  [{i['severity']}] {loc} -- {i['message']} ({i['rule']})")
        if len(self.blocking) > limit:
            lines.append(f"  ... and {len(self.blocking) - limit} more")
        return "\n".join(lines)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ pure helpers (unit-tested)

_HUNK = re.compile(r"^@@ -\d+(?:,\d+)? \+(\d+)(?:,(\d+))? @@")


def parse_changed_lines(diff_text: str) -> dict[str, set[int]]:
    """`git diff -U0` output -> {repo-relative path: line numbers added or changed in the NEW file}.
    Deleted files are left out (nothing left to analyse). A new or rewritten file lists all its lines."""
    changed: dict[str, set[int]] = {}
    current: str | None = None
    for line in diff_text.splitlines():
        if line.startswith("+++ "):
            target = line[4:].strip()
            if target == "/dev/null":
                current = None
            else:
                current = target[2:] if target.startswith("b/") else target
                if current.startswith('"') and current.endswith('"'):
                    current = current[1:-1]
                changed.setdefault(current, set())
            continue
        if current is None:
            continue
        m = _HUNK.match(line)
        if m:
            start = int(m.group(1))
            count = 1 if m.group(2) is None else int(m.group(2))
            changed[current].update(range(start, start + count))
    return changed


def match_changed_path(component_path: str, changed: dict[str, set[int]]) -> str | None:
    """Map a Sonar component path (relative to the scanner's base dir, which may be a sub-folder of the
    repo) to the repo-relative path in the diff. Longest suffix match on whole path segments."""
    comp = component_path.replace("\\", "/")
    while comp.startswith("./"):
        comp = comp[2:]
    comp = comp.lstrip("/")
    if not comp:
        return None
    if comp in changed:
        return comp
    hits = [p for p in changed if p.endswith("/" + comp) or comp.endswith("/" + p)]
    return max(hits, key=len) if hits else None


def issue_lines(issue: dict) -> set[int]:
    rng = issue.get("textRange") or {}
    start, end = rng.get("startLine"), rng.get("endLine")
    if isinstance(start, int) and isinstance(end, int) and end >= start:
        return set(range(start, end + 1))
    line = issue.get("line")
    return {line} if isinstance(line, int) else set()


def is_blocking(issue: dict, severities: list[str], impact_severities: list[str]) -> bool:
    """Standard experience uses issue.severity; MQR mode adds issue.impacts[].severity. Either counts."""
    sev = {s.upper() for s in severities}
    imp = {s.upper() for s in impact_severities}
    if str(issue.get("severity", "")).upper() in sev:
        return True
    return any(str(i.get("severity", "")).upper() in imp for i in issue.get("impacts") or [])


def classify_issues(issues: list[dict], changed: dict[str, set[int]], block_on: str,
                    severities: list[str], impact_severities: list[str]) -> tuple[list[dict], int]:
    """(blocking issues in the change, number of issues ignored)."""
    blocking: list[dict] = []
    ignored = 0
    for issue in issues:
        comp = str(issue.get("component", "")).split(":", 1)[-1]
        path = match_changed_path(comp, changed)
        if path is None or not is_blocking(issue, severities, impact_severities):
            ignored += 1
            continue
        lines = issue_lines(issue)
        if block_on == "changed_lines" and lines and not (lines & changed[path]):
            ignored += 1
            continue
        # A file-level issue (no line) on a changed file counts: the change may have caused it.
        blocking.append({
            "path": path, "line": issue.get("line"), "rule": issue.get("rule", ""),
            "message": issue.get("message", ""),
            "severity": issue.get("severity") or ",".join(
                f"{i.get('softwareQuality', '')}:{i.get('severity', '')}" for i in issue.get("impacts") or []),
        })
    blocking.sort(key=lambda i: (i["path"], i.get("line") or 0))
    return blocking, ignored


def safe_branch(prefix: str, *parts: object) -> str:
    raw = "-".join(str(p) for p in parts if str(p))
    slug = re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9._-]+", "-", raw).strip("-")[:80] or "run"
    return f"{prefix}/{slug}"


def read_task_id(metadata_file: Path, fallback_dir: Path | None = None, newer_than: float = 0.0) -> str:
    """ceTaskId from the scanner's report-task.txt. Falls back to the newest report-task.txt the scanner
    wrote (after `newer_than`) under fallback_dir (.scannerwork/ for the CLI, .sonarqube/out/.sonar/ for .NET)."""
    candidates = [metadata_file]
    if fallback_dir is not None:
        for sub in (".scannerwork", ".sonarqube"):
            root = fallback_dir / sub
            if root.is_dir():
                fresh = [p for p in root.rglob("report-task.txt") if p.stat().st_mtime >= newer_than - 2]
                candidates += sorted(fresh, key=lambda p: p.stat().st_mtime, reverse=True)
    for path in candidates:
        if not path.is_file():
            continue
        for line in path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").splitlines():
            if line.startswith("ceTaskId="):
                return line.split("=", 1)[1].strip()
    return ""


def redact(text: str, *secrets: str) -> str:
    for s in secrets:
        if s:
            text = text.replace(s, "***")
    return text


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ scan planning

def plan_units(app_dir: Path, changed_files: list[Path], branch_base: str) -> list[ScanUnit]:
    """CLI unit for app_dir (if any change is outside a .NET project) + one .NET unit per changed .NET
    project. Each unit gets its own temporary branch so the analyses never overwrite each other."""
    app_dir = app_dir.resolve()
    dotnet = [p for p in detect_projects(app_dir) if p.stack == "dotnet"]
    changed = [c.resolve() for c in changed_files]

    def inside(c: Path, root: Path) -> bool:
        return c == root or root in c.parents

    units: list[ScanUnit] = []
    for n, proj in enumerate(dotnet, 1):
        if any(inside(c, proj.path) for c in changed):
            units.append(ScanUnit(f"{proj.rel(app_dir)} (.NET)", "dotnet", proj.path,
                                  f"{branch_base}-net{n}", proj.commands[0].cmd if proj.commands else "dotnet build"))
    outside = [c for c in changed if not any(inside(c, u.cwd) for u in units)]
    if outside or not units:
        units.insert(0, ScanUnit(f"{app_dir.name or '.'} (sonar-scanner)", "cli", app_dir, branch_base))
    return units


def coverage_reports(scan_dir: Path, newer_than: float | None) -> tuple[list[Path], list[Path], list[Path]]:
    """(lcov, coverage.xml, jacoco) reports under scan_dir written after newer_than (None = any)."""
    lcov = find_lcov_reports(scan_dir, newer_than=newer_than)
    py, jacoco = [], []
    for d, _dirs, files in _walk(scan_dir):
        for f in files:
            path = d / f
            if f == "coverage.xml" or (f.startswith("jacoco") and f.endswith(".xml")):
                if newer_than is not None and path.stat().st_mtime < newer_than - 2:
                    continue   # stale report (e.g. committed to the repo), not from this run
                (py if f == "coverage.xml" else jacoco).append(path)
    return lcov, py, jacoco


def coverage_args(scan_dir: Path, newer_than: float | None) -> list[str]:
    """Coverage reports the build/test run just wrote, per language (reporting only; issues decide the gate)."""
    args: list[str] = []
    lcov, py, jacoco = coverage_reports(scan_dir, newer_than)
    if lcov:
        args.append("-Dsonar.javascript.lcov.reportPaths="
                    + ",".join(Path(os.path.relpath(r, scan_dir)).as_posix() for r in lcov))
    if py:
        args.append("-Dsonar.python.coverage.reportPaths="
                    + ",".join(Path(os.path.relpath(p, scan_dir)).as_posix() for p in py))
    if jacoco:
        args.append("-Dsonar.coverage.jacoco.xmlReportPaths="
                    + ",".join(Path(os.path.relpath(p, scan_dir)).as_posix() for p in jacoco))
    return args


def java_args(scan_dir: Path) -> list[str]:
    """sonar.java.binaries is mandatory when Java files are analysed; the build check has compiled them."""
    has_java = False
    binaries: list[str] = []
    for d, dirs, files in os.walk(scan_dir):
        rel = Path(d).relative_to(scan_dir).as_posix()
        if any(rel == b or rel.endswith("/" + b) for b in JAVA_BINARY_DIRS):
            binaries.append(rel)
            dirs[:] = []
            continue
        dirs[:] = [x for x in dirs if x not in SKIP_DIRS or x in ("target", "build", "out")]
        if not has_java and any(f.endswith((".java", ".kt")) for f in files):
            has_java = True
    if not has_java:
        return []
    return [f"-Dsonar.java.binaries={','.join(sorted(binaries)) or '.'}"]


_BUILD_OUTPUT_DIRS = ("target", "build")   # JaCoCo writes target/site/jacoco/ (Maven) or build/reports/ (Gradle)


def _walk(root: Path):
    for d, dirs, files in os.walk(root):
        dirs[:] = [x for x in dirs if (x not in SKIP_DIRS or x in _BUILD_OUTPUT_DIRS)
                   and x not in (".scannerwork", ".sonarqube")]
        yield Path(d), dirs, files


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ SonarQube Cloud Web API

class SonarApi:
    def __init__(self, host: str, token: str, project_key: str):
        self.host, self.token, self.key = host.rstrip("/"), token, project_key

    def _get(self, path: str, **params) -> dict:
        resp = requests.get(f"{self.host}{path}", params=params, auth=(self.token, ""), timeout=HTTP_TIMEOUT)
        resp.raise_for_status()
        return resp.json()

    def wait_for_task(self, task_id: str, wait_seconds: int, poll_seconds: int) -> tuple[str, str]:
        """(status, error message). Status: SUCCESS | FAILED | CANCELED | TIMEOUT."""
        deadline = time.time() + wait_seconds
        while True:
            task = self._get("/api/ce/task", id=task_id).get("task", {})
            status = task.get("status", "")
            if status in ("SUCCESS", "FAILED", "CANCELED"):
                return status, task.get("errorMessage", "")
            if time.time() >= deadline:
                return "TIMEOUT", f"analysis still {status or 'pending'} after {wait_seconds}s"
            time.sleep(poll_seconds)

    def branches(self) -> list[dict]:
        """[{name, isMain, analysisDate?, ...}] for the project."""
        return self._get("/api/project_branches/list", project=self.key).get("branches", [])

    def branch_issues(self, branch: str) -> list[dict]:
        issues: list[dict] = []
        for page in range(1, MAX_ISSUE_PAGES + 1):
            data = self._get("/api/issues/search", componentKeys=self.key, branch=branch,
                             resolved="false", ps=500, p=page)
            batch = data.get("issues", [])
            issues += batch
            total = data.get("paging", {}).get("total", data.get("total", len(issues)))
            if not batch or len(issues) >= total:
                break
        return issues

    def delete_branch(self, branch: str) -> None:
        try:
            resp = requests.post(f"{self.host}/api/project_branches/delete",
                                 params={"project": self.key, "branch": branch},
                                 auth=(self.token, ""), timeout=HTTP_TIMEOUT)
            print(f"[sonar-precheck] temporary branch {branch} deleted (HTTP {resp.status_code})", flush=True)
        except requests.RequestException as err:  # cleanup must never fail the step
            print(f"[sonar-precheck] could not delete temporary branch {branch}: {type(err).__name__}", flush=True)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ scanners

def _run(cmd: list[str], cwd: Path, env: dict, token: str) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
    proc = subprocess.run(cmd, cwd=cwd, capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", errors="replace",
                          env=env, timeout=SCAN_TIMEOUT_SECONDS)
    proc.stdout, proc.stderr = redact(proc.stdout or "", token), redact(proc.stderr or "", token)
    return proc


def run_cli_scan(unit: ScanUnit, conn: dict, metadata: Path, extra: list[str]) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
    scanner = shutil.which("sonar-scanner")
    if not scanner:
        raise RuntimeError("sonar-scanner CLI not found on PATH (the setup action installs it in the "
                           "test-gates job).")
    args = [scanner, f"-Dsonar.projectKey={conn['project_key']}", f"-Dsonar.host.url={conn['host']}",
            "-Dsonar.sourceEncoding=UTF-8", f"-Dsonar.scanner.metadataFilePath={metadata}"]
    if unit.branch:   # empty = analyse as the project's main branch (Step 7 baseline scan)
        args.append(f"-Dsonar.branch.name={unit.branch}")
    if conn.get("organization"):
        args.append(f"-Dsonar.organization={conn['organization']}")
    # Token via the environment, never argv.
    return _run(args + extra, unit.cwd, {**scanner_env(), "SONAR_TOKEN": conn["token"]}, conn["token"])


def dotnet_scanner() -> str:
    """SonarScanner for .NET, installed once per job into a temp tool path."""
    exe = DOTNET_SCANNER_DIR / "dotnet-sonarscanner"
    if exe.exists():
        return str(exe)
    if not shutil.which("dotnet"):
        raise RuntimeError("a .NET project changed but the dotnet SDK is not on PATH.")
    proc = subprocess.run(["dotnet", "tool", "install", "dotnet-sonarscanner", "--tool-path",
                           str(DOTNET_SCANNER_DIR)], capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=600)
    if proc.returncode != 0 and not exe.exists():
        raise RuntimeError(f"could not install dotnet-sonarscanner: {(proc.stderr or proc.stdout)[-500:]}")
    return str(exe)


def run_dotnet_scan(unit: ScanUnit, conn: dict, metadata: Path, extra: list[str]) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess:
    """begin -> build -> end. SonarScanner for .NET takes the token as /d:sonar.token (its documented
    usage on CI); the captured output is redacted and GitHub masks the secret in logs."""
    exe = dotnet_scanner()
    env = {**scanner_env(), "SONAR_TOKEN": conn["token"]}
    begin = [exe, "begin", f"/k:{conn['project_key']}", f"/d:sonar.host.url={conn['host']}",
             f"/d:sonar.token={conn['token']}", f"/d:sonar.branch.name={unit.branch}",
             f"/d:sonar.scanner.metadataFilePath={metadata}"]
    if conn.get("organization"):
        begin.append(f"/o:{conn['organization']}")
    begin += ["/d:" + a[2:] for a in extra if a.startswith("-D")]
    steps = [begin, ["bash", "-lc", unit.build_cmd], [exe, "end", f"/d:sonar.token={conn['token']}"]]
    out, err = "", ""
    for cmd in steps:
        proc = _run(cmd, unit.cwd, env, conn["token"])
        out, err = out + proc.stdout, err + proc.stderr
        if proc.returncode != 0:
            return subprocess.CompletedProcess(cmd[:2], proc.returncode, out, err)
    return subprocess.CompletedProcess(["dotnet-sonarscanner"], 0, out, err)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ entry point

def run_precheck(conn: dict, app_dir: Path, work_root: Path, diff_text: str, opts: dict | None = None,
                 run_id: str = "", newer_than: float | None = None, api: SonarApi | None = None,
                 scanners: dict | None = None) -> PrecheckResult:
    """conn: host, token, project_key, organization. diff_text: `git diff -U0 <base>` of the change.
    `api` / `scanners` are injectable for tests."""
    o = {**DEFAULTS, **(opts or {})}
    changed = parse_changed_lines(diff_text)
    if not changed:
        return PrecheckResult("skipped", message="no changed files to analyse")

    api = api or SonarApi(conn["host"], conn["token"], conn["project_key"])
    scanners = scanners or {"cli": run_cli_scan, "dotnet": run_dotnet_scan}
    branch_base = safe_branch(o["branch_prefix"], run_id or int(time.time()))
    units = plan_units(app_dir, [work_root / p for p in changed], branch_base)
    labels = [u.label for u in units]
    print(f"[sonar-precheck] {len(changed)} changed file(s); scan units: {labels}; block_on={o['block_on']}",
          flush=True)

    blocking: list[dict] = []
    ignored = 0
    scanned: list[str] = []
    details: dict = {"changed_files": len(changed), "changed_lines": sum(len(v) for v in changed.values()),
                     "branches": [], "processing_seconds": [], "coverage": [], "total_issues": 0,
                     "deleted": False, "block_on": o["block_on"],
                     "rule": "/".join(o["severities"]) + " (or impact " + "/".join(o["impact_severities"]) + ")"}

    def fail(message: str) -> PrecheckResult:
        return PrecheckResult("error", units=labels, message=message, details=details)
    try:
        for unit in units:
            extra = coverage_args(unit.cwd, newer_than)
            details["coverage"] += [a.split("=", 1)[1] for a in extra]
            if unit.kind == "cli":
                extra += java_args(unit.cwd)
            started = time.time()
            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="devpipe-sonar-") as tmp:
                metadata = Path(tmp) / "report-task.txt"
                try:
                    scan = scanners[unit.kind](unit, conn, metadata, extra)
                except (RuntimeError, OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired) as err:
                    return fail(f"{unit.label}: {err}")
                scanned.append(unit.branch)
                details["branches"].append(unit.branch)
                out_text = (scan.stdout or "") + "\n" + (scan.stderr or "")
                m = re.search(r"(\d+) files indexed", out_text)
                if m:
                    details["files_indexed"] = details.get("files_indexed", 0) + int(m.group(1))
                langs = details.setdefault("languages", [])
                for key in re.findall(r"Quality profile for (\w+):", out_text):
                    name = LANGUAGE_NAMES.get(key, key)
                    if name not in langs:
                        langs.append(name)
                for line in scanner_problems(scan, limit=20):
                    print(f"[sonar-precheck] {line}", flush=True)
                if scan.returncode != 0:
                    print(scanner_tail(scan), flush=True)
                    return fail(f"{unit.label}: scanner exited {scan.returncode} (see the job log)")
                task_id = read_task_id(metadata, unit.cwd, started)
            if not task_id:
                return fail(f"{unit.label}: no ceTaskId in the scanner output")
            waited = time.time()
            status, err = api.wait_for_task(task_id, int(o["wait_seconds"]), int(o["poll_seconds"]))
            details["processing_seconds"].append(round(time.time() - waited, 1))
            if status != "SUCCESS":
                return fail(f"{unit.label}: analysis {status} {err}".strip())
            branch_issues = api.branch_issues(unit.branch)
            details["total_issues"] += len(branch_issues)
            found, skipped = classify_issues(branch_issues, changed, o["block_on"],
                                             o["severities"], o["impact_severities"])
            print(f"[sonar-precheck] {unit.label}: {len(found)} blocking in changed code, {skipped} ignored",
                  flush=True)
            blocking += found
            ignored += skipped
    except requests.RequestException as err:
        return fail(f"SonarQube Cloud API: {type(err).__name__}: {err}"[:300])
    finally:
        if not o["keep_branch"]:
            for b in scanned:
                api.delete_branch(b)
            details["deleted"] = bool(scanned)

    # The same issue can be reported by two units (e.g. a file both scanners saw); keep one.
    unique = {(i["path"], i.get("line"), i["rule"], i["message"]): i for i in blocking}
    blocking = sorted(unique.values(), key=lambda i: (i["path"], i.get("line") or 0))
    return PrecheckResult("failed" if blocking else "passed", blocking, ignored, labels, details=details)
