FILE: dev_pipe/stack.py

"""Stack detection: works out how to build and test a repo from the files in it.

The pipeline used to assume Python (compileall + pytest) unless a repo was configured by hand. This module
looks for the usual project marker files instead, so any repo can be built without per-repo config:

    package.json            -> Node / TypeScript (npm, yarn or pnpm, picked from the lockfile)
    pyproject.toml, setup.py, setup.cfg, requirements.txt, Pipfile (or plain .py files) -> Python
    pom.xml                 -> Maven
    build.gradle(.kts)      -> Gradle
    *.sln / *.csproj / *.fsproj -> .NET
    go.mod                  -> Go

Monorepos: every project root under the app folder is found (frontend/, backend/, ...). When the changed
files are known, only the projects that contain a change are built.

No tests: a project without tests is NOT a failure. Its test command is skipped and a warning is
returned, so the run passes with the warning visible in the summary (coverage is enforced in Step 4).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import re
import shlex
import sys
import tempfile
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path

# Folders never searched for projects or tests (dependencies, build output, VCS, virtualenvs).
SKIP_DIRS = {
    "node_modules", ".git", ".hg", ".svn", ".venv", "venv", "env", ".tox", ".nox", "__pycache__",
    ".pytest_cache", ".mypy_cache", "dist", "build", "out", "target", "bin", "obj", "vendor",
    ".next", ".nuxt", ".turbo", ".cache", "coverage", "htmlcov", ".idea", ".vscode", ".gradle",
    ".target", "target-checkout", ".tools", "site-packages",
}
MAX_DEPTH = 5

PYTHON_MARKERS = ("pyproject.toml", "setup.py", "setup.cfg", "requirements.txt", "Pipfile")
NPM_DEFAULT_TEST = "no test specified"

# Names users may put in target.language, mapped to the detected stack names.
LANGUAGE_ALIASES = {
    "node": {"node"}, "javascript": {"node"}, "typescript": {"node"}, "js": {"node"}, "ts": {"node"},
    "react": {"node"}, "angular": {"node"}, "vue": {"node"},
    "python": {"python"}, "py": {"python"},
    "java": {"maven", "gradle"}, "kotlin": {"gradle", "maven"}, "maven": {"maven"}, "gradle": {"gradle"},
    "dotnet": {"dotnet"}, ".net": {"dotnet"}, "csharp": {"dotnet"}, "c#": {"dotnet"},
    "go": {"go"}, "golang": {"go"},
}


@dataclass
class Command:
    cmd: str
    ok_codes: tuple[int, ...] = (0,)
    install: bool = False           # downloads dependencies (needs network): run by the pipeline before the AI


@dataclass
class Project:
    path: Path                      # absolute project root
    stack: str                      # node | python | maven | gradle | dotnet | go
    commands: list[Command] = field(default_factory=list)
    has_tests: bool = True
    warnings: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    # "Install first, then no network": what the pipeline runs BEFORE the AI (downloads everything the build and
    # tests need), and the offline build/test commands the AI is told to use inside its network-less sandbox.
    prefetch: list[Command] = field(default_factory=list)
    offline: list[Command] | None = None   # None = the normal commands minus the install ones

    def agent_commands(self) -> list[Command]:
        return self.offline if self.offline is not None else [c for c in self.commands if not c.install]

    def install_commands(self) -> list[Command]:
        return [c for c in self.commands if c.install] + self.prefetch

    def rel(self, base: Path) -> str:
        try:
            r = self.path.resolve().relative_to(base.resolve()).as_posix()
        except ValueError:
            r = self.path.as_posix()
        return r or "."


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- helpers
def _walk(root: Path, max_depth: int = MAX_DEPTH):
    """os.walk limited in depth, skipping dependency/build folders. Yields (dir Path, filenames)."""
    root = root.resolve()
    base_depth = len(root.parts)
    for dirpath, dirnames, filenames in os.walk(root):
        p = Path(dirpath)
        depth = len(p.parts) - base_depth
        dirnames[:] = sorted(d for d in dirnames if d not in SKIP_DIRS and not d.startswith(".")) \
            if depth < max_depth else []
        yield p, filenames


def _any_file(root: Path, pattern: re.Pattern, max_depth: int = MAX_DEPTH) -> bool:
    return any(pattern.search(f) for _, files in _walk(root, max_depth) for f in files)


def load_jsonc(path: Path) -> dict:
    """Tolerant JSON loader for package.json / tsconfig.json (comments and trailing commas allowed)."""
    try:
        text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8-sig")
    except OSError:
        return {}
    try:
        return json.loads(text)
    except ValueError:
        pass
    out, i, n, in_str = [], 0, len(text), False
    while i < n:
        c = text[i]
        if in_str:
            out.append(c)
            if c == "\\" and i + 1 < n:
                out.append(text[i + 1])
                i += 1
            elif c == '"':
                in_str = False
        elif c == '"':
            in_str = True
            out.append(c)
        elif text.startswith("//", i):
            i = text.find("\n", i)
            i = n if i < 0 else i
            continue
        elif text.startswith("/*", i):
            j = text.find("*/", i + 2)
            i = n if j < 0 else j + 2
            continue
        else:
            out.append(c)
        i += 1
    cleaned = re.sub(r",(\s*[}\]])", r"\1", "".join(out))
    try:
        return json.loads(cleaned)
    except ValueError:
        return {}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- per-stack command builders
def _node_project(path: Path) -> Project:
    pkg = load_jsonc(path / "package.json")
    scripts = pkg.get("scripts") or {}
    deps = {**(pkg.get("dependencies") or {}), **(pkg.get("devDependencies") or {})}

    if (path / "pnpm-lock.yaml").exists():
        install, run = "corepack pnpm install --frozen-lockfile", "corepack pnpm run"
    elif (path / "yarn.lock").exists():
        berry = (path / ".yarnrc.yml").exists()
        install = "corepack yarn install --immutable" if berry else "yarn install --frozen-lockfile"
        run = "corepack yarn run" if berry else "yarn run"
    elif (path / "package-lock.json").exists() or (path / "npm-shrinkwrap.json").exists():
        install, run = "npm ci", "npm run"
    else:
        install, run = "npm install --no-audit --no-fund", "npm run"

    p = Project(path, "node", [Command(install, install=True)])

    build_script = str(scripts.get("build", ""))
    type_script = next((s for s in ("typecheck", "type-check", "tsc", "check-types") if s in scripts), None)
    if type_script:
        p.commands.append(Command(f"{run} {type_script}"))
    elif (path / "tsconfig.json").exists() and "typescript" in deps and "tsc" not in build_script:
        # Bundlers like Vite/esbuild strip types without checking them, so type-check explicitly.
        for cfg in _tsconfigs_to_check(path):
            p.commands.append(Command(f"npx --no-install tsc --noEmit -p {shlex.quote(cfg)}"))
    if "build" in scripts:
        p.commands.append(Command(f"{run} build"))

    test_script = str(scripts.get("test", ""))
    if not test_script or NPM_DEFAULT_TEST in test_script:
        p.has_tests = False
        p.warnings.append("no `test` script in package.json; tests skipped")
    elif not _node_has_test_files(path):
        p.has_tests = False
        p.warnings.append("a `test` script exists but no test files were found; tests skipped")
    else:
        p.commands.append(Command(f"{run} test"))
    if len(p.commands) == 1:
        p.warnings.append("no build, type-check or test script found; only dependencies were installed")
    return p


def _tsconfigs_to_check(path: Path) -> list[str]:
    """tsconfig.json, or the configs it references when it is a 'solution' file (files: [] + references)."""
    cfg = load_jsonc(path / "tsconfig.json")
    refs = [r.get("path", "") for r in (cfg.get("references") or []) if isinstance(r, dict)]
    if refs and cfg.get("files") == [] and not cfg.get("include"):
        out = []
        for ref in refs:
            ref_path = (path / ref)
            ref_path = ref_path / "tsconfig.json" if ref_path.is_dir() else ref_path
            if ref_path.exists():
                out.append(ref_path.relative_to(path).as_posix())
        return out or ["tsconfig.json"]
    return ["tsconfig.json"]


_NODE_TEST_FILE = re.compile(r"\.(test|spec)\.[cm]?[jt]sx?$")


def _node_has_test_files(path: Path) -> bool:
    for d, files in _walk(path):
        if d.name in ("__tests__", "tests", "test", "e2e", "cypress") and any(
                re.search(r"\.[cm]?[jt]sx?$", f) for f in files):
            return True
        if any(_NODE_TEST_FILE.search(f) for f in files):
            return True
    return False


_PY_TEST_FILE = re.compile(r"^(test_.*|.*_test)\.py$")

# Requirement files installed when present (the main one first, then the ones tests usually need).
PY_REQUIREMENT_FILES = (
    "requirements.txt", "requirements-dev.txt", "requirements_dev.txt", "dev-requirements.txt",
    "requirements-test.txt", "requirements_test.txt", "test-requirements.txt",
    "requirements/base.txt", "requirements/dev.txt", "requirements/test.txt",
)
PY_TEST_EXTRAS = ("test", "tests", "testing", "dev")
VENV_ROOT = Path(tempfile.gettempdir()) / "devpipe-venvs"


def _read_pyproject(path: Path) -> dict:
    f = path / "pyproject.toml"
    if not f.is_file():
        return {}
    text = f.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
    try:
        import tomllib  # Python 3.11+ (the pipeline runs on 3.12)
        return tomllib.loads(text)
    except ImportError:
        # Older Python: enough to tell the project type (no extras).
        return {k: {} for k in ("project", "build-system") if re.search(rf"^\[{re.escape(k)}\]", text, re.M)} | \
            ({"tool": {"poetry": {}}} if re.search(r"^\[tool\.poetry\]", text, re.M) else {})
    except ValueError:
        return {}


def python_install_commands(path: Path, py: str) -> list[str]:
    """Commands that install the project's own dependencies with interpreter `py` (already shell-quoted).
    Read from the project's files: requirements*.txt, pyproject.toml (PEP 621 / Poetry), setup.py/setup.cfg,
    Pipfile.lock. Empty when the project declares no dependencies."""
    cmds = [f"{py} -m pip install -q -r {shlex.quote(f)}" for f in PY_REQUIREMENT_FILES if (path / f).is_file()]
    pyproject = _read_pyproject(path)
    poetry = "poetry" in (pyproject.get("tool") or {}) and "project" not in pyproject
    if poetry:
        cmds.append(f"{py} -m pip install -q poetry && POETRY_VIRTUALENVS_CREATE=false "
                    f"{py} -m poetry install --no-interaction")
    elif "project" in pyproject or "build-system" in pyproject or (path / "setup.py").is_file() \
            or (path / "setup.cfg").is_file():
        extras = sorted(set((pyproject.get("project") or {}).get("optional-dependencies") or {}) & set(PY_TEST_EXTRAS))
        target = f".[{','.join(extras)}]" if extras else "."
        cmds.append(f"{py} -m pip install -q -e {shlex.quote(target)}")
    if (path / "Pipfile.lock").is_file() and not cmds:
        req = VENV_ROOT / "pipfile-requirements.txt"
        cmds.append(f"{py} -m pip install -q pipenv && {py} -m pipenv requirements --dev > {shlex.quote(str(req))} "
                    f"&& {py} -m pip install -q -r {shlex.quote(str(req))}")
    return cmds


def python_env(path: Path) -> tuple[str, list[Command]]:
    """(interpreter, setup commands) for a Python project.

    Projects that declare dependencies get their own virtualenv (outside the repo, so nothing ends up in the
    change set) with those dependencies plus pytest/pytest-cov installed. This keeps the target's packages
    from changing the pipeline's own environment. Projects without a dependency file use the pipeline's
    Python, as before."""
    probe = python_install_commands(path, "PY")
    if not probe:
        return sys.executable, []
    key = hashlib.sha1(str(path.resolve()).encode()).hexdigest()[:12]
    venv = VENV_ROOT / f"{path.name or 'root'}-{key}"
    py_path = venv / ("Scripts/python.exe" if os.name == "nt" else "bin/python")
    py = shlex.quote(str(py_path))
    create = (f"{shlex.quote(sys.executable)} -c "
              "\"import os, sys, venv; os.path.exists(sys.argv[2]) or venv.create(sys.argv[1], with_pip=True)\" "
              f"{shlex.quote(str(venv))} {py}")
    cmds = [Command(create, install=True)] + [Command(c, install=True) for c in python_install_commands(path, py)]
    cmds.append(Command(f"{py} -m pip install -q pytest pytest-cov", install=True))
    return str(py_path), cmds


def python_project(path: Path, test_dir: str = "tests") -> Project:
    py_exe, setup = python_env(path)
    py = shlex.quote(py_exe)
    excludes = shlex.quote(r"[\\/](node_modules|\.venv|venv|\.git|\.target|target-checkout|\.tox|site-packages)[\\/]")
    p = Project(path, "python", setup + [Command(f"{py} -m compileall -q -x {excludes} .")])
    if not setup:
        p.warnings.append("no dependency file (requirements*.txt, pyproject.toml, setup.py, Pipfile.lock) found; "
                          "using the pipeline's Python environment")
    candidates = [d for d in (test_dir, "tests", "test") if d]
    found = next((d for d in candidates if (path / d).is_dir()), None)
    if found:
        target = shlex.quote(found)
    elif _any_file(path, _PY_TEST_FILE):
        target = ""
    else:
        p.has_tests = False
        p.warnings.append("no test folder or test_*.py files found; tests skipped")
        return p
    # Exit code 5 = no tests collected: not a build failure.
    # In the project's own virtualenv pytest-cov is installed (python_env), so the run also writes
    # coverage.xml, which the Sonar pre-check (Step 5) and PR analysis (Step 7) send to SonarQube Cloud.
    # Its data file (.coverage) goes into the virtualenv folder, outside the repo, so Sonar never scans it.
    # No extra -q: with a repo's own `addopts = -q` it would hide the "N passed" summary the step logs read.
    cov = " --cov=. --cov-report=xml:coverage.xml" if setup else ""
    env = f"COVERAGE_FILE={shlex.quote(str(Path(py_exe).parent.parent / '.coverage'))} " if setup else ""
    p.commands.append(Command(f"{env}{py} -m pytest {target} -p no:cacheprovider{cov}".replace("  ", " "), (0, 5)))
    return p


def _maven_project(path: Path) -> Project:
    mvn = "sh ./mvnw" if (path / "mvnw").exists() else "mvn"
    p = Project(path, "maven", [Command(f"{mvn} -B -q verify")],
                prefetch=[Command(f"{mvn} -B -q dependency:go-offline test-compile")],
                offline=[Command(f"{mvn} -o -B -q verify")])
    if not any(d.name == "test" and d.parent.name == "src" for d, _ in _walk(path)):
        p.has_tests = False
        p.warnings.append("no src/test folder found; build ran without tests")
    return p


def _gradle_project(path: Path) -> Project:
    gradle = "sh ./gradlew" if (path / "gradlew").exists() else "gradle"
    p = Project(path, "gradle", [Command(f"{gradle} build --no-daemon -q")],
                prefetch=[Command(f"{gradle} testClasses --no-daemon -q")],
                offline=[Command(f"{gradle} build --offline --no-daemon -q")])
    if not any(d.name == "test" and d.parent.name == "src" for d, _ in _walk(path)):
        p.has_tests = False
        p.warnings.append("no src/test folder found; build ran without tests")
    return p


def _dotnet_project(path: Path, files: list[str]) -> Project:
    slns = sorted(f for f in files if f.endswith(".sln"))
    target = f" {shlex.quote(slns[0])}" if slns else ""
    p = Project(path, "dotnet", [Command(f"dotnet build{target} -nologo")],
                prefetch=[Command(f"dotnet restore{target} -nologo")],
                offline=[Command(f"dotnet build{target} --no-restore -nologo")])
    has_tests = False
    for d, fs in _walk(path):
        for f in fs:
            if f.endswith((".csproj", ".fsproj", ".vbproj")):
                try:
                    text = (d / f).read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
                except OSError:
                    continue
                if "Microsoft.NET.Test.Sdk" in text or re.search(r"test", f, re.I):
                    has_tests = True
                    break
        if has_tests:
            break
    if has_tests:
        p.commands.append(Command(f"dotnet test{target} --no-build -nologo"))
        p.offline.append(Command(f"dotnet test{target} --no-build -nologo"))
    else:
        p.has_tests = False
        p.warnings.append("no test project found; tests skipped")
    return p


def _go_project(path: Path) -> Project:
    p = Project(path, "go", [Command("go build ./..."), Command("go vet ./...")],
                prefetch=[Command("go mod download")])
    if _any_file(path, re.compile(r"_test\.go$")):
        p.commands.append(Command("go test ./..."))
    else:
        p.has_tests = False
        p.warnings.append("no *_test.go files found; tests skipped")
    return p


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- detection
def _markers(files: list[str]) -> list[str]:
    s = set(files)
    found = []
    if "package.json" in s:
        found.append("node")
    if any(m in s for m in PYTHON_MARKERS):
        found.append("python")
    if "pom.xml" in s:
        found.append("maven")
    if "build.gradle" in s or "build.gradle.kts" in s:
        found.append("gradle")
    if any(f.endswith((".sln", ".csproj", ".fsproj")) for f in files):
        found.append("dotnet")
    if "go.mod" in s:
        found.append("go")
    return found


def _covers_children(project_dir: Path, stack: str, files: list[str]) -> bool:
    """Does a project of this stack already build its sub-folders (so nested ones are skipped)?"""
    if stack == "node":
        # Separate apps (frontend/, admin/) each build on their own unless the root declares workspaces.
        return "pnpm-workspace.yaml" in files or bool(load_jsonc(project_dir / "package.json").get("workspaces"))
    if stack in ("go", "python"):
        # go: nested go.mod files are separate modules (`go build ./...` stops at them).
        # python: a root requirements.txt does not run backend/tests, so nested projects build on their own.
        return False
    return True                 # maven/gradle multi-module, dotnet solutions


def detect_projects(root: Path, test_dir: str = "tests") -> list[Project]:
    """All buildable projects under root (shallowest first)."""
    root = root.resolve()
    projects: list[Project] = []
    covered: list[tuple[str, Path]] = []        # (stack, dir) whose build already includes sub-folders
    for d, files in _walk(root):
        for stack in _markers(files):
            if any(s == stack and (d == c or c in d.parents) for s, c in covered):
                continue
            if stack == "node":
                proj = _node_project(d)
            elif stack == "python":
                proj = python_project(d, test_dir)
            elif stack == "maven":
                proj = _maven_project(d)
            elif stack == "gradle":
                proj = _gradle_project(d)
            elif stack == "dotnet":
                proj = _dotnet_project(d, files)
            else:
                proj = _go_project(d)
            projects.append(proj)
            if _covers_children(d, stack, files):
                covered.append((stack, d))
    # Plain Python with no packaging file (e.g. sample_app/: app/ + tests/): treat the root as a project.
    if not projects and _any_file(root, re.compile(r"\.py$"), max_depth=2):
        projects.append(python_project(root, test_dir))
    return projects


def select_projects(projects: list[Project], changed: list[Path] | None) -> tuple[list[Project], str]:
    """Projects that contain at least one changed file. (selected, note)."""
    if not changed:
        return projects, ""
    changed = [c.resolve() for c in changed]
    hit = [p for p in projects if any(c == p.path or p.path in c.parents for c in changed)]
    if not hit:
        return projects, "changed files are outside every detected project, so all projects were built"
    return hit, ""


def stacks_for_language(language: str) -> set[str] | None:
    """None = auto (any stack)."""
    lang = (language or "").strip().lower()
    if lang in ("", "auto"):
        return None
    return LANGUAGE_ALIASES.get(lang, {lang})

....................................................................................................

FILE: dev_pipe/steplog.py

"""Readable, step-by-step logs for every pipeline job, plus one pipeline report for the whole run.

Each job prints one block that anyone can follow without knowing the code:

    ==================== STEP 3: AUTONOMOUS DEVELOPMENT ====================
    What this step does : ...
    Input               : ...
    [3.1] ADO: story state ....................... New -> Active
          Assignee / iteration ................... copied from the story / Sprint 12
    ---------------------------- CODE CHANGES ----------------------------
    ...
    Decision            : ...
    RESULT              : PASSED (2m 40s)
    Output              : ... -> Step 4

Every step also records its result in out/steps.json (handed to the next job with the out/ artifact), and
out/pipeline-report.md is rebuilt from it, so the report always covers the steps that ran so far.

Rules: no personal names (people appear as roles or yes/no), never secrets; long text is shortened (the full
text stays in the out/ artifact). Standard library only: the approval-gate job uses it without installing deps.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import re
import time
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

OUT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "out"
# AI agent sessions and time in this job (updated by dev_pipe/agent_runner.py; summed in the report).
AI_TOTALS: dict = {"sessions": 0, "seconds": 0.0}
WIDTH = 46            # label column incl. dots
MAX_LINES = 40        # lines of a long text block before it is shortened
MAX_LINE_CHARS = 160


def fmt_duration(seconds: float | None) -> str:
    if seconds is None:
        return "n/a"
    seconds = int(round(seconds))
    if seconds < 60:
        return f"{seconds}s"
    m, s = divmod(seconds, 60)
    if m < 60:
        return f"{m}m {s:02d}s"
    h, m = divmod(m, 60)
    return f"{h}h {m:02d}m"


def shorten(text: str, max_lines: int = MAX_LINES, note: str = "") -> list[str]:
    lines = [ln.rstrip()[:MAX_LINE_CHARS] for ln in str(text or "").strip().splitlines()]
    lines = [ln for i, ln in enumerate(lines) if ln or (i and lines[i - 1])]   # collapse blank runs
    if len(lines) > max_lines:
        lines = lines[:max_lines] + [f"... ({len(lines) - max_lines} more lines{'; ' + note if note else ''})"]
    return lines or ["(none)"]


def _now_utc() -> str:
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%d-%b-%Y %H:%M UTC")


class StepLog:
    """with StepLog("3", "Write the code") as log: ...

    The readable block is collected while the step runs and printed at the end, right after a collapsed
    "Technical details" group that holds everything else the step printed (agent activity, build output,
    scanner output). On GitHub only the plain-English block is visible unless someone expands the group.
    An exception leaving the block prints "Result : FAILED" with the reason (and is re-raised)."""

    def __init__(self, number: str, name: str, what: str = "", out_dir: Path | None = None, key: str = ""):
        self.number, self.name, self.what = number, name, what
        self.key = key or number            # row id in steps.json (two jobs can share a step number)
        self.out = Path(out_dir) if out_dir else OUT
        self.prefix = number.split("-")[0]
        self.counter = 0
        self.started = time.time()
        self.facts: list[str] = []
        self.metrics: dict = {}
        self.lines: list[str] = []
        self.done = False
        self._grouped = False

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ output
    def _print(self, text: str = "") -> None:
        print(text, flush=True)

    def _add(self, text: str = "") -> None:
        self.lines.extend(str(text).splitlines() or [""])

    def __enter__(self) -> "StepLog":
        if os.environ.get("GITHUB_ACTIONS") == "true":
            self._print(f"::group::Step {self.number} technical details (click to expand)")
            self._grouped = True
        return self

    def __exit__(self, exc_type, exc, tb) -> bool:
        if not self.done and exc_type is not None:
            if isinstance(exc, SystemExit):
                code = exc.code
                if code in (0, None):
                    self.result("PASSED")
                    return False
                reason = code if isinstance(code, str) else f"exit code {code}"
            else:
                reason = f"{exc_type.__name__}: {exc}"
            self.fail(f"Stopped: {reason}"[:600])
            self.result("FAILED")
        elif not self.done:
            self.result("PASSED")
        return False   # never swallow the exception

    # plain-English building blocks
    def field(self, label: str, value) -> None:
        """`Label             : value` (multi-line values are indented)."""
        lines = str(value if value is not None else "not available").splitlines() or [""]
        self._add(f"{label:<18}: {lines[0]}")
        for ln in lines[1:]:
            self._add(f"{'':<20}{ln}")

    def heading(self, title: str) -> None:
        self._add("")
        self._add(title.upper())

    def ok(self, text: str) -> None:
        self._mark("\u2714", text)

    def fail(self, text: str) -> None:
        self._mark("\u2718", text)

    def warn(self, text: str) -> None:
        self._mark("!", text)

    def arrow(self, text: str) -> None:
        self._mark("\u2192", text)

    def detail(self, text: str, indent: int = 2) -> None:
        for ln in str(text).splitlines() or [""]:
            self._add(" " * indent + ln)

    def kv(self, label: str, value, indent: int = 2, width: int = 34) -> None:
        """Indented `label ..... value` row."""
        head = f"{' ' * indent}{label} "
        self._add(f"{head}{'.' * max(3, width - len(head))} {value}")

    def table(self, rows: list[list[str]], indent: int = 2) -> None:
        widths = [max(len(str(r[c])) for r in rows) for c in range(len(rows[0]))] if rows else []
        for r in rows:
            self._add(" " * indent + "  ".join(str(v).ljust(widths[i]) for i, v in enumerate(r)).rstrip())

    def _mark(self, sign: str, text: str) -> None:
        lines = str(text).splitlines() or [""]
        self._add(f"{sign} {lines[0]}")
        for ln in lines[1:]:
            self._add(f"  {ln}")

    # kept for older callers: numbered rows and dashed sections
    def item(self, label: str, value, num: str | None = None) -> None:
        self.ok(f"{label}: {value}")

    def sub(self, label: str, value) -> None:
        self.detail(f"{label}: {value}")

    def note(self, text: str) -> None:
        self.detail(f"({text})")

    def section(self, title: str, lines: list[str] | str) -> None:
        self.heading(title)
        for ln in (lines.splitlines() if isinstance(lines, str) else lines):
            self.detail(ln)

    def fact(self, text: str) -> None:
        """Short key fact for the pipeline report table."""
        if text:
            self.facts.append(text)

    def metric(self, name: str, value) -> None:
        """Numbers summed in the pipeline report (ai_sessions, ai_seconds, fix_rounds, approvals)."""
        self.metrics[name] = value

    # ------------------------------------------------------------ result
    def result(self, status: str, summary: str = "", next_step: str = "", *, decision: str = "",
               output: str = "") -> None:
        if self.done:
            return
        self.done = True
        took = time.time() - self.started
        if self._grouped:
            self._print("::endgroup::")
        title = f" STEP {self.number}: {self.name.upper()} "
        self._print("")
        self._print(f"{'=' * 20}{title}{'=' * 20}")
        for ln in self.lines:
            self._print(ln)
        self._print("")
        when = "" if status in ("APPROVED", "REJECTED", "TIMED OUT") else f" in {fmt_duration(took)}"
        extra = summary or decision
        self._print(f"{'Result':<8}: {status}{when}" + (f" \u2014 {extra}" if extra else ""))
        if next_step:
            self._print(f"{'Next':<8}: {next_step}")
        self._print("=" * (40 + len(title)))
        metrics = {"ai_sessions": AI_TOTALS["sessions"], "ai_seconds": round(AI_TOTALS["seconds"], 1),
                   **self.metrics}
        record_step(self.key, self.number, self.name, status, self.facts, took, self.out, metrics)
        if status not in ("PASSED", "SKIPPED", "APPROVED"):
            report_to_summary(self.out)   # the run stops here: show the whole run so far on the run page


def record_step(key: str, number: str, name: str, status: str, facts: list[str] | None = None,
                seconds: float | None = None, out: Path | None = None, metrics: dict | None = None) -> None:
    """Adds/replaces one row in out/steps.json and rebuilds out/pipeline-report.md. Never raises."""
    out = Path(out) if out else OUT
    try:
        out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        path = out / "steps.json"
        steps = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")) if path.exists() else []
        steps = [s for s in steps if s.get("key", s.get("step")) != key]   # a re-run replaces its row
        steps.append({"key": key, "step": number, "name": name, "result": status,
                      "seconds": None if seconds is None else round(seconds, 1), "facts": list(facts or []),
                      "metrics": dict(metrics or {}), "finished": _now_utc(),
                      "finished_ts": round(time.time(), 1)})
        path.write_text(json.dumps(steps, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
        (out / "pipeline-report.md").write_text(build_report(steps, out), encoding="utf-8")
    except (OSError, ValueError) as err:   # logging must never fail a step
        print(f"[steplog] could not record the step: {err}", flush=True)


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ pipeline report

def _order(step: str) -> int:
    m = re.match(r"\d+", str(step))
    return int(m.group()) if m else 99


SHORT_NAMES = {"1-2": "Plan", "3": "Code", "4": "Tests", "5": "Quality check", "6": "Pull request",
               "7": "Quality gate", "8-approval": "Approval", "8-ado": "ADO update"}
SIGN = {"PASSED": "\u2714 PASSED", "APPROVED": "\u2714 APPROVED", "SKIPPED": "- SKIPPED", "FAILED": "\u2718 FAILED",
        "REJECTED": "\u2718 REJECTED", "TIMED OUT": "\u2718 TIMED OUT"}


def build_report(steps: list[dict], out: Path | None = None) -> str:
    """Plain-English report of every step recorded so far (out/pipeline-report.md and the final run summary)."""
    out = Path(out) if out else OUT

    def load(name: str) -> dict:
        try:
            return json.loads((out / name).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        except (OSError, ValueError):
            return {}

    story, target, pr, ado = load("story.json"), load("target.json"), load("pr.json"), load("ado.json")
    steps = sorted(steps, key=lambda s: _order(s["step"]))
    stopped = [s for s in steps if s["result"] in ("FAILED", "REJECTED", "TIMED OUT")]
    done = any(s.get("key") == "8-ado" and s["result"] == "PASSED" for s in steps)
    overall = (f"STOPPED AT STEP {stopped[0]['step']}" if stopped else "COMPLETED" if done
               else "IN PROGRESS" if steps else "not available")
    repo = os.environ.get("GITHUB_REPOSITORY", "")
    run_no = os.environ.get("GITHUB_RUN_NUMBER", "")
    ends = [s.get("finished_ts") for s in steps if s.get("finished_ts")]
    starts = [s["finished_ts"] - (s.get("seconds") or 0) for s in steps if s.get("finished_ts")]
    span = ""
    if starts and ends:
        fmt = "%H:%M"
        span = (f"{datetime.fromtimestamp(min(starts), timezone.utc).strftime(fmt)} \u2192 "
                f"{datetime.fromtimestamp(max(ends), timezone.utc).strftime(fmt)} UTC  |  "
                f"Total {fmt_duration(max(ends) - min(starts))}  |  ")

    rows = [["Step", "Result", "Time", "Key facts"]]
    for s in steps:
        label = f"{s['step']:<4}{SHORT_NAMES.get(s.get('key', s['step']), s['name'])}"
        rows.append([label, SIGN.get(s["result"], s["result"]),
                     "-" if s.get("key") == "8-approval" else fmt_duration(s.get("seconds")),
                     ", ".join(s.get("facts") or []) or "-"])
    widths = [max(len(str(r[c])) for r in rows) for c in range(4)]
    table = ["  ".join(str(v).ljust(widths[i]) for i, v in enumerate(r)).rstrip() for r in rows]

    m = lambda name: sum((s.get("metrics") or {}).get(name, 0) or 0 for s in steps)
    ado_bits = []
    if ado.get("dev_task_id") or ado.get("pr_task_id"):
        n = sum(1 for k in ("dev_task_id", "pr_task_id") if ado.get(k))
        ado_bits.append(f"story Active, {n} task(s) ready" + (" and closed" if done else ""))
    if pr.get("url"):
        ado_bits.append("pull request linked")
    lines = [
        "```text",
        "DEV AGENT PIPELINE REPORT",
        f"Story        : #{story.get('id', '?')} \"{story.get('title', '')}\"",
        f"Repository   : {target.get('full_name') or repo or 'this repository'}"
        + (f" ({target.get('base_branch')})" if target.get("base_branch") else ""),
        "Pull request : " + (f"#{pr.get('number')}  {pr.get('url')}" if pr.get("url")
                              else "dry run (not created)" if pr.get("dry_run") else "not created yet"),
        "Run          : " + (f"#{run_no}  |  " if run_no else "") + span + f"RESULT: {overall}",
        "",
        *table,
        "",
        f"AI work      : {m('ai_sessions')} AI session(s), {m('fix_rounds')} fix round(s) needed, "
        f"about {fmt_duration(m('ai_seconds'))} of AI time",
        f"Human effort : {m('approvals')} approval(s)",
        f"Azure DevOps : {', '.join(ado_bits) or 'not updated yet'}",
        "```",
    ]
    if stopped:
        lines.append(f"\n**Stopped at Step {stopped[0]['step']} ({stopped[0]['name']}).** "
                     "Open that job's log for the reason.")
    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"


def write_summary(markdown: str) -> None:
    """Prints markdown and appends it to the GitHub run summary (visible on the run page). The ONE writer used
    by every step (dev_pipe/io_utils.summary) and the approval jobs; standard library only."""
    print(markdown, flush=True)
    target = os.environ.get("GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY")
    if target:
        with open(target, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(markdown + "\n")


def report_to_summary(out: Path | None = None) -> None:
    """Appends the pipeline report to the GitHub run summary (final step, or a failed step)."""
    out = Path(out) if out else OUT
    path = out / "pipeline-report.md"
    if not path.exists():
        return
    text = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    print(text, flush=True)
    target = os.environ.get("GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY")
    if target:
        with open(target, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write("\n" + text + "\n")


# ------------------------------------------------------------------ shared helpers for the steps

def trigger_text() -> str:
    """How the run started, without naming the person: 'push to feature/x | run #12 | 29-Oct-2026 10:41 UTC'."""
    event = os.environ.get("GITHUB_EVENT_NAME", "")
    ref = os.environ.get("GITHUB_REF_NAME", "")
    how = {"push": f"push to {ref}", "workflow_dispatch": f"manual run on {ref}"}.get(event, event or "local run")
    run = os.environ.get("GITHUB_RUN_NUMBER", "")
    return f"{how}{f' | run #{run}' if run else ''} | {_now_utc()}"


def story_link(story: dict) -> str:
    org = os.environ.get("ADO_ORG_URL", "").rstrip("/")
    project = os.environ.get("ADO_PROJECT", "")
    return f"{org}/{project}/_workitems/edit/{story.get('id')}" if org and project else "n/a"


def numbered(text: str) -> list[str]:
    """Acceptance criteria text -> list of items (one per non-empty line, list markers removed)."""
    items = []
    for ln in str(text or "").splitlines():
        ln = re.sub(r"^\s*(?:[-*\u2022]|\d+[.)])\s*", "", ln).strip()
        if ln:
            items.append(ln)
    return items


def story_lines(story: dict) -> list[str]:
    """The USER STORY block. No personal names: the assignee is shown as yes/no."""
    lines = [
        f"{'ID / Type / State':<20}: {story.get('id')} / {story.get('type') or 'n/a'} / {story.get('state') or 'n/a'}",
        f"{'Title':<20}: {story.get('title') or 'n/a'}",
        f"{'Assigned':<20}: {'yes' if story.get('assigned_to') else 'no'}",
        f"{'Iteration / Area':<20}: {story.get('iteration_path') or 'n/a'} / {story.get('area_path') or 'n/a'}",
        f"{'Link':<20}: {story_link(story)}",
        "Description:",
    ]
    lines += ["  " + ln for ln in shorten(story.get("description"), note="full text in story.json")]
    criteria = numbered(story.get("acceptance_criteria", ""))
    lines.append(f"Acceptance criteria ({len(criteria)}):")
    shown = criteria[:MAX_LINES]
    lines += [f"  {i}. {c[:MAX_LINE_CHARS]}" for i, c in enumerate(shown, 1)] or ["  (none)"]
    if len(criteria) > len(shown):
        lines.append(f"  ... ({len(criteria) - len(shown)} more; full text in story.json)")
    return lines


_COUNT = re.compile(r"(\d+)\s+(passed|failed|skipped|errors?)\b", re.I)
_DOTNET = re.compile(r"Failed:\s*(\d+),\s*Passed:\s*(\d+),\s*Skipped:\s*(\d+)", re.I)


def test_counts(log: str) -> dict | None:
    """Test totals from build/test output (pytest, Vitest, Jest, dotnet, Maven surefire). None = not found."""
    totals = {"passed": 0, "failed": 0, "skipped": 0}
    found = False
    for line in str(log or "").splitlines():
        if re.search(r"Test (Files|Suites)", line):         # file/suite totals, not test totals
            continue
        m = _DOTNET.search(line)
        if m:
            totals["failed"] += int(m.group(1)); totals["passed"] += int(m.group(2)); totals["skipped"] += int(m.group(3))
            found = True
            continue
        sure = re.search(r"Tests run:\s*(\d+),\s*Failures:\s*(\d+),\s*Errors:\s*(\d+),\s*Skipped:\s*(\d+)", line)
        if sure and "Time elapsed" not in line:
            run, fail, err, skip = map(int, sure.groups())
            totals["passed"] += run - fail - err - skip; totals["failed"] += fail + err; totals["skipped"] += skip
            found = True
            continue
        hits = _COUNT.findall(line)
        if hits and re.search(r"\b(passed|failed)\b", line, re.I) and ("in " in line or "Tests" in line
                                                                       or line.strip().startswith(("=", "-"))
                                                                       or re.match(r"^\s*\d+ (passed|failed)", line)):
            for n, kind in hits:
                key = "failed" if kind.lower().startswith(("fail", "error")) else kind.lower()
                totals[key] += int(n)
            found = True
    return totals if found else None


def counts_text(counts: dict | None) -> str:
    if not counts:
        return "n/a (no test summary in the output)"
    return f"{counts['passed']} passed, {counts['failed']} failed, {counts['skipped']} skipped"

FILE: dev_pipe/workspace.py

"""External mode: GitHub token handling and the target repo checkout (target-checkout/).

Security:
- The token is read once from TARGET_GITHUB_TOKEN (or GH_PAT) and REMOVED from os.environ, so the
  agent (which inherits the environment) never sees it.
- Git authenticates through per-command environment config (GIT_CONFIG_*), never through the
  remote URL or .git/config, so nothing on disk holds the token.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import os
import shutil
import subprocess

from dev_pipe.config import ROOT, TARGET_CHECKOUT, is_external, resolved_target, settings

_TOKEN_VARS = ("TARGET_GITHUB_TOKEN", "GH_PAT")
_token: str | None = None


def take_token() -> str:
    """GitHub token for the target repos. Removed from the environment on first call."""
    global _token
    if _token is None:
        _token = ""
        for name in _TOKEN_VARS:
            value = os.environ.pop(name, "").strip()
            _token = _token or value
    return _token


def git_auth_env(token: str | None = None) -> dict:
    token = take_token() if token is None else token
    if not token:
        return {}
    basic = base64.b64encode(f"x-access-token:{token}".encode()).decode()
    server = os.environ.get("GITHUB_SERVER_URL", "https://github.com").rstrip("/")   # GitHub Enterprise Server too
    return {"GIT_CONFIG_COUNT": "1", "GIT_CONFIG_KEY_0": f"http.{server}/.extraheader",
            "GIT_CONFIG_VALUE_0": f"AUTHORIZATION: basic {basic}", "GIT_TERMINAL_PROMPT": "0"}


_local_token: str | None = None


def take_local_token() -> str:
    """Local mode: token for the pipeline's own git remote commands (Step 7 pushes the AI's fixes). The AI jobs
    check out with persist-credentials: false, so no token is stored in .git/config; this one is passed per
    command instead. Removed from the environment on first call."""
    global _local_token
    if _local_token is None:
        _local_token = os.environ.pop("LOCAL_GIT_TOKEN", "").strip()
    return _local_token


def _git(*args: str, cwd=TARGET_CHECKOUT, auth: bool = False) -> str:
    env = {**os.environ, **(git_auth_env() if auth else {})}
    proc = subprocess.run(["git", *args], cwd=cwd, capture_output=True, text=True, env=env)
    if proc.returncode != 0:
        raise SystemExit(f"git {' '.join(args[:2])} (target repo) failed: {proc.stderr.strip()[:500]}")
    return proc.stdout.strip()


def prepare_checkout(target: dict | None = None) -> None:
    """Checks out the resolved repo at its base commit in target-checkout/ (no-op in local mode or if already there)."""
    if not is_external():
        return
    target = target or resolved_target()
    if not target.get("full_name") or not target.get("base_sha"):
        raise SystemExit("Missing out/target.json (repo + base commit). Did Steps 1-2 finish?")
    if (TARGET_CHECKOUT / ".git").exists():
        try:
            if _git("rev-parse", "HEAD") == target["base_sha"]:
                return
        except SystemExit:
            pass
        shutil.rmtree(TARGET_CHECKOUT)
    TARGET_CHECKOUT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    server = os.environ.get("GITHUB_SERVER_URL", "https://github.com").rstrip("/")
    _git("init", "-q")
    _git("remote", "add", "origin", f"{server}/{target['full_name']}.git")
    _git("fetch", "-q", "--depth", "1", "origin", target["base_sha"], auth=True)
    _git("checkout", "-q", "--detach", "FETCH_HEAD")
    install_skills()
    print(f"[workspace] {target['full_name']}@{target['base_branch']} ({target['base_sha'][:10]}) -> target-checkout/",
          flush=True)


def fetch_base_ref(branch: str) -> None:
    """Adds origin/<branch> to the depth-1 checkout (external mode), so the Sonar scanner finds the
    reference branch ("Could not find ref 'main'" warning otherwise). Never fails the step."""
    if not is_external() or not branch:
        return
    try:
        _git("fetch", "-q", "--depth", "1", "origin", f"+refs/heads/{branch}:refs/remotes/origin/{branch}", auth=True)
    except SystemExit as err:
        print(f"[workspace] could not fetch origin/{branch}: {err}", flush=True)


def prepare_full_clone(target: dict | None = None) -> None:
    """Replaces target-checkout/ with a regular, full `git clone` of the target repo (all history, normal
    remote-tracking refs). Used by Step 7: the SonarQube scanner could not compute a PR's changed
    lines in the init + depth-1 fetch + unshallow checkout that prepare_checkout builds
    ("SCM reported changed lines for 0 files" -> "0 New lines of code"), while the same commit
    scanned from a normal clone worked. Auth works as in prepare_checkout: the token goes in a
    per-command header, never into the remote URL or .git/config. No-op in local mode."""
    if not is_external():
        return
    target = target or resolved_target()
    if not target.get("full_name"):
        raise SystemExit("Missing out/target.json (repo + base commit). Did Steps 1-2 finish?")
    if TARGET_CHECKOUT.exists():
        shutil.rmtree(TARGET_CHECKOUT)
    TARGET_CHECKOUT.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    server = os.environ.get("GITHUB_SERVER_URL", "https://github.com").rstrip("/")
    _git("clone", "-q", "--no-tags", f"{server}/{target['full_name']}.git", str(TARGET_CHECKOUT),
         cwd=TARGET_CHECKOUT.parent, auth=True)
    install_skills()
    print(f"[workspace] {target['full_name']} full clone -> target-checkout/ (for the SonarQube scan)", flush=True)


def install_skills() -> None:
    """Copies this repo's agent skills into the checkout so the agent can load them there,
    and hides them from git so they never end up in the change set or PR."""
    names = settings().get("agent", {}).get("skills") or []
    src_root = ROOT / ".claude" / "skills"
    dst_root = TARGET_CHECKOUT / ".claude" / "skills"
    exclude = TARGET_CHECKOUT / ".git" / "info" / "exclude"
    exclude.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    lines = []
    for name in names:
        src, dst = src_root / name, dst_root / name
        if src.is_dir() and not dst.exists():
            shutil.copytree(src, dst)
            lines.append(f"/.claude/skills/{name}/")
    if lines:
        with open(exclude, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write("\n".join(lines) + "\n")

FILE: dev_pipe/steps/gate.py

"""Human approval gate (steps 3 to 9): opens a GitHub Issue and waits for `approve` / `reject: <reason>`.

    python -m dev_pipe.steps.gate approve-plan

Reads the previous stage's out/ folder to show the reviewer what they are approving.
Exit code 0 = approved (pipeline continues); non-zero = rejected or timed out (pipeline stops).
Standard library only (see dev_pipe/approval.py).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import re
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

from dev_pipe.approval import GitHubIssues, parse_approvers, wait_for_decision
from dev_pipe.steplog import StepLog, fmt_duration, write_summary

OUT = Path("out")
MAX_SECTION = 20000  # characters per section; GitHub issue bodies are limited to 65,536

# gate -> (diagram step, question for the reviewer, sections to show: (label, file, kind))
GATES: dict[str, tuple[str, str, list[tuple[str, str, str]]]] = {
    "approve-plan": ("Plan approval (before Step 3)", "Approve the development plan?",
                     [("Development plan", "plan.md", "markdown")]),
    "approve-implementation": ("Step 3 (Autonomous Development)", "Approve the implementation (code changes)?",
                               [("Agent's notes", "changes.md", "markdown"),
                                ("Changes to EXISTING code (check these carefully)", "changes.json", "risky"),
                                ("Diff", "changes.patch", "diff")]),
    "approve-changes": ("Developer Verification (before Step 4)", "Developer verification: did you test the change and does it work?",
                        [("How to verify", "", "verify_help"),
                         ("Files changed", "changes.json", "files")]),
    "approve-tests": ("Step 4 (Build + Test + Coverage)", "OK for the agent to write unit tests and run build, coverage and quality checks?",
                      [("Files changed so far", "changes.json", "files")]),
    "approve-pr": ("Step 6 (Pull Request Creation)", "OK to commit, push, raise the pull request and link it to the ADO story?",
                   [("Quality gate results", "gates.json", "json"),
                    ("Final diff (code + tests)", "changes.patch", "diff")]),
    "approve-quality-fix": ("Step 7 (SonarQube Quality Validation)", "OK to run the SonarQube quality gate on the PR and let the agent push fixes?",
                            [("Pull request", "pr.json", "json")]),
    "approve-ado-update": ("Step 8 (ADO Update & Development Complete)", "OK to post the development summary and close the DEV/PR tasks in ADO?",
                           [("Development log", "summary.md", "markdown"),
                            ("SonarQube quality gate", "quality.json", "json")]),
}

HOW_TO = """### How to respond
Comment on this issue with one of:
- `approve` to continue to the next step
- `reject: <reason>` to stop the pipeline (re-run with the reason in `DEV_FEEDBACK` so the agent can correct it)
"""


def _clip(text: str) -> str:
    return text if len(text) <= MAX_SECTION else text[:MAX_SECTION] + "\n\n... (truncated, see the run page for the full content)"


VERIFY_HELP = """1. Open the pipeline run (link above) and download the `dev-stage-2` artifact.
2. In your local clone, on the same commit as the run: `git apply out/changes.patch`
3. Run the app and tests, and check the change does what the story asks.
4. Undo locally when done: `git checkout -- . && git clean -fd` (careful: discards local changes)."""


def _section(label: str, path: Path, kind: str) -> str:
    if kind == "verify_help":
        return f"### {label}\n\n{VERIFY_HELP}\n"
    if not path.exists():
        return ""
    if kind == "diff":
        text = path.read_bytes().decode("utf-8", errors="replace").strip()
        return f"### {label}\n\n````diff\n{_clip(text) or '(no changes)'}\n````\n" if text else ""
    raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    if kind in ("risky", "files"):
        data = json.loads(raw)
        rows = data.get("risky" if kind == "risky" else "files", [])
        empty = "- none (only new code was added)" if kind == "risky" else "- none"
        lines = [f"- `{r['path']}`: +{r['added']} / -{r['removed']}" for r in rows] or [empty]
        return f"### {label}\n\n" + "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
    if kind == "json":
        return f"### {label}\n\n````json\n{_clip(raw.strip())}\n````\n"
    return f"### {label}\n\n{_clip(raw.strip())}\n"


def build_issue(gate: str, out: Path, run_url: str, approvers: set[str], timeout_min: int,
                wait_note: str = "", marker: str = "") -> tuple[str, str]:
    """wait_note replaces the default "waits up to N minutes" line; marker (an HTML comment) is appended,
    used by the comment-triggered approval (dev_pipe/steps/gate_request.py)."""
    step, question, sections = GATES[gate]
    story = {}
    if (out / "story.json").exists():
        story = json.loads((out / "story.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    story_ref = f"#{story.get('id')} {story.get('title', '')}".strip() if story else "(unknown story)"
    title = f"[Dev Pipeline] {step}: {question[:80]} | Story {story_ref}"[:250]
    body = "\n".join([
        f"## {step}: {question}",
        f"**Story:** {story_ref}  ",
        f"**Pipeline run:** {run_url} (full summary and downloadable artifacts)",
        "",
        *[s for s in (_section(label, out / name if name else out, kind) for label, name, kind in sections) if s],
        "---",
        HOW_TO,
        f"Allowed approvers: {', '.join('@' + a for a in sorted(approvers))}. "
        + (wait_note or f"The pipeline waits up to {timeout_min} minutes, then stops."),
        *([marker] if marker else []),
    ])
    return title, body


_summary = write_summary   # one shared writer (dev_pipe/steplog.py)


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None, gh: GitHubIssues | None = None, waiter=wait_for_decision) -> None:
    argv = sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv
    gate = (argv[0] if argv else os.environ.get("GATE", "")).strip()
    if gate not in GATES:
        raise SystemExit(f"Unknown approval gate '{gate}'. Expected one of: {', '.join(GATES)}")
    label = GATES[gate][0]
    found = re.search(r"Step (\d+)", label)
    number = found.group(1) if found else "-"
    with StepLog(number, "Human approval", key=f"{number}-approval") as log:
        approvers = parse_approvers(os.environ.get("APPROVERS", ""))
        if not approvers:
            raise SystemExit("No approvers configured. Set the APPROVERS repository variable (comma-separated GitHub logins).")
        timeout_min = int(os.environ.get("APPROVAL_TIMEOUT_MINUTES", "240") or 240)

        server = os.environ.get("GITHUB_SERVER_URL", "https://github.com")
        repo = os.environ.get("GITHUB_REPOSITORY", "")
        run_url = f"{server}/{repo}/actions/runs/{os.environ.get('GITHUB_RUN_ID', '')}"
        gh = gh or GitHubIssues(os.environ.get("GITHUB_TOKEN", ""), repo,
                                os.environ.get("GITHUB_API_URL", "https://api.github.com"))

        title, body = build_issue(gate, OUT, run_url, approvers, timeout_min)
        issue = gh.create_issue(title, body)
        issue_no, issue_url = issue["number"], issue.get("html_url", "")
        log.ok(f"Approval request opened: GitHub issue #{issue_no} (includes the pull request link and check results).")
        log.detail(f"{'Authorised reviewers':<21}: {len(approvers)}")
        log.detail(f"{'Time limit':<21}: {timeout_min} minutes")
        log.detail(f"{'Link':<21}: {issue_url}")
        _summary(f"## {label} approval: waiting\n\nApprove or reject in issue {issue_url}")

        # Stop polling ~2 minutes before the job's own timeout so the issue is always closed cleanly.
        started = time.time()
        decision = waiter(gh, issue_no, approvers, timeout_s=max(60, (timeout_min - 2) * 60))
        waited = fmt_duration(time.time() - started)

        if decision is None:
            gh.comment(issue_no, f"No decision within {timeout_min} minutes. The pipeline has stopped. Re-run it to try again.")
            gh.close(issue_no, completed=False)
            _summary(f"**Timed out** waiting for approval ({timeout_min} min). Pipeline stopped.")
            log.fail(f"No decision within {timeout_min} minutes. The pipeline stopped; re-run it to try again.")
            log.result("TIMED OUT")
            raise SystemExit(1)

        verdict, reason, login = decision
        if verdict == "approve":
            gh.comment(issue_no, f"Approved by @{login}. Continuing with the pipeline: {run_url}")
            gh.close(issue_no, completed=True)
            _summary(f"**Approved** by an allowed approver in {issue_url}")
            log.ok(f"Decision: APPROVED by an authorised reviewer after {waited}.")
            log.metric("approvals", 1)
            log.result("APPROVED", next_step="Update Azure DevOps and finish (Step 8).")
            return

        gh.comment(issue_no, f"Rejected by @{login}: {reason}\n\nThe pipeline has stopped. To retry with this feedback, "
                             "set the `DEV_FEEDBACK` repository variable (or the `feedback` input) and re-run.")
        gh.close(issue_no, completed=False)
        _summary(f"**Rejected** by an allowed approver: {reason}")
        log.fail(f"Decision: REJECTED by an authorised reviewer after {waited}.")
        log.detail(f"Reason: {reason[:300]}")
        log.detail("To retry, re-run the pipeline with this reason as feedback for the AI.")
        log.result("REJECTED")
        raise SystemExit(1)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

....................................................................................................

FILE: dev_pipe/steps/gate_comment.py

"""Comment-triggered approval (APPROVAL_MODE=comment): no runner waits while the reviewer decides.

The waiting gate (dev_pipe/steps/gate.py) keeps a runner busy for up to APPROVAL_TIMEOUT_MINUTES. Here the
pipeline run only opens the approval issue and ends; the `approve` / `reject: <reason>` comment then starts
.github/workflows/dev-pipe-approval-decision.yml, which finishes the work (Step 8 ADO update).

    python -m dev_pipe.steps.gate_comment prepare approve-ado-update   # pipeline run: write out/approval-request.json
    python -m dev_pipe.steps.gate_comment open                         # pipeline run: open the issue (after upload)
    python -m dev_pipe.steps.gate_comment locate                       # decision run: which pipeline run is this?
    python -m dev_pipe.steps.gate_comment decide                       # decision run: approve / reject / ignore

Trust rules:
- The issue only carries the pipeline run ID (in a hidden marker). Everything that decides the outcome
  (allowed approvers, time limit, dry run) comes from the run's own artifact, which nobody can edit.
- The run ID is checked against the GitHub API: it must be a run of .github/workflows/dev-pipe.yml in this repo.
- Comment text is untrusted: it is only matched against two keywords (dev_pipe/approval.py), never executed.
Standard library only (the decision job starts without installing dependencies).
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import re
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

from dev_pipe.approval import GitHubIssues, decide, parse_approvers
from dev_pipe.steps.gate import GATES, OUT, build_issue
from dev_pipe.steplog import StepLog, fmt_duration, write_summary

REQUEST_FILE = "approval-request.json"
WORKFLOW_PATH = ".github/workflows/dev-pipe.yml"
MARKER_RE = re.compile(r"<!-- dev-pipe-approval run_id=(\d+) -->")


def marker(run_id: str) -> str:
    return f"<!-- dev-pipe-approval run_id={int(run_id)} -->"


def _env(name: str, default: str = "") -> str:
    return os.environ.get(name, default).strip()


def _output(**values) -> None:
    """Step outputs for the workflow (GITHUB_OUTPUT), also printed for the log."""
    path = _env("GITHUB_OUTPUT")
    for key, value in values.items():
        print(f"[approval] {key}={value}", flush=True)
        if path:
            with open(path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                f.write(f"{key}={value}\n")


_summary = write_summary   # one shared writer (dev_pipe/steplog.py)


def _client(gh: GitHubIssues | None) -> GitHubIssues:
    return gh or GitHubIssues(_env("GITHUB_TOKEN"), _env("GITHUB_REPOSITORY"),
                              _env("GITHUB_API_URL", "https://api.github.com"))


def _run_url(run_id: str) -> str:
    return f"{_env('GITHUB_SERVER_URL', 'https://github.com')}/{_env('GITHUB_REPOSITORY')}/actions/runs/{run_id}"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- pipeline run
def prepare(gate: str, out: Path = OUT, now=time.time) -> dict:
    """Writes out/approval-request.json (uploaded with the stage artifact BEFORE the issue opens, so a fast
    approver never finds the artifact missing)."""
    if gate not in GATES:
        raise SystemExit(f"Unknown approval gate '{gate}'. Expected one of: {', '.join(GATES)}")
    approvers = parse_approvers(_env("APPROVERS"))
    if not approvers:
        raise SystemExit("No approvers configured. Set the APPROVERS repository variable (comma-separated GitHub logins).")
    request = {
        "gate": gate,
        "run_id": _env("GITHUB_RUN_ID"),
        "approvers": sorted(approvers),
        "timeout_minutes": int(_env("APPROVAL_TIMEOUT_MINUTES", "240") or 240),
        "dry_run": "false" if _env("DRY_RUN", "true").lower() == "false" else "true",
        "requested_at": now(),
    }
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    (out / REQUEST_FILE).write_text(json.dumps(request, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
    return request


def open_issue(out: Path = OUT, gh: GitHubIssues | None = None) -> dict:
    request = json.loads((out / REQUEST_FILE).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    gate, run_id, timeout_min = request["gate"], request["run_id"], int(request["timeout_minutes"])
    label = GATES[gate][0]
    with StepLog("8", "Human approval", key="8-approval") as log:
        note = (f"No runner waits for your answer: your comment starts the next step. "
                f"Answers after {timeout_min} minutes are refused (re-run the pipeline instead).")
        title, body = build_issue(gate, out, _run_url(run_id), set(request["approvers"]), timeout_min,
                                  wait_note=note, marker=marker(run_id))
        issue = _client(gh).create_issue(title, body)
        url = issue.get("html_url", "")
        log.ok(f"Approval request opened: GitHub issue #{issue['number']}.")
        log.detail(f"{'Authorised reviewers':<21}: {len(request['approvers'])}")
        log.detail(f"{'Time limit':<21}: {timeout_min} minutes")
        log.detail(f"{'Link':<21}: {url}")
        log.detail("This run ends now. An `approve` comment starts the \"Dev Pipeline approval decision\" "
                   "workflow, which runs Step 8.")
        _summary(f"## {label} approval: requested\n\nApprove or reject in issue {url}. Your comment starts the "
                 "**Dev Pipeline approval decision** workflow (no runner waits in the meantime).")
        log.result("WAITING", next_step="Comment `approve` or `reject: <reason>` on the issue.")
    return issue


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- decision run (issue_comment)
def _event() -> dict:
    path = _env("GITHUB_EVENT_PATH")
    if not path:
        raise SystemExit("GITHUB_EVENT_PATH is not set (this runs from an issue_comment event).")
    return json.loads(Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))


def locate(event: dict, gh: GitHubIssues | None = None) -> dict:
    """Finds and checks the pipeline run behind the commented issue. found=false = not an approval issue."""
    found = MARKER_RE.search((event.get("issue") or {}).get("body") or "")
    if not found:
        _output(found="false")
        return {"found": False}
    run_id = found.group(1)
    run = _client(gh).get_run(int(run_id))
    path = str(run.get("path") or "").split("@", 1)[0]
    if path != WORKFLOW_PATH:
        print(f"[approval] run {run_id} is not a {WORKFLOW_PATH} run ({path or 'unknown'}): ignored", flush=True)
        _output(found="false")
        return {"found": False}
    result = {"found": True, "run_id": run_id, "head_sha": run.get("head_sha", ""),
              "run_url": run.get("html_url") or _run_url(run_id)}
    _output(found="true", run_id=run_id, head_sha=result["head_sha"], run_url=result["run_url"])
    return result


def decide_comment(event: dict, out: Path = OUT, gh: GitHubIssues | None = None, now=time.time) -> str:
    """Acts on ONE new comment. Returns approve | reject | expired | none (not an approver, or no keyword)."""
    request = json.loads((out / REQUEST_FILE).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    issue_no = int((event.get("issue") or {})["number"])
    comment = event.get("comment") or {}
    decision = decide([comment], set(request["approvers"]))
    if decision is None:
        print("[approval] comment ignored: not from an allowed approver, or not `approve` / `reject: <reason>`",
              flush=True)
        _output(decision="none")
        return "none"

    gh = _client(gh)
    verdict, reason, login = decision
    timeout_min = int(request["timeout_minutes"])
    waited = now() - float(request["requested_at"])
    pipeline_url = _run_url(request["run_id"])
    this_run = _run_url(_env("GITHUB_RUN_ID")) if _env("GITHUB_RUN_ID") else "(this run)"
    with StepLog("8", "Human approval", key="8-approval") as log:
        if waited > timeout_min * 60:
            gh.comment(issue_no, f"This approval request expired ({timeout_min} minutes). Nothing was changed. "
                                 f"Re-run the pipeline to try again: {pipeline_url}")
            gh.close(issue_no, completed=False)
            log.fail(f"Answer came after {fmt_duration(waited)}, past the {timeout_min}-minute limit: refused.")
            log.result("TIMED OUT")
            _output(decision="expired")
            return "expired"
        if verdict == "approve":
            gh.comment(issue_no, f"Approved by @{login}. Running Step 8 (ADO Update & Development Complete): {this_run}")
            gh.close(issue_no, completed=True)
            log.ok(f"Decision: APPROVED by an authorised reviewer after {fmt_duration(waited)}.")
            log.metric("approvals", 1)
            log.result("APPROVED", next_step="Update Azure DevOps and finish (Step 8).")
            _output(decision="approve", dry_run=request.get("dry_run", "true"))
            return "approve"
        gh.comment(issue_no, f"Rejected by @{login}: {reason}\n\nNothing more runs for this pipeline run. To retry "
                             "with this feedback, set the `DEV_FEEDBACK` repository variable (or the `feedback` input) "
                             f"and re-run: {pipeline_url}")
        gh.close(issue_no, completed=False)
        log.fail(f"Decision: REJECTED by an authorised reviewer after {fmt_duration(waited)}.")
        log.detail(f"Reason: {reason[:300]}")
        log.result("REJECTED")
        _output(decision="reject")
        return "reject"


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> None:
    argv = sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv
    command = argv[0] if argv else ""
    if command == "prepare":
        prepare(argv[1] if len(argv) > 1 else _env("GATE"))
    elif command == "open":
        open_issue()
    elif command == "locate":
        locate(_event())
    elif command == "decide":
        result = decide_comment(_event())
        if result in ("reject", "expired"):
            raise SystemExit(1)   # shows the decision run as failed, like a rejected gate in the waiting mode
    else:
        raise SystemExit("Usage: python -m dev_pipe.steps.gate_comment prepare <gate> | open | locate | decide")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: dev_pipe/steps/notify_failure.py

"""Posts "pipeline run failed at Step X" on the ADO story when a run fails after Step 3 started.

Step 3 moves the story to Active, so without this a failed run leaves an Active story with no pull request
and nobody knows why. Runs as the last job of the Dev Pipeline (`notify-failure`, only on failure).

Input: NEEDS_JSON = the workflow's `toJSON(needs)` (each job's result). Skipped in dry run (AdoClient).
Never fails the run a second time: an ADO error is printed as a warning.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os

from dev_pipe.config import run_url, work_item_id

# Workflow job id -> what to tell the team. In run order: the first failed job is the one reported.
JOBS = [
    ("implement", "Step 3 (Autonomous Development: writing the code)"),
    ("test-gates", "Step 4 (Build + Test + Coverage)"),
    ("sonar-precheck", "Step 5 (Sonar Pre-check)"),
    ("create-pr", "Step 6 (Pull Request Creation)"),
    ("quality-gate", "Step 7 (SonarQube Quality Validation)"),
    ("gate-ado", "Step 8 (approval: rejected or no answer in time)"),
    ("request-ado-approval", "Step 8 (opening the approval request)"),
    ("ado-complete", "Step 8 (ADO Update & Development Complete)"),
]
PR_RAISED_AFTER = {"quality-gate", "gate-ado", "request-ado-approval", "ado-complete"}


def failed_step(needs: dict) -> tuple[str, str] | None:
    """(job id, label) of the first failed job, or None (nothing failed after Step 3 started)."""
    for job, label in JOBS:
        if (needs.get(job) or {}).get("result") == "failure":
            return job, label
    return None


def message(job: str, label: str, url: str) -> str:
    if job in PR_RAISED_AFTER:
        state = "The pull request was raised; its Development and PR tasks stay open until this is resolved."
    elif job == "create-pr":
        state = ("The pull request and/or its Development and PR tasks may not have been created. The story was "
                 "moved to Active when the run started.")
    else:
        state = ("No pull request was raised and no tasks were created. The story was moved to Active when the "
                 "run started.")
    return (f"Dev Agent pipeline run failed at {label}.\n{state}\n"
            f"Details (plain-English log per step): {url}\n"
            "To retry: fix the cause (or give the AI feedback in DEV_FEEDBACK) and re-run the pipeline. "
            "A re-run reuses this story's open tasks; it does not create duplicates.")


def main() -> None:
    needs = json.loads(os.environ.get("NEEDS_JSON") or "{}")
    found = failed_step(needs)
    if not found:
        print("[notify] no failed step after Step 3 started; nothing to post", flush=True)
        return
    job, label = found
    text = message(job, label, run_url())
    print(f"[notify] {text}", flush=True)
    try:
        from dev_pipe.ado import AdoClient
        AdoClient().add_comment(work_item_id(), text)
    except (SystemExit, Exception) as err:   # noqa: BLE001 - reporting only, the run has already failed
        print(f"[notify] WARNING: could not post the comment on the ADO story: {str(err)[:300]}", flush=True)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
