FILE: dev_pipe/steps/s1_2_story_analysis_planning.py

"""Step 1 (User Story Analysis) + Step 2 (Development Planning).

Fetches the ADO story. In external mode, also finds the target repo + branch (GitHub REST + AAVA Code
Context Service, see dev_pipe/resolver.py), checks it out in target-checkout/ and gives the agent the AAVA
code matches. Then reads the architecture context file and generates the development plan.

Outputs: out/story.json, out/plan.md, out/plan.json (+ out/target.json in external mode)
Next: Step 3 (Autonomous Development), no approval in between. The chosen repo/branch is not
confirmed by a human, so check the run summary and cancel the run if it is wrong.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import re
import time

from dev_pipe.ado import AdoClient
from dev_pipe.agent_runner import agent_brief, extract_json_block, plan_followup, run_agent, strip_json_block
from dev_pipe.config import (branch_name, feedback, is_dry_run, is_external, load_prompt, run_url, settings,
                             target_settings, work_item_id, work_root)
from dev_pipe.io_utils import append_log, cell, summary, write_json, write_text
from dev_pipe.steplog import StepLog, fmt_duration, numbered, shorten, story_link, trigger_text


def resolve_target(story: dict) -> dict:
    from dev_pipe.aava import Aava
    from dev_pipe.github_api import GitHub
    from dev_pipe.resolver import Resolver
    from dev_pipe.workspace import take_token

    cfg = settings()
    d = dict(cfg.get("discovery") or {})
    d["github_org"] = (d.get("github_org") or os.environ.get("DISCOVERY_ORG")
                       or os.environ.get("GITHUB_REPOSITORY_OWNER", ""))
    d["aava"] = cfg.get("aava") or {}
    d["base_branch"] = cfg.get("git", {}).get("base_branch", "")
    token = take_token()
    if not token:
        raise SystemExit("Missing TARGET_GITHUB_TOKEN (GH_PAT secret): needed to list and read the target repos.")
    a = d["aava"]
    aava = Aava(os.environ.get("AAVA_CONTEXT_BASE_URL") or a.get("base_url", ""),
                github_token=token if a.get("send_github_token", True) else "")
    return Resolver(GitHub(token), aava, d).resolve(
        story, os.environ.get("TARGET_REPO", "").strip(), os.environ.get("TARGET_BRANCH", "").strip())


def target_summary(target: dict) -> str:
    if not target:
        return ""
    alts = "\n".join(f"| {cell(a['full_name'])} | `{cell(a['branch'])}` | {a['similarity']} | {a['signal']} | "
                     f"{cell(a.get('last_commit', '')[:10])} | {cell(a.get('evidence', ''), 160)} |"
                     for a in target.get("alternatives", [])) or "| (none) | | | | | |"
    matches = "\n".join(f"- `{cell(m['filePath'])}:{m.get('startLine')}-{m.get('endLine')}` ({m['similarity']:.2f})"
                        for m in target.get("matches", [])[:8]) or "- (none)"
    warn = ("\n\n> **Low confidence: check the repo/branch below now.** If it is wrong, cancel this run and "
            "re-run with the `target_repo` / `target_branch` inputs." if target["confidence"] == "low" else "")
    return (
        f"### Target repository\n\n**{target['full_name']}** branch `{target['base_branch']}` "
        f"(commit `{target['base_sha'][:10]}`); confidence **{target['confidence']}**; {target['reason']}{warn}\n\n"
        f"PR will target `{target['base_branch']}`.\n\n"
        f"**AAVA matches used as context:**\n{matches}\n\n"
        f"<details{' open' if target['confidence'] == 'low' else ''}><summary>Other candidates</summary>\n\n"
        f"| Repo | Branch | AAVA similarity | Keyword score | Last commit | Evidence |\n"
        f"|---|---|---|---|---|---|\n{alts}\n\n</details>\n\n"
        f"<details><summary>Resolution log</summary>\n\n```\n" + "\n".join(target.get("trace", []))[:6000]
        + "\n```\n\n</details>\n\n"
    )


def plan_risks(plan_md: str, limit: int = 3) -> list[str]:
    """First bullet points under the plan's '## Risks and open questions' heading."""
    m = re.search(r"^##\s*Risks[^\n]*\n(.*?)(?=^##\s|\Z)", plan_md or "", re.M | re.S)
    items = [re.sub(r"^\s*[-*\d.)]+\s*", "", ln).strip() for ln in (m.group(1).splitlines() if m else [])]
    items = [i for i in items if i and i.lower() not in ("none", "none.", "n/a")]
    return [i[:140] for i in items[:limit]]


def main() -> None:
    wid = work_item_id()
    with StepLog("1-2", "Understand the story and plan the work") as log:
        log.field("Started", trigger_text().replace(" | ", "  |  "))
        log.field("Mode", "Dry run (nothing is written to Azure DevOps or GitHub)" if is_dry_run()
                  else "Live run (Azure DevOps and GitHub will be updated)")

        started = time.time()
        story = AdoClient().get_work_item(wid)
        criteria = numbered(story.get("acceptance_criteria", ""))
        log.heading("The user story")
        log.ok(f"Read story #{wid} from Azure DevOps in {fmt_duration(time.time() - started)}.")
        log.detail(f"{'Title':<12}: {story.get('title') or 'not available'}")
        log.detail(f"{'Type/State':<12}: {story.get('type') or '?'} / {story.get('state') or '?'}")
        log.detail(f"{'Assigned':<12}: {'Yes' if story.get('assigned_to') else 'No'}   |   "
                   f"Sprint: {story.get('iteration_path') or 'not set'}   |   Area: {story.get('area_path') or 'not set'}")
        log.detail(f"{'Link':<12}: {story_link(story)}")
        desc = shorten(story.get("description"), 12, "full text in story.json")
        log.detail(f"{'Description':<12}: {desc[0]}")
        for ln in desc[1:]:
            log.detail(f"{'':<14}{ln}")
        log.detail(f"Acceptance criteria ({len(criteria)}):")
        for n, c in enumerate(criteria[:15], 1):
            log.detail(f"{n}. {c[:150]}", indent=4)
        if len(criteria) > 15:
            log.detail(f"... and {len(criteria) - 15} more (full text in story.json)", indent=4)
        if not story["description"] and not story["acceptance_criteria"]:
            raise SystemExit(f"Story #{wid} has no description or acceptance criteria to implement.")
        write_json("story.json", story)
        log.fact(f"{len(criteria)} acceptance criteria")

        target: dict = {}
        code_context = "(local mode: no code context service lookup)"
        log.heading("Finding the code (AAVA)")
        if is_external():
            from dev_pipe.resolver import code_context as format_context
            from dev_pipe.workspace import prepare_checkout
            target = resolve_target(story)
            write_json("target.json", target)
            prepare_checkout(target)
            code_context = format_context(target)
            log.ok(f"Repository chosen : {target['full_name']}, branch \"{target['base_branch']}\" "
                   f"(commit {target['base_sha'][:7]})")
            manual = target.get("found_on") == "manual"
            log.detail(f"{'How it was chosen':<18}: " + ("Named in the run settings; AAVA was used to find the relevant files."
                                                         if manual else f"AAVA search ({target.get('reason', '')}), "
                                                         f"confidence {target.get('confidence', '?')}."))
            state = str(target.get("index_state") or "").upper()
            log.detail(f"{'AAVA index status':<18}: " + {
                "COMPLETED": "Already indexed (no new indexing needed).",
                "DONE": "Already indexed (no new indexing needed).",
                "NOT_INDEXED": "Not indexed; AAVA could not be searched for this branch.",
            }.get(state, f"{state.title() or 'Unknown'}"))
            if target.get("queries"):
                log.detail(f"AAVA searched with {target['queries']} questions taken from the story "
                           "(title, acceptance criteria, description).")
            matches = target.get("matches") or []
            if matches:
                log.detail("Top matches given to the AI as context:")
                for m in matches[:5]:
                    log.detail(f"- {m.get('filePath')}, lines {m.get('startLine') or '?'}-{m.get('endLine') or '?'} "
                               f"(match {float(m.get('similarity') or 0):.2f})", indent=4)
            else:
                log.detail("AAVA returned no matches; the AI explores the repository itself.")
            alts = target.get("alternatives") or []
            if alts:
                log.detail(f"Other repositories/branches considered: {len(alts)} (match scores: "
                           + ", ".join(f"{a.get('similarity')}" for a in alts[:5]) + ")")
            if target.get("confidence") == "low":
                log.warn("Low confidence in the repository choice. If it is wrong, cancel and re-run with target_repo.")
        else:
            log.ok(f"Working in this repository (app folder {target_settings()['app_dir']}); AAVA is not used in local mode.")

        t = target_settings()
        arch_path = work_root() / t["architecture_file"]
        if arch_path.exists():
            architecture = arch_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            log.ok(f"Architecture guide found: {t['architecture_file']} ({len(architecture.splitlines())} lines).")
            log.detail("The AI used it instead of reading the whole codebase, which saves time and AI cost.")
        else:
            architecture = (f"(No {t['architecture_file']} found. Explore only what you need; "
                            "it will be created during implementation.)")
            log.warn(f"No architecture guide at {t['architecture_file']}; the AI will create one in Step 3.")
        target_line = (f"{target['full_name']} @ {target['base_branch']} ({target['reason']})" if target
                       else "this repository")
        if feedback():
            log.ok("Reviewer feedback from an earlier run was given to the AI.")

        hint = branch_name(wid, story["title"])
        answer = run_agent(
            load_prompt("plan", story=json.dumps({k: v for k, v in story.items() if k != "assigned_to"}, indent=2),  # no personal data in prompts/logs
                        architecture=architecture,
                        feedback=feedback() or "(none)", app_dir=t["app_dir"], branch_hint=hint,
                        target=target_line, code_context=code_context),
            system_prompt=load_prompt("system", app_dir=t["app_dir"]),
            edit=False,
            check=plan_followup,  # stopped early / no ```json block -> ask it to finish (same conversation)
            step="plan",
        )
        meta = extract_json_block(answer)
        meta["branch"] = branch_name(wid, story["title"], meta.get("branch", ""))
        plan_md = strip_json_block(answer)
        from dev_pipe import leak_check   # the plan is shown on the run page and in the approval issue
        leak_check.enforce(leak_check.scan_text(answer, "the plan"), "the AI's plan")
        write_text("plan.md", plan_md)
        write_json("plan.json", meta)

        files = meta.get("files") or []
        log.heading("The plan")
        log.ok(f"The AI wrote the development plan {agent_brief()}.")
        log.detail(f"{'Branch name':<17}: {meta['branch']}")
        changes = [f for f in files if str(f.get("action", "")).lower() != "create"]
        creates = [f for f in files if str(f.get("action", "")).lower() == "create"]
        for label, group in (("Files to change", changes), ("Files to create", creates)):
            for n, f in enumerate(group):
                log.detail(f"{label if n == 0 else '':<17}: {f.get('path')}  → {str(f.get('reason', ''))[:90]}")
        if not files:
            log.detail(f"{'Files':<17}: none listed")
        modifies = meta.get("modifies_existing_code")
        log.detail(f"{'Existing code':<17}: " + ("will change" if modifies else "no changes to existing code"
                                                  if modifies is False else "not stated"))
        tests = [str(c) for c in meta.get("test_cases") or []]
        for n, c in enumerate(tests[:8], 1):
            log.detail(f"{'Test cases' if n == 1 else '':<17}: {n}. {c[:120]}")
        if not tests:
            log.detail(f"{'Test cases':<17}: none listed")
        est = meta.get("estimate_hours")
        log.detail(f"{'Estimate':<17}: " + (f"{est} hour(s)" if est not in (None, "") else "not given"))
        questions = [str(q) for q in meta.get("open_questions") or []]
        log.detail(f"{'Open questions':<17}: " + ("; ".join(q[:120] for q in questions[:3]) if questions else "None"))
        risks = plan_risks(plan_md)
        log.detail(f"{'Risks noted':<17}: " + ("; ".join(risks) if risks else "None"))
        log.fact(f"{len(files)} file(s) planned")
        if est not in (None, ""):
            log.fact(f"{est}h estimate")

        files_md = "\n".join(f"| {cell(f.get('action'))} | `{cell(f.get('path'))}` | {cell(f.get('reason'))} |"
                             for f in files)
        summary(
            f"## Steps 1-2: User Story Analysis + Development Planning (story #{wid})\n\n"
            f"**{cell(story['title'], 200)}** ({story['type']}, {story['state']}, "
            f"{'assigned' if story['assigned_to'] else 'not assigned'})\n\n"
            + target_summary(target)
            + f"Architecture file: {'found' if arch_path.exists() else 'not found (will be created)'} | "
            f"Branch: `{meta['branch']}` | Estimate: {meta.get('estimate_hours', '?')}h | "
            f"Changes existing code: {meta.get('modifies_existing_code', '?')}\n\n"
            f"| Action | File | Reason |\n|---|---|---|\n{files_md}\n\n"
            f"<details><summary>Full plan</summary>\n\n{plan_md}\n\n</details>\n\n"
            "**Next (Step 3: Autonomous Development):** runs automatically, no approval here. If the plan"
            + (" or the target repo/branch" if target else "")
            + " looks wrong, cancel the run and re-run with your reason in the `feedback` input."
        )
        append_log(f"- Steps 1-2 (User Story Analysis + Development Planning): plan generated for #{wid} "
                   f"(branch `{meta['branch']}`"
                   + (f", repo {target['full_name']} from `{target['base_branch']}`" if target else "")
                   + f"). Run: {run_url()}")
        log.result("PASSED", next_step="The AI writes the code (Step 3).")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: dev_pipe/steps/s4_autonomous_development.py

"""Step 3: Autonomous Development (runs right after Steps 1-2 planning).

ADO: story -> In Progress only. The DEV and PR tasks are created in Step 6, once the pull request exists,
so a failed run never leaves orphan tasks (dev_pipe/steps/s7_pull_request_creation.py).
Agent: implements the plan, no unit tests yet. Pipeline: build check, change set captured as a patch.
Outputs: out/changes.patch, out/changes.md
Next: Step 4 (Build + Test + Coverage), no approval in between.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import time
from typing import Callable

from dev_pipe.ado import AdoClient
from dev_pipe.agent_runner import LAST_RUN, agent_brief, changes_followup, first_of, question_followup, run_agent
from dev_pipe.build import build_check_text, check_build
from dev_pipe.config import (app_dir, feedback, is_dry_run, load_prompt, resolved_target, settings,
                             target_settings, work_root)
from dev_pipe.gitops import (changed_files_stats, changed_paths, fix_architecture_file, head_sha, risky_changes,
                             snapshot_patch)
from dev_pipe.workspace import prepare_checkout
from dev_pipe.io_utils import (append_log, cell, read_json, read_text, set_output, summary, write_bytes,
                               write_json, write_text)
from dev_pipe.report_data import command_steps
from dev_pipe.steplog import StepLog, counts_text, fmt_duration, shorten, test_counts


def update_ado_on_start(story: dict) -> bool:
    """Story -> In Progress (so the team sees the work started). Tasks are created in Step 6.
    Returns True if the state was changed (False: already in that state, e.g. a re-run)."""
    active = settings()["ado"]["states"]["story_in_progress"]
    if str(story.get("state") or "").strip().lower() == active.strip().lower():
        print(f"[ado] story #{story['id']} is already \"{active}\"; not changed", flush=True)
        return False
    AdoClient().set_state(story["id"], active)
    return True


BUILD_ERROR_CHARS = 4000


def build_followup(t: dict, changed: Callable[[], list[str]], fingerprint: Callable[[], str] | None = None,
                   last: dict | None = None) -> Callable[[str], str | None]:
    """Runs the pipeline's build check on the changed projects; on failure returns the exact errors as the
    follow-up, so the agent fixes them in the same conversation (same pattern as the Step 4 fix loop).
    last/fingerprint: the latest result is kept with a fingerprint of the code it ran on, so Step 3 can reuse
    it for its own report instead of building the same code again."""
    def check(_text: str) -> str | None:
        paths = changed()
        if not paths:
            return None
        started = time.time()
        result = check_build(t, app_dir(), [work_root() / p for p in paths])
        if last is not None and fingerprint is not None:
            last.clear()
            last.update(result=result, seconds=time.time() - started, fp=fingerprint())
        if result.ok:
            return None
        errors = result.report()[-BUILD_ERROR_CHARS:]
        return ("the build failed\n\nThe pipeline ran the build check on your changes and it FAILED:\n\n"
                f"```\n{errors}\n```\n\nFix these errors now. Re-read each affected file before editing it (it may "
                "already contain duplicated or partial code from earlier edits); remove anything duplicated, and "
                "never repeat an edit that failed or returned 'No changes to make'. Then run the build again and "
                "give your final summary.")
    return check


def main() -> None:
    with StepLog("3", "Write the code") as log:
        prepare_checkout()  # external mode: target repo at the approved base commit (no-op in local mode)
        t = target_settings()
        story = read_json("story.json")
        plan = read_text("plan.md")
        base = head_sha()
        target = resolved_target()
        log.field("Starting point", f"Repository {target.get('full_name') or 'this repository'} at commit {base[:7]}"
                  + (f" (branch {target['base_branch']})" if target.get("base_branch") else ""))

        cfg_ado = settings()["ado"]
        changed_state = update_ado_on_start(story)
        dry = " (dry run: not written)" if is_dry_run() else ""
        log.heading("Azure DevOps")
        if changed_state:
            log.ok(f"Story #{story['id']} moved from \"{story.get('state') or '?'}\" to "
                   f"\"{cfg_ado['states']['story_in_progress']}\"{dry}.")
        else:
            log.ok(f"Story #{story['id']} is already \"{cfg_ado['states']['story_in_progress']}\" (not changed).")
        log.detail("The Development and PR tasks are created in Step 6, once the pull request exists.")

        def code_changes() -> list[str]:
            return [p for p in changed_paths(base) if p != t["architecture_file"]]  # architecture file alone isn't the change

        def code_fingerprint() -> str:
            return hashlib.sha256(snapshot_patch(base)).hexdigest()

        last_build: dict = {}   # the AI loop's latest build check, reused below if the code did not change since

        answer = run_agent(
            load_prompt("implement", plan=plan, feedback=feedback() or "(none)", app_dir=t["app_dir"],
                        test_dir=t["test_dir"], architecture_file=t["architecture_file"],
                        build_check=build_check_text(t, app_dir())),
            system_prompt=load_prompt("system", app_dir=t["app_dir"]),
            edit=True,
            # Checked after the agent stops, in this order; the first problem found is sent back to it in the same
            # conversation: no edits yet -> build/tests fail (exact errors) -> it asked a question instead of finishing.
            check=first_of(changes_followup(code_changes), build_followup(t, code_changes, code_fingerprint, last_build),
                           question_followup),
            max_followups=int(settings()["gates"].get("build_fix_attempts", 3)),
            on_incomplete="warn",  # still failing: the summary + patch below are written and the step fails there
            step="implement",
        )
        followups = LAST_RUN["followups"]
        brief = agent_brief()
        moved = fix_architecture_file(base, t["architecture_file"])

        patch = snapshot_patch(base)
        from dev_pipe import leak_check   # the diff and notes are shown on the run page, then pushed in Step 6
        leak_check.enforce(leak_check.scan_patch(patch) + leak_check.scan_text(answer, "the AI's notes"),
                           "the AI's code change or notes")
        write_bytes("changes.patch", patch)
        stats = changed_files_stats(base)
        risky = risky_changes(stats)
        code_stats = [r for r in stats if r["path"] != t["architecture_file"]]

        log.heading("Writing the code")
        log.ok(f"The AI wrote the code {brief}.")
        log.detail("Files changed:" if code_stats else "Files changed: none")
        for r in code_stats[:15]:
            kind = "new file" if r["new"] else "modified"
            log.detail(f"{r['path']} {'.' * max(3, 34 - len(r['path']))} {kind:<9} "
                       f"+{r['added']} lines / -{r['removed']} lines", indent=4)
        if len(code_stats) > 15:
            log.detail(f"... and {len(code_stats) - 15} more", indent=4)
        if risky:
            log.detail("Changes to EXISTING code (please review):")
            for r in risky[:10]:
                log.detail(f"{r['path']}: {r['removed']} existing line(s) changed or removed", indent=4)
        else:
            log.detail("Only new code was added; no existing lines were changed.")
        notes = [ln.strip("-* ").strip() for ln in shorten(answer, 12) if ln.strip("-* ").strip()]
        if notes:
            log.detail("What the AI says it did:")
            for ln in notes[:8]:
                log.detail(f"- {ln[:140]}", indent=4)
        arch = t["architecture_file"]
        if moved:
            log.ok("Architecture guide: " + "; ".join(moved) + ".")
        elif (work_root() / arch).exists():
            log.ok(f"Architecture guide is in the right place ({arch}).")
        else:
            log.warn(f"No architecture guide at {arch}.")

        # Build only the projects the agent changed (all of them if detection can't tell). If the AI loop's last
        # build check ran on exactly this code, its result is reused: same code, same build, no second run.
        reused = bool(last_build) and last_build.get("fp") == hashlib.sha256(patch).hexdigest()
        if reused:
            build, build_secs = last_build["result"], last_build["seconds"]
            print("[build] same code as the last build check after the AI finished: result reused, not built again",
                  flush=True)
        else:
            build_started = time.time()
            build = check_build(t, app_dir(), [work_root() / s["path"] for s in stats] or None)
            build_secs = time.time() - build_started
        ok, build_log = build.ok, build.report()
        write_json("changes.json", {"base": base, "files": stats, "risky": risky, "build_ok": ok,
                                    "build_projects": build.projects, "build_warnings": build.warnings})
        set_output("existing_code_changed", "true" if risky else "false")
        write_text("changes.md", answer)

        counts = test_counts(build.log)
        log.heading("Our own check (we do not trust the AI's word)")
        log.ok(f"Project(s) checked : {', '.join(build.projects) or 'none detected'}")
        for label, step_ok in command_steps(build.log):
            if label == "Ran the tests":
                (log.ok if step_ok else log.fail)(f"Ran the existing tests .......... {counts_text(counts)}")
            else:
                (log.ok if step_ok else log.fail)(f"{label} {'.' * max(3, 32 - len(label))} {'OK' if step_ok else 'FAILED'}")
        log.detail(f"Build and tests took {fmt_duration(build_secs)}."
                   + (" (Run once, right after the AI finished; the code has not changed since, so it was not "
                      "run a second time.)" if reused else ""))
        for w in build.warnings:
            log.warn(w[:160])
        allowed = settings()["gates"].get("build_fix_attempts", 3)
        (log.ok if not followups else log.arrow)(f"Build fixes the AI needed ....... {followups} of {allowed} allowed")
        log.metric("fix_rounds", followups)
        log.fact(f"{len(code_stats)} file(s) changed")
        if risky:
            log.fact(f"{len(risky)} existing file(s) edited")
        log.fact("builds OK" if ok else "build FAILED")

        rows = "\n".join(f"| `{cell(r['path'])}` | {'new' if r['new'] else 'modified'} | +{r['added']} / -{r['removed']} |"
                         for r in stats) or "| (no changes) | | |"
        risky_md = "\n".join(f"- `{r['path']}`: {r['removed']} existing line(s) removed or rewritten"
                             for r in risky) or "- none"
        summary(
            f"## Step 3: Autonomous Development {'complete' if ok else 'FAILED build'}\n\n"
            f"ADO: story #{story['id']} -> In Progress{' (dry run: not written)' if is_dry_run() else ''}; "
            "DEV/PR tasks are created in Step 6 with the pull request\n\n"
            f"| File | Type | Lines |\n|---|---|---|\n{rows}\n\n"
            f"**Changes to existing code (check these):**\n{risky_md}\n\n"
            f"**Build + existing tests:** {'PASS' if ok else 'FAIL'}"
            f"{f' ({len(build.warnings)} warning(s))' if build.warnings else ''}\n```\n{build_log}\n```\n\n"
            f"<details><summary>Agent's notes</summary>\n\n{answer}\n\n</details>\n\n"
            "<details><summary>Diff</summary>\n\n```diff\n" + patch[:60000].decode("utf-8", errors="replace") + "\n```\n\n</details>\n\n"
            + "**Next (Step 4: Build + Test + Coverage):** runs automatically. Review this implementation in the PR"
            + (" (existing code was changed, check the list above)" if risky else "")
            + " before the final approval (Step 8)."
        )
        append_log(f"- Step 3 (Autonomous Development): implemented {len(stats)} file(s); {len(risky)} change(s) to existing code; "
                   f"build {'passed' if ok else 'failed'}"
                   f"{f' with {len(build.warnings)} warning(s)' if ok and build.warnings else ''}.")
        if not stats:
            raise SystemExit("The AI made no changes.")
        if not ok:
            raise SystemExit("The build or the existing tests failed after the AI's changes (details in the "
                             "technical section above).")
        log.result("PASSED", next_step="The AI writes tests and we check quality (Step 4).")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

....................................................................................................

FILE: dev_pipe/steps/s6_build_test_coverage.py

"""Step 4: Build + Test + Coverage (runs right after Step 3 Autonomous Development).

Writes unit tests, then runs build, coverage and test quality. The Sonar pre-check runs in its own job
right after this one (Step 5, dev_pipe/steps/s6b_sonar_precheck.py), which reuses run_gates/fix_loop below.

Reuses the existing gate checks in dev_agent/checks/ unchanged. Loops back to the agent with the
exact failures until all gates pass or gates.max_fix_attempts is reached.
Outputs: out/changes.patch (implementation + tests), out/gates.json
Next: Step 5 (Sonar Pre-check), then Step 6 (Pull Request Creation), no approval in between.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import time

from dev_agent.checks.coverage_gate import run_coverage_gate
from dev_agent.checks.test_quality_review import review_test_files
from dev_pipe import coverage_handover
from dev_pipe.agent_runner import agent_brief, first_of, question_followup, run_agent, tests_followup
from dev_pipe.build import build_check_text, check_build, is_python_target, prepare_python
from dev_pipe.config import (OUT, app_dir, feedback, load_prompt, settings, sonar_connection,
                             sonar_precheck_required, sonar_status, target_settings, work_root)
from dev_pipe.gitops import apply_patch, change_diff, changed_paths, fix_architecture_file, head_sha, snapshot_patch
from dev_pipe.io_utils import append_log, read_json, read_text, summary, write_bytes, write_json
from dev_pipe.lcov import find_lcov_reports, line_coverage
from dev_pipe.steplog import StepLog, counts_text, test_counts
from dev_pipe.sonar_precheck import run_precheck
from dev_pipe.report_data import file_coverage, is_test_path, new_test_names
from dev_pipe.workspace import prepare_checkout


def pct(value) -> str:
    return "not measured" if value is None else f"{value}%"


# The last Sonar pre-check result (for the Step 5 log).
LAST_PRECHECK: dict = {}


def sonar_gate(base: str, started: float | None) -> tuple[str | None, str, bool]:
    """Sonar pre-check on the agent's change, for every stack (dev_pipe/sonar_precheck.py).
    Returns (failure text for the agent or None, result label, infra_error).
    infra_error = a problem the agent cannot fix (Sonar not configured while required, scanner or
    SonarQube Cloud failure): the fix loop stops and the step fails, so no PR is raised."""
    enabled, reason = sonar_status()
    if not enabled:
        print(f"[gate] Sonar pre-check skipped: {reason}", flush=True)
        if sonar_precheck_required():
            return (f"Sonar pre-check is required (sonar.precheck.required) but cannot run: {reason}",
                    f"error ({reason})", True)
        return None, f"skipped ({reason})", False
    if not base:
        return None, "skipped (no base commit)", False
    result = run_precheck(sonar_connection(), app_dir(), work_root(), change_diff(base),
                          settings().get("sonar", {}).get("precheck") or {},
                          run_id="-".join(x for x in (os.environ.get("GITHUB_RUN_ID", ""),
                                                      os.environ.get("GITHUB_RUN_ATTEMPT", "")) if x),
                          newer_than=started)
    print(f"[gate] {result.report()}", flush=True)
    LAST_PRECHECK.clear()
    LAST_PRECHECK.update(result=result, project_key=sonar_connection()["project_key"])
    if result.status == "error":
        return f"Sonar pre-check could not run: {result.message}", result.label(), True
    if result.status == "failed":
        return result.report(), result.label(), False
    return None, result.label(), False


def run_gates(threshold: float, base: str = "", with_sonar: bool = True,
              reuse: dict | None = None) -> tuple[list[str], dict]:
    """Returns (failure descriptions, result data). Empty failures = all gates passed.
    with_sonar=False: Step 4 (build, tests, coverage, test quality only). with_sonar=True: Step 5 re-runs
    those gates AND the Sonar pre-check, so a Sonar fix that breaks a test is caught before the PR.
    reuse: {"fingerprint", "previous"}: if the previous step's coverage files are for exactly this code, they are
    restored and its results (tests, coverage, test quality) reused, so only the Sonar scan runs.
    data["sonar_infra_error"] = True when the Sonar pre-check failed for a reason the agent cannot fix."""
    t = target_settings()
    adir = app_dir()
    failures: list[str] = []
    started = time.time()

    if reuse and coverage_handover.restore(work_root(), reuse["fingerprint"], out=OUT):
        keep = {k: v for k, v in (reuse.get("previous") or {}).items()
                if k not in ("passed", "attempts", "history", "sonar_precheck", "sonar_infra_error")}
        data = {**keep, "coverage_reused": True}
        print("[gate] build/tests/coverage: results of the previous step reused (same code, not run again)",
              flush=True)
        if with_sonar:
            sonar_failure, data["sonar_precheck"], data["sonar_infra_error"] = sonar_gate(base, started)
            if sonar_failure:
                failures.append(sonar_failure)
        return failures, data

    if not is_python_target(t, adir):
        # Other stacks are gated on their build/test commands (configured, or detected from the repo's
        # files) and on the Sonar pre-check. Coverage is REPORTED (not gated) when the test run writes an
        # LCOV report (e.g. Vitest/Jest with the `lcov` reporter -> coverage/lcov.info).
        changed = [work_root() / p for p in changed_paths(base)] if base else None
        build = check_build(t, adir, changed or None)
        if not build.ok:
            failures.append(f"Build/test commands FAILED:\n{build.report()}")
        reports = find_lcov_reports(adir, newer_than=started)
        coverage = line_coverage(reports)
        if reports:
            print(f"[gate] LCOV coverage {pct(coverage)} from {len(reports)} report(s) (report only, not gated)",
                  flush=True)
        data = {"coverage_pct": coverage, "coverage_threshold": threshold, "coverage_passed": None,
                "coverage_mode": "report only (non-Python)",
                "test_quality_findings": "n/a (non-Python)",
                "build_projects": build.projects, "build_warnings": build.warnings,
                "tests": test_counts(getattr(build, "log", ""))}
        if not with_sonar:
            data["sonar_precheck"] = "runs in Step 5"
        elif build.ok:   # Sonar needs compiled code (Java binaries, .NET build); a broken build is fixed first
            sonar_failure, data["sonar_precheck"], data["sonar_infra_error"] = sonar_gate(base, started)
            if sonar_failure:
                failures.append(sonar_failure)
        else:
            data["sonar_precheck"] = "not run (build failed)"
        return failures, data

    # Install the repo's own dependencies (requirements*.txt / pyproject.toml / ...) into its virtualenv first.
    deps_ok, python, deps_log = prepare_python(adir)
    if not deps_ok:
        return [f"Installing the project's dependencies FAILED:\n{deps_log}"], {
            "coverage_pct": None, "coverage_threshold": threshold, "coverage_passed": False,
            "test_quality_findings": "n/a", "sonar_precheck": "skipped"}

    cov = run_coverage_gate(adir, package=t["app_package"], threshold=threshold, test_dir=t["test_dir"],
                            python=python)
    if not cov.passed:
        detail = cov.summary() if cov.files else f"{cov.summary()}\n{(cov.raw_stdout + cov.raw_stderr)[-2000:]}"
        failures.append(f"Coverage gate FAILED:\n{detail}")

    findings = review_test_files(sorted((adir / t["test_dir"]).rglob("test_*.py")))
    if findings:
        failures.append("Test quality gate FAILED:\n" +
                        "\n".join(f"- {f.file}::{f.test_name}: {f.reason}" for f in findings))

    sonar_failure, sonar_label, sonar_infra = (sonar_gate(base, started) if with_sonar
                                               else (None, "runs in Step 5", False))
    if sonar_failure:
        failures.append(sonar_failure)

    data = {
        "coverage_pct": round(cov.coverage_pct, 1),
        "coverage_threshold": threshold,
        "coverage_passed": cov.passed,
        "test_quality_findings": len(findings),
        "sonar_precheck": sonar_label,
        "sonar_infra_error": sonar_infra,
        "build_projects": [f"{t.get('app_dir', '.')} (python)"],
        "tests": test_counts(cov.raw_stdout),
    }
    return failures, data


def fix_loop(threshold: float, base: str, max_attempts: int, with_sonar: bool,
             step: str = "fix_gates", reuse: dict | None = None) -> tuple[list[str], dict, int]:
    """Run the gates; on failure send the exact failures to the agent and run them again, until they pass,
    max_attempts fixes were tried, or the Sonar pre-check could not run (not a code problem).
    reuse: only for the FIRST run (same code as the previous step); after an AI fix everything runs again.
    Returns (failures, data, number of gate runs)."""
    t = target_settings()
    attempt = 0
    history: list[dict] = []
    while True:
        attempt += 1
        failures, data = run_gates(threshold, base, with_sonar=with_sonar, reuse=reuse if attempt == 1 else None)
        print(f"[gate] attempt {attempt}: {data}", flush=True)
        history.append({"run": attempt, "failures": [f.splitlines()[0][:160] for f in failures],
                        "details": [ln.strip()[:160] for f in failures for ln in f.splitlines()[1:6] if ln.strip()]})
        data["history"] = history
        if not failures or attempt > max_attempts:
            break
        if data.get("sonar_infra_error"):
            print("[gate] Sonar pre-check could not run (not a code problem): stopping the fix loop", flush=True)
            break
        run_agent(
            load_prompt("fix_gates", attempt=attempt, max_attempts=max_attempts,
                        failures="\n\n".join(failures), app_dir=t["app_dir"], test_dir=t["test_dir"],
                        build_check=build_check_text(t, app_dir())),
            system_prompt=load_prompt("system", app_dir=t["app_dir"]),
            edit=True,
            step=step,
        )
        history[-1]["agent"] = agent_brief()
    return failures, data, attempt


def log_fix_loop(log: StepLog, data: dict, attempt: int, max_attempts: int) -> int:
    """Plain-English account of the fix loop. Returns the number of AI fix rounds."""
    history = data.get("history") or []
    fixes = sum(1 for h in history if h.get("agent"))
    if not history:
        return 0
    if not fixes and not history[-1]["failures"]:
        log.ok(f"Fix attempts ......... 0 of {max_attempts} allowed \u2014 everything passed on the first run")
        return 0
    for n, h in enumerate(history, 1):
        if not h["failures"]:
            log.ok(f"Check run {n}: everything passed.")
            continue
        log.fail(f"Check run {n}: {len(h['failures'])} check(s) failed.")
        for f in h["failures"]:
            log.detail(f"- {f}", indent=4)
        for d in h.get("details", [])[:4]:
            log.detail(d, indent=6)
        if h.get("agent"):
            log.arrow(f"Sent to the AI to fix (attempt {n} of {max_attempts}). The AI worked {h['agent']}.")
    return fixes


def main() -> None:
    with StepLog("4", "Test the code") as log:
        job_started = time.time()
        prepare_checkout()  # external mode: target repo at the approved base commit (no-op in local mode)
        t = target_settings()
        g = settings()["gates"]
        threshold = float(g["coverage_threshold"])
        max_attempts = int(g["max_fix_attempts"])

        base = head_sha()
        step3_files = {f["path"] for f in read_json("changes.json", default={}).get("files", [])}
        log.field("Input", f"The code from Step 3 ({len(step3_files)} file(s) changed)")
        apply_patch(OUT / "changes.patch")

        run_agent(
            load_prompt("unit_tests", plan=read_text("plan.md"), feedback=feedback() or "(none)",
                        app_dir=t["app_dir"], test_dir=t["test_dir"], app_package=t["app_package"],
                        threshold=threshold, build_check=build_check_text(t, app_dir())),
            system_prompt=load_prompt("system", app_dir=t["app_dir"]),
            edit=True,
            # Stopped without writing tests -> ask it to finish. Only a warning after that: the gates below
            # decide pass/fail (and a repo without a test setup passes with a warning).
            check=first_of(tests_followup(lambda: changed_paths(base)), question_followup), on_incomplete="warn",
            step="unit_tests",
        )
        brief = agent_brief()
        moved = fix_architecture_file(base, t["architecture_file"])
        test_files = [p for p in changed_paths(base) if p not in step3_files and is_test_path(p)]
        names = new_test_names(change_diff(base))
        new_count = sum(len(v) for v in names.values())

        failures, data, attempt = fix_loop(threshold, base, max_attempts, with_sonar=False)

        passed = not failures
        data.update({"passed": passed, "attempts": attempt})
        write_json("gates.json", data)
        patch = snapshot_patch(base)
        write_bytes("changes.patch", patch)
        if passed:   # Step 5 reuses these for its first scan instead of running the same tests again
            coverage_handover.save(app_dir(), work_root(), coverage_handover.code_fingerprint(base, patch), job_started,
                                   out=OUT)

        log.heading("Writing tests")
        log.ok(f"The AI wrote unit tests {brief}.")
        log.detail(f"{'New test file(s)':<16}: " + (", ".join(test_files[:6]) if test_files else "none"))
        all_names = [n for v in names.values() for n in v]
        for n, name in enumerate(all_names[:10]):
            log.detail(f"{'New tests' if n == 0 else '':<16}: {name}")
        if len(all_names) > 10:
            log.detail(f"{'':<16}  ... and {len(all_names) - 10} more")
        for note in moved:
            log.detail(f"Architecture guide: {note}")

        tests = data.get("tests")
        log.heading("Running the checks")
        tests_ok = not any(k in "\n".join(failures) for k in ("Build/test commands FAILED", "Installing the project"))
        detail = counts_text(tests)
        if tests and new_count:
            detail += f" ({new_count} new, {max(tests['passed'] + tests['failed'] - new_count, 0)} existing)"
        (log.ok if tests_ok else log.fail)(f"Tests ................ {detail}")
        cov = data.get("coverage_pct")
        changed_src = [p for p in changed_paths(base) if not is_test_path(p) and p != t["architecture_file"]]
        per_file = file_coverage(work_root(), changed_src)
        if data.get("coverage_mode"):
            log.ok(f"Code coverage ........ {pct(cov)} (measured, not enforced for this type of project)")
        else:
            (log.ok if data.get("coverage_passed") else log.fail)(
                f"Code coverage ........ {pct(cov)} overall   (our minimum: {threshold:g}%)")
        for path, value in list(per_file.items())[:6]:
            log.detail(f"{'':<21}{path}: {value:g}% of lines tested")
        tq = data.get("test_quality_findings")
        if isinstance(tq, int):
            (log.ok if tq == 0 else log.fail)(f"Test quality ......... {tq} problem(s)"
                                              + (" (no empty tests, every test checks a result)" if tq == 0 else ""))
        else:
            log.detail(f"Test quality ......... {tq}")
        fixes = log_fix_loop(log, data, attempt, max_attempts)
        log.metric("fix_rounds", fixes)
        for w in data.get("build_warnings") or []:
            log.warn(str(w)[:160])

        log.heading("Summary of checks")
        cov_ok = "PASS" if data.get("coverage_passed") else ("INFO" if data.get("coverage_mode") else "FAIL")
        tq_ok = "PASS" if tq == 0 else ("INFO" if not isinstance(tq, int) else "FAIL")
        log.table([["Check", "Result", "Detail"],
                   ["Build + tests", "PASS" if tests_ok else "FAIL", counts_text(tests)],
                   ["Coverage", cov_ok, f"{pct(cov)}" + ("" if data.get("coverage_mode") else f" (needs {threshold:g}%)")],
                   ["Test quality", tq_ok, f"{tq} problem(s)" if isinstance(tq, int) else str(tq)]])
        if new_count:
            log.fact(f"{new_count} new test(s)")
        if tests:
            log.fact(f"{tests['passed']} passing")
        log.fact(f"{pct(cov)} coverage")

        summary(
            f"## Step 4: Build + Test + Coverage: {'PASSED' if passed else 'FAILED'}\n\n"
            f"| Gate | Result |\n|---|---|\n"
            f"| Tests | {counts_text(tests)} |\n"
            f"| Coverage | {pct(data['coverage_pct'])} "
            f"({data['coverage_mode'] if data.get('coverage_mode') else f'threshold {threshold:g}%'}) |\n"
            f"| Test quality | {data['test_quality_findings']} finding(s) |\n"
            f"| Gate runs | {attempt} |\n\n"
            + "".join(f"> WARNING: {w}\n" for w in data.get("build_warnings") or [])
            + ("\n" if data.get("build_warnings") else "")
            + ("" if passed else "```\n" + "\n\n".join(failures)[:6000] + "\n```\n\n")
            + "**Next (Step 5: Sonar Pre-check):** scans the change on SonarQube Cloud before the pull request is raised."
        )
        append_log(f"- Step 4 (Build + Test + Coverage): unit tests written; coverage {pct(data['coverage_pct'])} "
                   f"(threshold {threshold:g}%), test quality findings {data['test_quality_findings']}.")
        if not passed:
            raise SystemExit(f"Checks still failing after {max_attempts} AI fix attempt(s).")
        log.result("PASSED", next_step="Security and quality scan before the pull request (Step 5).")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: dev_pipe/steps/s6b_sonar_precheck.py

"""Step 5: Sonar Pre-check, its own job between Step 4 (Build + Test + Coverage) and Step 6 (Pull Request
Creation). No pull request is raised until this passes.

Applies Step 4's change set (implementation + unit tests) to the same starting commit, then scans it on
SonarQube Cloud for every stack (dev_pipe/sonar_precheck.py). Blocking issues on the lines the agent changed
go back to the agent; after each fix the build, tests, coverage and test-quality gates run AGAIN together
with the scan (Step 4's run_gates), so a Sonar fix can never break what Step 4 already passed.

Outputs: out/changes.patch (updated with any Sonar fixes), out/gates.json (updated), out/sonar_precheck.json
Next: Step 6 (Pull Request Creation), no approval in between.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import time

from dev_pipe import coverage_handover
from dev_pipe.config import OUT, app_dir, resolved_target, settings, sonar_precheck_required, sonar_status, work_root
from dev_pipe.gitops import apply_patch, head_sha, snapshot_patch
from dev_pipe.io_utils import append_log, read_json, summary, write_bytes, write_json
from dev_pipe.steplog import StepLog, counts_text, fmt_duration
from dev_pipe.steps.s6_build_test_coverage import LAST_PRECHECK, fix_loop, log_fix_loop, pct
from dev_pipe.workspace import fetch_base_ref, prepare_checkout

DEFAULT_FIX_ATTEMPTS = 3


def main() -> None:
    with StepLog("5", "Quality and security check") as log:
        enabled, reason = sonar_status()
        if not enabled and not sonar_precheck_required():
            # Optional and switched off / not configured: pass the Step 4 output through unchanged.
            write_json("sonar_precheck.json", {"status": "skipped", "reason": reason})
            gates = read_json("gates.json", default={})
            gates["sonar_precheck"] = f"skipped ({reason})"
            write_json("gates.json", gates)
            summary(f"## Step 5: Sonar Pre-check: skipped ({reason})\n\n"
                    "**Next (Step 6: Pull Request Creation):** commits, pushes and opens the pull request automatically.")
            append_log(f"- Step 5 (Sonar Pre-check): skipped ({reason}).")
            log.warn(f"SonarQube check is switched off or not set up ({reason}), and it is optional, so it was skipped.")
            log.fact("skipped")
            log.result("SKIPPED", next_step="Create the pull request (Step 6).")
            return

        job_started = time.time()
        prepare_checkout()  # external mode: target repo at the approved base commit (no-op in local mode)
        fetch_base_ref(resolved_target().get("base_branch", ""))  # reference branch for the scanner
        cfg = settings()
        threshold = float(cfg["gates"]["coverage_threshold"])
        pre = cfg.get("sonar", {}).get("precheck") or {}
        max_attempts = int(pre.get("max_fix_attempts", DEFAULT_FIX_ATTEMPTS))

        base = head_sha()
        step4_patch = (OUT / "changes.patch").read_bytes() if (OUT / "changes.patch").exists() else b""
        apply_patch(OUT / "changes.patch")

        # Same code as Step 4 -> its coverage files and test results are reused for the first scan (no second test
        # run). Java/Kotlin: Sonar needs the compiled classes, so the build + tests run as before.
        reuse = None if coverage_handover.has_java(app_dir()) else {
            "fingerprint": coverage_handover.code_fingerprint(base, step4_patch),
            "previous": read_json("gates.json", default={})}
        failures, data, attempt = fix_loop(threshold, base, max_attempts, with_sonar=True, step="fix_sonar",
                                           reuse=reuse)

        passed = not failures
        infra = bool(data.get("sonar_infra_error"))
        gates = {**read_json("gates.json", default={}), **data, "passed": passed}
        write_json("gates.json", gates)
        write_json("sonar_precheck.json", {"status": "passed" if passed else ("error" if infra else "failed"),
                                           "result": data.get("sonar_precheck"), "gate_runs": attempt})
        final_patch = snapshot_patch(base)
        write_bytes("changes.patch", final_patch)
        if passed:   # Step 7 reuses these for its first PR scan if the PR still holds exactly this code
            coverage_handover.save(app_dir(), work_root(), coverage_handover.code_fingerprint(base, final_patch),
                                   job_started, out=OUT)

        result = LAST_PRECHECK.get("result")
        d = result.details if result else {}
        log.field("Input", f"Code and tests from Step 4 ({d.get('changed_files', 'n/a')} files, "
                           f"{d.get('changed_lines', 'n/a')} new or changed lines)")

        log.heading("The scan")
        if result and result.status != "error":
            log.ok("Scanned with SonarQube Cloud (project: " + LAST_PRECHECK.get("project_key", "?") + ").")
            langs = d.get("languages") or []
            log.detail(f"{'Languages scanned':<18}: " + (", ".join(langs) if langs else "not reported")
                       + (f" ({d['files_indexed']} files in total)" if d.get("files_indexed") else ""))
            secs = d.get("processing_seconds") or []
            log.detail(f"{'Time on SonarQube':<18}: {fmt_duration(sum(secs)) if secs else 'not available'}")
            cov = d.get("coverage") or []
            log.detail(f"{'Test coverage sent':<18}: " + (", ".join(cov) if cov else "none (no coverage report written)"))
        elif result:
            log.fail(f"SonarQube could not run: {result.message}")
        else:
            log.fail(f"SonarQube could not run: {data.get('sonar_precheck')}")

        if result and result.status != "error":
            history = data.get("history") or []
            fixes = sum(1 for h in history if h.get("agent"))
            log.heading("What SonarQube found" + (f" (final scan, after {fixes} fix round(s))" if fixes else ""))
            log.kv("Issues in the whole project", d.get("total_issues", "n/a"), indent=2, width=48)
            log.kv("Issues in the AI's new or changed lines", f"{len(result.blocking)}   ← only these can block",
                   indent=2, width=48)
            log.kv("Older issues (not part of this story)", f"{result.ignored}   → ignored", indent=2, width=48)
            sev = "/".join(s.title() for s in pre.get("severities", ["BLOCKER", "CRITICAL", "MAJOR"]))
            log.detail(f"Blocking rule: {sev} issues in the new lines")
            for i in result.blocking[:15]:
                loc = f"{i['path']}, line {i['line']}" if i.get("line") else i["path"]
                log.fail(f"[{str(i['severity']).title()}] {loc} — {i['message'][:110]} (rule {i['rule']})")

        log.heading("After the scan")
        tests = data.get("tests")
        history = data.get("history") or []
        if data.get("coverage_reused") and not any(h.get("agent") for h in history):
            log.ok(f"Tests: not run again (same code as Step 4, whose results and coverage were reused): "
                   f"{counts_text(tests)}.")
        else:
            (log.ok if tests and not tests.get("failed") else log.warn)(
                f"Tests run again after the scan: {counts_text(tests)}.")
        fixes = log_fix_loop(log, data, attempt, max_attempts)
        log.metric("fix_rounds", fixes)
        if d.get("branches"):
            (log.ok if d.get("deleted") else log.warn)(
                "Temporary scan data on SonarQube was deleted." if d.get("deleted")
                else "Temporary scan data on SonarQube was kept.")
        log.fact(f"{len(result.blocking) if result else 'n/a'} problems in new code"
                 + (f" ({result.ignored} older issues ignored)" if result and result.ignored else ""))
        if fixes:
            log.fact(f"{fixes} AI fix round(s)")

        summary(
            f"## Step 5: Sonar Pre-check: {'PASSED' if passed else ('COULD NOT RUN' if infra else 'FAILED')}\n\n"
            f"| Check | Result |\n|---|---|\n"
            f"| Sonar pre-check (changed lines) | {data.get('sonar_precheck')} |\n"
            f"| Build + tests re-run | {counts_text(data.get('tests'))} |\n"
            f"| Coverage | {pct(data.get('coverage_pct'))} |\n"
            f"| Gate runs | {attempt} (agent fix attempts allowed: {max_attempts}) |\n\n"
            + ("" if passed else "```\n" + "\n\n".join(failures)[:6000] + "\n```\n\n")
            + ("**Next (Step 6: Pull Request Creation):** commits, pushes and opens the pull request automatically."
               if passed else "**No pull request is raised.**")
        )
        append_log(f"- Step 5 (Sonar Pre-check): {data.get('sonar_precheck')} after {attempt} run(s).")
        if not passed:
            raise SystemExit("SonarQube could not run, so no pull request is raised." if infra
                             else f"Problems are still there after {max_attempts} AI fix attempt(s); no pull request is raised.")
        log.result("PASSED", summary="no known problems in the new code, safe to open the pull request.",
                   next_step="Create the pull request (Step 6).")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

FILE: dev_pipe/steps/s7_pull_request_creation.py

"""Step 6: Pull Request Creation (runs right after Step 4; the PR is opened, never merged).

Commits, pushes, opens the pull request and links it to the ADO story. Then creates the DEV and PR tasks
under the story (moved here from Step 3, so a failed run never leaves orphan tasks). An open task with the same
title from an earlier run of this story is reused, so re-runs never create duplicates.

Dry run: builds the commit locally and shows what would be pushed, but pushes nothing and writes
nothing to ADO (it still reads the story's existing tasks, to show what would be created or reused).
Outputs: out/pr.json, out/ado.json (task IDs, closed by Step 8)
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import os

from dev_pipe import coverage_handover
from dev_pipe.ado import AdoClient
from dev_pipe.config import OUT, is_dry_run, is_external, resolved_target, run_url, settings
from dev_pipe.gitops import apply_patch, configure_identity, create_pull_request, git, push_branch
from dev_pipe.io_utils import append_log, read_json, read_text, summary, write_json
from dev_pipe.steplog import StepLog
from dev_pipe.workspace import prepare_checkout


def pr_body(story: dict, plan_md: str, gates: dict, wid_ref: str) -> str:
    plan_summary = plan_md.split("## Approach", 1)[0].strip()[:4000]
    return (
        f"{wid_ref} {story['title']}\n\n"
        f"{plan_summary}\n\n"
        f"### Quality gates\n"
        f"- Coverage: {'not measured' if gates.get('coverage_pct') is None else str(gates.get('coverage_pct')) + '%'} (threshold {gates.get('coverage_threshold')}%)\n"
        f"- Test quality findings: {gates.get('test_quality_findings')}\n"
        f"- Sonar pre-check: {gates.get('sonar_precheck')}\n\n"
        f"Generated by the Dev Agent pipeline. Not yet reviewed by a human: please review before merging. Run: {run_url()}"
    )


def ensure_tasks(story: dict, ado: AdoClient | None = None) -> dict:
    """DEV and PR tasks under the story: reused if an earlier run of this story left them open, else created.
    A new DEV task is moved to the in-progress state. Returns the ado.json content."""
    cfg = settings()["ado"]
    ado = ado or AdoClient()
    existing = ado.child_tasks(story["id"])
    result = {"dev_task_id": 0, "pr_task_id": 0, "created": [], "reused": []}
    for key, kind in (("dev_task_id", "dev"), ("pr_task_id", "pr")):
        title = cfg["tasks"][kind].format(title=story["title"])[:255]
        task, created = ado.find_or_create_task(story, title, existing)
        result[key] = task.get("id", 0) or 0
        (result["created"] if created else result["reused"]).append(kind)
        if created and kind == "dev" and task.get("id"):
            ado.set_state(task["id"], cfg["states"]["task_in_progress"])
    return result


def main() -> None:
    with StepLog("6", "Create the pull request") as log:
        cfg = settings()
        story = read_json("story.json")
        meta = read_json("plan.json")
        gates = read_json("gates.json")
        wid = story["id"]
        wid_ref = cfg["git"]["commit_prefix"].format(id=wid)
        branch = meta["branch"]
        target = resolved_target()
        if is_external():
            # PR goes back into the branch the work started from (base or the feature branch holding the code).
            prepare_checkout(target)
            base = target["base_branch"]
        else:
            base = cfg["git"].get("base_branch") or os.environ.get("GITHUB_REF_NAME") or git("rev-parse", "--abbrev-ref", "HEAD")

        configure_identity()
        start_commit = git("rev-parse", "HEAD")
        git("checkout", "-b", branch)
        apply_patch(OUT / "changes.patch")
        title = f"{wid_ref}: {story['title'][:200]}"
        git("commit", "-m", title, "-m", f"Pipeline run: {run_url()}")
        files = git("show", "--stat", "--format=", "HEAD")
        numstat = [ln.split("\t", 2) for ln in git("show", "--numstat", "--format=", "HEAD").splitlines() if ln.count("\t") >= 2]
        new_files = set(git("show", "--diff-filter=A", "--name-only", "--format=", "HEAD").splitlines())
        log.field("Input", f"Final code from Step 5 ({len(numstat)} file(s))")

        # Last check before anything leaves the runner (push, PR description, ADO comment): no credentials in the
        # change or the plan. Values are never printed.
        from dev_pipe import leak_check
        from dev_pipe.workspace import take_token
        known = leak_check._known_values({"TARGET_GITHUB_TOKEN": take_token()} if is_external() else None)
        findings = leak_check.scan_patch((OUT / "changes.patch").read_bytes(), known) \
            + leak_check.scan_text(read_text("plan.md", default=""), "the plan", known)
        leak_check.enforce(findings, "the change set or the plan")
        if not findings:
            log.ok("Secret check : no credentials found in the change or the plan.")

        log.ok(f"Saved the changes as 1 commit ({git('rev-parse', '--short=8', 'HEAD')}) by the pipeline bot:")
        log.detail(f"\"{title[:110]}\"")
        log.detail("Files in the commit:")
        for added, removed, path in numstat[:15]:
            extra = " (new)" if path in new_files else ""
            change = f"+{added}{extra}" if path in new_files else f"+{added} / -{removed}"
            log.detail(f"{path} {'.' * max(3, 32 - len(path))} {change}", indent=4)
        if len(numstat) > 15:
            log.detail(f"... and {len(numstat) - 15} more", indent=4)

        if is_dry_run():
            pr = {"number": 0, "url": "", "head": branch, "base": base, "dry_run": True,
                  "repo": target.get("full_name") or os.environ.get("GITHUB_REPOSITORY", "")}
            print("[dry-run] push and pull request skipped", flush=True)
            log.warn(f"Dry run: branch {branch} was not pushed and no pull request was opened.")
        else:
            replaced = push_branch(branch, base)
            pr = create_pull_request(branch, base, title, pr_body(story, read_text("plan.md"), gates, wid_ref))
            pr["branch_replaced"] = replaced
            log.ok(f"{'Branch pushed':<14}: {branch}" + (" (replaced the branch from an earlier run)" if replaced else ""))
            log.ok(f"{'Pull request':<14}: #{pr['number']}  →  into \"{base}\""
                   + (" (existing pull request, updated by this run)" if pr.get("reused") else ""))
            log.detail(f"{'Link':<14}: {pr['url']}")
            log.detail(f"{'Description':<14}: story summary, plan summary, test and coverage results, SonarQube result")
            ado = AdoClient()
            ado.add_hyperlink(wid, pr["url"], f"Pull request #{pr['number']}")  # skipped if already linked
            if not pr.get("reused"):
                ado.add_comment(wid, f"Pull request raised by the Dev Agent pipeline: {pr['url']}")
            log.ok(f"{'Azure DevOps':<14}: pull request linked to story #{wid}"
                   + (", and a comment added." if not pr.get("reused") else " (already linked)."))
        log.detail("The pull request is NOT merged. That decision stays with the team.")

        ado_ids = ensure_tasks(story)
        write_json("ado.json", ado_ids)
        dry = " (dry run: not written)" if is_dry_run() else ""
        for key, kind, label in (("dev_task_id", "dev", "Development task"), ("pr_task_id", "pr", "PR task")):
            verb = "Created" if kind in ado_ids["created"] else "Reused (from an earlier run)"
            state = f" ({settings()['ado']['states']['task_in_progress']})" if kind == "dev" and verb == "Created" else ""
            log.ok(f"{'Azure DevOps':<14}: {verb} {label} #{ado_ids[key] or 'n/a'}{state}{dry if verb == 'Created' else ''}.")
        # Step 7 reuses Step 5's coverage files only if the PR still holds exactly this commit / this code.
        pr["commit"] = git("rev-parse", "HEAD")
        pr["code_fingerprint"] = coverage_handover.code_fingerprint(start_commit, (OUT / "changes.patch").read_bytes())
        write_json("pr.json", pr)
        log.fact(f"PR #{pr['number']} opened and linked to the story" if pr.get("number") else "dry run: no PR")
        if ado_ids["created"]:
            log.fact(f"{len(ado_ids['created'])} ADO task(s) created")
        if ado_ids["reused"]:
            log.fact(f"{len(ado_ids['reused'])} ADO task(s) reused")

        summary(
            f"## Step 6: Pull Request Creation {'(dry run: not pushed)' if is_dry_run() else 'created'}\n\n"
            + (f"Repository: **{target['full_name']}**\n\n" if target else "")
            + f"Branch `{branch}` -> `{base}`\n\n"
            + (f"PR: {pr['url']}" + (" (existing PR, updated by this re-run)" if pr.get("reused") else "") + "\n\n"
               if pr.get("url") else "")
            + ("> The branch from an earlier run of this story was replaced with this run's code.\n\n"
               if pr.get("branch_replaced") else "")
            + f"```\n{files[:4000]}\n```\n\n"
            + "**Next (Step 7: SonarQube Quality Validation):** runs the SonarQube check on the PR automatically."
        )
        append_log(f"- Step 6 (Pull Request Creation): branch `{branch}` -> `{base}`; PR {pr.get('url') or '(dry run)'}; "
                   f"ADO tasks DEV #{ado_ids['dev_task_id'] or 'n/a'}, PR #{ado_ids['pr_task_id'] or 'n/a'}"
                   f" ({', '.join(ado_ids['created']) or 'none'} created, {', '.join(ado_ids['reused']) or 'none'} reused).")
        log.result("PASSED", next_step="Official SonarQube check on the pull request (Step 7).")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
