FILE: tests/test_dev_pipe_ado_flow.py

"""ADO writes: story Active in Step 3, tasks created (or reused) in Step 6, failure comment on the story.
No network: ADO is faked."""
from __future__ import annotations

import pathlib

import yaml

from dev_pipe import ado as ado_mod
from dev_pipe.steps import notify_failure as nf
from dev_pipe.steps import s4_autonomous_development as s3
from dev_pipe.steps import s7_pull_request_creation as s6

STORY = {"id": 101, "title": "Add /concat", "state": "New", "url": "https://ado/wi/101",
         "assigned_to": "", "area_path": "P", "iteration_path": "P\\S1"}


# ---------- reuse rules
def test_pick_existing_task_reuses_only_open_tasks_with_same_title():
    tasks = [{"id": 1, "title": "DEV - Add /concat", "state": "Closed"},
             {"id": 2, "title": "dev - add /concat ", "state": "Active"},
             {"id": 3, "title": "PR - Add /concat", "state": "Removed"}]
    closed = {"Closed", "Removed"}
    assert ado_mod.pick_existing_task(tasks, "DEV - Add /concat", closed)["id"] == 2
    assert ado_mod.pick_existing_task(tasks, "PR - Add /concat", closed) is None


def test_child_tasks_reads_children_and_keeps_tasks_only():
    client = ado_mod.AdoClient.__new__(ado_mod.AdoClient)
    client.api, client.version, client.dry_run = "https://ado/_apis/wit", "7.1", True
    calls = []

    def fake_call(method, url, **kw):
        calls.append((url, kw.get("params")))
        if url.endswith("/workitems/101"):
            return {"relations": [
                {"rel": "System.LinkTypes.Hierarchy-Forward", "url": "https://ado/_apis/wit/workItems/7"},
                {"rel": "System.LinkTypes.Hierarchy-Forward", "url": "https://ado/_apis/wit/workItems/8"},
                {"rel": "Hyperlink", "url": "https://github.com/o/r/pull/1"}]}
        return {"value": [
            {"id": 7, "fields": {"System.Title": "DEV - Add /concat", "System.WorkItemType": "Task",
                                 "System.State": "Active"}},
            {"id": 8, "fields": {"System.Title": "A bug", "System.WorkItemType": "Bug", "System.State": "New"}}]}
    client._call = fake_call
    assert client.child_tasks(101) == [{"id": 7, "title": "DEV - Add /concat", "state": "Active"}]
    assert calls[1][1]["ids"] == "7,8"


# ---------- Step 3: story only
class FakeAdo:
    def __init__(self, existing=()):
        self.existing = list(existing)
        self.states, self.created = [], []
        self.next_id = 500

    def set_state(self, wid, state, extra=None):
        self.states.append((wid, state))

    def child_tasks(self, wid):
        return self.existing

    def find_or_create_task(self, story, title, existing):
        found = ado_mod.pick_existing_task(existing, title, {"Closed", "Removed"})
        if found:
            return found, False
        self.next_id += 1
        self.created.append(title)
        return {"id": self.next_id}, True


def test_step3_moves_story_to_active_and_creates_no_tasks(monkeypatch):
    fake = FakeAdo()
    monkeypatch.setattr(s3, "AdoClient", lambda: fake)
    assert s3.update_ado_on_start(dict(STORY)) is True
    assert fake.states == [(101, "Active")] and fake.created == []


def test_step3_leaves_an_already_active_story_alone(monkeypatch):
    fake = FakeAdo()
    monkeypatch.setattr(s3, "AdoClient", lambda: fake)
    assert s3.update_ado_on_start({**STORY, "state": "active"}) is False
    assert fake.states == []


# ---------- Step 6: tasks after the PR
def test_step6_creates_both_tasks_and_starts_dev_task():
    fake = FakeAdo()
    ids = s6.ensure_tasks(dict(STORY), ado=fake)
    assert fake.created == ["DEV - Add /concat", "PR - Add /concat"]
    assert ids["dev_task_id"] == 501 and ids["pr_task_id"] == 502
    assert ids["created"] == ["dev", "pr"] and ids["reused"] == []
    assert fake.states == [(501, "Active")]   # only the new DEV task


def test_step6_rerun_reuses_open_tasks_no_duplicates():
    fake = FakeAdo(existing=[{"id": 7, "title": "DEV - Add /concat", "state": "Active"},
                             {"id": 8, "title": "PR - Add /concat", "state": "New"}])
    ids = s6.ensure_tasks(dict(STORY), ado=fake)
    assert fake.created == [] and fake.states == []
    assert (ids["dev_task_id"], ids["pr_task_id"]) == (7, 8) and ids["reused"] == ["dev", "pr"]


# ---------- failure comment
def test_first_failed_step_is_reported():
    needs = {"plan": {"result": "success"}, "implement": {"result": "success"},
             "test-gates": {"result": "failure"}, "sonar-precheck": {"result": "skipped"}}
    job, label = nf.failed_step(needs)
    assert job == "test-gates" and "Step 4" in label
    text = nf.message(job, label, "https://run/1")
    assert "failed at Step 4" in text and "https://run/1" in text and "No pull request" in text


def test_failure_after_pr_says_pr_exists():
    job, label = nf.failed_step({"create-pr": {"result": "success"}, "quality-gate": {"result": "failure"}})
    assert "pull request was raised" in nf.message(job, label, "u")


def test_nothing_posted_when_no_step_after_step3_failed(monkeypatch, capsys):
    monkeypatch.setenv("NEEDS_JSON", '{"plan": {"result": "failure"}, "implement": {"result": "skipped"}}')
    nf.main()
    assert "nothing to post" in capsys.readouterr().out


def test_ado_error_never_fails_the_notify_job(monkeypatch, capsys):
    monkeypatch.setenv("NEEDS_JSON", '{"implement": {"result": "failure"}}')
    monkeypatch.setenv("WORK_ITEM_ID", "101")

    class Broken:
        def __init__(self):
            raise SystemExit("ADO down")
    monkeypatch.setattr(ado_mod, "AdoClient", Broken)
    nf.main()
    assert "could not post" in capsys.readouterr().out


def test_workflow_runs_notify_only_on_failure_after_step3():
    jobs = yaml.safe_load(pathlib.Path(".github/workflows/dev-pipe.yml").read_text())["jobs"]
    job = jobs["notify-failure"]
    assert "failure()" in job["if"] and "needs.implement.result != 'skipped'" in job["if"]
    assert {j for j, _ in nf.JOBS} <= set(job["needs"])

FILE: tests/test_dev_pipe_approval.py

"""Tests for the issue-based approval gates (no network: GitHub is faked)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest

from dev_pipe import approval
from dev_pipe.steps import gate


def c(login, body):
    return {"user": {"login": login}, "body": body}


# ---------- parsing and decisions
def test_parse_approvers_is_case_insensitive_and_strips_at():
    assert approval.parse_approvers("alice, @Bob  carol") == {"alice", "bob", "carol"}
    assert approval.parse_approvers("") == set()


def test_approve_and_reject_by_allowed_approver():
    assert approval.decide([c("Alice", "Approve")], {"alice"}) == ("approve", "", "Alice")
    assert approval.decide([c("alice", "reject: use TaskStore")], {"alice"}) == ("reject", "use TaskStore", "alice")
    assert approval.decide([c("alice", "/rejected")], {"alice"})[1] == "(no reason given)"


def test_comments_from_others_and_bot_are_ignored():
    comments = [c("mallory", "approve"), c("github-actions[bot]", "approve"), c("alice", "looks good, checking")]
    assert approval.decide(comments, {"alice"}) is None


def test_first_decision_wins_and_words_must_lead():
    assert approval.decide([c("alice", "I don't approve yet"), c("alice", "reject: wrong file")],
                           {"alice"}) == ("reject", "wrong file", "alice")
    assert approval.decide([c("alice", "approve"), c("alice", "reject: x")], {"alice"})[0] == "approve"


class FakeGitHub:
    def __init__(self, comment_batches):
        self.batches = list(comment_batches)
        self.created, self.comments, self.closed = [], [], []

    def create_issue(self, title, body):
        self.created.append((title, body))
        return {"number": 7, "html_url": "https://github.com/o/r/issues/7"}

    def list_comments(self, number):
        return self.batches.pop(0) if len(self.batches) > 1 else self.batches[0]

    def comment(self, number, body):
        self.comments.append(body)

    def close(self, number, completed):
        self.closed.append(completed)


def test_wait_polls_until_decision():
    gh = FakeGitHub([[], [], [c("alice", "approve")]])
    sleeps = []
    assert approval.wait_for_decision(gh, 7, {"alice"}, timeout_s=999, sleep=sleeps.append,
                                      now=lambda: 0) == ("approve", "", "alice")
    assert len(sleeps) == 2


def test_wait_times_out():
    clock = iter([0, 10, 61])
    assert approval.wait_for_decision(FakeGitHub([[]]), 7, {"alice"}, timeout_s=60,
                                      sleep=lambda s: None, now=lambda: next(clock)) is None


# ---------- gate step end to end
@pytest.fixture
def out_dir(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    out = tmp_path / "out"
    out.mkdir()
    (out / "story.json").write_text(json.dumps({"id": 101, "title": "Add /concat"}))
    (out / "plan.md").write_text("## Summary\nAdd a /concat endpoint")
    monkeypatch.setattr(gate, "OUT", out)
    monkeypatch.setenv("APPROVERS", "alice")
    monkeypatch.setenv("APPROVAL_TIMEOUT_MINUTES", "30")
    monkeypatch.delenv("GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY", raising=False)
    return out


def test_issue_shows_what_to_review(out_dir):
    title, body = gate.build_issue("approve-plan", out_dir, "https://run", {"alice"}, 30)
    assert "Step 3" in title and "#101" in title
    assert "Add a /concat endpoint" in body and "`approve`" in body and "@alice" in body


def test_gate_approved_closes_issue_and_continues(out_dir):
    gh = FakeGitHub([[c("alice", "approve")]])
    gate.main(["approve-plan"], gh=gh, waiter=lambda *a, **k: ("approve", "", "alice"))
    assert gh.closed == [True] and "Approved by @alice" in gh.comments[0]


def test_gate_rejected_stops_pipeline(out_dir):
    gh = FakeGitHub([[]])
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as err:
        gate.main(["approve-plan"], gh=gh, waiter=lambda *a, **k: ("reject", "wrong approach", "alice"))
    assert err.value.code == 1 and gh.closed == [False] and "wrong approach" in gh.comments[0]


def test_gate_timeout_stops_pipeline(out_dir):
    gh = FakeGitHub([[]])
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        gate.main(["approve-plan"], gh=gh, waiter=lambda *a, **k: None)
    assert gh.closed == [False] and "No decision" in gh.comments[0]


def test_unknown_gate_and_missing_approvers_fail_fast(out_dir, monkeypatch):
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        gate.main(["approve-everything"], gh=FakeGitHub([[]]))
    monkeypatch.setenv("APPROVERS", "")
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        gate.main(["approve-plan"], gh=FakeGitHub([[]]))


def test_every_gate_in_workflow_exists_in_gate_py():
    import pathlib
    import yaml
    wf = yaml.safe_load(pathlib.Path(".github/workflows/dev-pipe.yml").read_text())
    used = {job["with"]["gate"] for job in wf["jobs"].values() if "uses" in job}
    # The pipeline now has a single human approval (approve-ado-update); the other gates stay
    # defined in gate.py so they can be re-enabled, so only check that every gate used exists.
    assert used <= set(gate.GATES)
    assert used == {"approve-ado-update"}

"""Tests for the comment-triggered approval (APPROVAL_MODE=comment, dev_pipe/steps/gate_comment.py).
No network: GitHub is faked."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import pathlib

import pytest
import yaml

from dev_pipe.steps import gate_comment as gc


class FakeGitHub:
    def __init__(self, run_path=".github/workflows/dev-pipe.yml@refs/heads/feature/dev-pipe"):
        self.run_path = run_path
        self.created, self.comments, self.closed = [], [], []

    def create_issue(self, title, body):
        self.created.append((title, body))
        return {"number": 9, "html_url": "https://github.com/o/r/issues/9"}

    def comment(self, number, body):
        self.comments.append((number, body))

    def close(self, number, completed):
        self.closed.append((number, completed))

    def get_run(self, run_id):
        return {"path": self.run_path, "head_sha": "abc123", "html_url": f"https://github.com/o/r/actions/runs/{run_id}"}


@pytest.fixture
def out_dir(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    out = tmp_path / "out"
    out.mkdir()
    (out / "story.json").write_text(json.dumps({"id": 101, "title": "Add /concat"}))
    (out / "summary.md").write_text("- Step 3: implemented 1 file")
    monkeypatch.setenv("APPROVERS", "alice")
    monkeypatch.setenv("APPROVAL_TIMEOUT_MINUTES", "30")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GITHUB_RUN_ID", "555")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GITHUB_REPOSITORY", "o/r")
    monkeypatch.setenv("DRY_RUN", "false")
    monkeypatch.delenv("GITHUB_OUTPUT", raising=False)
    return out


def event(login, body, issue_body="", number=9):
    return {"issue": {"number": number, "body": issue_body}, "comment": {"user": {"login": login}, "body": body}}


def test_prepare_records_approvers_timeout_and_dry_run(out_dir):
    req = gc.prepare("approve-ado-update", out_dir, now=lambda: 1000.0)
    saved = json.loads((out_dir / gc.REQUEST_FILE).read_text())
    assert saved == req
    assert req["approvers"] == ["alice"] and req["timeout_minutes"] == 30
    assert req["dry_run"] == "false" and req["run_id"] == "555"


def test_prepare_fails_fast(out_dir, monkeypatch):
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        gc.prepare("approve-everything", out_dir)
    monkeypatch.setenv("APPROVERS", "")
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        gc.prepare("approve-ado-update", out_dir)


def test_dry_run_unless_explicitly_false(out_dir, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.delenv("DRY_RUN")
    assert gc.prepare("approve-ado-update", out_dir)["dry_run"] == "true"


def test_open_issue_carries_marker_and_does_not_wait(out_dir):
    gc.prepare("approve-ado-update", out_dir)
    gh = FakeGitHub()
    gc.open_issue(out_dir, gh=gh)
    title, body = gh.created[0]
    assert title.startswith("[Dev Pipeline]") and "#101" in title
    assert gc.marker("555") in body and "No runner waits" in body and "@alice" in body
    assert gc.MARKER_RE.search(body).group(1) == "555"


def test_locate_accepts_only_dev_pipe_runs():
    body = "...\n" + gc.marker("555")
    assert gc.locate(event("alice", "approve", body), gh=FakeGitHub())["run_id"] == "555"
    assert gc.locate(event("alice", "approve", body), gh=FakeGitHub(".github/workflows/other.yml"))["found"] is False
    assert gc.locate(event("alice", "approve", "no marker"), gh=FakeGitHub())["found"] is False


def test_approve_by_allowed_approver(out_dir):
    gc.prepare("approve-ado-update", out_dir, now=lambda: 1000.0)
    gh = FakeGitHub()
    assert gc.decide_comment(event("Alice", "approve"), out_dir, gh=gh, now=lambda: 1060.0) == "approve"
    assert gh.closed == [(9, True)] and "Approved by @Alice" in gh.comments[0][1]


def test_reject_closes_issue(out_dir):
    gc.prepare("approve-ado-update", out_dir, now=lambda: 1000.0)
    gh = FakeGitHub()
    assert gc.decide_comment(event("alice", "reject: wrong file"), out_dir, gh=gh, now=lambda: 1001.0) == "reject"
    assert gh.closed == [(9, False)] and "wrong file" in gh.comments[0][1]


def test_other_people_and_chatter_are_ignored(out_dir):
    gc.prepare("approve-ado-update", out_dir, now=lambda: 1000.0)
    gh = FakeGitHub()
    assert gc.decide_comment(event("mallory", "approve"), out_dir, gh=gh, now=lambda: 1001.0) == "none"
    assert gc.decide_comment(event("alice", "looking now"), out_dir, gh=gh, now=lambda: 1001.0) == "none"
    assert gh.comments == [] and gh.closed == []


def test_late_answer_is_refused(out_dir):
    gc.prepare("approve-ado-update", out_dir, now=lambda: 1000.0)
    gh = FakeGitHub()
    assert gc.decide_comment(event("alice", "approve"), out_dir, gh=gh, now=lambda: 1000.0 + 31 * 60) == "expired"
    assert gh.closed == [(9, False)] and "expired" in gh.comments[0][1]


def test_outputs_written_for_the_workflow(out_dir, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    output = tmp_path / "gh_output"
    monkeypatch.setenv("GITHUB_OUTPUT", str(output))
    gc.prepare("approve-ado-update", out_dir, now=lambda: 1000.0)
    gc.decide_comment(event("alice", "approve"), out_dir, gh=FakeGitHub(), now=lambda: 1001.0)
    assert "decision=approve" in output.read_text() and "dry_run=false" in output.read_text()


# ---------- workflow wiring
WF = pathlib.Path(".github/workflows")


def test_pipeline_has_both_approval_modes_and_dry_run_skip():
    jobs = yaml.safe_load((WF / "dev-pipe.yml").read_text())["jobs"]
    assert "APPROVAL_MODE != 'comment'" in jobs["gate-ado"]["if"]
    assert "APPROVAL_MODE == 'comment'" in jobs["request-ado-approval"]["if"]
    assert "'false'" in jobs["quality-gate"]["if"]   # Step 7 skipped in a dry run
    steps = jobs["request-ado-approval"]["steps"]
    names = [s.get("run", "") + str(s.get("uses", "")) for s in steps]
    upload = next(i for i, n in enumerate(names) if "save-stage" in n)
    opened = next(i for i, n in enumerate(names) if "gate_comment open" in n)
    assert upload < opened   # artifact exists before anyone can approve


def test_decision_workflow_only_gives_ado_pat_to_step_8():
    wf = yaml.safe_load((WF / "dev-pipe-approval-decision.yml").read_text())
    text = (WF / "dev-pipe-approval-decision.yml").read_text()
    assert text.count("secrets.ADO_PAT") == 1
    assert "ADO_PAT" not in json.dumps(wf["jobs"]["decide"])
    assert wf["jobs"]["ado-complete"]["if"] == "${{ needs.decide.outputs.decision == 'approve' }}"

"""Tests for external mode: repo/branch discovery (GitHub + AAVA faked), checkout and build commands.
No network, no LLM."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import subprocess
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from dev_pipe import aava, build, config, gitops, resolver, workspace

NOW = datetime(2026, 10, 22, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
RECENT = "2026-10-15T10:00:00Z"
OLD = "2025-01-01T10:00:00Z"

STORY = {"id": 4321, "title": "Add refund status to payments API",
         "acceptance_criteria": "Refund status is returned for each payment", "description": "Customers see refunds"}


# ---------- fakes
class FakeGitHub:
    def __init__(self, repos, branches=None, compares=None, pulls=None):
        self.repos = {r["full_name"]: r for r in repos}
        self.branches = branches or {}          # full -> {name: date}
        self.compares = compares or {}          # (full, head) -> compare dict
        self.pulls = pulls or {}                # full -> [pr]

    def list_owner_repos(self, owner, limit=100):
        return [r for r in self.repos.values() if r["full_name"].startswith(owner + "/")]

    def get_repo(self, full):
        return self.repos.get(full)

    def list_branches(self, full, limit=100):
        return list(self.branches.get(full, {}))

    def get_branch(self, full, branch):
        date = self.branches.get(full, {}).get(branch)
        return None if date is None else {"name": branch, "sha": f"sha-{full}-{branch}", "date": date}

    def compare(self, full, base, head):
        return self.compares.get((full, head), {"ahead_by": 0, "files": [], "messages": []})

    def open_pulls(self, full, limit=100):
        return self.pulls.get(full, [])


class FakeAava:
    """scores: (repo, branch) -> similarity; indexed: set of (repo, branch) already indexed."""
    def __init__(self, scores, indexed=None):
        self.scores, self.triggered, self.searched = scores, [], []
        self.indexed = set(scores if indexed is None else indexed)

    def progress(self, owner, repo, branch):
        return {"state": "COMPLETED"} if (repo, branch) in self.indexed else None

    state_of = staticmethod(aava.Aava.state_of)

    def trigger_index(self, owner, repo, branch):
        self.triggered.append((repo, branch))
        self.indexed.add((repo, branch))
        return "TRIGGERED"

    def wait_until_done(self, targets, timeout, poll):
        return {t: "COMPLETED" for t in targets}

    def search(self, owner, repo, branch, query, intent="feature_flow", limit=5):
        self.searched.append((repo, branch))
        sim = self.scores.get((repo, branch), 0.0)
        return [{"filePath": f"docs/{repo}.md", "name": "Refunds", "startLine": 1, "endLine": 9,
                 "contentPreview": "refund status", "similarity": sim}] if sim else []


def repo(name, default="main", desc=""):
    return {"full_name": f"org/{name}", "name": name, "default_branch": default, "description": desc,
            "archived": False, "fork": False, "topics": []}


CFG = {"github_org": "org", "base_min_similarity": 0.75, "min_margin": 0.05, "repos_for_branch_scan": 2,
       "max_branches_scanned": 50, "branch_active_days": 60, "branch_shortlist": 3,
       "exclude_branch_patterns": ["dependabot/*"], "aava": {"index_missing": True}}


def make(gh, av, **cfg):
    return resolver.Resolver(gh, av, {**CFG, **cfg}, log=lambda *_: None, now=NOW)


# ---------- pure helpers
def test_story_queries_and_keywords():
    qs = resolver.story_queries(STORY)
    assert qs[0] == STORY["title"] and len(qs) == 3
    kw = resolver.story_keywords(STORY)
    assert {"refund", "payment", "status"} <= kw and "user" not in kw


def test_branch_signals_use_keywords_only():
    kw = resolver.story_keywords(STORY)
    score, ev = resolver.branch_signals(
        kw, "feature/refund-status",
        {"ahead_by": 2, "files": ["api/refund_service.py"], "messages": ["refund flow"]},
        [{"number": 7, "head": "feature/refund-status", "title": "Refund status", "body": ""}])
    assert score > 0 and any("PR #7" in e for e in ev)
    # a generic branch name and commit message is normal: score 0, no evidence, but no error
    assert resolver.branch_signals(kw, "feature/sprint-12", {"files": [], "messages": ["fix"]}, []) == (0, [])


def test_decide_threshold_and_margin():
    a, b = {"similarity": 0.9}, {"similarity": 0.88}
    assert resolver.decide([a, b], 0.75, 0.05) == (a, "low")
    assert resolver.decide([a, {"similarity": 0.5}], 0.75, 0.05) == (a, "high")
    assert resolver.decide([{"similarity": 0.5}], 0.75, 0.05) == (None, "none")


def test_excluded_repo_patterns():
    assert resolver.excluded("sandbox-x", ["sandbox-*"]) and not resolver.excluded("payments", ["sandbox-*"])


# ---------- resolver scenarios
def test_found_on_base_branch_of_best_repo():
    gh = FakeGitHub([repo("payments"), repo("web")],
                    branches={"org/payments": {"main": RECENT}, "org/web": {"main": RECENT}})
    av = FakeAava({("payments", "main"): 0.86, ("web", "main"): 0.40})
    t = make(gh, av).resolve(STORY)
    assert (t["full_name"], t["base_branch"], t["found_on"], t["confidence"]) == ("org/payments", "main", "base", "high")
    assert t["base_sha"] == "sha-org/payments-main" and t["matches"]


def test_preferred_base_branch_used_when_it_exists():
    gh = FakeGitHub([repo("payments")], branches={"org/payments": {"main": RECENT, "develop": RECENT}})
    av = FakeAava({("payments", "develop"): 0.9})
    assert make(gh, av, base_branch="develop").resolve(STORY)["base_branch"] == "develop"


def test_missing_index_is_triggered():
    gh = FakeGitHub([repo("payments")], branches={"org/payments": {"main": RECENT}})
    av = FakeAava({("payments", "main"): 0.9}, indexed=set())
    make(gh, av).resolve(STORY)
    assert ("payments", "main") in av.triggered


def test_not_on_base_found_on_feature_branch():
    gh = FakeGitHub(
        [repo("payments", desc="payments service"), repo("web")],
        branches={"org/payments": {"main": RECENT, "feature/refunds": RECENT, "feature/old": OLD,
                                   "dependabot/x": RECENT, "feature/unrelated": RECENT},
                  "org/web": {"main": RECENT}},
        compares={("org/payments", "feature/refunds"): {"ahead_by": 3, "files": ["docs/refund.md"],
                                                             "messages": ["refund draft"]},
                  ("org/payments", "feature/unrelated"): {"ahead_by": 1, "files": ["x.md"], "messages": ["chore"]}})
    av = FakeAava({("payments", "main"): 0.41, ("web", "main"): 0.2, ("payments", "feature/refunds"): 0.82},
                  indexed={("payments", "main"), ("web", "main")})
    t = make(gh, av).resolve(STORY)
    assert (t["full_name"], t["base_branch"], t["found_on"], t["confidence"]) == \
        ("org/payments", "feature/refunds", "branch", "high")
    assert ("payments", "feature/refunds") in av.triggered          # branch indexed on demand
    # no filtering: old, bot and unrelated branches are all indexed and searched too
    for b in ("feature/old", "dependabot/x", "feature/unrelated"):
        assert ("payments", b) in av.searched


def test_branch_without_any_keyword_is_still_searched():
    """Generic branch names / commit messages: the code is found by AAVA on a recent branch."""
    gh = FakeGitHub(
        [repo("payments")],
        branches={"org/payments": {"main": RECENT, "feature/sprint-12": "2026-10-20T10:00:00Z",
                                   "feature/misc": "2026-09-01T10:00:00Z", "feature/merged": RECENT}},
        compares={("org/payments", "feature/sprint-12"): {"ahead_by": 4, "files": ["src/a.py"], "messages": ["wip"]},
                  ("org/payments", "feature/misc"): {"ahead_by": 1, "files": ["b.py"], "messages": ["fix"]}})
    av = FakeAava({("payments", "main"): 0.3, ("payments", "feature/sprint-12"): 0.8},
                  indexed={("payments", "main")})
    t = make(gh, av).resolve(STORY)
    assert (t["base_branch"], t["found_on"]) == ("feature/sprint-12", "branch")
    assert ("payments", "feature/merged") in av.searched          # no filtering: every branch is searched


def test_all_branches_searched_and_listed_when_nothing_qualifies():
    names = ["a", "b", "c", "d", "feature/refund", "old-branch"]
    gh = FakeGitHub([repo("payments")],
                    branches={"org/payments": {"main": RECENT, **{n: (OLD if n == "old-branch" else RECENT) for n in names}}})
    av = FakeAava({("payments", "main"): 0.1, ("payments", "c"): 0.6}, indexed={("payments", "main")})
    t = make(gh, av).resolve(STORY)
    searched = {b for r, b in av.searched if b != "main"}
    assert searched == set(names)                                 # every branch, no cap, no age filter
    assert set(names) <= {b for r, b in av.triggered}             # all indexed on demand
    assert t["confidence"] == "low" and t["base_branch"] == "main"
    assert t["alternatives"][0]["branch"] == "c"                  # best-scoring branch shown first to the reviewer
    assert {a["branch"] for a in t["alternatives"]} >= set(names)


def test_nothing_found_falls_back_to_most_likely_repo_with_low_confidence():
    gh = FakeGitHub([repo("payments"), repo("web")],
                    branches={"org/payments": {"main": RECENT}, "org/web": {"main": RECENT}})
    av = FakeAava({("payments", "main"): 0.5, ("web", "main"): 0.3})
    t = make(gh, av).resolve(STORY)
    assert (t["full_name"], t["confidence"]) == ("org/payments", "low")


def test_no_signal_anywhere_stops_with_instructions():
    gh = FakeGitHub([repo("alpha"), repo("beta")],
                    branches={"org/alpha": {"main": RECENT}, "org/beta": {"main": RECENT}})
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as err:
        make(gh, FakeAava({})).resolve(STORY)
    assert "target_repo" in str(err.value)


def test_manual_override_skips_detection():
    gh = FakeGitHub([repo("payments"), repo("web")],
                    branches={"org/web": {"main": RECENT, "release/2": RECENT}})
    t = make(gh, FakeAava({})).resolve(STORY, "org/web", "release/2")
    assert (t["full_name"], t["base_branch"], t["confidence"]) == ("org/web", "release/2", "manual")


def test_include_list_limits_candidates():
    gh = FakeGitHub([repo("payments"), repo("web")], branches={"org/web": {"main": RECENT}})
    r = make(gh, FakeAava({}), include_repos=["web"])
    assert [x["full_name"] for x in r.candidate_repos()] == ["org/web"]


def test_code_context_formats_matches():
    text = resolver.code_context({"matches": [{"filePath": "a.md", "startLine": 1, "endLine": 3, "name": "H",
                                               "contentPreview": "body", "similarity": 0.8}]})
    assert "a.md:1-3" in text and "body" in text
    assert "no matches" in resolver.code_context({})


# ---------- AAVA client parsing
@pytest.mark.parametrize("payload", [
    {"status": "SUCCESS", "data": {"matches": [{"filePath": "a.md", "similarity": 0.5}, {"filePath": "b.md", "similarity": 0.9}]}},
    {"data": [{"filePath": "a.md", "similarity": 0.5}, {"filePath": "b.md", "similarity": 0.9}]},
    {"results": [{"filePath": "a.md", "score": 0.5}, {"filePath": "b.md", "score": 0.9}]},
])
def test_normalize_matches_accepts_shapes(payload):
    m = aava.normalize_matches(payload)
    assert [x["filePath"] for x in m] == ["b.md", "a.md"]


def test_wait_until_done_times_out():
    t = {"now": 0.0}
    client = aava.Aava("http://x", sleep=lambda s: t.__setitem__("now", t["now"] + s), clock=lambda: t["now"])
    client.progress = lambda *a: {"state": "RUNNING"}
    states = client.wait_until_done([("o", "r", "main")], timeout=30, poll=10)
    assert states[("o", "r", "main")].startswith("TIMEOUT")


def test_search_sends_token_and_string_query():
    sent = {}

    class Resp:
        status_code, content = 200, b"{}"

        def json(self):
            return {"data": {"matches": []}}

    class Session:
        def post(self, url, json=None, timeout=None):
            sent.update(json)
            return Resp()

    aava.Aava("http://x", github_token="tok", session=Session()).search("o", "r", "main", None)
    assert sent["query"] == "" and sent["githubToken"] == "tok" and sent["branch"] == "main"


# ---------- token handling
def test_token_removed_from_environment_and_not_in_plain_text(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(workspace, "_token", None)
    monkeypatch.setenv("TARGET_GITHUB_TOKEN", "secret123")
    assert workspace.take_token() == "secret123"
    import os
    assert "TARGET_GITHUB_TOKEN" not in os.environ
    env = workspace.git_auth_env()
    assert "secret123" not in json.dumps(env) and env["GIT_CONFIG_KEY_0"].endswith(".extraheader")


# ---------- config in external mode
def test_target_settings_external_applies_overrides(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(config, "OUT", tmp_path)
    (tmp_path / "target.json").write_text(json.dumps({"full_name": "org/web"}))
    monkeypatch.setitem(config.settings(), "target", {
        "mode": "external", "app_dir": "sample_app", "app_package": "app", "test_dir": "tests",
        "architecture_file": "a.md",
        "overrides": {"org/web": {"language": "node", "build_commands": ["npm test"], "test_dir": ""}}})
    t = config.target_settings()
    assert t["app_dir"] == "." and t["language"] == "node" and t["build_commands"] == ["npm test"]
    assert t["test_dir"] == "tests"                      # empty override values are ignored
    assert config.work_root() == config.TARGET_CHECKOUT


# ---------- build commands
def test_run_build_with_commands(tmp_path):
    ok, log = build.run_build(tmp_path, "tests", ["echo hello", "exit 3", "echo never"])
    assert ok is False and "hello" in log and "exit 3" in log and "never" not in log
    assert build.run_build(tmp_path, "tests", ["echo ok"])[0] is True
    assert "npm test" in build.build_check_text({"app_dir": ".", "test_dir": "t", "build_commands": ["npm test"]})


# ---------- checkout of the target repo (real git, local "remote")
def test_prepare_checkout_and_patch_in_target(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    def run(*args, cwd):
        return subprocess.run(["git", *args], cwd=cwd, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()

    src = tmp_path / "src"
    src.mkdir()
    run("init", "-q", cwd=src)
    run("config", "user.email", "t@example.com", cwd=src)
    run("config", "user.name", "t", cwd=src)
    (src / "a.py").write_text("x = 1\n")
    run("add", ".", cwd=src)
    run("commit", "-qm", "init", cwd=src)
    sha = run("rev-parse", "HEAD", cwd=src)
    remote = tmp_path / "server" / "org" / "web.git"
    remote.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
    run("clone", "-q", "--bare", str(src), str(remote), cwd=tmp_path)
    run("config", "uploadpack.allowAnySHA1InWant", "true", cwd=remote)

    checkout = tmp_path / "root" / ".target"
    monkeypatch.setattr(workspace, "TARGET_CHECKOUT", checkout)
    monkeypatch.setattr(gitops, "TARGET_CHECKOUT", checkout)
    monkeypatch.setattr(workspace, "ROOT", config.ROOT)
    monkeypatch.setattr(workspace, "_token", "")
    monkeypatch.setitem(config.settings()["target"], "mode", "external")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GITHUB_SERVER_URL", f"file://{tmp_path / 'server'}")

    def _git(*args, cwd=checkout, auth=False):
        return run(*args, cwd=cwd)
    monkeypatch.setattr(workspace, "_git", _git)

    workspace.prepare_checkout({"full_name": "org/web", "base_sha": sha, "base_branch": "main"})
    assert (checkout / "a.py").read_text() == "x = 1\n"
    assert gitops.head_sha() == sha
    # agent skills are copied in but never become part of the change set
    assert (checkout / ".claude" / "skills").exists()
    (checkout / "a.py").write_text("x = 2\n")
    patch = gitops.snapshot_patch(sha)
    assert b"a.py" in patch and b".claude" not in patch
    workspace.prepare_checkout({"full_name": "org/web", "base_sha": sha, "base_branch": "main"})  # idempotent


def test_prepare_full_clone_has_history_and_branches(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """Step 7 scans from a regular full clone: full history (not shallow) and remote-tracking
    refs for every branch, so the Sonar scanner can diff the PR against its base."""
    def run(*args, cwd):
        return subprocess.run(["git", *args], cwd=cwd, check=True, capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()

    src = tmp_path / "src"
    src.mkdir()
    run("init", "-q", "-b", "main", cwd=src)
    run("config", "user.email", "[REDACTED_EMAIL_ADDRESS_1]", cwd=src)
    run("config", "user.name", "t", cwd=src)
    (src / "a.py").write_text("x = 1\n")
    run("add", ".", cwd=src)
    run("commit", "-qm", "init", cwd=src)
    run("checkout", "-qb", "feature/x", cwd=src)
    (src / "a.py").write_text("x = 2\n")
    run("commit", "-qam", "change", cwd=src)
    remote = tmp_path / "server" / "org" / "web.git"
    remote.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
    run("clone", "-q", "--bare", str(src), str(remote), cwd=tmp_path)

    checkout = tmp_path / "root" / ".target"
    checkout.mkdir(parents=True)
    (checkout / "stale.txt").write_text("old shallow checkout")
    monkeypatch.setattr(workspace, "TARGET_CHECKOUT", checkout)
    monkeypatch.setattr(workspace, "_token", "")
    monkeypatch.setitem(config.settings()["target"], "mode", "external")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GITHUB_SERVER_URL", f"file://{tmp_path / 'server'}")

    workspace.prepare_full_clone({"full_name": "org/web", "base_sha": "", "base_branch": "main"})
    assert not (checkout / "stale.txt").exists()
    assert run("rev-parse", "--is-shallow-repository", cwd=checkout) == "false"
    refs = run("branch", "-r", cwd=checkout)
    assert "origin/main" in refs and "origin/feature/x" in refs
    assert "a.py" in run("diff", "--name-only", "origin/main...origin/feature/x", cwd=checkout)
    assert "@" not in run("remote", "get-url", "origin", cwd=checkout)  # no credentials in the URL

FILE: tests/test_dev_pipe_followups.py

"""Tests for the 'agent stopped early' follow-up loop in dev_pipe/agent_runner.py. Fake agent, no LLM."""
from __future__ import annotations

import asyncio

import pytest
from claude_agent_sdk import ResultMessage

from dev_pipe import agent_runner as ar

PLAN = ("## Summary\nAdd delete.\n\n```json\n"
        '{"branch": "feature/1-x", "files": [{"path": "a.ts", "action": "modify", "reason": "r"}]}\n```')
STALL = "I need to examine HistoryModal.tsx. I'll read:\n- frontend/src/components/runs/HistoryModal.tsx"


class FakeClient:
    """Answers each query with the next scripted final message."""

    def __init__(self, answers, on_query=None):
        self.answers = list(answers)
        self.queries: list[str] = []
        self.on_query = on_query

    async def query(self, prompt):
        self.queries.append(prompt)
        if self.on_query:
            self.on_query(len(self.queries))

    async def receive_response(self):
        answer = self.answers.pop(0)
        if answer is MAX_TURNS:  # the SDK's result when agent.max_turns is used up
            yield ResultMessage(subtype="error_max_turns", duration_ms=1, duration_api_ms=1, is_error=True,
                                num_turns=150, session_id="s", result=None)
            return
        yield ResultMessage(subtype="success", duration_ms=1, duration_api_ms=1, is_error=False, num_turns=1,
                            session_id="s", result=answer)


MAX_TURNS = object()


def converse(client, check, max_followups=2, on_incomplete="fail"):
    return asyncio.run(ar._converse(client, ["make a plan"], check, max_followups, on_incomplete))


def test_plan_stall_gets_followup_in_same_conversation(capsys):
    client = FakeClient([STALL, PLAN])
    assert converse(client, ar.plan_followup) == PLAN
    assert len(client.queries) == 2 and "COMPLETE development plan" in client.queries[1]
    assert "asking it to continue (1/2)" in capsys.readouterr().out


def test_plan_fails_clearly_after_max_followups():
    client = FakeClient([STALL, STALL, STALL])
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match=r"stopped 3 time\(s\) without finishing .*AZURE_OPENAI_DEPLOYMENT"):
        converse(client, ar.plan_followup)
    assert len(client.queries) == 3


def test_complete_answer_needs_no_followup():
    client = FakeClient([PLAN])
    assert converse(client, ar.plan_followup) == PLAN and len(client.queries) == 1


def test_no_check_keeps_old_behaviour():
    client = FakeClient([STALL])
    assert converse(client, None) == STALL and len(client.queries) == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize("text,reason", [
    ("no block", "no ```json metadata block"),
    ("```json\n{\"files\": [}\n```", "not valid JSON"),
    ("```json\n{\"files\": []}\n```", "no `files` list"),
])
def test_plan_followup_reasons(text, reason):
    assert reason in ar.plan_followup(text).splitlines()[0]


def test_changes_followup_until_files_change(capsys):
    changed: list[str] = []
    client = FakeClient(["I'll edit HistoryModal.tsx next.", "Done: edited a.ts"],
                        on_query=lambda n: changed.append("frontend/a.ts") if n == 2 else None)
    out = converse(client, ar.changes_followup(lambda: changed))
    assert out == "Done: edited a.ts" and "Edit/Write" in client.queries[1]


def test_tests_followup_warn_mode_does_not_fail(capsys):
    client = FakeClient(["will write tests", "still nothing", "no test setup in this repo"])
    out = converse(client, ar.tests_followup(lambda: ["frontend/src/a.ts"]), on_incomplete="warn")
    assert out == "no test setup in this repo" and "WARNING" in capsys.readouterr().out


@pytest.mark.parametrize("path,is_test", [
    ("tests/test_main.py", True), ("sample_app/tests/x.py", True), ("app/test_util.py", True),
    ("pkg/foo_test.go", True), ("src/a.test.tsx", True), ("src/__tests__/a.ts", True),
    ("src/test/java/AppTest.java", True), ("App.Tests/UnitTests.cs", True),
    ("src/App.tsx", False), ("src/contest.java", False), ("frontend/src/services/runs.service.ts", False),
    ("latest/main.py", False),
])
def test_is_test_path(path, is_test):
    assert ar.is_test_path(path) is is_test


# ---------- per-step skills
def test_skills_for_step(monkeypatch):
    agent = {"skills": ["coverage-95-gate", "test-quality-review", "sonar-zero-issues"],
             "step_skills": {"plan": [], "implement": ["sonar-zero-issues", "not-installed"]}}
    monkeypatch.setitem(ar.settings(), "agent", agent)
    assert ar.skills_for("plan") == []
    assert ar.skills_for("implement") == ["sonar-zero-issues"]          # unknown names are dropped
    assert ar.skills_for("fix_gates") == agent["skills"]               # no entry: all installed skills
    assert ar.skills_for(None) == agent["skills"]


# ---------- Step 3: build errors and questions are sent back to the agent
@pytest.mark.parametrize("text,asks", [
    ("Please let me know if you'd like me to proceed with that refactor.", True),
    ("Would you like me to move TrashIcon into its own file?", True),
    ("Should I also update the backend?", True),
    ("Files changed: a.ts (added deleteRun). Build passes.", False),
    ("Check manually: the confirmation text.", False),
])
def test_question_followup(text, asks):
    assert (ar.question_followup(text) is not None) is asks


def test_first_of_returns_first_problem():
    check = ar.first_of(lambda t: None, lambda t: "second\nmsg", lambda t: "third")
    assert check("x") == "second\nmsg" and ar.first_of(lambda t: None)("x") is None


def test_step4_build_errors_are_fed_back_until_fixed(tmp_path, monkeypatch, capsys):
    """The failing run: the agent left a broken file. The pipeline builds, sends the errors back, the agent
    fixes the file on the follow-up, the build passes."""
    from dev_pipe.steps import s4_autonomous_development as s4
    src = tmp_path / "app.py"
    src.write_text("def f(:\n")                                    # broken, like the duplicated Icons.tsx
    monkeypatch.setattr(s4, "app_dir", lambda: tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(s4, "work_root", lambda: tmp_path)
    t = {"app_dir": ".", "test_dir": "tests", "language": "python", "build_commands": []}
    fix = lambda n: src.write_text("def f():\n    return 1\n") if n == 2 else None
    client = FakeClient(["Done.", "Fixed the syntax error."], on_query=fix)
    check = ar.first_of(ar.changes_followup(lambda: ["app.py"]), s4.build_followup(t, lambda: ["app.py"]),
                        ar.question_followup)
    out = converse(client, check, max_followups=3, on_incomplete="warn")
    assert out == "Fixed the syntax error." and len(client.queries) == 2
    assert "build check on your changes and it FAILED" in client.queries[1] and "app.py" in client.queries[1]
    assert "Re-read each affected file" in client.queries[1]
    assert "(the build failed); asking it to continue (1/3)" in capsys.readouterr().out


def test_step4_still_failing_warns_instead_of_raising(tmp_path, monkeypatch, capsys):
    from dev_pipe.steps import s4_autonomous_development as s4
    (tmp_path / "app.py").write_text("def f(:\n")

    monkeypatch.setattr(s4, "work_root", lambda: tmp_path)
    t = {"app_dir": ".", "test_dir": "tests", "language": "python", "build_commands": []}
    client = FakeClient(["a", "b", "c"])
    out = converse(client, s4.build_followup(t, lambda: ["app.py"]), max_followups=2, on_incomplete="warn")
    assert out == "c" and "WARNING" in capsys.readouterr().out

    client = FakeClient([MAX_TURNS, PLAN])
    assert converse(client, ar.plan_followup) == PLAN
    assert len(client.queries) == 2 and "ran out of turns" in client.queries[1]
    assert "reached the turn limit" in capsys.readouterr().out


def test_max_turns_every_time_fails_after_max_followups():

FILE: tests/test_dev_pipe_isolation.py

"""Security: secrets hidden from the AI, pipeline process unreadable, no network for the AI's shell (install
first), no stored git token in AI jobs, and the leak check before anything is pushed. No network."""
from __future__ import annotations

import base64
import os
import pathlib
import platform
import subprocess
import sys
import textwrap

import pytest
import yaml

from dev_pipe import agent_isolation as iso
from dev_pipe import agent_runner, build, gitops, leak_check
from dev_pipe.stack import Command, Project


# ---------- 1. secrets out of the AI's environment
def test_secret_names():
    for name in ("ADO_PAT", "GH_PAT", "SONAR_TOKEN", "AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY", "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "GITHUB_TOKEN",
                 "TARGET_GITHUB_TOKEN", "LOCAL_GIT_TOKEN", "ACTIONS_ID_TOKEN_REQUEST_TOKEN", "NPM_TOKEN"):
        assert iso.is_secret_name(name), name
    for name in ("PATH", "HOME", "ADO_ORG_URL", "SONAR_HOST_URL", "AZURE_OPENAI_ENDPOINT", "WORK_ITEM_ID"):
        assert not iso.is_secret_name(name), name


def test_hidden_while_inside_and_restored_after(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("ADO_PAT", "ado-secret-value-123")
    monkeypatch.setenv("NPM_TOKEN", "npm-secret-value-123")
    with iso.hidden_secrets():
        assert "ADO_PAT" not in os.environ and "NPM_TOKEN" not in os.environ
        assert iso.held("NPM_TOKEN") == "npm-secret-value-123"
        # a child process (like the AI and its shell) does not get them
        out = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-c", "import os; print(os.environ.get('ADO_PAT', 'none'))"],
                             capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
        assert out == "none"
        # the pipeline's own build checks still get the private-registry token
        assert build._build_env().get("NPM_TOKEN") == "npm-secret-value-123"
        assert "ADO_PAT" not in build._build_env()
    assert os.environ["ADO_PAT"] == "ado-secret-value-123" and iso.held("NPM_TOKEN") == ""


def test_run_agent_starts_the_ai_without_secrets(monkeypatch):
    from contextlib import contextmanager
    monkeypatch.setenv("ADO_PAT", "ado-secret-value-123")
    monkeypatch.setenv("SONAR_TOKEN", "sonar-secret-value-123")
    seen = {}

    @contextmanager
    def backend():
        yield "model", {}
    monkeypatch.setattr(agent_runner, "_backend_env", backend)
    monkeypatch.setattr(iso, "harden_process", lambda: True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(iso, "sandbox_options", lambda cfg, edit: {"enabled": True})
    monkeypatch.setattr(build, "install_for_agent", lambda: seen.setdefault("installed", True))

    async def fake_run(prompts, system_prompt, edit, model, env, check, max_followups, on_incomplete, step, sandbox):
        seen.update(ado=os.environ.get("ADO_PAT"), sonar=os.environ.get("SONAR_TOKEN"), sandbox=sandbox)
        return "done"
    monkeypatch.setattr(agent_runner, "_run", fake_run)
    assert agent_runner.run_agent("p", "s", edit=True) == "done"
    assert seen == {"installed": True, "ado": None, "sonar": None, "sandbox": {"enabled": True}}
    assert os.environ["ADO_PAT"] == "ado-secret-value-123"   # back for the pipeline's own ADO/Sonar calls


def test_proc_is_blocked_for_the_ai_tools():
    assert "Read(//proc/**)" in agent_runner.BLOCKED_TOOLS


@pytest.mark.skipif(platform.system() != "Linux", reason="prctl is Linux only")
def test_hardened_process_environment_is_unreadable_to_children(tmp_path):
    """A non-dumpable pipeline process: its child (the AI) cannot read /proc/<pipeline pid>/environ."""
    script = textwrap.dedent("""
        import os, subprocess, sys
        from dev_pipe import agent_isolation as iso
        os.environ["ADO_PAT"] = "x"
        print("hardened", iso.harden_process())
        child = "import sys; open('/proc/%d/environ' % int(sys.argv[1]), 'rb').read(); print('READABLE')"
        r = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-c", child, str(os.getpid())], capture_output=True, text=True)
        print("child:", r.stdout.strip() or ("blocked" if "Permission" in r.stderr else r.stderr.strip()[-80:]))
    """)
    out = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-c", script], capture_output=True, text=True,
                         cwd=pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent).stdout
    assert "hardened True" in out and "child: blocked" in out, out


# ---------- 2. no network for the AI's shell
def test_sandbox_settings(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(iso, "sandbox_available", lambda: (True, "available"))
    opts = iso.sandbox_options({"sandbox": "required"}, edit=True)
    assert opts["enabled"] and opts["allowUnsandboxedCommands"] is False and opts["network"]["allowedDomains"] == []
    assert iso.sandbox_options({"sandbox": "required"}, edit=False) is None     # read-only agent: no shell
    assert iso.sandbox_options({"sandbox": "off"}, edit=True) is None


def test_required_sandbox_never_silently_skipped(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(iso, "sandbox_available", lambda: (False, "missing bwrap"))
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="required but not available"):
        iso.sandbox_options({"sandbox": "required"}, edit=True)
    assert iso.sandbox_options({"sandbox": "best_effort"}, edit=True) is None


def test_install_first_and_offline_commands_for_the_ai():
    node = Project(pathlib.Path("/x"), "node", [Command("npm ci", install=True), Command("npm run build"),
                                                Command("npm run test")])
    assert [c.cmd for c in node.agent_commands()] == ["npm run build", "npm run test"]
    assert [c.cmd for c in node.install_commands()] == ["npm ci"]
    from dev_pipe.stack import _maven_project
    mvn = _maven_project(pathlib.Path("/repo"))
    assert [c.cmd for c in mvn.agent_commands()] == ["mvn -o -B -q verify"]
    assert "dependency:go-offline" in mvn.install_commands()[0].cmd


def test_install_for_agent_runs_install_commands_once(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    unit = build.BuildUnit("app (node)", tmp_path, [], [], [Command("npm ci", install=True)])
    runs = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(build, "_INSTALLED", False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(build, "plan_build", lambda t, a, changed=None: build.BuildPlan([unit]))
    monkeypatch.setattr(build, "_run_commands", lambda cwd, cmds: runs.append([c.cmd for c in cmds]) or (True, ""))
    monkeypatch.setattr("dev_pipe.config.target_settings", lambda: {"app_dir": "app"})
    monkeypatch.setattr("dev_pipe.config.app_dir", lambda: tmp_path)
    build.install_for_agent()
    build.install_for_agent()
    assert runs == [["npm ci"]]


# ---------- 3. no stored git token in AI jobs; Step 7 passes it per command
def test_local_mode_remote_commands_get_a_per_command_token(monkeypatch):
    from dev_pipe import workspace
    monkeypatch.setattr(gitops, "is_external", lambda: False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(workspace, "_local_token", None)
    monkeypatch.setenv("LOCAL_GIT_TOKEN", "local-token-123")
    env = gitops._env(("push", "origin", "b"))
    assert "LOCAL_GIT_TOKEN" not in os.environ   # taken out of the environment on first use
    expected = base64.b64encode(b"x-access-token:local-token-123").decode()
    assert env["GIT_CONFIG_VALUE_0"].endswith(expected)
    assert gitops._env(("status",)) is None


def test_workflow_ai_jobs_have_no_stored_token_and_use_exec():
    wf = yaml.safe_load(pathlib.Path(".github/workflows/dev-pipe.yml").read_text())["jobs"]
    for job in ("plan", "implement", "test-gates", "sonar-precheck", "quality-gate"):
        steps = wf[job]["steps"]
        checkout = next(s for s in steps if s.get("uses") == "actions/checkout@v4")
        assert checkout["with"]["persist-credentials"] is False, job
        assert any(str(s.get("run", "")).startswith("exec python -m dev_pipe.steps.") for s in steps), job
    step7 = next(s for s in wf["quality-gate"]["steps"] if "s8_sonarqube" in str(s.get("run", "")))
    assert "LOCAL_GIT_TOKEN" in step7["env"]


# ---------- 4. leak check before anything leaves the runner
GH = "ghp_" + "A" * 36


def patch_with(added: str, removed: str = "old line") -> str:
    return ("diff --git a/app/x.py b/app/x.py\n--- a/app/x.py\n+++ b/app/x.py\n@@ -10,2 +10,3 @@\n"
            f" context\n-{removed}\n+{added}\n+print('ok')\n")


def test_finds_token_on_added_line_with_line_number():
    found = leak_check.scan_patch(patch_with(f"TOKEN = '{GH}'"), known={})
    assert [str(f) for f in found] == ["app/x.py, line 11: GitHub token"]


def test_removed_lines_and_normal_code_are_not_flagged():
    assert leak_check.scan_patch(patch_with("x = compute_task_status(order)", removed=f"T='{GH}'"), known={}) == []


def test_finds_known_secret_values_even_base64(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("ADO_PAT", "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz012345")
    b64 = base64.b64encode(b"abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz012345").decode()
    kinds = [f.kind for f in leak_check.scan_text(f"note: {b64}", "notes")]
    assert kinds == ["the value of ADO_PAT (base64)"]


def test_enforce_blocks_or_warns(monkeypatch, capsys):
    findings = [leak_check.Finding("app/x.py, line 3", "GitHub token")]
    monkeypatch.setattr(leak_check, "mode", lambda: "block")
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="Nothing was pushed"):
        leak_check.enforce(findings, "the change")
    monkeypatch.setattr(leak_check, "mode", lambda: "warn")
    leak_check.enforce(findings, "the change")
    assert "WARNING" in capsys.readouterr().out
    leak_check.enforce([], "the change")   # nothing found: nothing happens


def test_prompt_treats_story_and_repo_as_data():
    system = pathlib.Path("prompts/dev/system.md").read_text()
    assert "DATA" in system and "Suspicious content" in system

FILE: tests/test_dev_pipe_rerun.py

"""Step 6 re-run in external mode: the target repo is checked out with only its latest commit (shallow).
Real git repos in a temp folder (no network)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import shutil
import subprocess

import pytest

from dev_pipe import gitops

pytestmark = pytest.mark.skipif(shutil.which("git") is None, reason="git not installed")

BOT = "bot@example.com"
DEV = "dev@example.com"


def run(cwd, *args, email=DEV):
    return subprocess.run(["git", "-c", f"user.email={email}", "-c", "user.name=x", *args], cwd=cwd,
                          check=True, capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()


@pytest.fixture
def remote(tmp_path):
    """Bare remote with main (3 developer commits) and an earlier pipeline branch feature/1-x = main + 1 bot commit;
    then main moves on by one more developer commit."""
    bare = tmp_path / "remote.git"
    run(tmp_path, "init", "-q", "--bare", "-b", "main", str(bare))
    dev = tmp_path / "dev"
    run(tmp_path, "clone", "-q", str(bare), str(dev))
    for i in range(3):
        (dev / f"f{i}").write_text(str(i))
        run(dev, "add", ".")
        run(dev, "commit", "-qm", f"c{i}")
    run(dev, "push", "-q", "origin", "HEAD:main")
    run(dev, "checkout", "-qb", "feature/1-x")
    run(dev, "commit", "-q", "--allow-empty", "-m", "pipeline run 1", email=BOT)
    run(dev, "push", "-q", "origin", "feature/1-x")
    run(dev, "checkout", "-q", "main")
    (dev / "f3").write_text("3")
    run(dev, "add", ".")
    run(dev, "commit", "-qm", "c3")
    run(dev, "push", "-q", "origin", "main")
    return bare, dev


def shallow_rerun(tmp_path, bare, monkeypatch):
    """What Step 6 does in external mode: depth-1 checkout of the (moved) base, new pipeline commit on the branch."""
    work = tmp_path / "target-checkout"
    work.mkdir()
    run(work, "init", "-q")
    run(work, "remote", "add", "origin", f"file://{bare}")
    run(work, "fetch", "-q", "--depth", "1", "origin", "main")
    run(work, "checkout", "-q", "--detach", "FETCH_HEAD")
    assert run(work, "rev-parse", "--is-shallow-repository") == "true"
    run(work, "config", "user.email", BOT)
    run(work, "checkout", "-qb", "feature/1-x")
    run(work, "commit", "-q", "--allow-empty", "-m", "pipeline run 2", email=BOT)
    monkeypatch.setattr(gitops, "_cwd", lambda: work)
    monkeypatch.setattr(gitops, "is_external", lambda: False)   # no token env needed for a local remote
    return work


def test_rerun_after_base_moved_replaces_the_pipeline_branch(tmp_path, remote, monkeypatch):
    bare, _ = remote
    work = shallow_rerun(tmp_path, bare, monkeypatch)
    assert gitops.push_branch("feature/1-x", "main") is True
    assert run(bare, "rev-parse", "feature/1-x") == run(work, "rev-parse", "HEAD")


def test_developer_commit_on_the_branch_still_blocks(tmp_path, remote, monkeypatch):
    bare, dev = remote
    run(dev, "fetch", "-q", "origin")
    run(dev, "checkout", "-q", "-b", "mine", "origin/feature/1-x")
    run(dev, "commit", "-q", "--allow-empty", "-m", "developer work")
    run(dev, "push", "-q", "origin", "HEAD:feature/1-x")
    shallow_rerun(tmp_path, bare, monkeypatch)
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match="Not overwriting"):
        gitops.push_branch("feature/1-x", "main")

FILE: tests/test_dev_pipe_reuse.py

"""Not doing the same work twice: coverage hand-over (Steps 4 -> 5 -> 7), Step 3's build reuse, Python packages
installed once per job, parallel repo search with a branch cap. No network."""
from __future__ import annotations

import os
import time

import pytest

from dev_pipe import build, coverage_handover as ch, resolver
from dev_pipe.build import BuildResult
from dev_pipe.github_api import GitHub, GitHubError
from dev_pipe.steps import s4_autonomous_development as s3
from dev_pipe.steps import s6_build_test_coverage as s4


# ---------- coverage hand-over
@pytest.fixture
def repo(tmp_path):
    root = tmp_path / "repo"
    app = root / "app"
    (app / "coverage").mkdir(parents=True)
    (app / "coverage.xml").write_text("<coverage line-rate='0.9'/>")
    (app / "coverage" / "lcov.info").write_text("SF:a.js\nLF:10\nLH:9\nend_of_record\n")
    out = tmp_path / "out"
    return root, app, out


def test_save_and_restore_same_code(repo):
    root, app, out = repo
    fp = ch.code_fingerprint("abc", b"patch")
    assert ch.save(app, root, fp, newer_than=None, out=out) == 2
    for f in (app / "coverage.xml", app / "coverage" / "lcov.info"):
        f.unlink()
    before = time.time()
    restored = ch.restore(root, fp, out=out)
    assert sorted(restored) == ["app/coverage.xml", "app/coverage/lcov.info"]
    assert (app / "coverage.xml").read_text() == "<coverage line-rate='0.9'/>"
    assert os.path.getmtime(app / "coverage.xml") >= before - 1   # counts as this run's report


def test_restore_refuses_other_code_or_missing_files(repo):
    root, app, out = repo
    ch.save(app, root, ch.code_fingerprint("abc", b"patch"), newer_than=None, out=out)
    assert ch.restore(root, ch.code_fingerprint("abc", b"other patch"), out=out) == []
    assert ch.restore(root, ch.code_fingerprint("def", b"patch"), out=out) == []
    (out / ch.FILES_DIR / "app" / "coverage.xml").unlink()
    assert ch.restore(root, ch.code_fingerprint("abc", b"patch"), out=out) == []


def test_stale_reports_are_not_handed_over(repo):
    root, app, out = repo
    old = time.time() - 3600
    for f in (app / "coverage.xml", app / "coverage" / "lcov.info"):
        os.utime(f, (old, old))
    assert ch.save(app, root, "fp", newer_than=time.time(), out=out) == 0
    assert not (out / ch.MANIFEST).exists()


def test_java_repos_never_reuse(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "src").mkdir()
    (tmp_path / "src" / "A.java").write_text("class A {}")
    assert ch.has_java(tmp_path) is True
    other = tmp_path / "py"
    other.mkdir()
    (other / "a.py").write_text("x = 1")
    assert ch.has_java(other) is False


def test_step5_first_run_reuses_and_only_scans(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setattr(s4, "target_settings", lambda: {"app_dir": "app", "test_dir": "tests", "app_package": "app"})
    monkeypatch.setattr(s4, "app_dir", lambda: tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(s4, "work_root", lambda: tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(s4.coverage_handover, "restore", lambda root, fp, out=None: ["app/coverage.xml"])
    monkeypatch.setattr(s4, "sonar_gate", lambda base, started: (None, "passed", False))

    def boom(*a, **k):
        raise AssertionError("tests must not run again")
    monkeypatch.setattr(s4, "prepare_python", boom)
    monkeypatch.setattr(s4, "check_build", boom)
    previous = {"passed": True, "coverage_pct": 91.0, "tests": {"passed": 12, "failed": 0}, "history": [1]}
    failures, data = s4.run_gates(85, "base", with_sonar=True, reuse={"fingerprint": "fp", "previous": previous})
    assert failures == [] and data["coverage_reused"] is True and data["coverage_pct"] == 91.0
    assert data["sonar_precheck"] == "passed" and "history" not in data and "passed" not in data


def test_step5_falls_back_when_files_not_reusable(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setattr(s4, "target_settings", lambda: {"app_dir": "app", "test_dir": "tests", "app_package": "app"})
    monkeypatch.setattr(s4, "app_dir", lambda: tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(s4, "work_root", lambda: tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(s4.coverage_handover, "restore", lambda root, fp, out=None: [])
    monkeypatch.setattr(s4, "is_python_target", lambda t, a: False)
    called = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(s4, "check_build", lambda *a, **k: called.append(1) or BuildResult(False, "boom"))
    failures, data = s4.run_gates(85, "", with_sonar=True, reuse={"fingerprint": "fp", "previous": {}})
    assert called == [1] and failures and "coverage_reused" not in data


# ---------- Step 3: last build check reused
def test_build_followup_records_result_with_fingerprint(monkeypatch):
    result = BuildResult(True, "$ pytest (exit 0)")
    monkeypatch.setattr(s3, "check_build", lambda *a, **k: result)
    monkeypatch.setattr(s3, "app_dir", lambda: "app")
    monkeypatch.setattr(s3, "work_root", lambda: __import__("pathlib").Path("."))
    last: dict = {}
    check = s3.build_followup({}, lambda: ["app/x.py"], lambda: "fp1", last)
    assert check("done") is None
    assert last["result"] is result and last["fp"] == "fp1" and last["seconds"] >= 0


# ---------- Python packages once per job
def test_prepare_python_installs_once_until_deps_change(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "requirements.txt").write_text("requests\n")
    runs = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(build, "python_env", lambda p: ("/venv/bin/python", ["pip install"]))
    monkeypatch.setattr(build, "_run_commands", lambda cwd, cmds: runs.append(1) or (True, "ok"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(build, "_PREPARED", {})
    assert build.prepare_python(tmp_path)[:2] == (True, "/venv/bin/python")
    assert build.prepare_python(tmp_path)[:2] == (True, "/venv/bin/python")
    assert len(runs) == 1
    (tmp_path / "requirements.txt").write_text("requests\nflask\n")   # the AI added a dependency
    build.prepare_python(tmp_path)
    assert len(runs) == 2


def test_failed_install_is_retried(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "requirements.txt").write_text("x\n")
    results = iter([(False, "err"), (True, "ok")])
    monkeypatch.setattr(build, "python_env", lambda p: ("py", ["pip install"]))
    monkeypatch.setattr(build, "_run_commands", lambda cwd, cmds: next(results))
    monkeypatch.setattr(build, "_PREPARED", {})
    assert build.prepare_python(tmp_path)[0] is False
    assert build.prepare_python(tmp_path)[0] is True


# ---------- resolver: branch cap, newest first, parallel
class GH:
    def __init__(self, n=10, bulk=True):
        self.n, self.bulk_ok = n, bulk
        self.single_calls = 0

    def open_pulls(self, full):
        return []

    def branches_with_dates(self, full):
        if not self.bulk_ok:
            raise GitHubError("graphql off")
        return [{"name": f"b{i}", "sha": f"s{i}", "date": f"2026-01-{i + 1:02d}T00:00:00Z"} for i in range(self.n)] + \
               [{"name": "main", "sha": "m", "date": "2026-02-01T00:00:00Z"}]

    def list_branches(self, full, limit=100):
        return ["main"] + [f"b{i}" for i in range(self.n)]

    def get_branch(self, full, name):
        self.single_calls += 1
        return {"name": name, "sha": "s", "date": "2026-02-01T00:00:00Z" if name == "main" else
                f"2026-01-{int(name[1:]) + 1:02d}T00:00:00Z"}


BASE = {"full_name": "org/r", "default_branch": "main", "branch": "main"}


def test_branch_cap_keeps_newest_and_skips_base():
    r = resolver.Resolver(GH(10), None, {"max_branches_scanned": 3}, log=lambda *_: None)
    names = [c["branch"] for c in r.scan_branches(BASE, set())]
    assert names == ["b9", "b8", "b7"]


def test_no_cap_means_all_branches():
    r = resolver.Resolver(GH(10), None, {"max_branches_scanned": 0}, log=lambda *_: None)
    assert len(r.scan_branches(BASE, set())) == 10


def test_falls_back_to_one_call_per_branch_in_parallel():
    gh = GH(10, bulk=False)
    r = resolver.Resolver(gh, None, {"max_branches_scanned": 2, "parallel_requests": 4}, log=lambda *_: None)
    assert [c["branch"] for c in r.scan_branches(BASE, set())] == ["b9", "b8"]
    assert gh.single_calls == 11


def test_parallel_keeps_order():
    r = resolver.Resolver(GH(), None, {"parallel_requests": 5}, log=lambda *_: None)
    assert r._parallel(lambda x: x * 2, list(range(20))) == [x * 2 for x in range(20)]


# ---------- GitHub GraphQL branch listing
class Resp:
    def __init__(self, body, status=200):
        self.body, self.status_code, self.content, self.text = body, status, b"x", str(body)

    def json(self):
        return self.body


class Session:
    def __init__(self, pages):
        self.pages, self.headers, self.posts = list(pages), {}, []

    def post(self, url, json=None, timeout=None):
        self.posts.append((url, json["variables"]["after"]))
        return self.pages.pop(0)


def page(names, more, cursor=None):
    return Resp({"data": {"repository": {"refs": {
        "pageInfo": {"hasNextPage": more, "endCursor": cursor},
        "nodes": [{"name": n, "target": {"oid": f"sha-{n}", "committedDate": "2026-01-01T00:00:00Z"}} for n in names]}}}})


def test_branches_with_dates_pages_through_graphql():
    s = Session([page(["a", "b"], True, "c1"), page(["c"], False)])
    gh = GitHub("t", api="https://api.github.com", session=s)
    out = gh.branches_with_dates("org/r")
    assert [b["name"] for b in out] == ["a", "b", "c"] and out[0]["sha"] == "sha-a"
    assert s.posts == [("https://api.github.com/graphql", None), ("https://api.github.com/graphql", "c1")]


def test_branches_with_dates_errors_raise():
    gh = GitHub("t", api="https://ghe.example.com/api/v3", session=Session([Resp({"errors": [{"message": "x"}]})]))
    with pytest.raises(GitHubError):
        gh.branches_with_dates("org/r")

FILE: tests/test_dev_pipe_setup.py

"""Workflow/setup optimisations: concurrency per story, bundled Claude CLI, cached Sonar scanner, Node only where
needed, Step 5's early Sonar check, uploads on failure. No network."""
from __future__ import annotations

import pathlib

import pytest
import yaml

from dev_pipe import config
from dev_pipe.steps import sonar_needed as sn

WF = pathlib.Path(".github/workflows/dev-pipe.yml")
ACTION = pathlib.Path(".github/actions/setup-dev-pipe/action.yml")


@pytest.fixture
def sonar_cfg(monkeypatch):
    def set_cfg(enabled: bool, required: bool, token: bool = True, host: str = "https://sonarcloud.io",
                key: str = "proj"):
        monkeypatch.setattr(config, "_settings", {"sonar": {"enabled": enabled, "precheck": {"required": required}},
                                                  "target": {"mode": "local"}})
        monkeypatch.setenv("SONAR_TOKEN_PRESENT", "true" if token else "false")
        monkeypatch.delenv("SONAR_TOKEN", raising=False)
        monkeypatch.setenv("SONAR_HOST_URL", host)
        monkeypatch.setenv("SONAR_PROJECT_KEY", key)
        monkeypatch.delenv("TARGET_MODE", raising=False)
    return set_cfg


# ---------- Step 5 early check
def test_sonar_enabled_needs_full_setup(sonar_cfg):
    sonar_cfg(enabled=True, required=True)
    assert sn.needed()[0] is True


def test_sonar_off_and_optional_skips_setup(sonar_cfg):
    sonar_cfg(enabled=False, required=False)
    assert sn.needed()[0] is False


def test_sonar_off_but_required_still_runs_the_step(sonar_cfg):
    sonar_cfg(enabled=False, required=True)
    assert sn.needed()[0] is True


def test_missing_token_and_optional_skips(sonar_cfg):
    sonar_cfg(enabled=True, required=False, token=False)
    assert sn.needed()[0] is False


def test_any_error_means_full_setup(monkeypatch):
    def boom():
        raise RuntimeError("no yaml")
    monkeypatch.setattr(config, "settings", boom)
    assert sn.needed()[0] is True


def test_output_written(sonar_cfg, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    sonar_cfg(enabled=False, required=False)
    out = tmp_path / "out.txt"
    monkeypatch.setenv("GITHUB_OUTPUT", str(out))
    sn.main()
    assert out.read_text() == "needed=false\n"


# ---------- workflow wiring
def jobs():
    return yaml.safe_load(WF.read_text())["jobs"]


def setup_inputs(job: dict) -> dict:
    return next(s["with"] for s in job["steps"] if s.get("uses") == "./.github/actions/setup-dev-pipe")


def test_concurrency_is_per_story():
    wf = yaml.safe_load(WF.read_text())
    assert "work_item_id" in wf["concurrency"]["group"] and "TEST_WORK_ITEM_ID" in wf["concurrency"]["group"]


def test_node_only_in_jobs_that_build_or_scan():
    j = jobs()
    for name in ("implement", "test-gates", "quality-gate"):
        assert setup_inputs(j[name]).get("node") == "true", name
    assert setup_inputs(j["sonar-precheck"]).get("node") == "${{ steps.sonar.outputs.needed }}"
    for name in ("plan", "create-pr", "ado-complete", "notify-failure"):
        assert "node" not in setup_inputs(j[name]), name


def test_step5_checks_before_setup():
    steps = jobs()["sonar-precheck"]["steps"]
    order = [s.get("id") or s.get("uses", "") for s in steps]
    assert order.index("sonar") < order.index("./.github/actions/setup-dev-pipe")
    check = next(s for s in steps if s.get("id") == "sonar")
    assert "secrets.SONAR_TOKEN" not in str(check.get("env", {})).replace("secrets.SONAR_TOKEN != ''", "")


def test_plan_and_create_pr_upload_on_failure():
    j = jobs()
    for name in ("plan", "create-pr"):
        upload = next(s for s in j[name]["steps"] if s.get("uses") == "./.github/actions/save-stage")
        assert upload.get("if") == "always()", name


def test_setup_uses_bundled_cli_and_cached_scanner():
    text = ACTION.read_text()
    steps = yaml.safe_load(text)["runs"]["steps"]
    npm = next(s for s in steps if "npm install" in s.get("run", ""))
    assert "bundled != 'true'" in npm["if"]                         # npm only as fallback
    assert "CLI_VERSION" in npm["run"]                              # pinned to the SDK's CLI version
    assert any(s.get("uses") == "actions/cache@v4" and "sonar-scanner" in s["with"]["key"] for s in steps)
    download = next(s for s in steps if "binaries.sonarsource.com" in s.get("run", ""))
    assert "cache-hit != 'true'" in download["if"]
    node = [s for s in steps if s.get("uses") == "actions/setup-node@v4"]
    assert all("if" in s for s in node)                             # never unconditional

FILE: tests/test_dev_pipe_stack.py

"""Tests for stack detection and the build check (dev_pipe/stack.py, dev_pipe/build.py). No network."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from dev_pipe import build, config, stack


def write(path: Path, text: str = "") -> Path:
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
    return path


def pkg(path: Path, scripts: dict, dev: dict | None = None, **extra) -> None:
    write(path / "package.json", json.dumps({"name": "x", "scripts": scripts, "devDependencies": dev or {}, **extra}))


def cmds(project) -> list[str]:
    return [c.cmd for c in project.commands]


T = {"app_dir": ".", "test_dir": "tests", "language": "auto", "build_commands": []}


# ---------- detection per stack
def test_react_frontend_without_tests_type_checks_and_warns(tmp_path):
    """The failing run: a Vite/React TS frontend with no tests. Must type-check, build, and warn (not fail)."""
    fe = tmp_path / "frontend"
    pkg(fe, {"build": "vite build", "dev": "vite"}, {"typescript": "^5.4.0", "vite": "^5"})
    write(fe / "package-lock.json", "{}")
    write(fe / "tsconfig.json", '{\n  // comment\n  "compilerOptions": {"strict": true,},\n}')
    write(fe / "src" / "App.tsx", "export {}")
    [p] = stack.detect_projects(tmp_path)
    assert p.stack == "node" and p.path == fe.resolve()
    assert cmds(p) == ["npm ci", "npx --no-install tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.json", "npm run build"]
    assert p.has_tests is False and "no `test` script" in p.warnings[0]


def test_node_solution_tsconfig_checks_referenced_configs(tmp_path):
    pkg(tmp_path, {"build": "vite build"}, {"typescript": "5"})
    write(tmp_path / "tsconfig.json", '{"files": [], "references": [{"path": "./tsconfig.app.json"}, {"path": "./tsconfig.node.json"}]}')
    write(tmp_path / "tsconfig.app.json", "{}")
    write(tmp_path / "tsconfig.node.json", "{}")
    [p] = stack.detect_projects(tmp_path)
    assert "npx --no-install tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.app.json" in cmds(p)
    assert "npx --no-install tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.node.json" in cmds(p)


def test_node_build_with_tsc_and_tests_and_lockfiles(tmp_path):
    pkg(tmp_path, {"build": "tsc -b && vite build", "test": "vitest run"}, {"typescript": "5"})
    write(tmp_path / "yarn.lock")
    write(tmp_path / "tsconfig.json", "{}")
    write(tmp_path / "src" / "a.test.ts")
    [p] = stack.detect_projects(tmp_path)
    assert cmds(p) == ["yarn install --frozen-lockfile", "yarn run build", "yarn run test"]  # tsc already in build
    assert p.has_tests and not p.warnings


def test_node_test_script_without_test_files_is_skipped(tmp_path):
    pkg(tmp_path, {"test": "jest"})
    write(tmp_path / "pnpm-lock.yaml")
    [p] = stack.detect_projects(tmp_path)
    assert cmds(p)[0] == "corepack pnpm install --frozen-lockfile"
    assert "corepack pnpm run test" not in cmds(p) and not p.has_tests


def test_node_typecheck_script_preferred_and_default_test_ignored(tmp_path):
    pkg(tmp_path, {"typecheck": "tsc --noEmit", "test": 'echo "Error: no test specified" && exit 1'}, {"typescript": "5"})
    write(tmp_path / "tsconfig.json", "{}")
    [p] = stack.detect_projects(tmp_path)
    assert cmds(p) == ["npm install --no-audit --no-fund", "npm run typecheck"]
    assert not p.has_tests


def test_python_maven_gradle_dotnet_go(tmp_path):
    write(tmp_path / "py" / "pyproject.toml")
    write(tmp_path / "py" / "tests" / "test_a.py")
    write(tmp_path / "jv" / "pom.xml")
    write(tmp_path / "jv" / "mvnw")
    write(tmp_path / "jv" / "src" / "test" / "java" / "ATest.java")
    write(tmp_path / "gr" / "build.gradle.kts")
    write(tmp_path / "net" / "App.sln")
    write(tmp_path / "net" / "App.Tests" / "App.Tests.csproj", "<PackageReference Include=\"Microsoft.NET.Test.Sdk\" />")
    write(tmp_path / "go" / "go.mod")
    by = {p.stack: p for p in stack.detect_projects(tmp_path)}
    assert set(by) == {"python", "maven", "gradle", "dotnet", "go"}
    assert any("pytest tests" in c for c in cmds(by["python"]))
    assert cmds(by["maven"]) == ["sh ./mvnw -B -q verify"] and by["maven"].has_tests
    assert cmds(by["gradle"]) == ["gradle build --no-daemon -q"] and not by["gradle"].has_tests
    assert cmds(by["dotnet"]) == ["dotnet build App.sln -nologo", "dotnet test App.sln --no-build -nologo"]
    assert "go test ./..." not in cmds(by["go"]) and not by["go"].has_tests


def test_python_without_tests_warns_instead_of_running_pytest(tmp_path):
    write(tmp_path / "requirements.txt")
    write(tmp_path / "app.py")
    [p] = stack.detect_projects(tmp_path)
    assert "compileall" in p.commands[-1].cmd and not any("pytest " in c.cmd and "pytest-cov" not in c.cmd
                                                          for c in p.commands)
    assert not p.has_tests


def test_plain_python_folder_like_sample_app(tmp_path):
    write(tmp_path / "app" / "main.py")
    write(tmp_path / "tests" / "test_main.py")
    [p] = stack.detect_projects(tmp_path)
    assert p.stack == "python" and p.path == tmp_path.resolve() and p.has_tests


def test_skips_dependency_folders_and_nested_modules(tmp_path):
    pkg(tmp_path / "fe", {"build": "x"})
    pkg(tmp_path / "fe" / "node_modules" / "dep", {"build": "x"})
    write(tmp_path / "jv" / "pom.xml")
    write(tmp_path / "jv" / "module-a" / "pom.xml")             # covered by the parent multi-module build
    pkg(tmp_path / "mono", {"build": "x"}, workspaces=["packages/*"])
    pkg(tmp_path / "mono" / "packages" / "ui", {"build": "x"})   # covered by the workspace root
    paths = sorted(p.rel(tmp_path) for p in stack.detect_projects(tmp_path))
    assert paths == ["fe", "jv", "mono"]


def test_separate_node_apps_are_separate_projects(tmp_path):
    pkg(tmp_path, {"build": "x"})
    pkg(tmp_path / "admin", {"build": "x"})
    assert sorted(p.rel(tmp_path) for p in stack.detect_projects(tmp_path)) == [".", "admin"]


def test_select_projects_by_changed_files(tmp_path):
    pkg(tmp_path / "frontend", {"build": "x"})
    write(tmp_path / "backend" / "pyproject.toml")
    projects = stack.detect_projects(tmp_path)
    sel, note = stack.select_projects(projects, [tmp_path / "frontend" / "src" / "a.tsx"])
    assert [p.stack for p in sel] == ["node"] and note == ""
    sel, note = stack.select_projects(projects, [tmp_path / "README.md"])
    assert len(sel) == 2 and "outside" in note


# ---------- build plan / precedence
def test_plan_uses_configured_commands_first(tmp_path):
    pkg(tmp_path, {"build": "x"})
    plan = build.plan_build({**T, "build_commands": ["make ci"]}, tmp_path)
    assert [c.cmd for c in plan.units[0].commands] == ["make ci"]


def test_plan_fails_clearly_when_nothing_detected(tmp_path):
    write(tmp_path / "README.md")
    plan = build.plan_build(T, tmp_path)
    assert not plan.units and "No build system detected" in plan.error
    result = build.check_build(T, tmp_path)
    assert result.ok is False and "No build system detected" in result.log


def test_language_limits_detection(tmp_path):
    pkg(tmp_path / "fe", {"build": "x"})
    write(tmp_path / "be" / "requirements.txt")
    plan = build.plan_build({**T, "language": "typescript"}, tmp_path)
    assert [u.label for u in plan.units] == ["fe (node)"]
    assert build.plan_build({**T, "language": "java"}, tmp_path).error


def test_is_python_target(tmp_path):
    write(tmp_path / "app" / "main.py")
    assert build.is_python_target(T, tmp_path)
    pkg(tmp_path / "web", {"build": "x"})
    assert not build.is_python_target(T, tmp_path)               # now a monorepo
    assert build.is_python_target({**T, "language": "python"}, tmp_path)
    assert not build.is_python_target({**T, "language": "python", "build_commands": ["x"]}, tmp_path)


def test_check_build_runs_detected_commands_and_reports_warnings(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    write(tmp_path / "go.mod")
    seen = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(build, "_run_commands", lambda cwd, cs: (seen.extend(c.cmd for c in cs), (True, "ok"))[1])
    result = build.check_build(T, tmp_path)
    assert result.ok and seen == ["go build ./...", "go vet ./..."]
    assert result.projects == [". (go)"] and "no *_test.go" in result.warnings[0]
    assert "WARNING" in result.report()


def test_check_build_real_python_project_without_tests_passes(tmp_path):
    write(tmp_path / "requirements.txt")
    write(tmp_path / "app.py", "x = 1\n")
    result = build.check_build(T, tmp_path)
    assert result.ok and result.warnings


def test_run_build_python_missing_test_dir_is_not_a_failure(tmp_path):
    """Regression: `pytest tests` on a repo without tests/ used to exit 4 and fail the step."""
    write(tmp_path / "app.py", "x = 1\n")
    ok, log = build.run_build(tmp_path, "tests")
    assert ok and "WARNING" in log


def test_build_check_text_lists_detected_commands(tmp_path):
    pkg(tmp_path / "frontend", {"build": "vite build"}, {"typescript": "5"})
    write(tmp_path / "frontend" / "tsconfig.json", "{}")
    text = build.build_check_text(T, tmp_path)
    # The AI's shell has no network (agent.sandbox): the pipeline installs first, so the AI is not told to install.
    assert "npm install" not in text
    assert "in `frontend` run `npx --no-install tsc --noEmit" in text and "only report a check as passed if you ran it" in text
