    assert "in `frontend` run `npm install --no-audit --no-fund`" in build.build_check_text(T, tmp_path)


def test_build_env_drops_pipeline_secrets(monkeypatch):
    for name in ("ADO_PAT", "AZURE_OPENAI_API_KEY", "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "GITHUB_TOKEN", "SONAR_TOKEN",
                 "TARGET_GITHUB_TOKEN"):
        monkeypatch.setenv(name, "s3cret")
    monkeypatch.setenv("NPM_TOKEN", "npm")
    env = build._build_env()
    assert "s3cret" not in env.values() and env["NPM_TOKEN"] == "npm" and env["CI"] == "true"
    assert "PATH" in env


def test_jsonc_loader():
    assert stack.load_jsonc(Path("/does/not/exist")) == {}


# ---------- repo-owned .devpipe.yml
def test_repo_file_settings_precedence(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(config, "OUT", tmp_path / "out")
    (tmp_path / "out").mkdir()
    (tmp_path / "out" / "target.json").write_text(json.dumps({"full_name": "org/web"}))
    checkout = tmp_path / "checkout"
    monkeypatch.setattr(config, "TARGET_CHECKOUT", checkout)
    write(checkout / ".devpipe.yml", "build_commands: ['make ci']\ntest_dir: spec\nsecret_thing: nope\n")
    target = {"mode": "external", "app_dir": "x", "app_package": "app", "test_dir": "tests",
              "architecture_file": "a.md", "language": "auto", "build_commands": [], "overrides": {}}
    monkeypatch.setitem(config.settings(), "target", target)
    t = config.target_settings()
    assert t["build_commands"] == ["make ci"] and t["test_dir"] == "spec" and "secret_thing" not in t
    target["overrides"] = {"org/web": {"build_commands": ["npm test"]}}
    assert config.target_settings()["build_commands"] == ["npm test"]   # config override wins


# ---------- Python target repos: their own dependencies are installed (separate virtualenv)
@pytest.mark.parametrize("files,expected", [
    ({"requirements.txt": "", "requirements-dev.txt": ""}, ["-r requirements.txt", "-r requirements-dev.txt"]),
    ({"setup.py": ""}, ["-e ."]),
    ({"pyproject.toml": '[project]\nname="x"\n[project.optional-dependencies]\ntest=["pytest"]\ndocs=["x"]\n'},
     ["-e '.[test]'"]),
    ({"pyproject.toml": '[build-system]\nrequires=["setuptools"]\n'}, ["-e ."]),
    ({"pyproject.toml": '[tool.poetry]\nname="x"\n'}, ["poetry install"]),
    ({"Pipfile": "", "Pipfile.lock": "{}"}, ["pipenv requirements --dev"]),
    ({"pyproject.toml": "[tool.black]\nline-length=100\n"}, []),     # tool config only: nothing to install
])
def test_python_install_commands(tmp_path, files, expected):
    if "[test]" in str(expected) and sys.version_info < (3, 11):
        pytest.skip("reading extras needs tomllib (Python 3.11+; the pipeline runs on 3.12)")
    for name, text in files.items():
        write(tmp_path / name, text)
    cmds = stack.python_install_commands(tmp_path, "PY")
    assert len(cmds) == len(expected)
    for cmd, part in zip(cmds, expected):
        assert part in cmd and cmd.startswith("PY -m pip install")


def test_python_project_with_requirements_uses_its_own_venv(tmp_path):
    write(tmp_path / "requirements.txt", "requests\n")
    write(tmp_path / "tests" / "test_a.py")
    [p] = stack.detect_projects(tmp_path)
    venv_py = str(stack.VENV_ROOT)
    assert "venv.create" in p.commands[0].cmd                              # 1. create the virtualenv
    assert "-r requirements.txt" in p.commands[1].cmd and venv_py in p.commands[1].cmd   # 2. repo deps
    assert "pytest pytest-cov" in p.commands[2].cmd                        # 3. test tools
    assert all(venv_py in c.cmd for c in p.commands[1:])                   # compileall + pytest use the venv
    assert not p.warnings


def test_python_project_without_dependency_file_warns(tmp_path):
    write(tmp_path / "app" / "main.py")
    [p] = stack.detect_projects(tmp_path)
    assert "compileall" in p.commands[0].cmd and "no dependency file" in p.warnings[0]


@pytest.mark.skipif(os.name == "nt" or os.environ.get("DEVPIPE_SKIP_NETWORK") == "1",
                    reason="creates a virtualenv and installs from PyPI")
def test_python_repo_dependencies_installed_end_to_end(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """A Python repo whose tests import a library the pipeline doesn't have: must pass after installing it."""
    monkeypatch.setattr(stack, "VENV_ROOT", tmp_path / "venvs")
    repo = tmp_path / "repo"
    write(repo / "requirements.txt", "tomli-w==1.0.0\n")
    write(repo / "app.py", "import tomli_w\n\ndef dump(d):\n    return tomli_w.dumps(d)\n")
    write(repo / "tests" / "test_app.py", "from app import dump\n\ndef test_dump():\n    assert dump({'a': 1}) == 'a = 1\\n'\n")
    write(repo / "conftest.py", "")
    result = build.check_build(T, repo)
    assert result.ok, result.log
    ok, python, _ = build.prepare_python(repo)
    assert ok and str(tmp_path / "venvs") in python


@pytest.mark.skipif(os.name == "nt", reason="uses sh")
def test_run_commands_respects_ok_codes(tmp_path):
    ok, _ = build._run_commands(tmp_path, [stack.Command("exit 5", (0, 5))])
    assert ok
    ok, log = build._run_commands(tmp_path, [stack.Command("exit 4", (0, 5))])
    assert not ok and "exit 4" in log

FILE: tests/test_azure_openai_proxy.py

"""Tests for azure_openai_proxy: pure translation-function unit tests plus
a full round-trip integration test against a stub "fake Azure" HTTP server
(no live Azure credentials needed -- see README.md's "What's been verified
vs. not" for why a real Azure OpenAI call itself isn't exercised here)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import subprocess
import sys
import threading
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
import requests

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from azure_openai_proxy import (
    anthropic_error,
    anthropic_messages_to_openai,
    anthropic_request_to_openai,
    anthropic_tool_choice_to_openai,
    anthropic_tools_to_openai,
    is_reasoning_model,
    openai_response_to_anthropic,
    sse_events_for_message,
    start_proxy,
    stop_proxy,
)
from azure_openai_proxy.server import azure_chat_completions_url, azure_headers

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Pure translation functions
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

def test_is_reasoning_model_matches_o_series_and_gpt5():
    assert is_reasoning_model("o4-mini")
    assert is_reasoning_model("O3")
    assert is_reasoning_model("gpt-5-thinking")
    assert not is_reasoning_model("gpt-4o")


def test_anthropic_messages_to_openai_plain_text_roundtrip():
    out = anthropic_messages_to_openai("You are helpful.", [{"role": "user", "content": "hi"}])
    assert out == [
        {"role": "system", "content": "You are helpful."},
        {"role": "user", "content": "hi"},
    ]


def test_anthropic_messages_to_openai_expands_tool_use_into_tool_calls():
    messages = [
        {"role": "user", "content": "read the file"},
        {
            "role": "assistant",
            "content": [
                {"type": "text", "text": "Sure, reading it."},
                {"type": "tool_use", "id": "call_1", "name": "Read", "input": {"file_path": "a.py"}},
            ],
        },
        {
            "role": "user",
            "content": [{"type": "tool_result", "tool_use_id": "call_1", "content": "file contents"}],
        },
    ]
    out = anthropic_messages_to_openai(None, messages)
    assert out[0] == {"role": "user", "content": "read the file"}
    assert out[1]["role"] == "assistant"
    assert out[1]["content"] == "Sure, reading it."
    assert out[1]["tool_calls"] == [
        {"id": "call_1", "type": "function", "function": {"name": "Read", "arguments": json.dumps({"file_path": "a.py"})}}
    ]
    assert out[2] == {"role": "tool", "tool_call_id": "call_1", "content": "file contents"}


def test_anthropic_messages_to_openai_system_as_content_block_list():
    out = anthropic_messages_to_openai(
        [{"type": "text", "text": "part one"}, {"type": "text", "text": "part two"}],
        [{"role": "user", "content": "hi"}],
    )
    assert out[0] == {"role": "system", "content": "part one\npart two"}


def test_anthropic_messages_to_openai_user_message_with_text_content_blocks():
    out = anthropic_messages_to_openai(None, [
        {"role": "user", "content": [{"type": "text", "text": "line one"}, {"type": "text", "text": "line two"}]},
    ])
    assert out == [{"role": "user", "content": "line one\nline two"}]


def test_anthropic_messages_to_openai_tool_result_with_list_content():
    messages = [{
        "role": "user",
        "content": [{"type": "tool_result", "tool_use_id": "c1", "content": [{"type": "text", "text": "ok"}]}],
    }]
    out = anthropic_messages_to_openai(None, messages)
    assert out == [{"role": "tool", "tool_call_id": "c1", "content": "ok"}]


def test_anthropic_tools_to_openai_maps_input_schema_to_parameters():
    tools = [{"name": "Read", "description": "reads a file", "input_schema": {"type": "object", "properties": {}}}]
    out = anthropic_tools_to_openai(tools)
    assert out == [{
        "type": "function",
        "function": {"name": "Read", "description": "reads a file", "parameters": {"type": "object", "properties": {}}},
    }]
    assert anthropic_tools_to_openai(None) is None
    assert anthropic_tools_to_openai([]) is None


@pytest.mark.parametrize("anthropic_choice,expected", [
    (None, None),
    ({"type": "auto"}, "auto"),
    ({"type": "any"}, "required"),
    ({"type": "tool", "name": "Read"}, {"type": "function", "function": {"name": "Read"}}),
])
def test_anthropic_tool_choice_to_openai(anthropic_choice, expected):
    assert anthropic_tool_choice_to_openai(anthropic_choice) == expected


def test_anthropic_request_to_openai_reasoning_model_uses_max_completion_tokens():
    body = {"model": "o4-mini", "max_tokens": 512, "temperature": 0.7, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "hi"}]}
    out = anthropic_request_to_openai(body)
    assert out["max_completion_tokens"] == 512
    assert "max_tokens" not in out
    assert "temperature" not in out  # reasoning models reject it


def test_anthropic_request_to_openai_normal_model_keeps_temperature_and_max_tokens():
    body = {"model": "gpt-4o", "max_tokens": 256, "temperature": 0.2, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "hi"}]}
    out = anthropic_request_to_openai(body)
    assert out["max_tokens"] == 256
    assert out["temperature"] == 0.2
    assert "max_completion_tokens" not in out


def test_anthropic_request_to_openai_includes_tools_and_tool_choice_only_when_present():
    body = {
        "model": "gpt-4o", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "hi"}],
        "tools": [{"name": "Read", "input_schema": {}}], "tool_choice": {"type": "any"},
    }
    out = anthropic_request_to_openai(body)
    assert out["tools"][0]["function"]["name"] == "Read"
    assert out["tool_choice"] == "required"

    body_no_tools = {"model": "gpt-4o", "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "hi"}]}
    assert "tools" not in anthropic_request_to_openai(body_no_tools)


def test_openai_response_to_anthropic_text_only():
    resp = {
        "id": "chatcmpl-1",
        "choices": [{"finish_reason": "stop", "message": {"role": "assistant", "content": "hello there"}}],
        "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 10, "completion_tokens": 3},
    }
    out = openai_response_to_anthropic(resp, "gpt-4o")
    assert out["content"] == [{"type": "text", "text": "hello there"}]
    assert out["stop_reason"] == "end_turn"
    assert out["usage"] == {"input_tokens": 10, "output_tokens": 3}


def test_openai_response_to_anthropic_tool_call():
    resp = {
        "id": "chatcmpl-2",
        "choices": [{
            "finish_reason": "tool_calls",
            "message": {
                "role": "assistant", "content": None,
                "tool_calls": [{"id": "call_1", "type": "function",
                                 "function": {"name": "Read", "arguments": '{"file_path": "a.py"}'}}],
            },
        }],
        "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 20, "completion_tokens": 5},
    }
    out = openai_response_to_anthropic(resp, "gpt-4o")
    assert out["content"] == [{"type": "tool_use", "id": "call_1", "name": "Read", "input": {"file_path": "a.py"}}]
    assert out["stop_reason"] == "tool_use"


def test_openai_response_to_anthropic_malformed_tool_arguments_default_to_empty_dict():
    resp = {
        "id": "chatcmpl-3",
        "choices": [{
            "finish_reason": "tool_calls",
            "message": {"role": "assistant", "content": None,
                        "tool_calls": [{"id": "call_1", "type": "function",
                                        "function": {"name": "Read", "arguments": "not json"}}]},
        }],
        "usage": {},
    }
    out = openai_response_to_anthropic(resp, "gpt-4o")
    assert out["content"][0]["input"] == {}


def test_openai_response_to_anthropic_no_content_and_no_tool_calls_falls_back_to_empty_text():
    resp = {
        "id": "chatcmpl-4",
        "choices": [{"finish_reason": "stop", "message": {"role": "assistant", "content": None}}],
        "usage": {},
    }
    out = openai_response_to_anthropic(resp, "gpt-4o")
    assert out["content"] == [{"type": "text", "text": ""}]


def test_anthropic_error_shape():
    err = anthropic_error("boom")
    assert err == {"type": "error", "error": {"type": "api_error", "message": "boom"}}


def test_sse_events_for_message_contains_matching_start_delta_stop_for_each_block():
    message = {
        "id": "msg_1", "type": "message", "role": "assistant", "model": "gpt-4o",
        "content": [
            {"type": "text", "text": "hi"},
            {"type": "tool_use", "id": "call_1", "name": "Read", "input": {"a": 1}},
        ],
        "stop_reason": "tool_use", "stop_sequence": None,
        "usage": {"input_tokens": 5, "output_tokens": 2},
    }
    stream = sse_events_for_message(message)
    assert stream.count("event: content_block_start") == 2
    assert stream.count("event: content_block_stop") == 2
    assert "event: message_start" in stream
    assert "event: message_stop" in stream
    assert '"partial_json": "{\\"a\\": 1}"' in stream

def test_azure_chat_completions_url_new_v1_shape_has_no_api_version():
    url = azure_chat_completions_url("https://res.openai.azure.com/openai/v1", "o4-mini", "2024-12-01-preview")
    assert url == "https://res.openai.azure.com/openai/v1/chat/completions"


def test_azure_chat_completions_url_classic_shape_includes_deployment_and_api_version():
    url = azure_chat_completions_url("https://res.openai.azure.com", "o4-mini", "2024-12-01-preview")
    assert url == "https://res.openai.azure.com/openai/deployments/o4-mini/chat/completions?api-version=2024-12-01-preview"


def test_azure_headers_new_v1_shape_uses_bearer():
    headers = azure_headers("https://res.openai.azure.com/openai/v1", "secret")
    assert headers["Authorization"] == "Bearer secret"


def test_azure_headers_classic_shape_uses_api_key():
    headers = azure_headers("https://res.openai.azure.com", "secret")
    assert headers["api-key"] == "secret"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Full round trip against a stub fake-Azure server
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

class _FakeAzureHandler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    response_body: dict = {}

    def log_message(self, format, *args):
        pass

    def do_POST(self):
        length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0))
        self.last_request = json.loads(self.rfile.read(length) or b"{}")
        _FakeAzureHandler.received.append(self.last_request)
        data = json.dumps(_FakeAzureHandler.response_body).encode("utf-8")
        self.send_response(200)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data)))
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(data)


@pytest.fixture
def fake_azure():
    _FakeAzureHandler.received = []
    _FakeAzureHandler.response_body = {
        "id": "chatcmpl-fake",
        "choices": [{"finish_reason": "stop", "message": {"role": "assistant", "content": "fake reply"}}],
        "usage": {"prompt_tokens": 7, "completion_tokens": 2},
    }
    server = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), _FakeAzureHandler)
    thread = threading.Thread(target=server.serve_forever, daemon=True)
    thread.start()
    port = server.server_address[1]
    yield f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/openai/v1", _FakeAzureHandler
    server.shutdown()
    server.server_close()
    thread.join(timeout=5)


@pytest.fixture
def proxy(fake_azure):
    endpoint, _ = fake_azure
    server, thread, port = start_proxy(endpoint=endpoint, api_key="fake-key", deployment="o4-mini")
    yield f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}"
    stop_proxy(server, thread)


def test_proxy_round_trip_non_streaming(proxy, fake_azure):
    _, fake_handler = fake_azure
    resp = requests.post(f"{proxy}/v1/messages", json={
        "model": "o4-mini", "max_tokens": 100,
        "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "hello"}],
    }, timeout=10)
    assert resp.status_code == 200
    body = resp.json()
    assert body["content"] == [{"type": "text", "text": "fake reply"}]
    assert body["stop_reason"] == "end_turn"
    # and confirm the outbound request the proxy sent to "Azure" was
    # correctly shaped for a reasoning model (max_completion_tokens, no temperature)
    sent = fake_handler.received[0]
    assert sent["max_completion_tokens"] == 100
    assert "temperature" not in sent


def test_proxy_round_trip_streaming_emits_valid_sse(proxy):
    resp = requests.post(f"{proxy}/v1/messages", json={
        "model": "o4-mini", "max_tokens": 100, "stream": True,
        "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "hello"}],
    }, timeout=10)
    assert resp.status_code == 200
    assert resp.headers["Content-Type"] == "text/event-stream"
    assert "event: message_start" in resp.text
    assert "event: message_stop" in resp.text


def test_proxy_returns_anthropic_shaped_error_on_azure_failure():
    # Point the proxy at a port nothing is listening on.
    server, thread, port = start_proxy(endpoint="http://127.0.0.1:1", api_key="k", deployment="o4-mini")
    try:
        resp = requests.post(f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/v1/messages", json={
            "model": "o4-mini", "max_tokens": 10, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "hi"}],
        }, timeout=10)
        assert resp.status_code == 502
        assert resp.json()["type"] == "error"
    finally:
        stop_proxy(server, thread)


def test_proxy_returns_400_on_invalid_json_body():
    server, thread, port = start_proxy(endpoint="http://unused", api_key="k", deployment="o4-mini")
    try:
        resp = requests.post(
            f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/v1/messages", data=b"not json",
            headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"}, timeout=10,
        )
        assert resp.status_code == 400
        assert resp.json()["type"] == "error"
    finally:
        stop_proxy(server, thread)


def test_proxy_surfaces_azure_http_error_status_and_body(fake_azure):
    endpoint, fake_handler = fake_azure

    class _ErrorHandler(_FakeAzureHandler):
        def do_POST(self):
            length = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0))
            self.rfile.read(length)  # drain the request body first, or the client's write gets aborted
            data = json.dumps({"error": {"message": "bad deployment"}}).encode("utf-8")
            self.send_response(400)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(data)))
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(data)

    error_server = ThreadingHTTPServer(("127.0.0.1", 0), _ErrorHandler)
    error_thread = threading.Thread(target=error_server.serve_forever, daemon=True)
    error_thread.start()
    error_endpoint = f"http://127.0.0.1:{error_server.server_address[1]}/openai/v1"

    server, thread, port = start_proxy(endpoint=error_endpoint, api_key="k", deployment="o4-mini")
    try:
        resp = requests.post(f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/v1/messages", json={
            "model": "o4-mini", "max_tokens": 10, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "hi"}],
        }, timeout=10)
        assert resp.status_code == 502
        body = resp.json()
        assert body["type"] == "error"
        assert "bad deployment" in body["error"]["message"]
    finally:
        stop_proxy(server, thread)
        error_server.shutdown()
        error_server.server_close()
        error_thread.join(timeout=5)


def test_proxy_health_check_get():
    server, thread, port = start_proxy(endpoint="http://unused", api_key="k", deployment="o4-mini")
    try:
        resp = requests.get(f"http://127.0.0.1:{port}/", timeout=10)
        assert resp.status_code == 200
        assert resp.json()["status"] == "ok"
    finally:
        stop_proxy(server, thread)

def test_module_is_runnable_via_dash_m_and_requires_endpoint_api_key_deployment():
    result = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, "-m", "azure_openai_proxy"], cwd=ROOT, capture_output=True, text=True,
    )
    assert result.returncode == 2
    assert "--endpoint" in result.stderr
    assert "--api-key" in result.stderr
    assert "--deployment" in result.stderr

FILE: tests/test_coverage_and_sonar_gates.py

"""Unit tests for the Dev Agent's three hard gates. These run without a
live `claude` CLI, Sonar server, or qTest -- exactly the parts of the
Dev Agent testable in a plain Python dev environment (no Node.js
available here; see README "What's been verified vs. not").
"""
import sys
import textwrap
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))

from dev_agent.checks.coverage_gate import parse_coverage_xml, run_coverage_gate
from dev_agent.checks.sonar_precheck import SonarIssue, evaluate_issues
from dev_agent.checks.test_quality_review import gate_passed, review_test_file, review_test_files

SAMPLE_APP = ROOT / "sample_app"


# ---- coverage gate ----------------------------------------------------------

def test_coverage_gate_passes_on_sample_app_full_suite():
    result = run_coverage_gate(SAMPLE_APP, package="app")
    assert result.passed, result.summary()
    assert result.coverage_pct >= 95.0


def test_coverage_gate_fails_when_a_test_is_skipped(tmp_path):
    # Copy sample_app's app/ package but omit most of the test suite, so
    # coverage genuinely drops below the 95% threshold.
    import shutil
    project = tmp_path / "proj"
    shutil.copytree(SAMPLE_APP / "app", project / "app")
    (project / "tests").mkdir()
    (project / "tests" / "__init__.py").write_text("")
    (project / "tests" / "test_main.py").write_text(textwrap.dedent("""
        from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
        from app.main import app

        def test_health():
            resp = TestClient(app).get("/health")
            assert resp.status_code == 200
    """))
    result = run_coverage_gate(project, package="app")
    assert not result.passed
    assert result.coverage_pct < 95.0


def test_parse_coverage_xml_reports_missing_lines(tmp_path):
    xml = tmp_path / "coverage.xml"
    xml.write_text(textwrap.dedent("""\
        <?xml version="1.0" ?>
        <coverage line-rate="0.5">
          <packages>
            <package>
              <classes>
                <class filename="app/main.py" line-rate="0.5">
                  <lines>
                    <line number="1" hits="1"/>
                    <line number="2" hits="0"/>
                  </lines>
                </class>
              </classes>
            </package>
          </packages>
        </coverage>
    """))
    result = parse_coverage_xml(xml)
    assert result.coverage_pct == 50.0
    assert result.files[0].missing_lines == [2]


# ---- test quality gate --------------------------------------------------------

def test_review_flags_assertion_free_test(tmp_path):
    f = tmp_path / "test_bad.py"
    f.write_text(textwrap.dedent("""
        def test_does_something():
            x = 1 + 1
    """))
    findings = review_test_file(f)
    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert "no assertion" in findings[0].reason


def test_review_flags_vacuous_assert(tmp_path):
    f = tmp_path / "test_bad.py"
    f.write_text(textwrap.dedent("""
        def test_does_something():
            assert True
    """))
    findings = review_test_file(f)
    assert len(findings) == 1
    assert "vacuous" in findings[0].reason


def test_review_accepts_real_assertion(tmp_path):
    f = tmp_path / "test_good.py"
    f.write_text(textwrap.dedent("""
        def test_addition():
            assert 1 + 1 == 2
    """))
    assert review_test_file(f) == []


def test_review_accepts_pytest_raises_with_no_bare_assert(tmp_path):
    f = tmp_path / "test_good.py"
    f.write_text(textwrap.dedent("""
        import pytest

        def test_raises_on_bad_input():
            with pytest.raises(ValueError):
                int("not a number")
    """))
    assert review_test_file(f) == []


def test_review_test_files_and_gate_passed_on_real_sample_app_suite():
    test_files = list((SAMPLE_APP / "tests").glob("test_*.py"))
    assert test_files, "expected sample_app to have test files"
    findings = review_test_files(test_files)
    assert gate_passed(findings), findings


# ---- sonar gate ----------------------------------------------------------------

def test_evaluate_issues_passes_with_no_issues():
    result = evaluate_issues([])
    assert result.passed
    assert result.blocking_issues == []


def test_evaluate_issues_fails_on_critical_severity():
    result = evaluate_issues([
        {"key": "k1", "rule": "python:S1128", "severity": "CRITICAL",
         "message": "Remove this unused import", "component": "app/storage.py", "line": 3},
    ])
    assert not result.passed
    assert len(result.blocking_issues) == 1
    assert isinstance(result.blocking_issues[0], SonarIssue)
    assert "unused import" in result.summary()


def test_evaluate_issues_ignores_minor_and_info_severity():
    result = evaluate_issues([
        {"key": "k1", "rule": "r1", "severity": "MINOR", "message": "nit", "component": "app/main.py"},
        {"key": "k2", "rule": "r2", "severity": "INFO", "message": "fyi", "component": "app/main.py"},
    ])
    assert result.passed
    assert len(result.info_issues) == 2


def test_scanner_env_drops_pipeline_ci_variables(monkeypatch):
    """The scanner must not see the pipeline run's GitHub Actions context (it is not the PR's)."""
    from dev_pipe import sonar
    monkeypatch.setenv("GITHUB_ACTIONS", "true")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GITHUB_SHA", "abc")
    monkeypatch.setenv("ACTIONS_RUNTIME_URL", "x")
    monkeypatch.setenv("CI", "true")
    monkeypatch.setenv("JAVA_HOME", "/opt/java")
    env = sonar.scanner_env()
    assert not any(k in env for k in ("GITHUB_ACTIONS", "GITHUB_SHA", "ACTIONS_RUNTIME_URL", "CI"))
    assert env["JAVA_HOME"] == "/opt/java" and "PATH" in env


def test_fetch_pr_measures_reads_period_values(monkeypatch):
    from dev_pipe import sonar

    class Resp:
        def raise_for_status(self):
            pass

        def json(self):
            return {"component": {"measures": [
                {"metric": "new_lines", "periods": [{"index": 1, "value": "249"}]},
                {"metric": "ncloc", "value": "1191"}]}}
    monkeypatch.setattr(sonar.requests, "get", lambda *a, **k: Resp())
    assert sonar.fetch_pr_measures("https://h", "t", "k", 4) == {"new_lines": "249", "ncloc": "1191"}

FILE: tests/test_lcov.py

import os
import time

from dev_pipe.lcov import find_lcov_reports, line_coverage, report_paths_arg

LCOV = "TN:\nSF:src/a.ts\nLF:10\nLH:8\nend_of_record\nSF:src/b.ts\nLF:10\nLH:2\nend_of_record\n"


def _write(path, text=LCOV):
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
    return path


def test_finds_reports_and_skips_node_modules(tmp_path):
    fe = _write(tmp_path / "frontend" / "coverage" / "lcov.info")
    _write(tmp_path / "frontend" / "node_modules" / "pkg" / "coverage" / "lcov.info")
    _write(tmp_path / "frontend" / "lcov.info")  # not inside a coverage/ folder
    assert find_lcov_reports(tmp_path) == [fe]


def test_newer_than_ignores_stale_reports(tmp_path):
    stale = _write(tmp_path / "backend" / "coverage" / "lcov.info")
    old = time.time() - 3600
    os.utime(stale, (old, old))
    fresh = _write(tmp_path / "frontend" / "coverage" / "lcov.info")
    assert find_lcov_reports(tmp_path, newer_than=time.time() - 60) == [fresh]


def test_report_written_right_after_start_is_found(tmp_path):
    started = time.time()
    fresh = _write(tmp_path / "coverage" / "lcov.info")
    assert find_lcov_reports(tmp_path, newer_than=started) == [fresh]


def test_line_coverage_sums_all_files_and_reports(tmp_path):
    a = _write(tmp_path / "a" / "coverage" / "lcov.info")                        # 10 / 20
    b = _write(tmp_path / "b" / "coverage" / "lcov.info", "SF:x\nLF:20\nLH:20\n")  # 20 / 20
    assert line_coverage([a]) == 50.0
    assert line_coverage([a, b]) == 75.0


def test_line_coverage_none_when_no_lines(tmp_path):
    empty = _write(tmp_path / "coverage" / "lcov.info", "TN:\n")
    assert line_coverage([empty]) is None
    assert line_coverage([]) is None


def test_report_paths_are_relative_posix(tmp_path):
    r = _write(tmp_path / "frontend" / "coverage" / "lcov.info")
    assert report_paths_arg([r], tmp_path) == "frontend/coverage/lcov.info"


def test_finds_angular_cli_report_in_a_project_subfolder(tmp_path):
    ng = _write(tmp_path / "coverage" / "my-app" / "lcov.info")            # ng test --code-coverage
    _write(tmp_path / "src" / "deep" / "x" / "lcov.info")                    # not a coverage folder
    assert find_lcov_reports(tmp_path) == [ng]

FILE: tests/test_sonar_precheck.py

"""Step 4 Sonar pre-check for every stack (dev_pipe/sonar_precheck.py). No network: scanners and the
SonarQube Cloud API are faked."""
from __future__ import annotations

import subprocess
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
import requests

from dev_pipe import config, sonar_precheck as sp
from dev_pipe.steps import s6_build_test_coverage as s6

CONN = {"host": "https://sonarcloud.io", "token": "tok-secret", "project_key": "org_repo", "organization": "org"}

DIFF = """diff --git a/web/src/app/pay.service.ts b/web/src/app/pay.service.ts
index 1111111..2222222 100644
--- a/web/src/app/pay.service.ts
+++ b/web/src/app/pay.service.ts
@@ -10,0 +11,3 @@ export class PayService {
+  refund(id: string) {
+    return this.http.post(`/refund/${id}`, {});
+  }
@@ -40 +43 @@ export class PayService {
-  old()
+  newer()
diff --git a/api/Refunds/RefundController.cs b/api/Refunds/RefundController.cs
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ b/api/Refunds/RefundController.cs
@@ -0,0 +1,4 @@
+public class RefundController {
+  public int Get() { return 1; }
+}
+
diff --git a/web/src/legacy.ts b/web/src/legacy.ts
deleted file mode 100644
--- a/web/src/legacy.ts
+++ /dev/null
@@ -1,2 +0,0 @@
-a
-b
"""


def issue(path, line=None, severity="MAJOR", rule="ts:S1", impacts=None, key="org_repo", end=None):
    i = {"component": f"{key}:{path}", "severity": severity, "rule": rule, "message": f"msg {path}:{line}"}
    if line is not None:
        i["line"] = line
        i["textRange"] = {"startLine": line, "endLine": end or line}
    if impacts is not None:
        i["impacts"] = impacts
    return i


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- pure helpers
def test_parse_changed_lines_added_modified_new_and_deleted_files():
    changed = sp.parse_changed_lines(DIFF)
    assert changed["web/src/app/pay.service.ts"] == {11, 12, 13, 43}
    assert changed["api/Refunds/RefundController.cs"] == {1, 2, 3, 4}
    assert "web/src/legacy.ts" not in changed          # deleted: nothing to analyse


def test_match_changed_path_handles_scanner_base_dir_in_a_subfolder():
    changed = {"web/src/app/pay.service.ts": {1}, ".github/x.yml": {1}}
    assert sp.match_changed_path("web/src/app/pay.service.ts", changed) == "web/src/app/pay.service.ts"
    assert sp.match_changed_path("src/app/pay.service.ts", changed) == "web/src/app/pay.service.ts"  # scanned from web/
    assert sp.match_changed_path(".github/x.yml", changed) == ".github/x.yml"
    assert sp.match_changed_path("app/pay.service.ts.bak", changed) is None
    assert sp.match_changed_path("other/pay.service.ts", changed) is None


def test_classify_only_blocks_issues_on_changed_lines_with_blocking_severity():
    changed = sp.parse_changed_lines(DIFF)
    issues = [
        issue("web/src/app/pay.service.ts", 12, "CRITICAL"),            # changed line -> blocks
        issue("web/src/app/pay.service.ts", 30, "BLOCKER"),             # same file, untouched line -> ignored
        issue("web/src/app/pay.service.ts", 43, "MINOR"),               # changed line, low severity -> ignored
        issue("web/src/other.ts", 5, "BLOCKER"),                        # file not changed -> ignored
        issue("api/Refunds/RefundController.cs", None, "MAJOR"),        # file-level issue on a changed file -> blocks
        issue("web/src/app/pay.service.ts", 8, "MAJOR", end=11),        # range overlaps a changed line -> blocks
    ]
    blocking, ignored = sp.classify_issues(issues, changed, "changed_lines", sp.DEFAULTS["severities"],
                                           sp.DEFAULTS["impact_severities"])
    assert [(b["path"], b["line"]) for b in blocking] == [
        ("api/Refunds/RefundController.cs", None), ("web/src/app/pay.service.ts", 8),
        ("web/src/app/pay.service.ts", 12)]
    assert ignored == 3


def test_classify_changed_files_mode_blocks_old_issues_in_touched_files():
    changed = sp.parse_changed_lines(DIFF)
    blocking, _ = sp.classify_issues([issue("web/src/app/pay.service.ts", 30, "BLOCKER")], changed,
                                     "changed_files", ["BLOCKER"], [])
    assert len(blocking) == 1


def test_mqr_mode_impact_severity_counts():
    changed = {"a.py": {3}}
    mqr = issue("a.py", 3, severity="", impacts=[{"softwareQuality": "MAINTAINABILITY", "severity": "HIGH"}])
    low = issue("a.py", 3, severity="", impacts=[{"softwareQuality": "MAINTAINABILITY", "severity": "LOW"}])
    blocking, ignored = sp.classify_issues([mqr, low], changed, "changed_lines", ["BLOCKER"], ["HIGH"])
    assert len(blocking) == 1 and ignored == 1
    assert blocking[0]["severity"] == "MAINTAINABILITY:HIGH"


def test_read_task_id_and_redact(tmp_path):
    f = tmp_path / "report-task.txt"
    f.write_text("projectKey=org_repo\nceTaskId=AYx123\n", encoding="utf-8")
    assert sp.read_task_id(f) == "AYx123"
    assert sp.read_task_id(tmp_path / "missing.txt") == ""
    net = tmp_path / ".sonarqube" / "out" / ".sonar"
    net.mkdir(parents=True)
    (net / "report-task.txt").write_text("ceTaskId=NET1\n", encoding="utf-8")
    assert sp.read_task_id(tmp_path / "missing.txt", tmp_path) == "NET1"   # .NET scanner's own location
    assert sp.redact("token tok-secret here", "tok-secret") == "token *** here"


def test_safe_branch_name():
    assert sp.safe_branch("devpipe-precheck", "123", "1") == "devpipe-precheck/123-1"
    assert sp.safe_branch("devpipe-precheck", "a b/c") == "devpipe-precheck/a-b-c"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- scan planning
def make_monorepo(root: Path) -> None:
    (root / "web").mkdir(parents=True)
    (root / "web" / "package.json").write_text('{"scripts": {"build": "ng build"}}', encoding="utf-8")
    (root / "api" / "Refunds").mkdir(parents=True)
    (root / "api" / "Api.sln").write_text("", encoding="utf-8")
    (root / "api" / "Refunds" / "Refunds.csproj").write_text("<Project/>", encoding="utf-8")


def test_plan_units_cli_for_js_plus_dotnet_scanner_for_changed_dotnet_project(tmp_path):
    make_monorepo(tmp_path)
    units = sp.plan_units(tmp_path, [tmp_path / "web/src/app/pay.service.ts",
                                     tmp_path / "api/Refunds/RefundController.cs"], "devpipe-precheck/9")
    assert [(u.kind, u.cwd.name, u.branch) for u in units] == [
        ("cli", tmp_path.name, "devpipe-precheck/9"), ("dotnet", "api", "devpipe-precheck/9-net1")]
    assert units[1].build_cmd.startswith("dotnet build") and "Api.sln" in units[1].build_cmd


def test_plan_units_dotnet_only_change_needs_no_cli_scan(tmp_path):
    make_monorepo(tmp_path)
    units = sp.plan_units(tmp_path, [tmp_path / "api/Refunds/RefundController.cs"], "b")
    assert [u.kind for u in units] == ["dotnet"]


def test_plan_units_js_only_change_skips_dotnet(tmp_path):
    make_monorepo(tmp_path)
    assert [u.kind for u in sp.plan_units(tmp_path, [tmp_path / "web/a.ts"], "b")] == ["cli"]


def test_java_args_point_at_compiled_classes(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "src/main/java").mkdir(parents=True)
    (tmp_path / "src/main/java/A.java").write_text("class A {}", encoding="utf-8")
    assert sp.java_args(tmp_path) == ["-Dsonar.java.binaries=."]           # not built: scanner says why
    (tmp_path / "target/classes").mkdir(parents=True)
    assert sp.java_args(tmp_path) == ["-Dsonar.java.binaries=target/classes"]
    other = tmp_path / "js"
    other.mkdir()
    assert sp.java_args(other) == []


def test_coverage_args_per_language(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "coverage").mkdir()
    (tmp_path / "coverage/lcov.info").write_text("LF:1\nLH:1\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / "coverage.xml").write_text("<coverage/>", encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / "target/site/jacoco").mkdir(parents=True)
    (tmp_path / "target/site/jacoco/jacoco.xml").write_text("<report/>", encoding="utf-8")
    args = sp.coverage_args(tmp_path, None)
    assert "-Dsonar.javascript.lcov.reportPaths=coverage/lcov.info" in args
    assert "-Dsonar.python.coverage.reportPaths=coverage.xml" in args
    assert "-Dsonar.coverage.jacoco.xmlReportPaths=target/site/jacoco/jacoco.xml" in args
    (tmp_path / "node_modules/x/coverage").mkdir(parents=True)          # dependencies are never scanned
    (tmp_path / "node_modules/x/coverage.xml").write_text("<coverage/>", encoding="utf-8")
    assert "-Dsonar.python.coverage.reportPaths=coverage.xml" in sp.coverage_args(tmp_path, None)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- run_precheck end to end (faked)
class FakeApi:
    def __init__(self, issues_by_branch, status="SUCCESS"):
        self.issues_by_branch, self.status = issues_by_branch, status
        self.deleted: list[str] = []
        self.waited: list[str] = []

    def wait_for_task(self, task_id, wait_seconds, poll_seconds):
        self.waited.append(task_id)
        return self.status, "" if self.status == "SUCCESS" else "boom"

    def branch_issues(self, branch):
        return self.issues_by_branch.get(branch, [])

    def delete_branch(self, branch):
        self.deleted.append(branch)


def fake_scanner(returncode=0, task="T1", calls=None):
    def scan(unit, conn, metadata, extra):
        if calls is not None:
            calls.append((unit.kind, unit.branch, list(extra)))
        if task:
            metadata.write_text(f"ceTaskId={task}-{unit.kind}\n", encoding="utf-8")
        return subprocess.CompletedProcess(["scanner"], returncode, "INFO done", "")
    return scan


def test_run_precheck_fails_on_issue_in_changed_code_and_cleans_up_branches(tmp_path):
    make_monorepo(tmp_path)
    api = FakeApi({
        "devpipe-precheck/77": [issue("web/src/app/pay.service.ts", 12, "CRITICAL"),
                                issue("web/src/old.ts", 3, "BLOCKER")],             # legacy: ignored
        "devpipe-precheck/77-net1": [issue("Refunds/RefundController.cs", 2, "MAJOR")],  # .NET base dir = api/
    })
    calls: list = []
    scanner = fake_scanner(calls=calls)
    result = sp.run_precheck(CONN, tmp_path, tmp_path, DIFF, run_id="77", api=api,
                             scanners={"cli": scanner, "dotnet": scanner})
    assert result.status == "failed" and not result.passed
    assert [(b["path"], b["line"]) for b in result.blocking] == [
        ("api/Refunds/RefundController.cs", 2), ("web/src/app/pay.service.ts", 12)]
    assert result.ignored == 1
    assert api.waited == ["T1-cli", "T1-dotnet"]
    assert sorted(api.deleted) == ["devpipe-precheck/77", "devpipe-precheck/77-net1"]
    report = result.report()
    assert "api/Refunds/RefundController.cs:2" in report and "web/src/old.ts" not in report


def test_run_precheck_passes_when_only_legacy_issues(tmp_path):
    api = FakeApi({"devpipe-precheck/1": [issue("web/src/old.ts", 3, "BLOCKER")]})
    diff = DIFF.split("diff --git a/api")[0]
    result = sp.run_precheck(CONN, tmp_path, tmp_path, diff, run_id="1", api=api,
                             scanners={"cli": fake_scanner()})
    assert result.status == "passed" and result.passed and result.ignored == 1
    assert "passed" in result.label()


def test_run_precheck_scanner_failure_is_an_error_not_a_code_problem(tmp_path):
    api = FakeApi({})
    result = sp.run_precheck(CONN, tmp_path, tmp_path, "+++ b/a.py\n@@ -0,0 +1 @@\n+x\n", run_id="2", api=api,
                             scanners={"cli": fake_scanner(returncode=3)})
    assert result.status == "error" and "exited 3" in result.message
    assert api.deleted == ["devpipe-precheck/2"]


def test_run_precheck_analysis_failed_or_no_task_id(tmp_path):
    diff = "+++ b/a.py\n@@ -0,0 +1 @@\n+x\n"
    r = sp.run_precheck(CONN, tmp_path, tmp_path, diff, run_id="3", api=FakeApi({}, status="FAILED"),
                        scanners={"cli": fake_scanner()})
    assert r.status == "error" and "FAILED" in r.message
    r = sp.run_precheck(CONN, tmp_path, tmp_path, diff, run_id="3", api=FakeApi({}),
                        scanners={"cli": fake_scanner(task="")})
    assert r.status == "error" and "ceTaskId" in r.message


def test_run_precheck_api_error_is_an_error(tmp_path):
    class Broken(FakeApi):
        def branch_issues(self, branch):
            raise requests.ConnectionError("down")
    api = Broken({})
    r = sp.run_precheck(CONN, tmp_path, tmp_path, "+++ b/a.py\n@@ -0,0 +1 @@\n+x\n", run_id="4", api=api,
                        scanners={"cli": fake_scanner()})
    assert r.status == "error" and "ConnectionError" in r.message
    assert api.deleted == ["devpipe-precheck/4"]


def test_run_precheck_keep_branch_and_no_changes(tmp_path):
    api = FakeApi({})
    r = sp.run_precheck(CONN, tmp_path, tmp_path, "+++ b/a.py\n@@ -0,0 +1 @@\n+x\n", {"keep_branch": True},
                        run_id="5", api=api, scanners={"cli": fake_scanner()})
    assert r.status == "passed" and api.deleted == []
    assert sp.run_precheck(CONN, tmp_path, tmp_path, "", api=api).status == "skipped"


def test_cli_scanner_missing_is_reported(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(sp.shutil, "which", lambda name: None)

def test_cli_scan_passes_branch_and_keeps_token_out_of_argv(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    seen = {}

    def fake_run(cmd, cwd, capture_output, text, encoding, errors, env, timeout):
        seen.update(cmd=cmd, env=env)
        return subprocess.CompletedProcess(cmd, 0, "using tok-secret", "")
    monkeypatch.setattr(sp.shutil, "which", lambda name: "/bin/sonar-scanner")
    monkeypatch.setattr(sp.subprocess, "run", fake_run)
    monkeypatch.setenv("GITHUB_SHA", "pipeline-sha")
    unit = sp.ScanUnit("app", "cli", tmp_path, "devpipe-precheck/9")
    proc = sp.run_cli_scan(unit, CONN, tmp_path / "rt.txt", ["-Dx=y"])
    assert "-Dsonar.branch.name=devpipe-precheck/9" in seen["cmd"] and "-Dx=y" in seen["cmd"]
    assert "-Dsonar.organization=org" in seen["cmd"]
    assert not any("tok-secret" in a for a in seen["cmd"])
    assert seen["env"]["SONAR_TOKEN"] == "tok-secret" and "GITHUB_SHA" not in seen["env"]
    assert "tok-secret" not in proc.stdout


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- Step 4 wiring + config
def sonar_on(monkeypatch, required=True):
    monkeypatch.setitem(config.settings(), "sonar", {"enabled": True, "precheck": {"required": required}})
    for k, v in (("SONAR_TOKEN", "t"), ("SONAR_HOST_URL", "https://sonarcloud.io"), ("SONAR_PROJECT_KEY", "k")):
        monkeypatch.setenv(k, v)


def test_sonar_gate_required_but_not_configured_stops_the_pipeline(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setitem(config.settings(), "sonar", {"enabled": True, "precheck": {"required": True}})
    monkeypatch.delenv("SONAR_TOKEN", raising=False)
    failure, label, infra = s6.sonar_gate("abc", None)
    assert infra is True and "SONAR_TOKEN" in failure and label.startswith("error")


def test_sonar_gate_optional_and_off_is_skipped(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setitem(config.settings(), "sonar", {"enabled": False})
    assert s6.sonar_gate("abc", None) == (None, "skipped (disabled in config (sonar.enabled: false))", False)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("status,expect_failure,expect_infra", [
    ("failed", True, False), ("error", True, True), ("passed", False, False)])
def test_sonar_gate_maps_precheck_result(monkeypatch, status, expect_failure, expect_infra):
    sonar_on(monkeypatch)
    monkeypatch.setattr(s6, "change_diff", lambda base: "+++ b/a.py\n@@ -0,0 +1 @@\n+x\n")
    seen = {}

    def fake_precheck(conn, app_dir, work_root, diff, opts, run_id, newer_than):
        seen.update(conn=conn, opts=opts)
        blocking = [{"path": "a.py", "line": 1, "rule": "r", "message": "m", "severity": "MAJOR"}]
        return sp.PrecheckResult(status, blocking if status == "failed" else [], 0, ["app"], "boom")
    monkeypatch.setattr(s6, "run_precheck", fake_precheck)
    failure, label, infra = s6.sonar_gate("abc", None)
    assert (failure is not None) is expect_failure and infra is expect_infra
    assert seen["conn"]["project_key"] == "k" and seen["opts"] == {"required": True}
    if status == "failed":
        assert "a.py:1" in failure


def test_non_python_target_now_runs_the_sonar_precheck(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    """Before: non-Python stacks always reported `skipped (non-Python)` and the PR was raised unchecked."""
    monkeypatch.setattr(s6, "target_settings", lambda: {"build_commands": ["npm test"], "app_dir": "."})
    monkeypatch.setattr(s6, "app_dir", lambda: tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(s6, "changed_paths", lambda base: ["a.ts"])
    monkeypatch.setattr(s6, "check_build", lambda *a: type("B", (), {"ok": True, "projects": ["."],
                                                                       "warnings": [], "report": lambda s: ""})())
    monkeypatch.setattr(s6, "sonar_gate", lambda base, started: ("Sonar pre-check FAILED: 1 blocking", "failed (1)", False))
    failures, data = s6.run_gates(80, "abc")
    assert failures == ["Sonar pre-check FAILED: 1 blocking"] and data["sonar_precheck"] == "failed (1)"

    monkeypatch.setattr(s6, "check_build", lambda *a: type("B", (), {"ok": False, "projects": ["."],
                                                                       "warnings": [], "report": lambda s: "err"})())
    failures, data = s6.run_gates(80, "abc")
    assert data["sonar_precheck"] == "not run (build failed)" and len(failures) == 1


def test_project_key_override_per_target_repo(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setitem(config.settings(), "sonar", {"enabled": True, "project_keys": {"org/payments": "org_payments"}})
    monkeypatch.setenv("SONAR_PROJECT_KEY", "shared")
    monkeypatch.setattr(config, "is_external", lambda: True)
    monkeypatch.setattr(config, "resolved_target", lambda: {"full_name": "org/payments"})
    assert config.sonar_project_key() == "org_payments"
    monkeypatch.setattr(config, "resolved_target", lambda: {"full_name": "org/other"})

def test_change_diff_lists_exact_changed_lines_of_a_real_repo(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    """gitops.change_diff + parse_changed_lines on a real git repo: edits, new untracked files, tool output."""
    from dev_pipe import gitops
    monkeypatch.setattr(gitops, "ROOT", tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(gitops, "is_external", lambda: False)
    run = lambda *a: subprocess.run(["git", *a], cwd=tmp_path, check=True, capture_output=True)
    run("init", "-q")
    run("config", "user.email", "t@example.com")
    run("config", "user.name", "t")
    (tmp_path / "app.py").write_text("a = 1\nb = 2\nc = 3\n", encoding="utf-8")
    run("add", "-A")
    run("commit", "-qm", "base")
    base = gitops.head_sha()
    (tmp_path / "app.py").write_text("a = 1\nb = 20\nc = 3\nd = 4\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / "new file.py").write_text("x = 1\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / ".scannerwork").mkdir()
    (tmp_path / ".scannerwork/report-task.txt").write_text("ceTaskId=1\n", encoding="utf-8")
    changed = sp.parse_changed_lines(gitops.change_diff(base))
    assert changed == {"app.py": {2, 4}, "new file.py": {1}}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- Step 5: separate job
def test_step6_no_longer_runs_sonar(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    monkeypatch.setattr(s6, "target_settings", lambda: {"build_commands": ["npm test"], "app_dir": "."})
    monkeypatch.setattr(s6, "app_dir", lambda: tmp_path)

                                                                       "warnings": [], "report": lambda s: ""})())
    monkeypatch.setattr(s6, "sonar_gate", lambda *a: pytest.fail("Step 4 must not run Sonar"))
    failures, data = s6.run_gates(80, "abc", with_sonar=False)
    assert failures == [] and data["sonar_precheck"] == "runs in Step 5"


def _out(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    from dev_pipe import io_utils
    out = tmp_path / "out"
    out.mkdir()
    monkeypatch.setattr(io_utils, "OUT", out)
    monkeypatch.setenv("GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY", str(tmp_path / "summary.md"))
    return out


def test_step6b_skips_when_sonar_optional_and_off(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    import json
    from dev_pipe.steps import s6b_sonar_precheck as s6b
    out = _out(monkeypatch, tmp_path)
    (out / "gates.json").write_text('{"coverage_pct": 90}', encoding="utf-8")
    monkeypatch.setattr(s6b, "sonar_status", lambda: (False, "disabled in config"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(s6b, "sonar_precheck_required", lambda: False)
    monkeypatch.setattr(s6b, "prepare_checkout", lambda: pytest.fail("no checkout needed when skipped"))
    s6b.main()
    gates = json.loads((out / "gates.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert gates == {"coverage_pct": 90, "sonar_precheck": "skipped (disabled in config)"}


@pytest.mark.parametrize("failures,infra,expect_exit", [([], False, None), (["Sonar FAILED"], False, "still there"),
                                                       (["could not run"], True, "could not run")])
def test_step6b_runs_the_fix_loop_with_sonar_and_blocks_the_pr(monkeypatch, tmp_path, failures, infra, expect_exit):
    import json
    from dev_pipe.steps import s6b_sonar_precheck as s6b
    out = _out(monkeypatch, tmp_path)
    (out / "gates.json").write_text('{"coverage_pct": 90, "passed": true}', encoding="utf-8")
    seen = {}
    monkeypatch.setattr(s6b, "OUT", out)
    monkeypatch.setattr(s6b, "sonar_status", lambda: (True, "enabled"))
    monkeypatch.setattr(s6b, "prepare_checkout", lambda: None)
    monkeypatch.setattr(s6b, "head_sha", lambda: "base1")
    monkeypatch.setattr(s6b, "apply_patch", lambda p: seen.setdefault("patch", p.name))
    monkeypatch.setattr(s6b, "snapshot_patch", lambda base: b"diff")

    def fake_loop(threshold, base, max_attempts, with_sonar, step, reuse=None):
        seen.update(base=base, max_attempts=max_attempts, with_sonar=with_sonar, step=step)
        return failures, {"coverage_pct": 91, "sonar_precheck": "label", "sonar_infra_error": infra}, 2
    monkeypatch.setattr(s6b, "fix_loop", fake_loop)
    if expect_exit:
        with pytest.raises(SystemExit, match=expect_exit):
            s6b.main()
    else:
        s6b.main()
    assert seen == {"patch": "changes.patch", "base": "base1", "max_attempts": 3, "with_sonar": True,
                    "step": "fix_sonar"}
    gates = json.loads((out / "gates.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    assert gates["passed"] is (not failures) and gates["coverage_pct"] == 91 and gates["sonar_precheck"] == "label"
    assert (out / "changes.patch").read_bytes() == b"diff"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- coverage + Step 7 baseline scan
def test_coverage_args_skip_stale_reports(tmp_path):
    import os, time
    old = tmp_path / "coverage.xml"
    old.write_text("<coverage/>", encoding="utf-8")
    os.utime(old, (time.time() - 3600, time.time() - 3600))
    assert sp.coverage_args(tmp_path, time.time()) == []
    (tmp_path / "backend").mkdir()
    (tmp_path / "backend" / "coverage.xml").write_text("<coverage/>", encoding="utf-8")
    assert sp.coverage_args(tmp_path, time.time() - 1) == ["-Dsonar.python.coverage.reportPaths=backend/coverage.xml"]


def test_python_project_in_own_venv_writes_coverage_xml(tmp_path):
    from dev_pipe import stack
    (tmp_path / "requirements.txt").write_text("fastapi\n", encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / "tests").mkdir()
    cmd = stack.python_project(tmp_path).commands[-1].cmd
    assert "-m pytest tests" in cmd and "--cov=. --cov-report=xml:coverage.xml" in cmd
    bare = tmp_path / "bare"
    (bare / "tests").mkdir(parents=True)
    assert "--cov" not in stack.python_project(bare).commands[-1].cmd   # pipeline's Python: no pytest-cov promise


def test_cli_scan_without_branch_analyses_main(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    seen = {}
    monkeypatch.setattr(sp.shutil, "which", lambda name: "/bin/sonar-scanner")
    monkeypatch.setattr(sp.subprocess, "run", lambda cmd, **kw: seen.setdefault("cmd", cmd)
                        and subprocess.CompletedProcess(cmd, 0, "", ""))
    sp.run_cli_scan(sp.ScanUnit("baseline", "cli", tmp_path, ""), CONN, tmp_path / "rt.txt", [])
    assert not any(a.startswith("-Dsonar.branch.name") for a in seen["cmd"])


class BranchApi(FakeApi):
    def __init__(self, branches, status="SUCCESS"):
        super().__init__({}, status)
        self._branches = branches

    def branches(self):
        return self._branches


@pytest.mark.parametrize("branches,expect_scan,expect_branch", [
    ([{"name": "main", "isMain": True, "analysisDate": "2026-10-01"}], False, None),     # already analysed
    ([{"name": "main", "isMain": True}], True, ""),                                      # main never analysed
    ([], True, ""),                                                                       # brand-new project
    ([{"name": "master", "isMain": True, "analysisDate": "x"}], True, "main"),           # main analysed, base unknown
])
def test_step7_baseline_scan_only_when_target_branch_has_no_analysis(monkeypatch, tmp_path, branches,
                                                                     expect_scan, expect_branch):
    from dev_pipe.steps import s8_sonarqube_quality_validation as s8
    api = BranchApi(branches)
    scans, gits = [], []
    monkeypatch.setattr(s8, "SonarApi", lambda *a: api)
    monkeypatch.setattr(s8, "app_dir", lambda: tmp_path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(s8, "git", lambda *a, **k: gits.append(a) or "sha1")

    def fake_scan(unit, conn, metadata, extra):
        scans.append(unit.branch)
        metadata.write_text("ceTaskId=B1\n", encoding="utf-8")
        return subprocess.CompletedProcess(["s"], 0, "", "")
    monkeypatch.setattr(s8, "run_cli_scan", fake_scan)
    s8.ensure_base_analysed(CONN, "main", "feature/1-x", 60)
    assert (scans == [expect_branch]) if expect_scan else scans == []
    if expect_scan:
        assert api.waited == ["B1"]
        assert gits[0][:3] == ("checkout", "-q", "--detach") and gits[-1] == ("checkout", "-q", "-B", "feature/1-x",
                                                                            "origin/feature/1-x")


def test_step7_baseline_scan_never_fails_the_step(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
    from dev_pipe.steps import s8_sonarqube_quality_validation as s8

    class Down(BranchApi):
        def branches(self):
            raise requests.ConnectionError("down")
    monkeypatch.setattr(s8, "SonarApi", lambda *a: Down([]))
    monkeypatch.setattr(s8, "git", lambda *a, **k: pytest.fail("no checkout when Sonar is unreachable"))
    s8.ensure_base_analysed(CONN, "main", "feature/1-x", 60)   # no exception

FILE: tests/test_steplog.py

"""Readable step logs + pipeline report (dev_pipe/steplog.py)."""
from __future__ import annotations

import json

import pytest

from dev_pipe import steplog
from dev_pipe.steplog import StepLog, build_report, counts_text, story_lines, trigger_text
from dev_pipe.steplog import test_counts as count_tests

STORY = {"id": 118377, "type": "User Story", "state": "New", "title": "Show the app version",
         "assigned_to": "person@example.com", "iteration_path": "POC\\Sprint 12", "area_path": "POC",
         "description": "As a support engineer...", "acceptance_criteria": "1. First\n- Second\n\n3) Third"}


def test_story_block_has_no_personal_names(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("ADO_ORG_URL", "https://dev.azure.com/org")
    monkeypatch.setenv("ADO_PROJECT", "proj")
    text = "\n".join(story_lines(STORY))
    assert "person@example.com" not in text and "Assigned            : yes" in text
    assert "Acceptance criteria (3):" in text and "  2. Second" in text
    assert "https://dev.azure.com/org/proj/_workitems/edit/118377" in text


def test_trigger_text_has_no_actor(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("GITHUB_EVENT_NAME", "push")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GITHUB_REF_NAME", "feature/dev-pipe")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GITHUB_RUN_NUMBER", "12")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GITHUB_ACTOR", "someone")
    text = trigger_text()
    assert text.startswith("push to feature/dev-pipe | run #12 |") and "someone" not in text


def test_step_block_and_report(tmp_path, capsys, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setenv("DRY_RUN", "false")
    monkeypatch.setenv("GITHUB_ACTIONS", "true")
    (tmp_path / "story.json").write_text(json.dumps(STORY), encoding="utf-8")
    with StepLog("3", "Write the code", out_dir=tmp_path) as log:
        print("[agent] noisy technical line")
        log.heading("Azure DevOps")
        log.ok('Story #118377 moved from "New" to "Active".')
        log.detail("Both tasks copy the story's assignee and sprint.")
        log.fact("1 file(s) changed")
        log.metric("fix_rounds", 2)
        log.result("PASSED", next_step="The AI writes tests and we check quality (Step 4).")
    out = capsys.readouterr().out
    # technical output is folded away, the readable block comes after it
    assert out.index("::group::Step 3 technical details") < out.index("[agent] noisy") < out.index("::endgroup::")
    block = out[out.index("::endgroup::"):]
    assert "==================== STEP 3: WRITE THE CODE ====================" in block
    assert "AZURE DEVOPS" in block and '\u2714 Story #118377 moved from "New" to "Active".' in block
    assert "What happens here" not in out
    assert "Result  : PASSED in" in block and "Next    : The AI writes tests" in block
    steps = json.loads((tmp_path / "steps.json").read_text())
    assert steps[0]["result"] == "PASSED" and steps[0]["metrics"]["fix_rounds"] == 2
    report = (tmp_path / "pipeline-report.md").read_text()
    assert 'Story        : #118377 "Show the app version"' in report
    assert "3   Code" in report and "\u2714 PASSED" in report and "2 fix round(s) needed" in report


def test_failure_is_logged_and_reraised(tmp_path, capsys):
    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
        with StepLog("5", "Quality and security check", out_dir=tmp_path):
            raise SystemExit("still failing after 3 fix attempts")
    out = capsys.readouterr().out
    assert "\u2718 Stopped: still failing after 3 fix attempts" in out
    assert "Result  : FAILED" in out
    assert "STOPPED AT STEP 5" in (tmp_path / "pipeline-report.md").read_text()


def test_rerun_replaces_row_and_rows_are_ordered(tmp_path):
    for key, num in (("5", "5"), ("1-2", "1-2"), ("8-approval", "8"), ("8-ado", "8"), ("5", "5")):
        steplog.record_step(key, num, f"step {key}", "PASSED", out=tmp_path)
    steps = json.loads((tmp_path / "steps.json").read_text())
    assert len(steps) == 4
    report = build_report(steps, tmp_path)
    assert report.index("1-2 Plan") < report.index("5   Quality check") < report.index("8   Approval") \
        < report.index("8   ADO update")
    assert "RESULT: COMPLETED" in report


@pytest.mark.parametrize("text,expected", [
    ("14 passed in 0.52s", (14, 0, 0)),
    ("==== 3 failed, 10 passed, 1 skipped in 2.1s ====", (10, 3, 1)),
    ("      Tests  5 passed (5)\n Test Files  1 passed (1)", (5, 0, 0)),
    ("Tests:       2 failed, 5 passed, 7 total", (5, 2, 0)),
    ("Passed!  - Failed:     0, Passed:    12, Skipped:     1, Total: 13", (12, 0, 1)),
])
def test_test_counts(text, expected):
    c = count_tests(text)
    assert (c["passed"], c["failed"], c["skipped"]) == expected


def test_no_counts():
    assert count_tests("compiled fine") is None
    assert counts_text(None).startswith("n/a")


def test_quality_gate_table():
    from dev_pipe.steps.s8_sonarqube_quality_validation import gate_table
    rows = gate_table({"status": "OK", "conditions": [
        {"metricKey": "new_coverage", "comparator": "LT", "errorThreshold": "80", "actualValue": "95.0", "status": "OK"},
        {"metricKey": "new_reliability_rating", "comparator": "GT", "errorThreshold": "1", "actualValue": "1",
         "status": "OK"}]})
    assert "Coverage on new code" in rows[1] and "95.0%" in rows[1] and ">= 80.0%" in rows[1] and rows[1].endswith("OK")
    assert "Reliability rating" in rows[2] and "<= A" in rows[2]


def test_new_test_names_and_test_paths():
    from dev_pipe.report_data import is_test_path, new_test_names
    diff = """+++ b/backend/tests/test_health.py
@@ -0,0 +1,5 @@
+def test_health_returns_version():
+    assert True
+async def test_health_default():
+++ b/frontend/src/App.test.jsx
@@ -1,0 +2,2 @@
+  it('shows the version', () => {})
+++ b/backend/app/main.py
@@ -1 +1 @@
+def test_not_a_test_file():
"""
    assert new_test_names(diff) == {"backend/tests/test_health.py": ["test_health_returns_version",
                                                                    "test_health_default"],
                                    "frontend/src/App.test.jsx": ["shows the version"]}
    assert is_test_path("backend/tests/test_x.py") and is_test_path("src/a.spec.ts")
    assert not is_test_path("backend/app/main.py")


def test_file_coverage_from_cobertura_and_lcov(tmp_path):
    from dev_pipe.report_data import file_coverage
    (tmp_path / "backend").mkdir()
    (tmp_path / "backend" / "coverage.xml").write_text(
        f'<coverage><sources><source>{tmp_path / "backend"}</source></sources><packages><package><classes>'
        '<class filename="app/main.py" line-rate="0.96"/></classes></package></packages></coverage>', encoding="utf-8")
    (tmp_path / "frontend" / "coverage").mkdir(parents=True)
    (tmp_path / "frontend" / "coverage" / "lcov.info").write_text(
        "SF:frontend/src/App.jsx\nLF:10\nLH:8\nend_of_record\n", encoding="utf-8")
    got = file_coverage(tmp_path, ["backend/app/main.py", "frontend/src/App.jsx", "other.py"])
    assert got == {"backend/app/main.py": 96.0, "frontend/src/App.jsx": 80.0}


def test_command_steps_in_plain_words():
    from dev_pipe.report_data import command_steps
    log = "$ /v/bin/python -m pip install -q -r requirements.txt  (exit 0)\n$ /v/bin/python -m compileall -q .  (exit 0)\n" \
          "$ COVERAGE_FILE=x /v/bin/python -m pytest tests -p no:cacheprovider  (exit 1)\n"
    assert command_steps(log) == [("Installed dependencies", True), ("Compiled / built the code", True),
                                  ("Ran the tests", False)]

FILE: sample_app/app/main.py

"""Task Tracker -- a small FastAPI app that exists as a demo target for
the Dev Agent to extend per ADO work items, and for the QE Agent to
generate/execute tests against. Not production infrastructure."""
from fastapi import FastAPI, HTTPException

from app.models import Task, TaskCreate, TaskUpdate
from app.storage import TaskNotFoundError, TaskStore

app = FastAPI(title="Task Tracker", version="0.1.0")
store = TaskStore()


@app.get("/health")
def health() -> dict[str, str]:
    return {"status": "ok"}


@app.get("/tasks", response_model=list[Task])
def list_tasks() -> list[Task]:
    return store.list_tasks()


@app.post("/tasks", response_model=Task, status_code=201)
def create_task(data: TaskCreate) -> Task:
    return store.create_task(data)


@app.get("/tasks/{task_id}", response_model=Task)
def get_task(task_id: int) -> Task:
    try:
        return store.get_task(task_id)
    except TaskNotFoundError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=str(exc)) from exc


@app.patch("/tasks/{task_id}", response_model=Task)
def update_task(task_id: int, data: TaskUpdate) -> Task:
    try:
        return store.update_task(task_id, data)
    except TaskNotFoundError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=str(exc)) from exc


@app.delete("/tasks/{task_id}", status_code=204)
def delete_task(task_id: int) -> None:
    try:
        store.delete_task(task_id)
    except TaskNotFoundError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=str(exc)) from exc

FILE: sample_app/app/models.py

"""Domain model for the Task Tracker sample app."""
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field


class TaskCreate(BaseModel):
    title: str = Field(min_length=1, max_length=200)
    description: str = ""


class TaskUpdate(BaseModel):
    title: str | None = Field(default=None, min_length=1, max_length=200)
    description: str | None = None
    done: bool | None = None


class Task(BaseModel):
    id: int
    title: str
    description: str = ""
    done: bool = False

....................................................................................................

FILE: sample_app/app/storage.py

"""In-memory task storage. Deliberately simple (no DB) -- this app exists
as a demo target for the Dev Agent to extend per ADO work items, not as
production infrastructure."""
from app.models import Task, TaskCreate, TaskUpdate


class TaskNotFoundError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, task_id: int):
        super().__init__(f"Task {task_id} not found")
        self.task_id = task_id


class TaskStore:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._tasks: dict[int, Task] = {}
        self._next_id = 1

    def list_tasks(self) -> list[Task]:
        return list(self._tasks.values())

    def create_task(self, data: TaskCreate) -> Task:
        task = Task(id=self._next_id, title=data.title, description=data.description, done=False)
        self._tasks[task.id] = task
        self._next_id += 1
        return task

    def get_task(self, task_id: int) -> Task:
        task = self._tasks.get(task_id)
        if task is None:
            raise TaskNotFoundError(task_id)
        return task

    def update_task(self, task_id: int, data: TaskUpdate) -> Task:
        task = self.get_task(task_id)
        updated = task.model_copy(
            update={k: v for k, v in data.model_dump(exclude_unset=True).items() if v is not None}
        )
        self._tasks[task_id] = updated
        return updated

    def delete_task(self, task_id: int) -> None:
        if task_id not in self._tasks:
            raise TaskNotFoundError(task_id)
        del self._tasks[task_id]

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.main import app, store


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def reset_store():
    store._tasks.clear()
    store._next_id = 1
    yield


@pytest.fixture
def client():
    return TestClient(app)


def test_health(client):
    resp = client.get("/health")
    assert resp.status_code == 200
    assert resp.json() == {"status": "ok"}


def test_list_tasks_empty(client):
    resp = client.get("/tasks")
    assert resp.status_code == 200
    assert resp.json() == []


def test_create_and_get_task(client):
    resp = client.post("/tasks", json={"title": "Write docs", "description": "for the API"})
    assert resp.status_code == 201
    body = resp.json()
    assert body["title"] == "Write docs"
    assert body["done"] is False

    resp2 = client.get(f"/tasks/{body['id']}")
    assert resp2.status_code == 200
    assert resp2.json() == body


def test_create_task_requires_title(client):
    resp = client.post("/tasks", json={"title": ""})
    assert resp.status_code == 422


def test_get_missing_task_returns_404(client):
    resp = client.get("/tasks/999")
    assert resp.status_code == 404


def test_update_task(client):
    created = client.post("/tasks", json={"title": "Task A"}).json()
    resp = client.patch(f"/tasks/{created['id']}", json={"done": True})
    assert resp.status_code == 200
    assert resp.json()["done"] is True
    assert resp.json()["title"] == "Task A"


def test_update_missing_task_returns_404(client):
    resp = client.patch("/tasks/999", json={"done": True})
    assert resp.status_code == 404


def test_delete_task(client):
    created = client.post("/tasks", json={"title": "Temp"}).json()
    resp = client.delete(f"/tasks/{created['id']}")
    assert resp.status_code == 204
    assert client.get(f"/tasks/{created['id']}").status_code == 404


def test_delete_missing_task_returns_404(client):
    resp = client.delete("/tasks/999")
    assert resp.status_code == 404


def test_list_tasks_returns_created_items(client):
    client.post("/tasks", json={"title": "A"})
    client.post("/tasks", json={"title": "B"})
    resp = client.get("/tasks")
    assert len(resp.json()) == 2
